# EFFECTS_QUICK_REFERENCE
- `SearchLibraryForCreatureWithSameTotalPowerToughnessEffect()` — Wild Pair trigger marker; the enter-trigger collector captures the entering creature's effective power and toughness, offers the controller a may choice, and delegates the exact-total creature search to `SearchLibraryEffect(..., BATTLEFIELD)` using existing predicates. The captured values provide last-known information if the entering creature leaves before resolution.
- `UntapSearchedPermanentsEffect()` — untaps every permanent placed onto the battlefield by the preceding library search without targeting.
- `AnyPlayerMayDiscardCardsToCounterSpellEffect(int cardsToDiscard)` — ON_SELF_CAST: offers each player in APNAP order the option to discard exactly that many cards; a player who does counters the triggering spell, and remaining players still receive their choices. Used by Phantasmagorian (PLC).
- `AnyPlayerMayPayLifeToCounterSpellEffect(int lifeCost)` / `(DynamicAmount lifeCost)` — ON_SELF_CAST: offers each player the life payment in APNAP order; a payment counters the triggering spell immediately, while remaining players still receive their choices. Dynamic amounts are evaluated separately for each player. Used by Dash Hopes and Temporal Extortion (PLC).
- `ChoosePowerToughnessFormEffect(List<PowerToughnessForm>)` — entry/face-up marker for a permanent that becomes one of the named base P/T forms. Each form may also carry keywords that are stamped persistently with the selected base P/T. Add the same effect to `ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD` and `ON_TURNED_FACE_UP` when the choice is made both on entry and after morph (Aquamorph Entity); the selected values are stamped as durable base-P/T overrides before ETB or face-up triggers continue.
- `CreateTokenWithTotalDyingCreaturesPowerEffect(CreateTokenEffect tokenTemplate)` — `ON_ALLY_NONTOKEN_CREATURE_DIES` trigger marker: one simultaneous death event creates one token whose base power and toughness equal the summed last-known effective power of the controller's nontoken creatures that died. The trigger collector snapshots the total before delegating to the ordinary token-creation handler. Used by The Skullspore Nexus
- `DiscardCardOrSacrificePermanentCost(PermanentPredicate, String)` — additional cast cost: discard a card or sacrifice one matching permanent. Exactly one option uses `discardHandCardIndex` or `sacrificePermanentId`.
- `SetPowerToughnessToAmountEffect(a, new Sum(a, new Fixed(1)))` with `a = CardsInGraveyard(new CardIsPermanentPredicate(), CountScope.CONTROLLER)` — dynamic power/toughness for a creature whose power counts permanent cards in its controller's graveyard and whose toughness is one higher.
- `MayCastDiscardedCardWithoutPayingManaCostEffect()` — after a discard follow-up, offer that exact nonland card from its controller's graveyard for a free cast when the source has at least four chorus counters. Malcolm, Alluring Scoundrel.
- `ChooseCreatureForEachPlayerDestroyRestEffect(PermanentPredicate choiceFilter)` — the controller chooses one matching creature for each player, then all other creatures are destroyed (Unstable Glyphbridge)
- `OpponentCantAttackSourceControllerThisTurnEffect()` — resolves to a player-scoped until-end-of-turn restriction for the opponent whose spell caused the trigger (Sandswirl Wanderglyph)
- `ControllerCanCastSpellsOnlyDuringOwnTurnEffect()` - static restriction that limits only this permanent's controller to casting spells during their own turn; Fires of Invention
- `AlternativeCostForSpellsEffect.zeroManaValueAtMost(CardPredicate, DynamicAmount)` - lets the applicable player cast matching spells for free when their mana value is at most the evaluated dynamic cap; Fires of Invention
- `CreateTokenWhenEquippedCreatureDealsCombatDamageToCreatureEffect(CreateTokenEffect)` — `ON_ALLY_CREATURE_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_CREATURE` Equipment marker, combat-only: when the equipped creature deals combat damage to a creature, expands into the wrapped token creation effect. Giant's Skewer
- `SpellCastTriggerEffect(CardPredicate, List<CardEffect>)` + `StackEntryManaValueEqualsSourcePowerPredicate` — gate a spell-cast trigger on the cast spell's mana value equaling the source permanent's effective power (Jackal, Genius Geneticist)
- `GrantColorEffect(CardColor, GrantScope.TARGET, true)` — resolving targeted ability form: permanently replaces the target's colors; the normal handler stores it as a floating layer-5 effect.
- `GrantSubtypeEffect(CardSubtype, GrantScope.TARGET, true)` — resolving targeted ability form: permanently replaces the target's creature subtypes; the normal handler stores it as a floating layer-4 effect.
- `PutCounterOnTargetForEachLeavingSourceCounterEffect(CounterType[, PermanentPredicate])` — leaves-the-battlefield trigger: snapshot the named counter type on the source and put that many counters of the same type on the target creature. The trigger still targets when the snapshot count is zero; use it for text without an intervening-if condition (Selfless Police Captain)
- `EmblemStepTriggerEffect(EmblemTriggerStep.OPPONENT_DRAW_STEP, effects, reminderText)` - emblem marker for "at the beginning of each opponent's draw step"; `StepTriggerService` queues the effects for the emblem controller, so an emblem controlled by player A makes A draw while player B's draw step is active (Teferi, Who Slows the Sunset -7)
- `SOURCE_PERMANENT_COLORS` on `AwardAnyColorManaEffect` restricts the choice to the source permanent's current colors and produces no mana when it is colorless (Katilda, Dawnhart Prime).
- `DestroyTargetPermanentThenEffect(EventStat.BASIC_LAND_SEARCH_COUNT, SearchLibraryEffect(EventValue(), basicLand(), BATTLEFIELD_TAPPED), TARGET_CONTROLLER)` — Dire-Strain Rampage: snapshots two only when a targeted land is actually destroyed; otherwise the restricted search count is one.
- `MayPayLifeAndDrawEqualToDyingPowerEffect()` — `ON_EQUIPPED_CREATURE_DIES` marker: snapshot the equipped creature's last-known effective power, then offer the controller an optional payment of that much life; accepting draws that many cards (Mask of Griselbrand).
- `NoteControllerLifeTotalEffect()` — stores the source permanent controller's current life total in the permanent's note state; reusable for effects that compare against a prior noted life total.
- `DrawCardIfControllerLifeTotalAtLeastNotedEffect()` — `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`: draws one card when the source permanent controller's life total is at least the source's noted total, then updates the note to the current life total (Sigarda's Splendor).
- `EnduringAngelLifeTotalReplacementEffect()` — STATIC replacement: when the controller's life total would be reduced to 0 or less, the loss path transforms the source permanent to its back face and sets that player's life total to 3; if it cannot transform, the normal life-loss outcome applies
- `PreventAllDamageDealtByEnchantedCreatureEffect([boolean combatOnly])` — STATIC aura effect: prevent all damage dealt by the enchanted creature (default), or only its combat damage when `combatOnly=true` (Candletrap); damage dealt to it is unaffected
- `AnyPlayerMayTakeDamageOrTargetPlayerDrawEffect(int damage, int drawCount)` — SPELL: players in APNAP order may have the spell deal damage to them; the first acceptance deals damage and ends the choices, otherwise the targeted player draws the fallback amount; Browbeat (5, 3)
- `GrantAllCreatureTypesToOwnCreaturesEffect([GrantScope scope])` / `.toSelf()` — STATIC layer 4: creatures in the scope are every creature type; the default `OWN_CREATURES` mode also covers owned creature cards outside the battlefield, while `.toSelf()` is the all-zones characteristic-defining form used by Mistform Ultimus
- `LoseAllButOneLifeEffect()` - ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD: the controller loses all but 1 life and the actual life lost is recorded on the source permanent.
- `GainLifeEqualToLifeLostWhenEnteredEffect()` - ON_SELF_LEAVES_BATTLEFIELD marker: the controller gains the amount recorded by `LoseAllButOneLifeEffect()` when its source entered.

- `BoostCreaturesSharingColorWithTopCardEffect(int, int)` - static anthem: when the controller's
  top library card is a creature card, creatures they control sharing at least one color with it
  get the stated boost; colorless top creature cards match nothing (Crown of Convergence)

- `ExileHandFaceDownWithSourceEffect()` - exile every card from the controller's hand face down tracked with the source permanent. Bottled Cloister's opponent-upkeep trigger.
- `PutAllCardsExiledWithSourceIntoOwnersHandsEffect(boolean onlyControllerOwned)` - return source-tracked cards to their owners' hands; when true, return only cards owned by the ability controller. Bottled Cloister uses true.
- `ChooseAttackersThisCombatEffect()` — "You choose which creatures attack this turn" (Master Warcraft). Registers a `DelayedAttackerDeclarationControl`; the declare-attackers `PendingInteraction` gets `chooserId` = the resolving controller. Cleared at end of combat.
- `ExileTargetCreatureCardFromGraveyardAndBecomeCopyEffect()` — exile target creature card from any graveyard, then make the source a copy of it and retain the source's activated abilities (Dimir Doppelganger)
- `CreatureSpellAdditionalCountersCostEffect()` — STATIC global marker used by Chorus of the Conclave: creature spells may pay any number of generic `{1}` payments as an additional cost, and the creature enters with that many additional +1/+1 counters. The cost is injected by `AdditionalSpellCostService`; the counters use the spell's additional-entry-counter snapshot.
- `GrantStaticEffectToSourceEffect(CardEffect[, EffectDuration])` — resolution-time effect that makes the source permanent carry a layer-6 static effect for the chosen source-linked duration; use when an Aura gains a static ability that follows its current attachment (Earthbind)
- `DamageDealtToControllerByArtifactsThisTurn()` — `DynamicAmount` for damage dealt to the controller by artifact sources this turn; Reverse Polarity uses `Scaled(..., 2)` for its life gain

- `DealDamageToTargetCreatureDamagedPlayerControlsEffect(DynamicAmount, boolean)` uses the damaged player (or a targeted planeswalker's controller) as the chooser when the boolean is true; Flaming Gambit combines it with `MayEffect` and `ConditionalReplacementEffect` for its optional redirection.

- `SearchLibraryForCardsToTopEffect.exact(DynamicAmount)` — search for a quantity of cards and put that many, or as many as possible, on top after shuffling; the chosen cards are not publicly revealed (Insidious Dreams)
- `AnyPlayerMayExileCardFromGraveyardAndAssignNoCombatDamageEffect(int cardsToExile)` — ON_ATTACK / ON_BLOCK: offers each player with at least `cardsToExile` cards in their graveyard the option to exile exactly that many cards; every accepted exile suppresses combat damage from the source permanent for the turn (Carrion Rats uses the no-arg one-card form; Carrion Wurm uses `(3)`).
- `ReduceCastCostForSameNameCardsInGraveyardEffect()` - spells you cast cost {1} less for each matching non-token card in your graveyard; reduces generic mana only and excludes the spell itself when casting from a graveyard (Locket of Yesterdays)
- `TargetPowerPlusToughness()` - target permanent's effective power plus toughness at resolution, preserving negative values. Combine with `Max(Fixed(0), ...)` for Phthisis.
- `RevealTopCardsAndSeparateEffect(5, CardPileDisposition.ONE_FROM_CHOSEN_HAND_AND_BOTTOM, true)` — Truth or Tale: the controller separates five revealed cards, an opponent chooses a pile, the controller chooses one card from that pile for hand, and all other cards are ordered onto the library bottom.
- `SkipNextEffect(SkipKind.UNTAP_STEP, SkipRecipient.EACH_OPPONENT)` — queues one skipped untap step for every player other than the resolving controller; non-targeting. Used by Brine Elemental (`TSP`).
- `PutTargetCardFromExileIntoOwnersGraveyardEffect()` - SPELL: puts target face-up exiled card into its owner's graveyard; supports any player's exile and uses the normal graveyard replacement/trigger pipeline. Used by Pull from Eternity (`TSP`)
- `PreventFixedDamageFromSpellsEffect(int amount)` - STATIC prevention; prevents `amount` damage from each spell damage event to each permanent or player. Global across controllers and spell types; combat, abilities, and permanent-sourced fight/bite damage are unaffected, and the effect is ignored when damage cannot be prevented. Used by Plated Pegasus
- `RemoveTimeCounterFromExiledCardEffect(UUID cardId)` - suspend upkeep trigger: remove one time counter from the owner's suspended exiled card; on the last counter, offer its owner a free cast through `MayPlayExiledCardWithoutPayingManaCostEffect` with haste granted to a creature cast this way (Duskrider Peregrine)
- `ON_SELF_TIME_COUNTER_REMOVED_FROM_EXILE` - non-targeting trigger slot for a card suspended in exile; it fires whenever one of that card's time counters is removed, including the last counter (Benalish Commander)
- `PutTimeCountersOnSuspendedCardEffect(int amount)` - contingent follow-up for a suspended card's upkeep ability: after the active player actually sacrifices a permanent, adds the specified number of time counters to the still-suspended source card (Curse of the Cabal)
- `SourceCardSuspended()` - intervening condition for suspended-card triggers; true only while the source card remains in exile with a positive time-counter entry (Curse of the Cabal)
- `RemoveTimeCounterWhenOpponentCastsSpellEffect()` - suspended-card trigger descriptor: when an opponent casts a spell, queue `RemoveTimeCounterFromExiledCardEffect` for this card; only fires while the card has a time counter in exile (Deep-Sea Kraken)
- `DamageToTargetCreatureCantBePreventedOrRedirectedThisTurnEffect()` - activated ability (harmful CREATURE target): damage that would be dealt to the target creature this turn can't be prevented or redirected to another permanent or player. Marks the permanent so both combat and noncombat damage skip prevention and redirection for that recipient. Used by Whippoorwill
- `ExileTargetCreatureDrawPowerReturnAtControllerUpkeepDiscardToughnessEffect()` — SPELL: exile the target creature, draw cards equal to its effective power before exile, then return the card under its owner's control at the effect controller's next upkeep and discard cards equal to the returned permanent's effective toughness. Tokens and cards that return without being creatures do not cause the discard. Used by Vanish into Memory.
- `PutCountersOnStackEntryCardEffect(CounterType[, int count])` — counters on the source spell's stack object; use for spells with activated abilities that refer to counters on that spell (Lightning Storm). `CountersOnStackEntryCard(CounterType)` reads the resulting count as a `DynamicAmount`.
- `BalduvianWarlordEffect()` — activated ability targeting a blocking creature; removes it from combat, makes a former attacker unblocked only when no other creature blocked it earlier in the combat, then lets the ability controller choose a legal attacking creature for the removed blocker to block. Reapplies the normal block and relevant block/becomes-blocked trigger pipeline (Balduvian Warlord)
`GuidedPassageEffect()` — reveal the whole controller library; an opponent chooses one available creature, land, and noncreature, nonland card through `PendingInteraction.GuidedPassageChoice`, the chosen cards go to hand, and the library is shuffled. In multiplayer the controller first chooses which opponent makes the card choice.
- `DestroyTwoTargetCreaturesIfSameColorsEffect()` — destroys two target creatures without allowing regeneration only when their effective color sets exactly match; colors are checked on resolution (Dead Ringers)
- `MayPayPayer.ANY_OTHER_PLAYER` — resolution-time `MayPayManaEffect` payer mode that offers the payment in APNAP order to every player except the triggering spell's caster, carried in the trigger entry's `activePlayerId` (Ice Cave)
- `CounterUnlessOtherPlayerPaysManaCostOnSpellCastEffect()` — ON_ANY_PLAYER_CASTS_SPELL: any player other than the spell's caster may pay that spell's printed mana cost, including the chosen value of X, to counter it (Ice Cave); the collector snapshots the concrete cost and queues `MayPayManaEffect` with `MayPayPayer.ANY_OTHER_PLAYER`
- `DestroyTargetPermanentThenDealManaValueDamageToTargetCreatureEffect(int damageTargetGroup)` — destroy the primary target, then deal damage equal to its mana value to the creature chosen in the separate target group; the mana value is captured before destruction and the spell is the damage source (Orim's Thunder)
- `OpponentsCantSearchLibrariesEffect()` — spells and abilities controlled by this permanent's opponents can't cause their controller to search that controller's library (Ashiok, Dream Render); passive rules-modifier checked at search initiation with the resolving spell or ability controller
- `ChooseNonlandCardNameRevealTopCardsToHandRestToGraveyardEffect(int count)` - controller names a nonland card, reveals the top `count` cards of their library, puts matching cards into hand, and puts the rest into the graveyard (Tamiyo, Collector of Tales = 4)
- `OpponentEffectsCantCauseDiscardEffect()` - static effect preventing opponent-controlled spells and abilities from causing the controller to discard cards (Tamiyo, Collector of Tales)
- `AllowCastFromTopOfLibraryByPayingLifeEqualToManaValueEffect()` — static permission to cast nonland spells from the top of your library by paying life equal to mana value instead of mana cost (Bolas's Citadel)
- `ExileTopCardsRepeatIfLastNonlandCreateTokensEffect(int cardsPerIteration, CreateTokenEffect tokenTemplate)` - exile full groups from the controller's library, repeat while the last card in a full group is nonland, then create one token per nonland card exiled; a short final group does not repeat (Rally the Horde)
- `ExileTopCardsMayPlayThisTurnAndCreateTokensEffect(int count, CreateTokenEffect landToken, CreateTokenEffect nonlandToken)` - exile the top `count` cards of the controller's library, grant normal-cost play permission until end of turn, then create one `landToken` if any exiled card is a land and one `nonlandToken` if any exiled card is a nonland (Bonehoard Dracosaur)
- `PainsRewardEffect()` — SPELL: controller chooses the opening life bid, then players top the high bid in turn order; the high bidder loses that much life and draws four cards (Pain's Reward)
- `SetSelfBasePowerToughnessFromCombatOpponentEffect()` — combat-trigger effect: sets the source's base P/T to the current effective P/T of its combat opponent until end of turn; the opponent is carried as the trigger's non-targeting target (Shape Stealer)

- `CombatTapCostEffect` - static combat cost paid by tapping untapped creatures that were not declared as attackers or blockers in the combat. `tapCount()` is paid once per restricted creature as the declaration is committed; `CombatTapCostService` chooses eligible creatures in battlefield order and fires normal tap triggers. Impl `CantAttackOrBlockUnlessTapEffect` (Hollow Warrior)

- `ExileTokensWithSameNameAsLeavingCreatureEffect()` — `ON_ANOTHER_CREATURE_LEAVES_BATTLEFIELD`: the collector binds the name of a leaving nontoken creature, then the handler exiles every token with that name across all battlefields. Dual Nature
- `CreateTokenCopyOfTargetPermanentEffect.trackedForTargetController()` — internal global-enter form: records created tokens under the source and creates them for the copied permanent's controller. Dual Nature

- `MillActivePlayerAndCreateTokensByManaValueEffect(CreateTokenEffect tokenTemplate)` — the active player mills one card, then creates that card's mana value in copies of the supplied token blueprint under their control (Infernal Genesis). Untargeted; creates nothing on an empty library, if a replacement effect diverts the card, or when its mana value is 0

- `ChooseCardsFromTargetHandEffect(..., grantPlayPermission, returnAtNextEndStep)` — an EXILE choice can grant the chooser normal-cost permission to cast the card and return it to its owner's hand at the next end step if it remains exiled (Psychic Theft, PCY)
- `SpectersShriekEffect()` — target opponent reveals their hand and may have you choose a nonland card to exile; if the chosen card is nonblack and you have a card in hand, you exile one of your hand cards (Specter's Shriek, ELD)

- `DealDamageToEnchantedPermanentControllerEffect(DynamicAmount amount)`; `(int damage)` — non-targeted Aura damage to the current controller of the permanent attached to the source Aura; uses the normal damage prevention, multiplier, and outcome pipeline. Gremlin Infestation

- `ExileTargetNonlandCardFromGraveyardAndSearchSameNameEffect()` — exile target nonland card from your graveyard, then search your library for any number of cards with the same name, reveal them, put them into your hand, and shuffle. Secret Salvage

- `CreateTokenCopyOfEnchantedPermanentEffect()` — create a token copy of the permanent attached to the source Aura; uses the attachment and its last-known snapshot at resolution (Mechanized Production)

- `DiscardIfPlayerDealtDamageThisWayEffect()` — same-resolution rider: each player actually dealt damage by an earlier effect in the stack entry discards one card; fully prevented damage and damage to permanents do not qualify

- `ScryIfPlayerDealtDamageThisWayEffect()` â€” same-resolution rider: queues `ScryEffect(1)` when an earlier effect in the stack entry actually dealt damage to a player; fully prevented damage and damage to permanents do not qualify

`SearchLibraryEffect(CardPredicate, LibrarySearchDestination, CounterType)` additionally puts one
counter of the specified type on each permanent entering from that search before its
enters-the-battlefield triggers are processed (Magmatic Hellkite).

- `CopyTriggeringSpellEffect()` — copies the spell that caused the resolving spell-cast trigger; targeted copies use the normal choose-new-targets prompt
- `ExileTriggeringSpellWithSuspendCountersEffect(int counters)` — removes the triggering spell from the stack, exiles it under its owner's identity, and tracks its time counters
- `RemoveSuspendCounterFromExiledSpellEffect(UUID cardId)` — upkeep effect for suspend tracking; removes one time counter and queues the shared free-cast choice after the last one, granting haste to creature spells

- `CastSameNameCardFromGraveyardOnSpellCastEffect()` — `ON_CONTROLLER_CASTS_SPELL` trigger for an instant or sorcery cast from hand; snapshots that spell's name, chooses a matching card from the controller's graveyard, then offers to cast it for its normal cost through `CastTargetInstantOrSorceryFromGraveyardEffect` (Harness the Storm)
- `SearchSameNameCardToBattlefieldOnArtifactSpellCastEffect()` — `ON_CONTROLLER_CASTS_SPELL` trigger descriptor for an artifact spell; snapshots the cast spell's name and offers the controller a may choice whose accepted branch reuses `SearchZonesForCardNamedToBattlefieldEffect` (Mishra, Artificer Prodigy)

- `TargetPlayerSacrificesCreatureAndPlaneswalkerEffect()` — target player sacrifices a creature object and a planeswalker object when possible. A single permanent that is both can satisfy only one requirement, so the handler prompts for two distinct permanents when a valid pair exists and sacrifices the chosen permanents simultaneously. Used by To the Slaughter's delirium branch
- `EachOpponentSacrificesArtifactAndNonartifactCreatureEffect()` — each opponent chooses an artifact creature and a nonartifact creature when possible; if only one category is present, only one creature is sacrificed. Choices are made in APNAP order and all selected creatures are sacrificed simultaneously. Used by Perilous Predicament

- `ReturnToHandEffect.self()` supplies `SOURCE_SELF` combat-trigger context when used in `ON_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER`, so self-bounce effects retain the source permanent while remaining player-only triggers.
- `ColorlessSubtypeSpellsOrAbilities(CardSubtype)` — fixed colorless mana spendable only for colorless spells or activated abilities of the given subtype; uses a dedicated bucket and is used by Eldrazi Temple.

- `CreateTokenCopyOfSourceEffect(removeLegendary, amount, grantHaste, exileAtEndStep)` — plain source copies may gain haste and be exiled at the beginning of the next end step (Splinter Twin)

- `ExileTargetPlayerGraveyardCardsEffect(CardPredicate filter, int lifeLossPerExiledCard, int lifeGainPerExiledCard)` — exact-X spell targeting a player, then choosing X matching cards from that player's graveyard; cards still in that graveyard are exiled at resolution and the per-card life loss/gain uses the cards actually exiled (Suffer the Past)

- `CasterLosesLifeOnChosenColorSpellCastEffect(int amount)` — ON_ANY_PLAYER_CASTS_SPELL: the caster loses N life when the spell contains the source permanent's chosen color (Curse of Wizardry); the collector presets the caster as the life-loss target
- `ChooseCreatureTypeCost()` - SPELL-slot additional cast cost that records a chosen creature subtype without consuming a resource; the choice rides on `PlayCardRequest.beholdCreatureType` and is stored on the entering permanent for source-relative effects such as Caller of the Hunt.

- `PutSelfOnTopOfLibraryAtEndOfCombatEffect()` — schedule the source permanent to be put on top of its owner's library at end of combat. Put on ON_ATTACK / ON_BLOCK; the delayed action skips the source if it already left the battlefield
- `ForcedCostOrElseEffect.enchantedControllerMayPay(PutCounterOnControlledCreatureCost(...), ...)` asks the stack `targetId` player to choose a creature they control for the counter; no eligible creature means the fallback resolves immediately (Tourach's Chant).

- `GrantKeywordToBlockingCreaturesUnlessAttackerPaysEffect(Keyword, PermanentPredicate, String)` - snapshots matching attacking creatures, queues one independent payment choice for each controller, and if unpaid grants the configured keyword until end of turn to creatures controlled by the ability's controller blocking that attacker (Tidal Flats).

- `PreventCombatDamageFromAttackingCreaturesUnlessPaysEffect(PermanentPredicate, String)` — one independent payment choice per matching attacking creature; its controller pays the supplied mana cost or that creature's combat damage is prevented for the turn (Heroism). Matching attackers are snapshotted when the ability resolves and choices are offered in APNAP order.

- `FirstMulticoloredSpellCastTriggerEffect(List<CardEffect>)` — "whenever a player casts their first multicolored spell each turn"; checks the multicolored-spell count independently for the casting player in `ON_ANY_PLAYER_CASTS_SPELL`, then reuses the generic spell-cast trigger stack plumbing (Zenith Chronicler)
- `FirstNoncreatureSpellCastTriggerEffect(List<CardEffect>)` — "whenever the first noncreature spell of a turn is cast"; checks the noncreature-spell count globally across all players in `ON_ANY_PLAYER_CASTS_SPELL`, then reuses the generic spell-cast trigger stack plumbing (Nullstone Gargoyle)

- `MustAttackPlayerEffect()` - static marker for a creature that must attack a player each combat if able; used by the temporary targeted requirement below
- `TargetCreatureMustAttackPlayerUntilNextTurnEffect()` - targets up to one creature and gives it a floating requirement to attack a player each combat if able until the ability controller's next turn; planeswalker attack targets do not satisfy it (Nahiri, the Unforgiving)

`SacrificeOtherCreatureThenRevealUntilLowerManaValueEffect(CardPredicate)` — optional effect that
prompts for another creature, then reveals until a matching card with strictly lesser mana value;
the found card enters the battlefield and the rest go to the bottom in random order (Kethek,
Crucible Goliath).

`SacrificeAnotherCreatureDrawAndMayPutPermanentEffect()` — optional attack-trigger effect that
sacrifices another creature, draws cards equal to its mana value, then offers a permanent card
with mana value up to that value from hand onto the battlefield (Venom, Lethal Protector).

`ReduceActivatedAbilityCostForTargetingSourceEffect(int)` — activated abilities of Equipment
controlled by the source's controller cost less when they target the source permanent; the
activation-cost path checks the chosen target IDs (Bladegraft Aspirant).

- `IncreaseOwnCastCostIfTargetingPermanentEffect(PermanentPredicate, int)` — spell-self (STATIC): this spell costs `amount` more to cast if its first target is a matching permanent (Vanish into Eternity: creature/{3}); evaluated by `CastingCostService.getTargetBasedCostIncrease`

- `SkipDrawReplacementEffect` — capability for a global static replacement that skips every card draw
- `PossessedPortalDrawReplacementEffect()` — every player's draw is replaced by skipping that draw; used by Possessed Portal
- `EachPlayerSacrificesPermanentUnlessDiscardEffect()` — each player in APNAP order sacrifices a permanent unless they discard a card; a single available action resolves automatically, while both actions open a choice
- `CreepingDreadEffect()` — each player discards one card in APNAP order; after all choices complete, each opponent whose discarded card shares a card type with the controller's discarded card loses 3 life
- `EachPlayerSacrificesPermanentOrDiscardsEffect(PermanentPredicate filter)` — each player chooses a matching permanent in APNAP order; all chosen permanents are sacrificed simultaneously, then players who could not choose one choose cards to discard in APNAP order and discard those cards simultaneously (Plaguecrafter)
- `EachOpponentSacrificesPermanentUnlessPaysEffect(String manaCost)` — each opponent independently pays the mana cost or sacrifices a permanent of their choice in APNAP order
- `EachOpponentMayDiscardOrSacrificePermanentEffect(int damageIfNeither[, ChoiceStage])` — each opponent may discard a card, may instead sacrifice a permanent, or decline both; declining both or having neither option deals `damageIfNeither` damage. APNAP opponent prompts reuse the pending-may, discard, and forced-sacrifice flows (Zoyowa Lava-Tongue)
- `ExileTargetCreatureCardCreateTokenForOwnerEffect(CreateTokenEffect tokenTemplate)` - activated ability: exile a target creature card from any graveyard, then create one copy of `tokenTemplate` under that card's owner's control. No token is created if the target leaves the graveyard before resolution (Pharika, God of Affliction)
- `ExileTargetCardFromGraveyardCreateTokenForOwnerEffect(CreateTokenEffect tokenTemplate)` - target any card in any graveyard, exile it, then create one copy of `tokenTemplate` under that card's owner's control. No token is created if the target leaves the graveyard before resolution (Funeral Pyre)

- `SacrificeSelfThenCreateTokensEqualToEnteringManaValueEffect(CreateTokenEffect tokenTemplate)` - `ON_ANY_OTHER_CREATURE_ENTERS_BATTLEFIELD` trigger marker: snapshot the entering creature's mana value, sacrifice the source, then create that many copies of the token template only if the sacrifice succeeds (Monkey Cage)

- `RevealFirstDrawEffect(CardEffect onLand, CardEffect onNonland)` - controller-draw marker: reveal the first card drawn on each controller turn and route a land or nonland result through the normal draw-trigger pipeline (Keranos, God of Storms)
- `RevealFirstDrawInstantOrSorceryEffect()` - offers to reveal the first card drawn on the controller's turn and returns a reduced-cost copy effect for an instant or sorcery when accepted (God-Eternal Kefnet)

- `RevealTopCardDealManaValueDamageToAnyTargetEffect()` - reveal the top card of the controller's library, then deal damage equal to its mana value to the chosen any-target; the card stays on top (Riddle of Lightning, after `ScryEffect(3)`)
- `RevealTopCardAnyOpponentMayGraveyardOrDealDamageEffect()` - `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`: reveal the controller's top card; opponents choose in turn order, and the first opponent who puts it into the graveyard is dealt damage equal to its mana value; if all decline, it goes to hand (Sin Prodder)
- `RevealTopCardPutIntoHandThenEachOpponentLosesLifeEffect()` - reveal the controller's top card, put it into hand, then each opponent loses life equal to its mana value (Sorin, Grim Nemesis)
- `ExileTopUntilNonlandDealManaValueDamageToAnyTargetEffect()` - exile cards from the controller's library until a nonland card is exiled, then deal damage equal to that card's mana value to the chosen any-target (Undying Flames)

- `RevealTopCardDrawManaValueEffect()` - reveal the top card of the controller's library; draw cards equal to its mana value; the card stays on top and is the first card drawn when the mana value is greater than zero (Interpret the Signs, after `ScryEffect(3)`)

- `TopCardOfLibraryManaValue()` - `DynamicAmount` for the mana value of the controller's current top library card, or zero for an empty library; pair with `RevealTopCardOfLibraryEffect` and `BoostSelfEffect` when a revealed top card determines a self-boost (Stormchaser Chimera)

- `TargetCardsManaValueSum()` - `DynamicAmount` summing the mana values of the graveyard cards named by the resolving spell's target list; use when damage or another amount depends on all chosen graveyard cards (Command the Dreadhorde)

- `GreatestOpponentHandSize()` — `DynamicAmount` for the largest hand size among the controller's opponents; 0 with no opponent or while the controller is unknown. Use for "the opponent with the most cards in hand" characteristic-defining P/T wordings such as Adamaro, First to Desire.

- `PermanentCounterSum(CounterType, PermanentPredicate, CountScope)` — `DynamicAmount` summing a
  counter type on matching battlefield permanents in the requested scope; use for
  "for each [counter] on [matching permanents]" as-enters counts (Bioessence Hydra).

- `PreventAllDamageToSelfFromTargetCreatureEffect()` — activated ability: prevent all damage from the targeted creature to the ability's source permanent this turn (Stonewise Fortifier). Uses a turn-scoped target/source shield, so the creature can still damage other recipients and other creatures can still damage the source.
- `BoostReferencedPermanentEffect(PermanentReference, DynamicAmount, DynamicAmount)` — gives the referenced permanent +X/+Y until end of turn without targeting. `PermanentReference.TRIGGERING` reads `StackEntry.triggeringPermanentId`, such as Veteran Motorist's Vehicle crew trigger.

- `PayEnergyToGainControlOfTargetEffect(DynamicAmount energyAmount)` — pays the resolved amount of energy and, if payment succeeds, gains permanent control of the target; use with `MayEffect` for optional dynamic energy payments (Confiscation Coup).
- `PayAnyAmountOfEnergyToBoostTargetCreatureEffect()` — prompts for 0 up to the controller's current energy, pays that amount, and reuses `BoostTargetCreatureEffect` to give the target creature -1/-1 per energy paid until end of turn (Die Young).
- `PayAnyAmountOfEnergyToDealDamageToTargetCreatureEffect()` — prompts for 0 up to the controller's current energy, pays that amount, and deals that much damage to the target creature through the normal damage pipeline (Harnessed Lightning).
- `PutTargetCreatureCardFromGraveyardOnTopAndDealPowerDamageEffect([int graveyardTargetGroup, int creatureTargetGroup])` — put the targeted creature card from the controller's graveyard on top of their library, then deal damage equal to its printed power to the targeted creature. Two independent target groups (defaults 0 and 1); Dead Reckoning (`WWK`)
- `CantWinGameEffect()` — STATIC: the controller can't win the game and their opponents can't lose. `GameOutcomeService.canPlayerWinGame` checks the controller's restriction; loss dispatch checks the effect against opposing players (Abyssal Persecutor)
- `PlayerWithHighestLifeWinsOrDrawEffect()` — the player with the highest life total wins at resolution; if multiple players are tied for highest, the game is a draw (Celestial Convergence)

- `LookAtTopCardMayRevealMatchingAndPutCounterEffect(CardPredicate, CounterType)` — look at the controller's top card (private); if it matches, the controller may reveal it and put one counter of the specified type on the source permanent. The card stays on top. Quest for Ula's Temple uses it for a creature card and a quest counter

- `ExileTopCardOfTriggeringPlayerLibraryAndMayCastFreeEffect()` — an opponent-cast trigger checks that this is that opponent's first spell of the turn, then exiles the top card of that player's library; the source's controller may immediately cast a nonland card from exile without paying its mana cost (Mind's Dilation). Lands and declined cards remain exiled; the free cast uses the shared `MayPlayExiledCardWithoutPayingManaCostEffect` flow.
- `ColorManaPairsSpentToCast(ManaColor)` — complete same-color mana pairs spent to cast the source spell; use as a `DynamicAmount` for "for each {C}{C} spent" effects.
- `TriggeringSpellColorCount()` — the number of effective colors of the spell that caused the
  current triggered ability; use as a `DynamicAmount` for "for each of that spell's colors".

- `ReduceCastCostForMatchingSpellsUntilEndOfTurnEffect(CardPredicate, int)` — creates a temporary SELF-scoped cost reduction for matching spells cast by the controller.

- `EachPlayerRevealsTopCardsToBattlefieldRestToGraveyardEffect(DynamicAmount amount)` - each player reveals that many cards from their own library, with the amount evaluated separately for each player; permanent cards enter that player's battlefield and the rest go to that player's graveyard (Over the Top)
- `RevealUntilCountMatchingCardsForDestroyedPermanentControllersEffect(Set<CardType>)` - for each controller of a permanent destroyed earlier on the same stack entry, reveal until that many artifact/creature-type matches, exile the matches, put them onto the battlefield simultaneously, and shuffle the other revealed cards back

- `ExileMatchingCardFromTargetGraveyardEffect(CardPredicate filter)` — during resolution, the controller chooses one matching card from the targeted player's graveyard and exiles it; no prompt is opened when that graveyard has no matching card. Uses the resolution-time `MultiGraveyardChoice` flow and resumes the paused effect resolution (Dreams of Steel and Oil)
- `CopyControllerCastSpellOnSpellCastEffect.tokenCopy(CardPredicate, String, Condition)` — optional mana-paid copy of a permanent spell that enters as a token and keeps the original spell's targets (Mendicant Core, Guidelight)

- `PermanentsEnteredBattlefieldThisTurn(CardPredicate, CountScope)` — counts matching permanents that entered under players in the requested scope this turn, including permanents that have since left the battlefield. Use `CardAnyOfPredicate` for an "and/or" subtype list.
- `CreaturesLeftBattlefieldThisTurn(CountScope)` — counts creatures that left the battlefield under players in the requested scope this turn, including creatures returned to hand or otherwise moved to another zone.

- `DealDamageToAnyTargetOnControllerArtifactEntersEffect(int damage)` — emblem marker for an artifact-entry damage trigger; the trigger collector queues the shared any-target damage flow.
- `EachOpponentSacrificesCreatureOrVehicleOrDiscardsEffect()` — each opponent sacrifices a creature or Vehicle of their choice; an opponent who cannot do so discards a card instead. Uses the shared forced-sacrifice and discard choice flows.
- `ControllerSpeed()` — current speed of the resolving effect's controller, or zero before they start their engines.
- `AdditionalControllerDamageToOpponentsAndTheirPermanentsEffect(int)` — STATIC: sources controlled by this permanent's controller deal that much damage plus N to opponents or permanents their opponents control instead. Applies to combat and noncombat damage; stacks additively and only when damage > 0. Queried by `GameQueryService.getControllerDamageToOpponentBonus`.
- `AdditionalDamageToOpponentsAndTheirPermanentsEffect(int)` — STATIC: spells deal that much damage plus N to opponents of this permanent's controller or permanents those opponents control instead. Applies regardless of who controls the spell; combat and ability damage are unaffected. Queried by `GameQueryService.getAdditionalSpellDamageToOpponentsBonus`.

- `ExtraTurnSkipReplacementEffect` — capability for a static replacement that skips an extra turn as it would begin while the source remains on the battlefield
- `ExileAndTakeExtraTurnReplacementEffect` — capability for a self-replacement that exiles the source from the battlefield instead of putting it into a graveyard and queues an extra turn for its last controller
- `UginNexusReplacementEffect()` — combines the two Ugin's Nexus replacement capabilities
- `PayXManaDrawXCardsEffect()` — life-gain trigger: controller chooses X up to the life gained and available mana, pays generic mana, and draws X cards. The life-gain amount is snapshotted when the trigger is collected; X=0 declines. Well of Lost Dreams (`ON_CONTROLLER_GAINS_LIFE`)

- `AllCardsAreColorlessEffect()` — STATIC global color-setting effect: every permanent, spell, and card not on the battlefield is colorless. Battlefield permanents use the layered static pass; spells and other-zone cards use `GameQueryService.getEffectiveCardColors` (Mycosynth Lattice)
- `BecomeColorlessEffect(GrantScope[, PermanentPredicate])` — STATIC layer-5 color-setting effect: permanents in scope matching the optional predicate become colorless; `ALL_PERMANENTS` includes the source through the self-handler (Ghostflame Sliver, Thran Lens)
- `SpendManaAsAnyColorEffect()` — STATIC global mana permission: players may spend mana as though it were mana of any color. Reflected into each player's `ManaPool` and honored by `ManaCost` affordability/payment paths (Mycosynth Lattice)

- `PutCreatureFromHandWithManaValueEqualToSourceCountersEffect(CounterType)` — activated ability: you may put a creature card from hand onto the battlefield when its mana value equals the number of the given counter type on the source permanent (Aether Vial)
- `MayCastInstantOrSorceryFromHandWithManaValueEqualToSourceCountersEffect(CounterType)` — activated ability: you may cast an instant or sorcery card from hand without paying its mana cost when its mana value equals the number of the given counter type on the source permanent (Brain in a Jar)
- `MayCastArtifactFromHandWithManaValueAtMostSourceCountersEffect(CounterType)` — activated ability: you may cast a nonland artifact card from hand without paying its mana cost when its mana value is at most the number of the given counter type on the source permanent; reuses the shared per-card `MayCastFromHandWithoutPayingManaCostEffect` flow (Lady Octopus, Inspired Inventor)
- `FirstOrSecondCardDrawTriggerEffect(CardEffect)` — `ON_CONTROLLER_DRAWS` trigger marker that unwraps its payload when the controller draws their first or second card of the turn (Lady Octopus, Inspired Inventor)

- `PutCreatureFromHandWithManaValueXEffect()` — activated ability: you may put a creature card with mana value exactly the activated ability's X from hand onto the battlefield (Mercadian Lift)

- `JeweledBirdAnteEffect()` — activated ability: ante the source artifact, move the controller's other anted cards to their owners' graveyards, then draw a card. The ante zone is represented by exile plus `GameData.antedCardIds`, so unrelated exile entries are untouched. Jeweled Bird
- `AnteTopCardEffect(AnteRecipient)` — mandatory spell effect: `CONTROLLER` moves the controller's top library card to the ante zone, while `EACH_PLAYER` moves every player's top library card; the ante zone is represented by exile plus `GameData.antedCardIds`; an empty library has no card to ante. Contract from Below, Demonic Attorney
- `ExchangeTargetAnteCardWithTopOfLibraryEffect()` — targeted spell effect: exchange a card the controller owns in the ante with the top card of their library; ante is represented by exile plus `GameData.antedCardIds`. Darkpact

- `CreateTokenAndLinkToSourceEffect(CreateTokenEffect token[, boolean linkTokenToSource])` — creates the wrapped token(s) and records them under the source in `GameData.sourceCreatedTokens`; the default also gives each token a back-reference to the source for a paired leaves-the-battlefield trigger, while `false` tracks tokens without that reciprocal ability. If the source has already left before resolution, the token is still created without any link. Stangg; Mysterio, Master of Illusion
- `CreateTokenAndLinkToSourceEffect(CreateTokenEffect token)` — creates the wrapped token(s), records them under the source in `GameData.sourceCreatedTokens`, and gives each token a back-reference to the source for a paired leaves-the-battlefield trigger. If the source has already left before resolution, the token is still created without any link. Stangg
- `ExileTopCardAndCreateTokenEffect(CreateTokenEffect token)` — exiles the controller's top library card face down, then creates the supplied token. Each created token gets a leaves-the-battlefield trigger bound to that exact exiled card, so multiple activations remain paired independently (Ugin, the Ineffable)
- `ExileTokensCreatedWithSourceEffect()` — `ON_SELF_LEAVES_BATTLEFIELD`: exiles every surviving token tracked as created by the source, clearing each token's back-reference first so its reciprocal trigger cannot return to the departed source. Stangg

- `GrantChosenLandwalkEffect(GrantScope scope)` — prompts for a basic land type, then grants the matching plain landwalk until end of turn to the source (`SELF`) or target (`TARGET`) creature. Uses the basic-land-type choice protocol. Giant Slug uses the self form from its delayed upkeep trigger.
- `RegisterChosenLandwalkAtNextUpkeepEffect()` — activated-ability registrar that queues a one-shot `GrantChosenLandwalkAtNextUpkeep` for the activating player's next upkeep. The delayed trigger carries the source permanent id and pushes `GrantChosenLandwalkEffect(SELF)` onto the stack; it is discarded if the source has left the battlefield. Giant Slug.
- `GrantTargetingRestrictionToTargetUntilEndOfTurnEffect(TargetingRestrictionEffect)` — target permanent gains the supplied spells/abilities targeting restriction until end of turn. Use `TargetingRestrictionEffect.opponentSpellsAndAbilities()` for Vines of Vastwood, where the restriction is not the hexproof keyword and is not bypassed by hexproof-ignoring effects.

- `PutCountersOnSourceCardEffect(CounterType[, int count])` — counters on the battlefield permanent represented by the ability's source card; use for a resolution-time may trigger collected from another permanent's event when the text says to put counters on the ability source (Beastmaster Ascension)
- `ReduceColoredCastCostForMatchingSpellsEffect(CardPredicate, ManaCost, CostModificationScope)` — matching spells lose only matching colored mana components from their cast cost; unmatched colored reduction does not reduce generic mana (Ragemonger uses `new ManaCost("{B}{R}")`)

- `PayXManaPutCountersOnSelfEffect(CounterType counterType)` — resolution-time "you may pay {X}. If you do, put X counters on this": prompts X up to available mana, pays it, and puts X counters of the specified type on the source permanent (X=0 declines). Hero of Leina Tower.
- `PayManaAnyNumberOfTimesPutCountersOnSelfEffect(String manaCost, CounterType counterType)` — resolution-time "you may pay {manaCost} any number of times; put that many counters on this": prompts for the number of fixed-cost payments, pays each one, and puts one counter per payment on the source permanent. Intrepid Adversary.

- `ExchangeControlOfSourceAndTriggeringSpellEffect()` — exchanges control of the source permanent and the spell that caused its spell-cast trigger; preserves the spell's owner for zone changes and offers new target choices after the exchange when applicable (Perplexing Chimera)
- `GainControlOfTargetSpellEffect()` — gains control of a target noncreature spell on the stack, preserves its owner for zone changes, and offers its new controller a choice to retarget it (Commandeer)

- `TributeEffect(int counterCount)` — opponent's as-enters tribute choice; the entering permanent records whether the required +1/+1 counters were actually placed.
- `MayReturnPermanentToHandAndEnterWithCountersEffect(PermanentPredicate, CounterType, int, String)` — controller's as-enters choice to return a matching permanent to its owner's hand; if the return succeeds, the entering permanent receives the configured counters. The choice is handled before ETB effects are collected.
- `TributeNotPaidEffect(CardEffect wrapped)` — ETB intervening-if wrapper; resolves to `wrapped` only when tribute was not paid.
- `AdditionalDamageFromColorSpellsEffect(Set<CardColor>, int)` — STATIC: a spell of any listed color deals that much damage plus N to a permanent or player instead (Sulfuric Vapors). Global across controllers, spells only, and applies to both damage destinations; stacks additively and only when damage > 0. Queried by `GameQueryService.getSpellDamageBonus`
- `ExileCardsFromGraveyardEffect(int maxTargets, int lifeGain, boolean singleGraveyard)` — activated-ability/hand-ability form that requires all directly supplied targets to share one graveyard; Carrion Beetles uses `(3, 0, true)`. Use `maxTargets == 0` when the printed up-to limit is the ability's X value.
- `EachPlayerMayExileGraveyardCardsThenLoseLifeEffect()` — SPELL: in APNAP order, each player may exile any number of cards from their own graveyard; after all choices, each player simultaneously loses 1 life for each card remaining there. Grave Consequences.
- `CopySpellForEachPriorInstantOrSorceryEffect()` — `ON_CONTROLLER_CASTS_SPELL`: when the controller casts an instant or sorcery, queues a `StormCopyEffect` with one copy for each other instant or sorcery that controller cast earlier in the turn. The count and spell snapshot are fixed when the trigger is collected (Thousand-Year Storm)

- `ReduceBuybackCostEffect(int)` — static battlefield effect: reduces the generic mana component of every mana buyback cost by N while the source remains on the battlefield (Memory Crystal). It affects all players' buyback costs, not the spell's normal cast cost or non-mana buyback costs

- `FlipCoinForEachBlockingCreatureEffect()` — for each blocking creature, flip independently; on a win, prevent all combat damage that creature would deal this turn (Fighting Chance). Resolves against the blockers currently on the battlefield and adds winning blockers to `GameData.creaturesPreventedFromDealingCombatDamage`, cleared at turn cleanup

- `MoveCountersFromAllCreaturesToSourceEffect(CounterType)` — moves all counters of the given kind from every creature on every battlefield onto the source permanent, including the source when it is a creature. Resolves from the source permanent id and is untargeted; the source must still be on the battlefield. Uses shared counter placement for counter locks, replacement effects, and counter triggers. Spike Cannibal (`PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ONE`)
- `MoveCountersFromControlledPermanentsToSourceEffect(CounterType)` — moves any number of the given counters from other permanents controlled by the ability controller onto the source. It prompts for an amount from each eligible permanent in turn, includes noncreatures, excludes the source, and uses shared counter placement. Aetherborn Marauder (`PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ONE`)
`CantAttackUnlessReturnToHandEffect(count, PermanentPredicate, description)` is a STATIC additional attack cost: return `count` matching permanents to their owners' hands as attackers are declared. It is read by `AttackReturnToHandCostService`; pair it with a `CantAttackUnlessEffect(new ControlsPermanentCount(count, filter), …)` legality gate (Floodtide Serpent).
- `GrantEffectToSourceEffect(EffectSlot slot, CardEffect grantedEffect)` — when resolved, grants the source permanent an indefinite triggered ability. Use for ETB effects such as “it gains ‘When this creature dies, …’”; persistent source grants are included in self-death trigger collection.
Thought Prison uses `ChooseCardsFromTargetHandEffect(..., HandChoiceDestination.EXILE, true)` to store the selected exiled card on the source permanent for imprint-based trigger predicates.

- `CreateTokenForImprintedCardOwnerEffect(CreateTokenEffect tokenTemplate)` — `ON_SELF_LEAVES_BATTLEFIELD` trigger descriptor: the owner of the source's imprinted card creates the supplied token. The leave-trigger collector freezes dynamic power/toughness against the source's last-known state; resolution checks that the imprinted card is still in exile and creates the token under that card's owner (Severance Priest).

- `KrarksThumbEffect()` — static replacement effect: each logical coin flip made by its controller flips two coins and keeps the preferred result. Count all copies controlled by the flipping player; the replacements apply once each, so every logical flip resolves to one chosen result.
- `ExileAllOpponentsHandsEffect()` — non-targeting exile of every card from every opponent's hand; it is not a discard.
- `ExileTopCardsToSourceEffect(count, false, false, TARGET_OPPONENT, true)` — target opponent exiles the top cards of their library, tracked with the source.
- `ExileTopCardMayCastOrDealDamageEffect(damage)` — exile the controller's top card and offer to cast it during the current resolution; if it is not cast, the source deals `damage` to each opponent.
- `DealDamageToAnyTargetOnControllerSpellCastEffect(damage)` — emblem marker for fixed damage to any target whenever its controller casts a spell; the spell-cast trigger queues the ordinary `DealDamageToAnyTargetEffect` for target selection.
- `ExileTopCardOfTargetPlayerLibraryEffect(lifeGainIfLand)` — target player exiles the top card of their library; the ability controller gains the configured life amount if that card is a land. Scrib Nibblers (`WWK`).
- `ExileTopCardsToSourceEffect(DynamicAmount count, false, false, TARGET_OPPONENT, true)` — same source-tracked exile with a resolving X count, typically `new XValue()` for an activated ability.
- `ExileTopCardsAndMayCastSpellsEffect(new EventValue(), TARGET_OPPONENT, true, new EventValue())` — combat-damage trigger shape for exiling as many cards as damage dealt from the damaged player's library, tracking them with the source, then offering any number of nonland spells with mana value at most that damage during resolution (Kotis, the Fangkeeper).
- `ExileTopCardOfDamagedPlayerLibraryFaceDownAndGrantCreatureControllerPlayPermissionEffect()` — global combat-damage trigger effect for Gonti, Night Minister: exiles the damaged opponent's top card face down and grants the damaging creature's controller permission to play it indefinitely, with any mana type usable for spells.
- `ReturnCardExiledWithSourceToBattlefieldEffect(filter, requiresManaValueEqualsX, grantedSubtype)` — returns a matching source-exiled card under the ability controller's control, optionally filtering by the loyalty X and granting a persistent subtype.
- `ReturnExiledCardNamedToBattlefieldUnderOwnerControlEffect(String cardName)` — returns one face-up exiled card with that exact name under its owner's control; opens a single-card choice when multiple matches exist. Used by Sengir Nosferatu's Bat token.

- `EachTargetCreatureDealsPowerDamageToSourceEffect()` — every creature in the bound target group deals damage equal to its power to the ability's source permanent; used by Polukranos, World Eater.
- `MonstrosityEffect(DynamicAmount amount)` / `MonstrosityEffect(int amount)` — resolves monstrosity once, evaluating the amount from the stack entry (including X).
- `HarnessEffect()` — marks the source permanent as harnessed; use `SourceIsHarnessed` to gate abilities that become active after harnessing.
- `DealDividedDamageEffect.xAmongTargetCreaturesAtResolution()` — keeps a target-creature group on a trigger and prompts for each positive damage allocation while the ability resolves.
- `CastMatchingInstantOrSorceryFromGraveyardWithoutPayingManaCostEffect(CardPredicate)` — offer each matching instant or sorcery in the controller's graveyard for a free cast; non-targeting and reuses the normal graveyard-cast flow
- `CastAnyNumberOfEldraziSpellsFromOutsideGameEffect()` — offer each Eldrazi spell in the controller's outside-the-game card pool for a free cast; each accepted card is cast independently so any number may be chosen. Spawnsire of Ulamog.

- `PutSourceAndBlockingCreaturesOnTopOfLibraryEffect([includeCreaturesBlockingSource])` - put the source permanent and creatures it is blocking on top of their owners' libraries, optionally also including creatures blocking it, then shuffle each affected owner's library (Gomazoa, Vortex Elemental)
- `AllowPlayMatchingCardsFromGraveyardThisTurnEffect(CardPredicate filter)` — grants play permission to matching cards currently in the controller's graveyard until end of turn; covers both casting spells and playing lands (Kethis, the Hidden Hand)
- `AllowPlayFromTopOfLibraryUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — resolution-time permission to look at and play cards from the controller's top library card until end of turn; covers both casting spells and playing lands while preserving normal costs and timing (The Belligerent)
- `PlayLandsFromTopOfLibraryEffect()` — static permission to play lands from the top of your library; normal land-play timing, land-per-turn limits, and land-play restrictions still apply (Courser of Kruphix)
- `ExileOwnCardsInsteadOfGraveyardUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — turn-scoped replacement that exiles the controller's cards instead of putting them into that player's graveyard; used by Yawgmoth's Will

- `ReturnEnchantedCreatureToBattlefieldOnDeathEffect(boolean underAuraControllersControl[, boolean enterTapped][, CounterType enterWithCounter])` — Aura death trigger returning the enchanted creature to the battlefield, optionally tapped and/or with one counter as it enters; Unholy Indenture uses `PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ONE`, Fungal Fortitude uses `enterTapped`.
- `AllowPlayFromOtherGraveyardsThisTurnEffect()` — grants the controller permission to cast spells and play lands from opponents' graveyards until end of turn (Shaman's Trance)
- `OtherPlayersCantPlayFromGraveyardsThisTurnEffect()` — stops every player except the resolving effect's controller from casting spells or playing lands from their graveyard until end of turn (Shaman's Trance)
- `PlayLandsFromTopOfLibraryEffect()` — static permission to play lands from the top of your library; normal land-play timing, land-per-turn limits, and land-play restrictions still apply (Courser of Kruphix)
- `ExileOwnCardsInsteadOfGraveyardUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — turn-scoped replacement that exiles the controller's cards instead of putting them into that player's graveyard; used by Yawgmoth's Will

- `ReturnEnchantedCreatureToBattlefieldOnDeathEffect(boolean underAuraControllersControl[, CounterType enterWithCounter])` — Aura death trigger returning the enchanted creature to the battlefield, optionally with one counter as it enters; Unholy Indenture uses `PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ONE`.
- `ReturnEnchantedPermanentToBattlefieldOnDeathOrExileEffect(UUID cardId, Zone fromZone)` — bound Aura trigger returning an enchanted creature or planeswalker from the exact graveyard or exile zone it entered, under the Aura controller's control; the collector binds the card ID and destination zone.

- `GainActivatedAbilitiesOfTargetCreatureUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — source gains the target creature's currently effective activated abilities until end of turn
- `SpendBlueManaAsAnyColorForActivatedAbilitiesEffect()` — source-scoped static permission for a creature's activated ability costs; blue mana may pay colored requirements of those costs
- `EquipAbilitiesCanBeActivatedAtInstantSpeedEffect()` — controller-scoped static permission that lets equip abilities be activated at instant speed; queried by `GameQueryService` during activation legality and auto-pass checks (Leonin Shikari)
- `DealDamageToTargetPlayerEqualToChosenColorCardsInHandEffect()` / `(CardColor fixedColor)` — with no argument, the controller chooses a color; with a fixed color, the target player reveals their hand and takes damage equal to the number of cards of that color. The effect routes damage through `DamageSupport` (Darigaaz, the Igniter; Inquisition uses `CardColor.WHITE`).
- `DealDamageToTargetPlayerEqualToChosenCardTypeCardsInHandEffect(int damagePerCard)` / `()` — controller chooses a card type, then the target player reveals their hand and takes `damagePerCard` damage for each card of that type. The effect offers every current `CardType`, pauses for the resolution-time choice, and routes damage through `DamageSupport` (Blood Oath).

- `VoidEffect()` — controller chooses a number, then all artifacts and creatures with that mana value are destroyed and a target player reveals their hand and discards all nonland cards with that mana value. The number is chosen at resolution and stored on `GameData` while the effect is rerun.

- `ChooseColorThenDiscardFromTargetHandEffect()` — controller chooses a color, then target player reveals their hand and the controller chooses one card of that color for the target to discard. Pair with `target(PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(ANY))`; player-targeting spec (Addle)

Protective Sphere uses `PreventDamageFromChosenSourceEffect.allDamageToYouOfActivationManaColor()`. Its source-choice filter is snapshotted from the colored mana spent on the activation; colorless mana yields no legal source choice.

Compact lookup: effect name + constructor signature, organized by category.

- `PreventNextDamageBySelfEffect()` — activated ability: prevents the next damage event the ability's source permanent would deal to any recipient this turn. No source choice; installs a `SourceNextDamageToAnyTargetShield` keyed to the source permanent.

- `OpponentDrewAtLeastCardsThisTurn(n)` — an opponent drew at least `n` cards this turn; use as an alternate-cast availability condition for Runeflare Trap.

- `DealDamageToEachOpponentEqualToCardsInHandEffect()` — deal damage to each opponent equal to the number of cards that opponent has in hand; evaluates the amount independently for each opponent. Stormbreath Dragon
- `BoostCreatedTokensEffect(powerBoost, toughnessBoost)` — gives every token created earlier in the same spell or ability resolution a +P/+T modifier until end of turn. Put it after the token-creation effect; the handler reads `StackEntry.createdPermanentIds`. Wrap it in `ConditionalEffect(new Kicked(), …)` for a kicked-only token boost (Elemental Appeal).

- `DestroyEnchantedCreatureOnLeaveEffect()` / `(UUID enchantedPermanentId, boolean cannotBeRegenerated)` — `ON_SELF_LEAVES_BATTLEFIELD`: destroy the creature the source Aura enchanted. The collector captures the host's permanent ID before the Aura disappears; use the card-definition constructor for the default no-regeneration form
- `DistributeCountersAmongTargetsEffect.chosenAmongTargetCreaturesEtb(CounterType, int)` — fixed chosen counter distribution for an ETB ability; target-to-counter assignments come from the shared ETB assignment buffer (Wurmskin Forger)
For detailed descriptions, targeting info, and examples, see EFFECTS_INDEX.md.

- `DistributeCountersAmongTargetsEffect.evenlyAmongTargetPermanents(CounterType, int, PermanentPredicate)` — fixed even counter distribution across a target group narrowed to matching permanents, such as Vehicles and/or creatures you control.

`SacrificeCreatureThenMassDamageEqualToPowerEffect()` — resolution-time sacrifice, then damage equal to the sacrificed creature's power to each creature without flying and each player (Rupture); no creature means no damage.

`EachPlayerExilesFromHandEffect(N)` — each player exiles N cards from hand (their choice), non-targeting, APNAP (Mind Swords). Fewer than N means the whole hand. Reuses `EXILE_FROM_HAND_CHOICE` with a remaining-choosers queue.
- `DrawCardsForEachOpponentOfTriggeringPlayerEffect(DynamicAmount|int)` — each opponent of the player who caused a spell-cast trigger draws the evaluated amount. Standstill uses `(3)` after sacrificing its source.
- `RedirectChosenColorSpellDamageToControllerEffect()` — static replacement; an instant or sorcery spell of the source permanent's chosen color that would deal damage to its controller instead deals that damage to the spell's controller (Harsh Judgment).

`EachPlayerSacrificesOneOfEachTypeEffect()` — each player chooses an artifact, creature, enchantment, land, and planeswalker in active-player order; a multi-typed permanent may satisfy more than one type, and choosing it more than once has the same result as choosing it once. All chosen permanents are sacrificed together. Used by Release (Catch // Release)
`RegisterEchoAtNextUpkeepEffect(String manaCost)` or `(DynamicAmount)` registers a one-shot echo pay-or-sacrifice trigger for the source permanent's next applicable controller upkeep. A dynamic cost is evaluated at that upkeep. The `EchoAtNextUpkeep` action follows the permanent's current controller and is discarded if the permanent leaves; the upkeep handler pushes `ForcedCostOrElseEffect(PayEchoCost, [SacrificeSelfEffect], true)`.

`PayEchoCost(String echoCost)` — internal echo cost descriptor: at forced-cost resolution it uses the controller's applicable `AlternativeEchoCostEffect`, if any, and otherwise uses `echoCost`.

`AlternativeEchoCostEffect(String manaCost)` — STATIC: offers the supplied alternative mana cost for echo abilities of permanents controlled by its controller; the upkeep echo trigger uses it instead of the printed echo cost.

- `ChooseOpponentGainsControlOfSourceEffect()` — non-targeting (`ControlStealingEffect`, PERMANENT): the ability controller chooses one of their opponents; that player gains permanent control of the source permanent. With one opponent the choice is automatic; with multiple opponents it uses the shared player-choice prompt. Goblin Festival
`PlayerWithLowestLifeGainsControlOfSourceCreatureEffect()` - Loxodon Peacekeeper: `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`; the player with the lowest life gains permanent control of this creature. If players are tied for lowest life, the source's controller chooses one of them.

`EachPlayerSacrificesOneOfEachTypeEffect()` - each player chooses an artifact, creature, enchantment, land, and planeswalker in active-player order; a multi-typed permanent may satisfy more than one type, and choosing it more than once has the same result as choosing it once. All chosen permanents are sacrificed together. Used by Release (Catch // Release)

`StrongholdGambitEffect()` — each player makes a mandatory hidden card choice from hand; all chosen cards are then revealed, and every creature card tied for the lowest mana value enters under its owner's control. Choices are collected in APNAP order, and tied winners enter as one simultaneous batch.
- `GoblinGameEffect()` — each player secretly chooses a positive item count; after all counts are revealed, each player loses that count of life, then every player tied for the fewest loses half their current life rounded up. Uses private `XValueChoice` prompts and a game-local progress state.
- `RevealTopCardsOfTargetPlayerUntilInstantOrSorceryAndCastEffect()` — target player exiles from the top of their library until an instant or sorcery is found; the controller may cast it for free, then the remaining revealed cards are put on the bottom of that library in a random order (Chaos Wand). Reuses the `CAST_WITHOUT_PAYING` library-search flow.
- `RevealTopCardsOfTargetSpellControllerUntilInstantOrSorceryAndCastEffect()` — the controller of the targeted spell reveals from the top of their library until an instant or sorcery is found; that player may cast it for free, then the revealed cards not cast are shuffled back into that library (Spellshift). Reuses the library-search free-cast flow with `CAST_WITHOUT_PAYING_AND_SHUFFLE_LIBRARY`.
- `RevealUntilNonlandCardWithManaValueAndCastEffect(int maxManaValue)` — reveal from the controller's library until the first nonland card with mana value at most `maxManaValue`; offer that card for a free cast and put the other revealed cards on the bottom in a random order (Treasure Keeper).
- `DiscoverEffect(DynamicAmount discoverValue)` — exile from the controller's library until the first nonland card with mana value at most the evaluated value; offer that card for a free cast or put it into hand, and put the other revealed cards on the bottom in a random order. Uses the `DISCOVER` library-search destination; pair with `TargetSpellManaValue` when the value comes from a target spell (Hurl into History).
- `ShuffleTargetPermanentIntoLibraryThenDiscoverEffect()` — shuffle a target artifact or creature with mana value 1 or greater into its owner's library, snapshot its mana value before removal, then have that player discover with the captured value (Zoyowa's Justice).

`EachPlayerChoosesLandOfEachBasicTypeThenSacrificeRestEffect()` — each player chooses one land with each basic land type they control in active-player order; a land with multiple basic land types may satisfy multiple choices, and all other lands are sacrificed after every player chooses. Used by Global Ruin
`EachPlayerChoosesLandOfEachBasicTypeThenReturnToHandEffect()` — each player chooses one land with each basic land type they control in active-player order; a land with multiple basic land types may satisfy multiple choices, and all chosen lands return to their owners' hands after every player chooses. Used by Planar Overlay
`EachPlayerChoosesLandsThenDestroyRestEffect(int landsToKeep)` — each player chooses up to the configured number of lands they control in active-player order; players with fewer lands keep all of them, and all other permanents are destroyed together after every player chooses. Used by Urza's Sylex
`RaidingPartyEffect()` — each player chooses any number of untapped white creatures they control to tap, then chooses up to two Plains per creature tapped; all unchosen Plains are destroyed after every player chooses. Uses global Plains candidates and the shared multi-player choice flow. Used by Raiding Party

`ChooseLandOfEachBasicTypeThenDestroyEffect()` — the controller chooses one land with each basic land type from among all battlefields; a land with multiple basic land types may satisfy multiple choices, and the chosen lands are destroyed simultaneously. Used by Sundering Titan

`BendOrBreakEffect()` — each player separates their nontoken lands into two piles, chooses an opponent to choose one of those piles, destroys the chosen piles, and taps the other piles. Uses a queued multi-player interaction flow; chosen lands are destroyed with normal regeneration rules and the other lands are tapped. Used by Bend or Break

`WhimsOfTheFatesEffect()` — starting with the spell's controller, each player separates all permanents they control into three piles by choosing Pile 1 and Pile 2; the remaining permanents form Pile 3. One pile is selected uniformly at random for each player, and all selected permanents are sacrificed together. Uses a queued multi-player interaction flow.

`PutAttackingCreaturesOnTopOrBottomOfLibraryEffect()` — for each attacking creature, its owner chooses top or bottom of their library; resolution prompts each owner through the shared multi-permanent choice flow (Aetherspouts).

`PutMatchingPermanentsOnTopOfOwnersLibrariesEffect(filter)` — puts every matching permanent on top of its owner's library; each owner chooses the order when they have multiple matching permanents (Harmonic Convergence).

- `PreventAllDamageToTargetFromChosenColorEffect()` — activated ability: on resolution, choose a color, then prevent all damage from that color to the target creature, player, or planeswalker until end of turn (Avacyn, Guardian Angel). Uses a target-specific turn-scoped shield and does not grant protection.

Protection from multicolored sources uses `ProtectionFromMulticoloredEffect(GrantScope scope)`;
`GrantScope.ENCHANTED_CREATURE` is the Aura form used by Guildscorn Ward.

`CantBeEquippedEffect()` is a static marker that prevents Equipment from attaching to the permanent carrying it; equipment attachment helpers and ongoing attachment legality checks read it.

**How to use:** Search this file for keywords from the card text (e.g. "graveyard", "life", "shuffle", "destroy"). Once you find a candidate effect, grep EFFECTS_INDEX.md for its name to get full details.

Implementation note: `ExileTargetPermanentUntilSourceLeavesEffect(boolean imprint, PermanentPredicate targetPredicate)` supports narrowed harmful permanent targets; `GrantTriggeredAbilityEffect` grants `ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD` abilities from the entering permanent's current static grants.

- `RegisterDelayedReturnSourceTransformedEffect()` — ON_DEATH effect that registers a delayed end-step return from the source card's owner's graveyard to the battlefield transformed. Used by Loyal Cathar-style "When this dies, return it transformed at the beginning of the next end step." `(true, true)` = only on sacrifice + under owner's control (Biolume Egg).
- `ReturnSourceTransformedFromGraveyardEffect()` — ON_DEATH effect that returns the source card from its owner's graveyard to the battlefield transformed **immediately on resolution**, under the trigger controller's control. Harvest Hand // Scrounged Scythe. Immediate sibling of `RegisterDelayedReturnSourceTransformedEffect` (no end-step wait); both share `GraveyardTransformedReturnService`. Fizzles if the card is no longer in a graveyard. The `(boolean tapped)` overload enters tapped; `(boolean tapped, CounterType, int)` also adds counters before battlefield-entry processing (Ojer Pakpatiq); `(boolean tapped, boolean underOwnersControl)` returns it under its owner's control (Aclazotz, Deepest Betrayal).
- `ReturnSourceFromExileTransformedEffect()` — craft-resolution effect that returns the source card from exile to its owner's battlefield transformed. Fizzles if the source card is no longer in exile.
- `CraftMaterialCost(CardSubtype)` — craft material requiring exactly one permanent or card with the given subtype, chosen from another controlled permanent or the controller's graveyard.
- `CraftMaterialCost(count, null, false, false)` — craft material requiring exactly that many other permanents and/or cards from the controller's graveyard.
- `CraftMaterialCost.oneOrMore([CardSubtype])` — craft material requiring one or more matching permanents and/or cards from the controller's graveyard.
- `CraftMaterialCost.nonlandsWithActivatedAbilities(int)` — craft material requiring at least the given number of nonland permanents/cards with activated abilities, chosen from other controlled permanents and the controller's graveyard.
- `AwardOneManaOfEachColorAmongCardsExiledWithSourceEffect()` — mana ability effect that adds one mana of each color among cards exiled with the source permanent.
- `ReturnSourceTransformedFromGraveyardAttachedToTargetPlayerEffect()` — targeted ON_DEATH sibling that returns the source transformed under the trigger controller's control and attaches the back-face Aura to the targeted player. Accursed Witch // Infectious Curse.
- `ReturnSourceTransformedFromGraveyardAttachedToTargetPermanentEffect()` — targeted ON_DEATH sibling that returns the source transformed under the trigger controller's control and attaches the back-face Aura to a targeted opposing creature or planeswalker. Vengeful Strangler // Strangling Grasp.
- `RegisterDelayedSelfReturnFromGraveyardEffect(CounterType, int)` — registers a delayed end-step return of the SOURCE CARD from its owner's graveyard to the battlefield under that owner's control, entering with the given counters (`()` = no counters). Sand Golem = `ON_SELF_DISCARDED_BY_OPPONENT` + `(PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ONE, 1)`. Fizzles if the card isn't in a graveyard when the trigger resolves; the delayed return itself is skipped if the card has left by the end step. Untransformed sibling of `RegisterDelayedReturnSourceTransformedEffect` (queues `DelayedGraveyardToBattlefieldSelfReturn`, drained in `StepTriggerService.handleEndStepTriggers`). `RegisterDelayedSelfReturnFromGraveyardEffect.tappedAtOwnersNextUpkeep()` instead returns it **tapped** at the beginning of the **owner's** next upkeep (`atNextUpkeep`/`tapped` flags on the action; drained in `handleUpkeepTriggers` only when the owner is the active player) — Phytotitan `ON_DEATH`.
- `RegisterDelayedSelfReturnFromGraveyardWithOneFewerCounterEffect(CounterType)` — `ON_DEATH` marker that fires only when the dying source had at least one counter of the given type, snapshots that count, and registers the ordinary delayed self-return with one fewer counter. Nine-Lives Familiar uses `REVIVAL`.
- `RegisterDelayedReturnTargetAndSacrificedCardsEffect()` — spell effect for Rescue from the Underworld: queues the targeted creature card and the creature sacrificed as the additional cost in one `DelayedGraveyardCardsToBattlefieldUnderControl` action for the controller's next upkeep. Cards still in graveyards enter simultaneously under the controller's control; cards that left their graveyards are skipped.
- `RegisterDelayedCreateTokenEffect(CreateTokenEffect tokenEffect)` — registers a delayed trigger that resolves `tokenEffect` at the beginning of the next end step, creating the token(s) under the resolving controller's control. Used by Rukh Egg-style "When this dies, create a ... token at the beginning of the next end step."
- `RegisterDiscardAtNextEndStepEffect(int count)` — registers a delayed trigger that makes the controller discard that many cards at the beginning of the next end step, using the normal chosen-discard interaction. Ideas Unbound (`3`).
- `RegisterExileCardsFromOwnGraveyardAtNextEndStepEffect(int count)` — registers a delayed trigger that makes the controller exile that many cards from their graveyard at the beginning of the next end step; fewer available cards are all exiled, otherwise the normal sequential graveyard-choice interaction is used. False Memories (`7`).
- `RegisterDelayedLoseLifeAndReturnFromGraveyardToHandEffect(int lifeLoss)` — registers a delayed trigger that at the next end step pushes `LoseLifeEffect(lifeLoss)` + self-return `ReturnCardFromGraveyardEffect` onto the stack as one ability. Brood of Cockroaches (`1`).
- `NecropotenceSetAsideTopCardEffect()` — `CardDrawingEffect` (1). Activated-ability effect (Necropotence `Pay 1 life:`): exile the top library card face down, then return it to hand at the **controller's own** next end step via a `ReturnExiledCardToHandAtEndStep` delayed action (drained in `StepTriggerService.handleEndStepTriggers`, `activePlayerId == ownerId`). Pair with `PayLifeCost(1)` in the ability's effect list.
- `ExileDiscardedCardFromGraveyardEffect([boolean trackWithSource])` — discard-trigger marker: "whenever you discard a card, exile that card from your graveyard" (Necropotence). The default is collected inline; `trackWithSource=true` queues the trigger and tracks the discarded card with the triggering permanent (Bag of Holding). `withStashCounter()` queues the trigger and marks the card with a stash counter without source tracking (Tinybones, Bauble Burglar). The tracked forms recheck the card's graveyard presence at resolution.
- `ReturnDiscardedCardFromGraveyardToHandEffect()` — discard-trigger payload: returns the exact discarded card carried by the trigger from its owner's graveyard to that player's hand; does nothing if the card has left the graveyard. Pair with `MayEffect(SacrificeSelfThenEffect(...))` for Pitchstone Wall.
