Covers spell/ability/each-creature counters, wither & infect damage, proliferate, enters-with, persist, and put-counter costs (Devoted Druid)
- `ScryBeforeExploreReplacementEffect()` — STATIC replacement marker: before a creature you control explores, you scry 1. `ExploreEffectHandler` counts one marker per controlled copy and inserts one `ScryEffect` before each replacement-exempt explore continuation. Twists and Turns (LCI 217)
- `AddOnePlusOneCountersEffect()` — STATIC replacement marker (Hardened Scales): "if one or more +1/+1 counters would be put on a creature you control, that many plus one are put on it instead." Uses the shared +1/+1 counter replacement pipeline, adding one per marker the affected creature's controller controls. Covers spell/ability/each-creature counters, proliferate, enters-with, undying, explore, unleash, devour, tokens-with-counters, Vigor prevention, and put-counter costs. Multiple copies stack
- `AddOnePlusOneCountersToSourceEffect()` — STATIC source-bound replacement marker (Mowu, Loyal Companion): adds one +1/+1 counter only when +1/+1 counters would be put on the permanent carrying the marker, including that permanent entering with counters
- `DoubleCountersOnControlledPermanentsEffect()` — STATIC replacement marker (Doubling Season): doubles any counter type put on a permanent its controller controls. Uses the shared typed counter replacement pipeline, so it covers +1/+1, -1/-1, loyalty, charge, proliferate, enters-with, and other counter placement paths. Multiple copies stack multiplicatively
- `AddOnePlusOneCountersToCreaturesAndVehiclesEffect()` — STATIC replacement marker (Caradora, Heart of Alacria): the same replacement as `AddOnePlusOneCountersEffect`, but also applies to noncreature Vehicles the controller controls
- `AddOneCounterToArtifactOrCreatureEffect()` — STATIC replacement marker (Winding Constrictor): adds one of each counter type to counters put on an artifact or creature its controller controls. Uses the shared typed counter replacement pipeline
- `AddOnePlayerCounterEffect()` — STATIC replacement marker (Winding Constrictor): adds one of each tracked player counter whenever its controller would get one or more counters. Uses `GameQueryService.replacePoisonCounters` and `replaceEnergyCounters`
- `DoubleProliferateEffect()` — STATIC replacement marker (Tekuthal, Inquiry Dominus): each proliferate event controlled by this permanent's controller becomes two sequential proliferate events. Multiple copies stack multiplicatively
- `CantHavePlusOnePlusOneCountersEffect()` — STATIC per-creature marker (Blightbeetle): blocks +1/+1 counter placement on the creature carrying it, including counters from spells, abilities, proliferate, as-enters replacements, and damage-prevention effects. Usually granted with `GrantEffectEffect(..., GrantScope.OPPONENT_CREATURES)`; it does not block other counter types
- `PreventDamageToSelfFromCreaturesEffect(sourcePredicate)` — STATIC: "Prevent all damage that would be dealt to this creature by creatures" (Uncle Istvan), optionally restricted by a source predicate (Argothian Pixies uses artifact creatures). Prevents all damage (combat + noncombat) to the permanent carrying it whenever the source is a matching creature; noncreature sources are unaffected. Combat damage is prevented in `CombatDamageService`/`DamagePreventionService`; noncombat via `GameQueryService.isCreatureSourceDamageToSelfPrevented` hooked in `DamageSupport.dealCreatureDamage`
- `PreventDamageBySelfToCreaturesEffect(targetFilter)` — STATIC: prevent all damage dealt by this creature to matching creatures; `null` matches every creature. The target filter is re-evaluated for each damage event, so it follows current characteristics. Checked in `GameQueryService.isDamageFromPermanentSourceToCreaturePrevented` from both combat and noncombat creature-damage paths
- `PreventAllDamageToSelfFromCreaturesItBlocksEffect()` — STATIC: prevents all damage (combat + noncombat) to the source creature from creatures it is blocking (Wall of Vapor). The current blocking assignment is checked in both combat and noncombat creature-damage paths
- `WallOnlyTargetingRestrictionEffect()` — STATIC marker: the permanent cannot be targeted by a spell or ability whose legal targets are restricted to Walls. Checked by the targeting services, including modal spells' full mode union
- `PreventDamageToSelfAndSourceControllerDrawsEffect()` — STATIC: "If a source would deal damage to this creature, prevent that damage. The source's controller draws cards equal to the damage prevented this way" (Swans of Bryn Argoll). Prevents all damage (combat + noncombat, any source) to the permanent carrying it; the source's controller draws one card per point prevented. Via `DamagePreventionService.applySwansSourceControllerDraw`, hooked in `DamageSupport.dealCreatureDamage` (noncombat, covers burn/mass/bite) and `CombatDamageService.applyCombatCreatureDamage` (combat, per source)
- `PreventDamageToSelfAndDealThatMuchDamageEffect()` — STATIC: "If damage would be dealt to this creature, prevent that damage. When damage is prevented this way, this creature deals that much damage to any other target" (Phyrexian Vindicator). Prevents all damage (combat + noncombat) to itself; queues an `XValue` any-target reflexive trigger with the prevented amount and filters out the source permanent from permanent targets
- `PreventCombatDamageToSelfAndExileFromLibraryEffect()` — STATIC: "If combat damage would be dealt to this creature, prevent that damage and exile that many cards from the top of your library" (Gloom Surgeon). Combat damage only (noncombat burn/bite/mass damage still applies); its controller exiles that many cards off their library top, stopping early on an empty library. Via `DamagePreventionService.applyPreventCombatDamageToSelfAndExile`, hooked only in `CombatDamageService.applyCombatCreatureDamage`
- `PreventNextDamageFromSelfToYouEffect()` — activated ability: "The next time this creature would deal damage to you this turn, prevent that damage" (Mercenaries). No source choice — shields the activating player from the ability's source permanent via `playerSourceNextDamageShields`. Pair with `.withActivatableByAnyPlayer()`
- `PreventDamageFromChosenSourceEffect` (factories `nextDamageToYou()` / `nextHalfDamageToYou()` / `nextDamageToYouAndGainLife()` / `nextDamageToYouAndDamageSourceController()` / `nextDamageToYouAndExileFromLibrary()` / `nextDamageToYou(filter, label)` / `nextDamageToYouOfChosenColor()` / `nextDamageToAnyTarget()` / `nextDamageToAnyTargetAndDamageRedSourceController()` / `nextDamageToYouAndYourCreatures()` / `allDamageToYou()` / `allDamage(filter, label)` / `allDamageFromSourceSharingColorWithImprintedCard()`) — "a source of your choice would deal damage" prevention; source chosen on resolution, optionally restricted by a `PermanentPredicate` (Circle of Protection cycle) or by sharing a color with the imprinted card (Mourner's Shield). Dark Sphere's one-shot variant consumes the next matching event and prevents `damage / 2` rounded down, leaving the rest to be dealt. One-shot next-damage shields protect you (`playerSourceNextDamageShields`, Reverse Damage's variant also gains that much life; Deflecting Palm's variant also deals the prevented amount to the chosen source's controller) or any target (`sourceNextDamageToAnyTargetShields`, Sanctum Guardian; Honorable Passage adds a red-source rider that deals the prevented amount to the source's controller); the all-damage scope shields for the whole turn, controller-only (Auriok Replica) or globally (Burrenton Forge-Tender and Mourner's Shield)
- `PreventDamageFromChosenSourceEffect` (factories `nextDamageToYou()` / `nextDamageToYouAndGainLife()` / `nextDamageToYouAndDamageSourceController()` / `nextDamageToYouAndDamageSourceControllerAndDrawCards()` / `nextDamageToYouAndExileFromLibrary()` / `nextDamageToYou(filter, label)` / `nextDamageToYouOfChosenColor()` / `nextDamageToAnyTarget()` / `nextDamageToAnyTargetAndDamageRedSourceController()` / `nextDamageToYouAndYourCreatures()` / `allDamageToYou()` / `allDamage(filter, label)` / `allDamageFromSourceSharingColorWithImprintedCard()`) — "a source of your choice would deal damage" prevention; source chosen on resolution, optionally restricted by a `PermanentPredicate` (Circle of Protection cycle) or by sharing a color with the imprinted card (Mourner's Shield). One-shot next-damage shields protect you (`playerSourceNextDamageShields`, Reverse Damage's variant also gains that much life; Deflecting Palm's variant also deals the prevented amount to the chosen source's controller; New Way Forward's variant also draws that many cards) or any target (`sourceNextDamageToAnyTargetShields`, Sanctum Guardian; Honorable Passage adds a red-source rider that deals the prevented amount to the source's controller); the chosen-source key supports battlefield permanents and spells on the stack. The all-damage scope shields for the whole turn, controller-only (Auriok Replica) or globally (Burrenton Forge-Tender and Mourner's Shield)
- `RedirectTargetCreatureDamageFromChosenSourceToSelfEffect()` — activated ability targeting a creature: all damage a chosen source (picked on resolution) would deal to that creature this turn is dealt to the source permanent instead (Oracle's Attendants). Shield in `GameData.creatureDamageRedirectShields`, checked in both combat and noncombat creature-damage paths via `DamagePreventionService.applyCreatureRedirectShields`; reuses `pendingSourceRedirectDamage`
- `RedirectTargetCreatureDamageFromChosenSourceToTargetEffect()` — SPELL with two creature targets: all damage a chosen source (picked on resolution) would deal to the first target creature this turn is dealt to the distinct second target creature instead (Kor Chant). Reuses `CreatureDamageRedirectShield` with an unlimited amount and the existing source-choice interaction.
- `RedirectTargetCreatureNextDamageFromChosenSourceToControllerEffect()` — activated ability targeting a creature: the next single damage event a chosen source (picked on resolution) would deal to that creature this turn is dealt to the ability's controller (you) instead, then consumed (Jade Monolith). Same `creatureDamageRedirectShields` machinery as Oracle's Attendants, but redirects to a player and only for the next event (`CreatureDamageRedirectShield.NEXT_EVENT`)
- `RedirectNextCombatDamageFromTargetAttackingCreatureToSelfEffect()` — activated ability targeting an attacking creature: the next combat damage that creature would deal to the ability's source creature this turn is dealt to the attacking creature instead (Shield Dancer). Uses a source-specific next-event `CreatureDamageRedirectShield` with the combat-only flag; noncombat damage from the attacker is unaffected
- `RedirectNextDamageFromChosenSourceToTargetCreatureEffect()` — activated ability targeting a creature you control: the next damage event a chosen source (picked on resolution) would deal to the controller this turn is dealt to that creature instead, then consumed (General's Regalia). Uses a player-only source-keyed redirect shield and the shared pending redirect damage path for combat and noncombat damage
- `RedirectNextDamageEffect(RedirectRole protectedRole, RedirectRole destinationRole, DynamicAmount|int amount, TargetPredicate declaredTarget[, PermanentPredicate targetPredicate])` — "the next N damage that would be dealt to X this turn is dealt to Y instead", any source, consumed once N damage has been redirected. `RedirectNextDamageEffect.nextEvent(...)` handles "the next time damage" by redirecting the entire next damage event and then consuming the shield (Mirrorwood Treefolk). `RedirectRole` (`SOURCE_PERMANENT` / `TARGET` / `CONTROLLER` / `ENCHANTED_PERMANENT`, the last being the Aura source's attached permanent) fills both ends. A permanent-protecting instance installs an amount-limited or next-event `creatureDamageRedirectShield`; a player-protecting one installs a `PlayerNextDamageRedirectShield` (the player only, not their permanents). `targetSpec()` is harmful iff `destinationRole == TARGET` — that object takes the damage, so CR 702.16b protection applies; `null` means no target. Zhalfirin Crusader `(SOURCE_PERMANENT, TARGET, 1, ANY_TARGET)`, Zealous Inquisitor `(…, CREATURE)`, Personal Incarnation `(SOURCE_PERMANENT, CONTROLLER, 1, NONE)`, Martyrdom `(TARGET, SOURCE_PERMANENT, 1, ANY_TARGET)`, Hazduhr the Abbot / Daughter of Autumn `(TARGET, SOURCE_PERMANENT, XValue()/Fixed(1), CREATURE, whiteCreature)`, Vassal's Duty `(TARGET, CONTROLLER, Fixed(1), CREATURE)`, Ward of Piety `(ENCHANTED_PERMANENT, TARGET, 1, ANY_TARGET)`
- `RedirectNextDamageFromTargetToAnotherTargetEffect(DynamicAmount)` — SPELL with two distinct any-target groups: the next X damage that would be dealt to the first target creature, planeswalker, or player this turn is dealt to the second target instead (Captain's Maneuver). Uses the amount-limited creature/player redirect shields and shared pending-damage path; combat and noncombat damage can be redirected to any of the three recipient kinds.
- `RedirectNextInstantOrSorceryDamageToControllerEffect()` — activated ability: "the next time an instant or sorcery spell would deal damage to you this turn, that spell deals that damage to its controller instead" (Aegis of Honor). Installs one player shield per activation; only direct damage from an instant/sorcery spell matches, so combat and activated/triggered ability damage are unaffected. The shield is consumed on the next matching damage event and cleared at turn cleanup.
- `RedirectYourDamageToEnchantedCreatureThisTurnEffect()` — aura ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD (Saving Grace): "all damage this turn to you and permanents you control is dealt to enchanted creature instead." Installs a turn-scoped `TurnDamageRedirectToCreatureShield(controller, enchantedCreatureId)`; applied at all four damage paths via `DamagePreventionService.applyTurnDamageRedirectToCreature` (any source, unlimited, reuses `pendingSourceRedirectDamage`). Destination locked in at resolution; skipped while it's not a creature on the battlefield or when the damaged permanent IS the destination. Pair with `StaticBoostEffect(0, 3, ENCHANTED_CREATURE)`
- `RedirectYourDamageToChosenCreatureOrPlaneswalkerThisTurnEffect()` — SPELL (Gideon's Sacrifice): at resolution, choose a creature or planeswalker you control; all damage this turn to you and permanents you control is dealt to that chosen permanent instead. Uses the same turn-scoped `TurnDamageRedirectToCreatureShield` pipeline with planeswalker destinations enabled; the redirect is skipped if the chosen permanent is no longer a creature or planeswalker, or if the damage would be dealt to it itself.
- `DoubleCombatDamageToCreaturesThisTurnEffect()` — one-shot SPELL: for the rest of the turn, a creature dealing combat damage to a creature deals double instead (damage to players untouched). Increments `GameData.combatDamageToCreaturesDoublingsThisTurn`, applied in `GameQueryService.applyCombatDamageMultiplier` when the recipient is a creature; cleared at turn cleanup. Blind Fury = this + `RemoveKeywordEffect(TRAMPLE, ALL_CREATURES)`
- `DoubleControllerDamageThisTurnEffect()` — one-shot SPELL: for the rest of the turn, sources the controller controls deal double damage (combat + noncombat). Increments `GameData.controllerDamageDoublingsThisTurn` for the caster, applied in `GameQueryService.getControllerDamageMultiplier`; cleared at turn cleanup. Insult = this + `DamageCantBePreventedThisTurnEffect()`
- `DoubleDamageFromTargetPermanentThisTurnEffect()` — one-shot targeted SPELL: for the rest of the turn, the targeted permanent deals double damage (combat + noncombat). Keyed by permanent identity in `GameData.permanentDamageDoublingsThisTurn`, so multiple Overblazes stack and damage from other permanents is unaffected
- `RegisterOpponentGraveyardLifeLossThisTurnEffect()` — one-shot activated-ability effect: for the rest of the turn, whenever a card is put into an opponent's graveyard **from anywhere**, that player loses 1 life (Duskmantle Guildmage). Adds an `OpponentGraveyardLifeLossWatcher(controllerId, sourceCard)` to `GameData.opponentGraveyardLifeLossWatchers`; `GraveyardService.addCardToGraveyard` pushes one `LoseLifeEffect(1, TARGET_PLAYER)` trigger per watcher whose controller is an opponent of the graveyard owner. Each activation adds its own watcher (they stack) and the watcher outlives the source permanent; cleared at turn cleanup
- `RegisterLifeGainOpponentLifeLossThisTurnEffect()` — one-shot activated-ability effect: for the rest of the turn, whenever you gain life, each opponent loses that much life (Vizkopa Guildmage). Adds a `LifeGainOpponentLifeLossWatcher(controllerId, sourceCard)` to `GameData.lifeGainOpponentLifeLossWatchers`; `TriggerCollectionService.checkLifeGainTriggers` pushes one `LoseLifeEffect(EventValue(), EACH_OPPONENT)` trigger per watcher whose controller gained the life. Each activation adds its own watcher (they stack) and the watcher outlives the source permanent; cleared at turn cleanup
- `RegisterGlobalTriggeredAbilityUntilEndOfTurnEffect(EffectSlot, CardEffect)` — registers a turn-scoped global trigger whose source is a resolving spell rather than a permanent. The trigger collector supports `ON_ANY_CREATURE_DEALT_DAMAGE`, `ON_ANY_CREATURE_DIES`, and `ON_OPPONENT_DISCARDS`; the creature slots stamp the affected creature or its controller as a non-targeting target and stamp the event value, while the discard slot fires only for opponent-caused discards. Stored in `GameData.temporaryGlobalTriggeredAbilities`, copied for simulation, and cleared at turn cleanup. Shriveling Rot, Pure Intentions
- `RegisterGlobalTriggeredAbilityUntilEndOfTurnEffect(EffectSlot, CardEffect)` — registers a turn-scoped global trigger whose source is a resolving spell rather than a permanent. The trigger collector supports `ON_ANY_CREATURE_DEALT_DAMAGE`, `ON_ANY_CREATURE_DIES`, and `ON_ANY_CREATURE_BLOCKS`; it stamps the affected creature, blocker, or dying creature's controller as a non-targeting target and stamps the event value where applicable. Stored in `GameData.temporaryGlobalTriggeredAbilities`, copied for simulation, and cleared at turn cleanup. Shriveling Rot, Noxious Assault
- `RegisterGlobalTriggeredAbilityUntilEndOfTurnEffect(EffectSlot, CardEffect)` — registers a turn-scoped global trigger whose source is a resolving spell rather than a permanent. The trigger collector supports `ON_ALLY_CREATURE_ENTERS_BATTLEFIELD` for the registering player's creatures and `ON_ALLY_CREATURE_OR_ENCHANTMENT_ENTERS_BATTLEFIELD` for the registering player's creatures or enchantments, plus `ON_ANY_CREATURE_DEALT_DAMAGE` and `ON_ANY_CREATURE_DIES`; affected creatures or controllers are stamped as non-targeting event context where needed. Stored in `GameData.temporaryGlobalTriggeredAbilities`, copied for simulation, and cleared at turn cleanup. Shriveling Rot, Thunder of Unity, Rite of Harmony
- `DoubleDamageEffect()` — double all damage (static)
- `DoubleDamageToEnchantedPlayerEffect()` — double damage dealt to enchanted player (static Curse)
- `DoubleDamageToControllerAndSelfEffect()` — double damage dealt to this permanent's controller or to this permanent (static)
- `DoubleControllerDamageEffect(StackEntryPredicate, boolean)` — double controller's damage
- `DoubleDamageFromChosenSubtypeEffect()` — STATIC: double damage from sources carrying the subtype
  chosen for this permanent as it entered the battlefield; stacks multiplicatively with other damage
  multipliers and applies to combat and noncombat damage
- `DoubleDamageFromCreaturesEnteredThisTurnEffect()` — STATIC: double damage from creatures
  controlled by this permanent's controller that entered the battlefield this turn; applies to
  combat and noncombat damage and stacks multiplicatively with other source multipliers
- `AdditionalDamageToPlayersFromColorSourcesEffect(Set<CardColor>, int)` — STATIC: a source of any of those colours deals that much damage plus N to a **player** instead (Tok-Tok, Volcano Born = RED, 1). Every player, any controller, combat and noncombat alike; damage to permanents unaffected; stacks additively; only when damage > 0. Queried by `GameQueryService.getDamageToPlayerColorSourceBonus` from `DamageSupport.dealDamageToPlayer` and `CombatDamageService`
- `AdditionalDamageToOpponentsFromRedOrArtifactSourcesEffect(int)` — STATIC: red or artifact sources you control deal that much damage plus N to an opponent or a permanent an opponent controls instead. Covers spell/ability and combat damage, including creatures, planeswalkers, and battles; stacks additively. Queried by `GameQueryService.getAdditionalDamageToOpponentsBonus`
- `AdditionalDamageToOpponentsFromColorSourcesEffect(int, CardColor)` — STATIC: sources of the listed color you control deal that much damage plus N to an opponent or a permanent an opponent controls instead. Covers spell/ability and combat damage; stacks additively. Queried by `GameQueryService.getAdditionalDamageToOpponentsBonus`
- `AdditionalControllerDamageEffect(int, StackEntryPredicate)` — STATIC: matching controller sources deal that much damage plus N instead (Pyromancer's Gauntlet). Filter e.g. AnyOf[AllOf(TypeIn(INSTANT,SORCERY), ColorIn(RED)), AllOf(CardTypeIn(PLANESWALKER), ColorIn(RED))]; combat unaffected; stacks additively; only when damage > 0. Queried by `GameQueryService.getControllerDamageBonus` from `applyDamageMultiplier(GameData, int, StackEntry)`
- `AdditionalColorSourceDamageEffect(int, CardColor)` — STATIC: any *other* source of that color you control deals that much damage plus N instead (Embermaw Hellion). Unlike `AdditionalControllerDamageEffect` this covers combat damage too, and never boosts the permanent carrying it; two copies boost each other. Stacks additively; only when damage > 0. Queried by `GameQueryService.getColorSourcePermanentDamageBonus` from `applyDamageMultiplier(GameData, int, StackEntry)` and `applyCombatDamageMultiplier`
- `DoubleDamageToOpponentsAndTheirPermanentsEffect()` — STATIC, recipient-scoped: "if a source would deal damage to an opponent or a permanent an opponent controls, that source deals double that damage instead" (Gisela, Blade of Goldnight). Read via `GameQueryService.getDamageToRecipientMultiplier` at all four damage entry points (`DamageSupport.dealDamageToPlayer` / `dealCreatureDamageFromSource`, `CombatDamageService.applyPlayerDamage`, `GameQueryService.applyCombatDamageMultiplier`); source controller is irrelevant, only the recipient. Multiple instances under one controller stack multiplicatively
- `DoubleControllerDamageToOpponentsAndTheirPermanentsEffect()` — STATIC, source-and-recipient-scoped: "if a source you control would deal damage to an opponent or a permanent an opponent controls, it deals double that damage instead" (Twinflame Tyrant). `DoubleControllerDamageToOpponentsAndTheirPermanentsEffect(true)` restricts the same replacement to noncombat damage (Solphim, Mayhem Dominus). Read through `ControllerRecipientDamageMultiplyingEffect`; only the effect controller's sources qualify, and multiple instances stack multiplicatively
- `PreventHalfDamageToControllerAndTheirPermanentsEffect()` — STATIC, recipient-scoped: "if a source would deal damage to you or a permanent you control, prevent half that damage, rounded up" (Gisela, Blade of Goldnight). Leaves `damage / 2` rounded down; applied in `DamagePreventionService.applyHalfDamagePrevention`, reached from the two prevention choke points `applyPlayerPreventionShield` and `applyCreaturePreventionShield`, so it covers combat and noncombat alike. Inert while damage can't be prevented
- `SacrificePermanentThenEffect(PermanentPredicate, CardEffect, String[, boolean targetBeforeSacrifice])` — sacrifice then effect; normally a target needed by `thenEffect` is chosen as the reflexive trigger goes on the stack. Set `targetBeforeSacrifice` for abilities such as Daretti, Rocketeer Engineer that choose their target before offering the sacrifice
- `SacrificePermanentOrElseEffect(PermanentPredicate, CardEffect, String)` — the active player must sacrifice one matching permanent when a legal choice exists; otherwise the alternate effect resolves (Doom Foretold)
- `SacrificePermanentsOrElseEffect(PermanentPredicate, int count, CardEffect sacrificedEffect, CardEffect elseEffect, String description)` — at resolution the controller chooses none or exactly `count` matching permanents; the chosen permanents are sacrificed before `sacrificedEffect`, while no choice or an unavailable exact count resolves `elseEffect` (Giant Opportunity)
- `SacrificePermanentAndBoostSelfEffect(PermanentPredicate, int power, int toughness, String)` — synchronously sacrifice one matching permanent, then give the source +power/+toughness until end of turn; use for "if you do" self-boost wording. Wrap in `MayEffect` for optional sacrifice
- `SacrificePermanentAndGrantKeywordSelfEffect(PermanentPredicate, Set<Keyword>, String)` — synchronously sacrifice one matching permanent, then grant the source the configured keyword(s) until end of turn; use for "if you do" self-keyword wording. Wrap in `MayEffect` for optional sacrifice
- `MaySacrificePermanentForCounterOrTapSourceEffect(PermanentPredicate, String description)` — "you may sacrifice a [permanent]. If you do, put a +1/+1 counter on this creature. If you don't, tap this creature" (Ravenous Vampire, `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`). Prompts only when the controller has something matching (otherwise the source just taps); accepting with several legal permanents opens a `PermanentChoice`. Unlike `ForcedCostOrElseEffect` this one has a reward for paying, not only a penalty for declining
- `MaySacrificePermanentForCounterOrRemoveSourceCounterEffect(PermanentPredicate, CounterType, String description)` — "you may sacrifice a [permanent]. If you do, put a counter on this creature. If you don't, remove a counter from this creature" (Crovax the Cursed, `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`). Prompts only when the controller has something matching; no matching permanent applies the removal branch automatically. Accepting with several legal permanents opens a `PermanentChoice`
- `GargantuanGorillaUpkeepEffect()` — "you may sacrifice a Forest. If you sacrifice a snow Forest this way, this creature gains trample until end of turn. If you don't sacrifice a Forest, sacrifice this creature and it deals 7 damage to you" (Gargantuan Gorilla, `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`). Same shape as the Ravenous Vampire may-prompt/permanent-choice pair, but the reward is gated on the sacrificed Forest being snow (`hasEffectiveSupertype`, read before it leaves the battlefield). No Forest ⇒ no prompt, penalty applies
- `MagmasaurUpkeepEffect()` — "you may remove a +1/+1 counter from this creature. If you don't, sacrifice this creature and it deals damage equal to the number of +1/+1 counters on it to each creature without flying and each player" (Magmasaur, `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`). Same may-prompt shape as `GargantuanGorillaUpkeepEffect`; the penalty reads the counter count before the sacrifice (CR 608.2h last known information) and reuses `MassDamageEffect(Fixed(n), true, false, Not(HasKeyword(FLYING)))` through a synthetic stack entry. No counter ⇒ no prompt, sacrifice with no damage
- `DiscardCardThenEffect(CardPredicate, CardEffect, String)` — discard a matching hand card, then push thenEffect as a reflexive trigger. Wrap in `MayEffect` for "you may discard a [land] card. If you do, …" (Pack Guardian). Uses `DiscardFollowUp.thenEffect`
- `DiscardCardAndPutCounterOnSourceEffect(CardPredicate cardFilter, String cardDescription)` — discard one matching hand card, then put one +1/+1 counter on the source permanent. Wrap in `MayEffect` for "you may discard a card. If you do, put a +1/+1 counter on this creature"; uses `DiscardFollowUp.plusOnePlusOneCounters` so the source remains bound through the discard interaction
- `ShuffleCardsFromHandIntoLibraryThenEffect(int count, CardEffect thenEffect)` / `(CardEffect)` (count 1) — mandatory "shuffle N cards from your hand into your library. If you do, [thenEffect]". Begins the shuffle-in variant of `PendingInteraction.PutCardsFromHandOnLibraryCardChoice` (`shuffleIntoLibrary(...)`); the chosen cards go into the library, it is shuffled, and `thenEffect` is pushed as a reflexive trigger. Empty hand = nothing happens (no rider). Lat-Nam's Legacy = `ShuffleCardsFromHandIntoLibraryThenEffect(new RegisterDrawCardsAtNextUpkeepEffect(2))`
- `ScrollRackEffect()` — activated ability: exile any number of hand cards face down, put that many cards from the top of your library into your hand, then put the exiled cards back on top in any order (Scroll Rack). Begins the `swapWithLibraryTop(...)` variant of `PendingInteraction.PutCardsFromHandOnLibraryCardChoice`, which chains a `LibraryReorder` for the ordering. The library-to-hand move is not a draw
- `SacrificeAnotherCreatureDealPowerDamageToAnyTargetEffect()` — ETB `MayEffect`: sacrifice another creature, source deals that creature's power to any target (Heart-Piercer Manticore)
- `SacrificeAnotherCreatureGainLifeAndDrawEqualToPowerEffect()` — mandatory ETB: sacrifice another creature, gain X life and draw X cards where X is that creature's power (Disciple of Bolas)
- `AnyOpponentMaySacrificeCreatureTapAndGainLifeAndDrawSourceEffect()` — `BEGINNING_OF_COMBAT_TRIGGERED`: every opponent may sacrifice a creature in APNAP order; if any opponent does, tap the source, its controller gains 3 life, and draws a card (Clackbridge Troll)
- `SacrificeAnotherCreatureDrawAndMayPutPermanentEffect()` — optional attack trigger: sacrifice another creature, draw X cards, then optionally put a permanent card with mana value X or less from hand onto the battlefield, where X is the sacrificed creature's mana value (Venom, Lethal Protector)
- `SpellCastTriggerEffect(CardPredicate, List<CardEffect>)` + overloads — spell cast trigger. `SpellCastTriggerEffect.nth(int, CardPredicate, List<CardEffect>)` additionally fires only on the controller's Nth matching spell this turn (Vengevine). Gate on cast-spell MV == source counters via `StackEntryManaValueEqualsSourceCountersPredicate` as `castSpellTargetCondition` (Imminent Doom); collector snapshots `CountersOnSource` damage amounts to Fixed at trigger time
- `DealDamageEqualToSpellManaValueToAnyTargetEffect(CardPredicate)` — spell-cast trigger that deals damage equal to the triggering spell's mana value to any target; usable in controller- or opponent-cast slots (Rage Extractor, Kaervek the Merciless)
- `SacrificeOtherCreatureOrDamageEffect(DynamicAmount, DamageRecipient)` / `(int)` — sacrifice another creature; if none can be sacrificed, deal the evaluated damage to the selected player. The compact form uses the source controller and excludes the source; `ENCHANTED_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER` uses the enchanted permanent's controller and excludes the enchanted permanent (Lord of the Pit, Rite of Belzenlok, Unnatural Hunger)
- `SpellCastTriggerEffect(CardPredicate, List<CardEffect>)` + overloads — spell cast trigger. Gate on cast-spell MV == source counters via `StackEntryManaValueEqualsSourceCountersPredicate` as `castSpellTargetCondition` (Imminent Doom); collector snapshots `CountersOnSource` damage amounts to Fixed at trigger time
- `SpellCastTriggerEffect.anyPlayer(CardPredicate, List<CardEffect>)` — emblem spell-cast trigger that fires for every player's matching spells rather than only the emblem controller's spells (Kaito, Cunning Infiltrator)
- `SpellCopyTriggerEffect(CardPredicate, List<CardEffect>)` — controller spell-copy trigger in `ON_CONTROLLER_COPIES_SPELL`; copy creation paths dispatch it after putting the copy on the stack, and targeted resolved effects use the normal triggered-target flow (Ral, Storm Conduit)
- `SpellweaverHelixTriggerEffect()` — ON_ANY_PLAYER_CASTS_SPELL trigger for Spellweaver Helix; compares the cast card's name with its two source-tracked exiled sorceries, offers to copy the other card, then reuses `CopyImprintedCardAndMayCastCopyEffect` for the free-cast choice. Cast copies are ignored
- `CreateTokenForTriggeringPlayerEffect(CreateTokenEffect token)` — creates the token under the player whose spell cast caused the surrounding `SpellCastTriggerEffect`; the collector carries that caster without making them a target and snapshots the triggering spell's full mana value for `EventValue` power/toughness. Pure Reflection
- `PutPlusOnePlusOneCounterOnSourceOnChosenColorSpellCastEffect(int amount)` — ON_CONTROLLER_CASTS_SPELL trigger descriptor: put +1/+1 counters on the source when its controller casts a spell containing the source permanent's chosen color (Diamond Knight); pair with `ChooseColorOnEnterEffect()`
- `BoostSelfByCastSpellManaValueEffect(CardPredicate spellFilter)` — ON_CONTROLLER_CASTS_SPELL: source gets +X/+X until end of turn where X = cast spell's mana value (`null` filter = any spell). Manaplasm
- `MillTargetPlayerByCastSpellManaValueEffect(CardPredicate spellFilter)` — ON_CONTROLLER_CASTS_SPELL: target player mills X cards where X = the triggering spell's mana value; the collector snapshots the value and uses the normal player-target mill flow. Cloudhoof Kirin
- `GainLifeEqualToCastSpellManaValueEffect(CardPredicate spellFilter)` — ON_CONTROLLER_CASTS_SPELL: controller gains X life where X = the triggering spell's full mana value; the collector snapshots the value and queues a normal `GainLifeEffect`. Bounteous Kirin
- `GainControlOfTargetCreatureByCastSpellManaValueEffect(CardPredicate spellFilter)` — ON_CONTROLLER_CASTS_SPELL: optionally gain control of a target creature with mana value equal to the triggering spell's full mana value until end of turn; resolves through `GainControlOfTargetEffect` with an exact creature/mana-value predicate. Skyfire Kirin
- `DiscardAllCardsWithCastSpellManaValueEffect(CardPredicate spellFilter)` — ON_CONTROLLER_CASTS_SPELL: target player reveals their hand and discards every card with the triggering spell's mana value; the collector snapshots the value and reuses `RevealHandAndDiscardMatchingCardsEffect` with exact min/max mana-value predicates. Infernal Kirin
- `DestroyAllPermanentsWithCastSpellManaValueEffect(CardPredicate spellFilter)` — ON_CONTROLLER_CASTS_SPELL: destroy all permanents with the triggering spell's mana value; the collector snapshots the value and composes exact min/max mana-value predicates. Celestial Kirin
- `CasterLosesLifeOnSpellCastEffect(CardPredicate spellFilter, int amount)` — ON_ANY_PLAYER_CASTS_SPELL: "that player" (the caster) loses N life (Soot Imp)
- `CasterLosesLifeOnSpellCastEffect(CardPredicate spellFilter, int amount[, boolean useSpellManaValue])` — ON_ANY_PLAYER_CASTS_SPELL or ON_CONTROLLER_CASTS_SPELL: "that player" (the caster) loses fixed life, or the cast spell's mana value when `useSpellManaValue` is true (Soot Imp, Teval, Arbiter of Virtue)
- `GainLifeForSameNameCardsInGraveyardsOnSpellCastEffect()` — ON_ANY_PLAYER_CASTS_SPELL: the caster gains life equal to matching cards in all graveyards (Aven Shrine); the collector snapshots the spell name and resolves the amount with `CardsInGraveyard(..., ANY_PLAYER)` and `GainLifeRecipient.TRIGGERING_PLAYER`
- `CreateSquirrelTokensForSameNameCardsInGraveyardsOnSpellCastEffect()` — ON_ANY_PLAYER_CASTS_SPELL: the caster creates one 1/1 green Squirrel token per matching card in all graveyards (Nantuko Shrine); the collector snapshots the spell name and queues `CreateTokenForTargetPlayerEffect(CreateTokenEffect(CardsInGraveyard(..., ANY_PLAYER), Squirrel 1/1))` with the caster as the non-target recipient
- `ReturnSameNameCardsFromGraveyardsToBattlefieldOnCreatureSpellCastEffect()` — ON_ANY_PLAYER_CASTS_SPELL: when a creature spell is cast, each player returns every card with that name from their graveyard to their battlefield (Bloodbond March); the collector snapshots the spell name and reuses `EachPlayerReturnsCardsFromGraveyardToBattlefieldEffect(Integer.MAX_VALUE, CardNamedPredicate)`
- `CounterUnlessPaysForSameNameCardsInGraveyardsOnSpellCastEffect()` — ON_ANY_PLAYER_CASTS_SPELL: counter the cast spell unless its caster pays the number of matching cards in all graveyards (Cephalid Shrine); the collector snapshots the spell name and queues `CounterUnlessPaysEffect(new CardsInGraveyard(..., ANY_PLAYER))`
- `DiscardForSameNameCardsInGraveyardsOnSpellCastEffect()` — ON_ANY_PLAYER_CASTS_SPELL: the caster discards the number of matching cards in all graveyards (Cabal Shrine); the collector snapshots the spell name and queues `DiscardEffect(new CardsInGraveyard(..., ANY_PLAYER), TARGET_PLAYER)` with the caster bound as a non-target
- `BecomePreparedEffect()` — source becomes "prepared" (Strixhaven); exiles a castable copy of its prepare spell (back face)
- `MakeTargetCreaturePreparedEffect()` — target creature becomes prepared; no-op if already prepared or no prepare spell
- `MakeTargetCreatureUnpreparedEffect()` — target creature becomes unprepared; no-op if not prepared

- `DealDamageForSameNameCardsInGraveyardsOnSpellCastEffect()` — ON_ANY_PLAYER_CASTS_SPELL: the caster is dealt twice the number of matching cards in all graveyards (Dwarven Shrine); the collector snapshots the spell name and queues `DealDamageToPlayersEffect(Scaled(CardsInGraveyard(..., ANY_PLAYER), 2), TRIGGERING_PLAYER)` with the caster bound as a non-target

See EFFECTS_INDEX.md "Damage" section for 15+ additional niche damage effects.

## Destruction / sacrifice
- `MaySacrificePermanentForCounterSourceEffect(PermanentPredicate, String description)` — resolution-time optional sacrifice of one matching permanent; if accepted and paid, put a +1/+1 counter on the source. Declining or having no matching permanent does nothing. Uses `MaySacrificeForCounterSupport` and `PermanentChoiceContext.MaySacrificeForCounterOnSource`; High-Society Hunter (`ON_ATTACK`)

- `DestroyTargetCreaturesThenPutCountersOnSourceIfOwnDestroyedEffect()` — destroys every chosen target creature in one batch and puts two +1/+1 counters on the source if a creature controlled by the effect controller was actually destroyed; pair with `target(TargetFilters.creature(), 0, 99)` and `MultiTargetConstraint.AT_MOST_ONE_PER_CONTROLLER` for “for each player, destroy up to one target creature that player controls” (Demonic Junker)

- `DestroyTargetPermanentEffect(boolean cantRegen)` or `(boolean, CreateTokenEffect)` — destroy target; `forTargetGroup(int)` reads a later activated-ability target group
- `DismantleEffect()` — destroy target artifact, snapshot its total counter count, then choose +1/+1 or charge counters and put that many on an artifact you control; the target must be an artifact, and the post-destruction choices happen at resolution
- `DestroyTargetNonblackCreatureAndGainControlOfAttachedEquipmentEffect()` — destroy target nonblack creature without allowing regeneration, then gain permanent control of every Equipment that was attached to it. The handler snapshots the attached Equipment before destruction; indestructible prevents only the destruction, not the control gain. Murderous Spoils
- `DestroyTargetEquipmentAndDamageAttachedCreatureEffect()` — destroy target Equipment, then deal 2 damage through the normal spell-damage pipeline to the creature it was attached to when destruction succeeded. The handler snapshots the attached creature before destruction; no damage is dealt when the Equipment is unattached or indestructible. Unforge
- `ExileTargetCreatureAndSameNameFromBattlefieldHandAndGraveyardEffect()` — exile target creature with mana value 2 or less, same-name creatures controlled by its controller, and same-name cards from that player's hand and graveyard after revealing the hand; the card target filter supplies opponent control. Legion's End
- `ExileTargetCreatureAndSameNameControlledByItsControllerThenInvestigateEffect()` — exile target creature and all other same-name creatures controlled by its controller, then that player creates one Clue for each nontoken creature exiled this way. Declaration in Stone

`DestroyTargetPermanentEffect(PermanentPredicate targetFilter)` narrows the declared permanent target while retaining the default regeneration behavior.

- `DestroyTargetPermanentEffect(boolean cantRegen)` or `(boolean, CreateTokenEffect)` — destroy target
- `DestroyTargetPermanentAndAllWithSameNameEffect(PermanentPredicate targetPredicate[, PermanentPredicate sameNamePredicate])` — destroy target matching `targetPredicate` + every other same-name permanent matching `sameNamePredicate` across all battlefields (regeneration/indestructible respected); the one-argument form applies the target predicate to same-name permanents too; `RemovalEffect` (DESTROY). Maelstrom Pulse, Wake of Destruction
- `DestroyTargetAndOtherEnchantmentsSharingColorEffect()` — destroy target enchantment and every other enchantment sharing at least one effective color with it; target colors are read at resolution and the whole batch respects indestructible/regeneration. Leave No Trace
- `ExileTargetPermanentAndAllWithSameNameFromZonesEffect(PermanentPredicate[, CardSubtype, boolean])` — exile target permanent, then exile every same-name card from its controller's graveyard, hand, and library and shuffle; the optional subtype gates the zone search and the optional boolean changes it to an any-number choice; `RemovalEffect` (EXILE), harmful spec narrowed by the supplied predicate; fizzles if the target leaves. Scour (enchantment), Eradicate (nonblack creature), Deicide (God enchantment, any number)
- `DestroyOtherPermanentsWithEnteringNameEffect()` — ON_ANY_PERMANENT_ENTERS_BATTLEFIELD: destroy all *other* permanents with the entering permanent's name (can't regenerate); reads `triggeringPermanentId` / `triggeringCardId`. Pair with `TriggeringCardConditionalEffect` excluding basic lands. Eye of Singularity
- `DestroyOtherCreaturesSharingColorWithEnteringCreatureEffect()` — ON_ANY_PERMANENT_ENTERS_BATTLEFIELD: destroy all *other creatures* sharing at least one color with the entering creature (can't regenerate); reads `triggeringPermanentId` / `triggeringCardId`. Pair with `TriggeringCardConditionalEffect(CardTypePredicate(CREATURE))`. Spreading Plague
- `DestroyReferencedPermanentEffect(PermanentReference[, boolean cannotBeRegenerated])` — destroy one permanent named without targeting it; `cannotBeRegenerated` ignores regeneration shields. Never fizzles — a reference that no longer resolves is a silent no-op. `SOURCE` = the ability's own permanent ("destroy this Aura": Arachnus Web on `END_STEP_TRIGGERED`, Ice Cage on `ON_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_SPELL_OR_ABILITY`, Aether Storm's pay-4-life ability with `true`). `ATTACHED` = the permanent the source Aura/Equipment is attached to (Spreading Algae / Blight / Brink of Disaster on `ON_ENCHANTED_PERMANENT_TAPPED`, Yoke of the Damned on `ON_ANY_CREATURE_DIES`, Mortal Wound / Hot Soup on `ON_ENCHANTED_CREATURE_DEALT_DAMAGE`, Spinal Graft on `ON_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_SPELL_OR_ABILITY` with `true`, Aggression under a `ConditionalEffect` — those collectors propagate the Aura's source-permanent id so the host is re-derived at resolution). `TRIGGERING` = the permanent whose event produced the trigger (`StackEntry.triggeringPermanentId`); pair with `TriggeringCardConditionalEffect` on `ON_ANY_PERMANENT_ENTERS_BATTLEFIELD` (Suleiman's Legacy: destroy entering Djinn/Efreet, can't regenerate)
- `DestroyEachTargetPermanentEffect(boolean cantRegen[, PermanentPredicate filter])` or `()` — destroy every matching target in the group; an optional filter is checked at resolution while the target group remains broader (Blow Your House Down). Bind to one multi-target group. Pair with `targetX(filter, cap)` for "Destroy X target …" (Dregs of Sorrow). Records the count actually put into a graveyard this way onto the entry's event value for a later `EventValue` reader (Volcanic Eruption + `MassDamageEffect(EventValue(), true)`)
- `DestroyEachTargetArtifactThenCreateTokenCopyEffect()` — destroy every targeted artifact; at X=5 or more, create a hasty token copy of each artifact actually destroyed and exile those tokens at the next end step (Red Sun's Twilight)
- `DestroyTargetPermanentAtEndStepEffect()` — destroy at end step
- `DestroyTargetIfDidNotAttackAtEndStepEffect()` — destroy target at next end step if it didn't attack this turn (queues `DestroyPermanentIfDidNotAttackAtEndStep`). Pair with `SetCombatRequirementThisTurnEffect(CombatRequirement.MUST_ATTACK)` for Norritt
- `SacrificeTargetPermanentAtEndStepEffect()` — sacrifice the target at next end step (Lowland Oaf); sacrifice, not destruction (ignores indestructible/regeneration). `new SacrificeTargetPermanentAtEndStepEffect(true)` flips first and sacrifices only on a loss (Goblin Kites)
- `SacrificeTargetPermanentAtEndStepAndGainLifeEqualToToughnessEffect()` — at the next end step, sacrifice the target creature only if the controller still controls it, then gain life equal to its current toughness (Spinal Embrace)
- `DestroyTargetCreatureAndCreateSpiritCopyToSacrificeEffect()` — destroy target creature (can't be regenerated), then create a black Spirit token for the controller with the destroyed creature's last-known power/toughness, sacrificed at next end step. Token created even if destruction fails (indestructible). Card supplies the target filter (Broken Visage: nonartifact attacking creature)
- `DestroyTargetCreatureThenCreateTokenEqualToPowerToughnessEffect(CreateTokenEffect)` — destroy target creature, then create one token under the effect controller only if the creature was destroyed; the token template receives the creature's last-known effective power and toughness. Used by Kalitas, Bloodchief of Ghet
- `ReturnTargetPermanentToHandAtEndStepEffect()` — return the target to its owner's hand at next end step (Dragon Mask); pair with a pump on the shared target
- `ReturnSelfToHandAtEndStepEffect()` — return the source permanent to its owner's hand at the beginning of the next end step (Rakalite); use on an activated ability when the source itself is the delayed return
- `ExileSelfAtEndStepEffect()` — exile the source permanent (no target) at next end step (Dark Maze); pair with `CanAttackAsThoughNoDefenderEffect` on a `{0}` attack ability
- `ExileSelfEffect()` — exile the source permanent immediately (no target); the exile sibling of `SacrificeSelfEffect`. Use as the then-effect of `ExileTargetPermanentThenEffect` for "Exile this creature and target creature ..." (Giant Trap Door Spider) so the self-exile is part of resolution, not a cost
- `ExileSelfReturnAtNextUpkeepWithHasteEffect()` — exile the source permanent (no target) and queue a `PendingExileReturn` that brings it back under its owner's control at the beginning of that owner's **next upkeep**, with haste for as long as it stays on the battlefield. Wrap in `MayEffect` for "you may exile ~" (Obzedat, Ghost Council, `CONTROLLER_END_STEP_TRIGGERED`). The `onlyOnControllersTurn` flag on `PendingExileReturn` is what turns "the next upkeep" into "*your* next upkeep"; the returning permanent is flagged `Permanent.enteredFromExile`, so a mandatory targeted ETB targets as the trigger goes on the stack (CR 603.3b)
- `ExileSelfReturnAtNextTurnDeclareAttackersEffect()` — exile the source permanent (no target) and queue a `PendingExileReturn` that brings it back under its controller's control, tapped and attacking, at the beginning of that controller's next declare-attackers step. The controller chooses an opponent or opposing planeswalker for the returning creature to attack (Meandering Towershell)
- `SacrificeCreatedPermanentsAtEndStepEffect()` — sacrifice every permanent created earlier in the SAME resolution (`StackEntry.createdPermanentIds`) at the beginning of the next end step; place in the same slot right after a `CreateTokenEffect` for "Create a … token. Sacrifice it at the beginning of the next end step." (Tidal Wave). Sacrifice, not destruction
- `ExileCreatedPermanentsAtControllerEndStepEffect()` — exile every permanent created earlier in the SAME resolution (`StackEntry.createdPermanentIds`) at the beginning of the resolving controller's next end step; place in the same slot right after a `CreateTokenEffect` for "Create a … token. Exile it at the beginning of your next end step." (Stone Idol Trap). Skips intervening opponent end steps
- `SacrificeCreatedPermanentsAtEndOfCombatEffect()` — sacrifice every permanent created earlier in the SAME resolution (`StackEntry.createdPermanentIds`) at end of combat; place in the same slot right after a `CreateTokenEffect` for "Create a … token. Sacrifice it at end of combat." (Gemini Engine). Sacrifice, not destruction
- `ExileCreatedPermanentsAtNextCleanupEffect()` — exile every permanent created earlier in the SAME resolution (`StackEntry.createdPermanentIds`) at the beginning of the next cleanup step; place in the same slot right after a `CreateTokenEffect` for Waylay-style token cleanup.
- `MakeCreatedPermanentsAttackingEffect()` — make every permanent created earlier in the SAME resolution attack without tapping, carrying the source permanent's attack target onto them (Gemini Engine)
- `SacrificeOwnCreaturesAtEndStepEffect(PermanentPredicate)` — capture the controller's matching creatures at resolution and schedule them for sacrifice at the beginning of the next end step. The captured set does not change if they stop attacking; use a filter such as `PermanentIsAttackingPredicate` for Tears of Rage.
- `SacrificeSelfAtEndStepEffect()` — sacrifice the source permanent (no target) at next end step (Brackwater Elemental); put on `ON_ATTACK`/`ON_BLOCK` for "when this creature attacks or blocks, sacrifice it at the beginning of the next end step". Sacrifice, not destruction
- `SacrificeSelfAtNextCleanupEffect()` — flag the source permanent (no target) for sacrifice at the beginning of the next cleanup step; put on `ON_ATTACK`/`ON_BLOCK` for an Aura that sacrifices when its enchanted creature attacks or blocks (Cunning)
- `DestroySelfAtEndStepEffect()` — destroy the source permanent (no target) at next end step (Crazed Armodon's `{G}` pump ability); regular destruction, so indestructible/regeneration apply
- `DestroyAllPermanentsEffect(PermanentPredicate)` / `(PermanentPredicate, boolean)` / `(PermanentPredicate, CardEffect thenEffect)` / `(PermanentPredicate, EachPermanentScope, CardEffect thenEffect)` — board wipe, optionally scoped to the targeted player's battlefield (`TARGET_PLAYER` declares its own `TargetSpec.harmful(player())`, so "destroy all lands target player controls" needs no extra filter — Ajani Vengeant's ultimate is `(PermanentIsLandPredicate, TARGET_PLAYER, null)`; add a `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter` only to narrow it to an opponent) and/or with a per-destroyed-count rider: destroyed count lands on `eventValue`, so "gain 2 life for each destroyed" = `GainLifeEffect(Scaled(EventValue(), 2))`, "draw a card for each" = `DrawCardEffect(EventValue())`, "X/X token where X = destroyed" = `CreateTokenEffect` with `EventValue()` P/T (Phyrexian Rebirth)
- `DestroyAllPermanentsOfChosenColorEffect()` / `(PermanentPredicate filter)` — the controller chooses a color at resolution, then the engine composes that color with the optional permanent filter and resolves a normal `DestroyAllPermanentsEffect` across all battlefields. Used for Root Greevil's colored enchantment wipe.
- `DestroyAllPermanentsEffect.sparingPermanentsCreatedThisResolution(PermanentPredicate)` — "destroy all *other* creatures" wipe that spares permanents this same resolution created (`StackEntry.createdPermanentIds`); pair after an unconditional `CreateTokenEffect` so the just-made tokens survive (Martial Coup)
- `SeasonOfTheWitchEffect()` — END_STEP_TRIGGERED, non-targeting: destroy the active player's untapped creatures that did not attack this turn but could have attacked. Uses `CombatAttackService` so creatures unable to attack are spared; ordinary destruction means indestructible and regeneration apply
- `DestroyLandsUnlessAnyPlayerPaysLifeEffect(int lifeCost)` — non-targeting per-land sequence: lands are processed in APNAP order (the resolving controller's battlefield order within each controller), and each land is offered to players in APNAP order; the first acceptance pays the life and keeps that land, while a full decline destroys it. Used by Cleansing `(1)`.
- `SelfExiledFromBattlefieldEffect(CardEffect wrapped)` — trigger descriptor for a wrapped effect that fires from `ON_SELF_LEAVES_BATTLEFIELD` only when the source is put into exile from the battlefield. Used by Urza's Sylex's optional planeswalker search
- `EachPlayerChoosesCreatureDestroyRestEffect()` — choose one, destroy rest
- `ChooseUpToOneCreatureDestroyRestEffect()` — the spell's controller chooses up to one creature on any battlefield; destroy every other creature
- `DestroyTargetAndEachPlayerSearchesBasicLandToBattlefieldEffect()` — destroy + each searches
- `EachOpponentMaySearchLibraryForBasicLandToBattlefieldTappedEffect()` — opponents search
- `EachOpponentMaySearchLibraryForCreatureToBattlefieldEffect()` — each opponent may tutor a creature onto the battlefield (untapped), then shuffle; APNAP; Boldwyr Heavyweights
- `EachOpponentMaySearchLibraryForLandToBattlefieldEffect()` — each opponent may tutor a land onto the battlefield untapped, then shuffle; APNAP; Hired Giant
- `EachOtherPlayerMayReturnCardsFromGraveyardToBattlefieldEffect(int maxCount, CardPredicate)` — each player other than the resolving controller may independently return up to `maxCount` matching cards from their own graveyard to the battlefield under their control, in APNAP order; choices remain optional even when only one card matches (Enslaved Horror)
- `AnyOpponentMayTakeDamageSacrificeSourceEffect(int)` — ETB: any opponent may take N damage from the source; if any do, sacrifice it (even if damage prevented). APNAP may prompts; Vexing Devil (4)
- `AnyPlayerMayTakeDamageOrBoostSelfEffect(int damage, int powerBoost, int toughnessBoost)` — activated ability: players in APNAP order may have the source deal damage to themselves; the first acceptance stops the sequence, while an all-decline sequence gives the source the temporary self-boost. Barbarian Bully (4, +2/+2)
- `AnyPlayerMayTakeDamageOrMillTargetPlayerEffect(int damage, int millCount)` — SPELL: players in APNAP order may have the source deal damage to themselves; the first acceptance stops the sequence, while an all-decline sequence mills the target player. Book Burning (6, 6)
- `AnyPlayerMayTakeDamageOrDrawTargetPlayerEffect(int damage, int drawCount)` — SPELL: players in APNAP order may have the source deal damage to themselves; the first acceptance stops the sequence, while an all-decline sequence makes the target player draw the specified number of cards. Browbeat (5, 3)
- `AnyPlayerMayTakeDamageOrDestroyAllCreaturesEffect(int damage)` — SPELL: players in APNAP order may have the source deal damage to themselves; the first acceptance stops the sequence, while an all-decline sequence destroys all creatures without allowing regeneration. Breaking Point (6)
- `WormsOfTheEarthEffect()` — each upkeep, players in APNAP order may first choose to sacrifice two lands, or instead accept 5 damage; the first player who accepts either option pays that cost and destroys the source. If the sacrifice option is declined, the same player receives the damage option; if both are declined, the next player is offered the choice
- `PutCreatureFromHandThenSacrificeUnlessPayReducedEffect(int genericReduction)` — SPELL: you may put a creature from your hand onto the battlefield; then sacrifice it unless you pay its mana cost reduced by `{genericReduction}` (generic only, floored at 0). Declinable card choice + a resolution-time pay-or-sacrifice may ability; Flash (`2`)
- `PutCardToBattlefieldEffect(...).returningToHandAtEndStep()` — hand-card choice rider: return the chosen permanent to its owner's hand at the beginning of the next end step, only if it is still on the battlefield (Surprise Deployment)
- `PutCardToBattlefieldThenEffect(predicate, label, thenCondition, thenEffect)` — declinable hand-card choice that puts a matching card onto the battlefield and resolves the follow-up only when the chosen card matches the condition (Spelunking)
- `PutCardFromHandToBattlefieldWithArtifactCountersEffect(CardPredicate, String label, int counterCount)` — activated-ability effect: choose a matching card from hand to put onto the battlefield; if the entered permanent is an artifact, place `counterCount` +1/+1 counters on it. Reuses the normal hand-card choice and counter-placement support (Oviya, Automech Artisan)
- `PutCreatureFromHandWithManaValueXThenReturnSourceToHandEffect()` — activated ability: you may put a creature card with mana value exactly X from your hand onto the battlefield; if chosen, return the source creature to its owner's hand. The source return is inserted only after a card is chosen, so declining or having no exact-X creature leaves both cards in place (Metathran Aerostat)
- `EachPlayerMaySearchLibraryForCardsToHandEffect(DynamicAmount count, boolean creatureOnly)` — each player (APNAP) may tutor up to `count` cards to hand; `()` = up to X creatures, revealed (Weird Harvest); `oneCard()` = one card of any kind, not revealed (Noble Benefactor)
- `SearchLibraryAndOrGraveyardForCardToHandEffect(CardPredicate)` — choose up to one matching card from the controller's library and/or graveyard, reveal it, and put it into hand; library selections and failed library searches shuffle, while graveyard-only selections do not (Fang-Druid Summoner)
- `SearchLibraryForSameNameAsDyingCreatureToHandEffect()` — death-trigger effect for a nontoken creature you control: may search your library for a card with the dying creature's snapshotted name, reveal it, put it into your hand, then shuffle (Remembrance). The nontoken-death collector binds the name before queuing the may ability; resolution delegates to the restricted `SearchLibraryEffect` path.
- `SearchZonesForCardNamedToBattlefieldEffect(String cardName[, boolean includeHand, boolean attachToTarget])` — search the controller's graveyard, optionally hand, and/or library for a card named `cardName` and put it onto the battlefield; graveyard then hand matches are auto-taken, only the library search prompts + shuffles (Gate to the Afterlife → God-Pharaoh's Gift). `includeHand=false` skips the hand; `attachToTarget=true` attaches the found card to the target creature (harmful CREATURE target; illegal target fizzles the ability before any search) — Arachnus Spinner → Arachnus Web
- `DestroyTargetPermanentAndDamageControllerIfDestroyedEffect(int)` — destroy + conditional damage
- `DestroyTargetPermanentUnlessControllerTakesDamageEffect(int damage)` — the targeted permanent's controller chooses whether the source deals `damage` to them; declining destroys the targeted permanent. Implements `RemovalEffect(DESTROY)` and `DamageDealingEffect` (players only), with a harmful permanent target. Used by Dwarven Driller.
- `DealDamageToTargetCreatureUnlessControllerTakesDamageEffect(int targetDamage, int controllerDamage)` — the targeted creature's controller chooses whether the source deals `controllerDamage` to them; declining deals `targetDamage` to the creature. Harmful creature target. Used by Dwarven Scorcher.
- `DestroyUpToTargetsThenReturnFromGraveyardEffect()` — destroy each targeted permanent and return cards put into graveyard this way under your control (multi-target via ability `minTargets`/`maxTargets`)
- `DestroyTargetPermanentThenEffect(EventStat, CardEffect thenEffect, ThenEffectRecipient[, PermanentPredicate])` — collapsed destroy-plus-value family. Destroy the target, then resolve an existing then-effect. `recipient` CONTROLLER (you) / TARGET_CONTROLLER (destroyed permanent's controller — also for "its controller searches" riders) / TARGET_OWNER / TARGET_CONTROLLER_AS_TARGET (rider stays under the caster, derived entry's `targetId` = destroyed permanent's controller — for `DealDamageToPlayersEffect(N, TARGET_PLAYER)` "deals N damage to its controller" riders, Cryoclasm/MeltTerrain). `EventStat` NONE/MANA_VALUE/TOUGHNESS/POWER snapshots the destroyed permanent's last-known stat onto `eventValue` for a `GainLifeEffect(EventValue())` / `BoostSelfEffect(EventValue(), Fixed(0))` then-effect. Then-effects: `GainLifeEffect`, `BoostSelfEffect`, `LoseLifeEffect`, `GivePoisonCountersEffect`, `DealDamageToPlayersEffect`, `SearchLibraryEffect` (Ghost Quarter/Erode "may search" = plain restricted search — it can always fail to find). Optional `PermanentPredicate` gates the then-effect on the destroyed permanent's state (Death's Caress HUMAN). Then-effect happens even if destruction fails (indestructible) unless the trailing `requiresDestruction` flag is set ("if a white creature dies this way", Cinder Cloud)
- `DestroyTargetPermanentsThenEffect(CardEffect thenEffect[, boolean cannotBeRegenerated])` — multi-target sibling: destroys **every** permanent in the entry's target group, then resolves the rider once with the number of permanents *actually* destroyed on `eventValue`. Rider is skipped entirely when nothing was destroyed (so a zero-count `SearchLibraryEffect` never shuffles). `RemovalEffect` (DESTROY). Sylvan Primordial = `(SearchLibraryEffect(EventValue(), CardSubtypePredicate(FOREST), BATTLEFIELD_TAPPED))` with `target(TargetFilters.noncreaturePermanentAnOpponentControls(), 0, 99)` + `AT_MOST_ONE_PER_CONTROLLER`
- `CreateTokenForEachDestroyedPermanentControllerEffect(CreateTokenEffect)` — after `DestroyEachTargetPermanentEffect`, creates one token under each destroyed permanent's controller for every permanent actually put into a graveyard (Terastodon)
- `DestroySourceAndDamageControllerIfDestroyedEffect(int damage)` — destroy source; deal `damage` to its controller **only if actually destroyed** (regeneration/indestructible respected). Self analog of `DestroyTargetPermanentAndDamageControllerIfDestroyedEffect`; used as a `ForcedCostOrElseEffect` fallback (Cosmic Horror). Interpreted in `DestructionSupport.resolveForcedCostElseEffects`, not the dispatch registry
- `DestroySourceThenDealDamageToControllerEffect(DynamicAmount damage)` — destroy the source permanent, then have that source deal the evaluated damage to its controller; the amount is evaluated before destruction and the damage happens even if destruction is prevented. Voodoo Doll
- `DealDamageToControllerThenTapSourceIfDamageDealtEffect(int damage)` — ForcedCostOrElse fallback: deal N to controller, then tap the source **iff** life dropped ("if it deals damage to you this way, tap it"). Minion of Leshrac (5). Prevention/redirect leaves source untapped. Interpreted in `DestructionSupport.resolveForcedCostElseEffects`, not the dispatch registry
- `DestroyCreatureAttachedToEnchantedEquipmentEffect()` — destroy the creature the Equipment enchanted by the source Aura is attached to (Artificer's Hex, on `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`)
- `DestroyEnchantedPermanentUnlessPaysManaOrLifeEffect(String manaCost, int lifeCost)` — `ENCHANTED_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER_UPKEEP_TRIGGERED` punisher: the enchanted permanent's controller may pay `manaCost` **or** `lifeCost` life to save it, else it's destroyed. Erosion (`"{1}", 1`). Accept spends floating mana if available, otherwise life; can't-pay-either auto-destroys
- `TargetPlayerMayPayManaOrLifeEffect(String manaCost, int lifeCost, CardEffect wrapped, String prompt)` — "that player may pay {M} or N life. If the player does, [wrapped]". Payer = stack entry `targetId` (active player under `EACH_UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`), unlike `MayPayManaEffect` which asks the controller. No prompt when neither resource is payable; accept spends mana if available, otherwise life. Emberwilde Djinn (`"{R}{R}", 2, TargetPlayerGainsControlOfSourceCreatureEffect()`)
- `DestroyCreatureBlockingThisEffect()` — destroy blocker
- `ReturnCombatOpponentToHandAtEndOfCombatEffect()` — "Whenever this creature blocks a creature, return that creature to its owner's hand at end of combat" (Kaijin of the Vanishing Touch). The bounce sibling of `DestroyCombatOpponentAtEndOfCombatEffect`; put on ON_BLOCK and/or ON_BECOMES_BLOCKED (`TriggerMode.PER_BLOCKER`). Non-targeting (`CombatOpponentReferencingEffect`); queues `DelayedPermanentAction(RETURN_TO_HAND_AT_END_OF_COMBAT)`, so the creature still deals combat damage and is returned only if still on the battlefield
- `ReturnSelfToHandAtEndOfCombatEffect()` — "When this creature attacks or blocks, return it to its owner's hand at end of combat" (Quicksilver Behemoth). Put on ON_ATTACK and ON_BLOCK; queues `DelayedPermanentAction(RETURN_TO_HAND_AT_END_OF_COMBAT)`, so the source still deals combat damage and is returned only if it is still on the battlefield
- `DestroyCombatOpponentAtEndOfCombatEffect(PermanentPredicate filter, boolean cannotBeRegenerated)` — Basilisk-style "blocks or becomes blocked by a [filter] creature, destroy that creature at end of combat". Put on ON_BLOCK + ON_BECOMES_BLOCKED (`TriggerMode.PER_BLOCKER`); filter re-checked at resolution (Deathgazer nonblack). Destroys at end of combat, not immediately. Also works on an **Aura/Equipment** — it implements `CombatOpponentReferencingEffect`, so `CombatTriggerService` auto-targets the enchanted/equipped creature's combat opponent (Venom = non-Wall filter, `PermanentNotPredicate(PermanentHasSubtypePredicate(WALL))`)
- `DestroyCombatOpponentEffect(boolean cannotBeRegenerated)` — immediate combat trigger: destroy the creature this permanent blocks or that blocks it. Put on ON_BLOCK and/or ON_BECOMES_BLOCKED (`TriggerMode.PER_BLOCKER`); the combat opponent is carried as a non-targeting target through `CombatOpponentReferencingEffect`. Evil Eye of Urborg
- `TapCombatOpponentAtEndOfCombatEffect()` — "At end of combat, tap all creatures that blocked this creature this turn. They don't untap during their controller's next untap step." Put on ON_BECOMES_BLOCKED (`TriggerMode.PER_BLOCKER`). A `CombatOpponentReferencingEffect`, so the blocker is baked in as the non-targeting `targetId`. Resolution queues a `TapAndSkipUntapAtEndOfCombat`; `CombatService.processEndOfCombatTaps()` taps it and bumps `skipUntapCount`. Joven's Ferrets
- `SacrificeCombatOpponentAtEndOfCombatEffect(CreateTokenEffect tokenForSacrificingPlayer)` — "Whenever this creature becomes blocked by a creature, that creature's controller sacrifices it at end of combat. If the player does, they create a [token]." Put on ON_BECOMES_BLOCKED (`TriggerMode.PER_BLOCKER`) and/or ON_BLOCK. A `CombatOpponentReferencingEffect`, so the opponent is baked in as the non-targeting `targetId`. Resolution queues a `SacrificeAtEndOfCombat` carrying the token rider; `CombatService.processEndOfCombatSacrifices()` sacrifices the creature (bypassing regeneration/indestructible) and only then creates the token for **that creature's controller** — nothing is created if it already left the battlefield. Pass `null` for no rider. Basalt Golem (0/2 colorless Wall artifact creature token with defender)
- `PutCounterOnCombatOpponentAtEndOfCombatEffect(CounterType counterType, int amount)` / `(…, boolean alsoTap)` — Greater Werewolf-style "At end of combat, put a [counter] on each creature blocking or blocked by this creature". Put on ON_BLOCK + ON_BECOMES_BLOCKED (`TriggerMode.PER_BLOCKER`); the combat opponent is queued a `PutCounterOnPermanentAtEndOfCombat` delayed action, applied in `CombatService.processEndOfCombatOpponentCounters` (respects `cantHaveCounters`). Greater Werewolf = `(CounterType.MINUS_ZERO_MINUS_TWO, 1)`. Dread Wight = `(CounterType.PARALYZATION, 1, true)` — also taps; paralyzation counters prevent untap during the untap step and grant `{4}: Remove a paralyzation counter` on placement. The `MINUS_ZERO_MINUS_TWO` counter subtracts 2 toughness each (`Permanent.getEffectiveToughness`/`getToughnessModifiers`)
- `PutCounterOnCombatOpponentEffect(CounterType, int[, CardEffect grantedStaticEffect, CardEffect grantedUpkeepEffect])` — immediate counters on the creature this blocks / that blocks it, optionally granting source-specific persistent abilities when counters are placed. A `CombatOpponentReferencingEffect`, so the block pipeline auto-binds the opponent as a non-targeting target. Mindbender Spores supplies its fungus-counter untap lock and upkeep-removal ability through the optional fields; fungus counters themselves have no intrinsic rules
- `CantAttackNextTurnCombatOpponentEffect()` — Wall of Dust "Whenever this creature blocks a creature, that creature can't attack during its controller's next turn." Put on ON_BLOCK; a `CombatOpponentReferencingEffect`, so `CombatBlockService` auto-targets the blocked attacker (non-targeting). Sets `Permanent.cantAttackNextTurn`; `TurnProgressionService` promotes it to `cantAttackThisTurn` at that creature's controller's next turn start (only for the active player's permanents, so it never arms on the opponent's turn) and self-clears after that one turn; barred in `CombatAttackService.canCreatureAttack`
- `DestroySelfAtEndOfCombatEffect()` — schedule the **source** permanent for destruction at end of combat (regeneration/indestructible apply, unlike `SacrificeAtEndOfCombatEffect`). "When this creature blocks/attacks, destroy it at end of combat." Put on ON_BLOCK / ON_ATTACK. Cinder Wall
- `ReturnSelfToHandAtEndOfCombatEffect()` — schedule the **source** permanent to be returned to its owner's hand at end of combat. "When this creature attacks or blocks, return it to its owner's hand at end of combat." Put on ON_ATTACK / ON_BLOCK. The delayed action skips the source if it already left the battlefield. Phantom Whelp
- `DelayedEndOfCombatEffect(CardEffect effect)` — schedule the wrapped non-targeting ability to trigger at the beginning of the end-of-combat step. The delayed trigger carries the source card and controller, so it still triggers if the source permanent has left the battlefield. Put on ON_ATTACK / ON_BLOCK for wording such as "flip a coin at end of combat." Fickle Efreet
- `DestroyTargetPermanentAtEndOfCombatEffect()` / `(boolean cannotBeRegenerated)` — schedule the **targeted** permanent for destruction at end of combat (`RemovalEffect` DESTROY; `targetSpec()` harmful CREATURE). "Destroy it at end of combat" riders on a spell/activated ability that also does something else to the same target. Goblin Sappers (paired with `MakeCreatureUnblockableEffect`, plus `DestroySelfAtEndOfCombatEffect` on the cheap mode)
- `DealDamageToTargetCreatureAtEndOfCombatEffect(int damage)` / `(DynamicAmount)` — schedule the **source** to deal `damage` to the **targeted** creature at end of combat (delayed sibling of `DealDamageToTargetCreatureEffect`). `DamageDealingEffect`; `targetSpec()` harmful CREATURE. Queues a `DealDamageToPermanentAtEndOfCombat` carrying the source card (last-known information), drained in `CombatService.processEndOfCombatDamage()` which then runs state-based actions. Dwarven Sea Clan
- `DestroyCombatOpponentsOfTargetAtEndOfCombatEffect()` — "Choose target creature. At this turn's next end of combat, destroy all creatures that blocked or were blocked by it this turn" (Venomous Breath). `RemovalEffect` DESTROY; `targetSpec()` harmful CREATURE. Queues a `DestroyCombatOpponentsAtEndOfCombat` delayed action; the opponent set is read from `GameData.combatBlockOpponentIdsThisTurn` at drain time in `CombatService.processEndOfCombatCombatOpponentDestructions()`, so blocks declared after resolution count
- `GrantTrampleToCreaturesBlockedByTargetEffect()` — "Creatures that were blocked by that creature this combat gain trample until end of turn" (Ride Down). Its benign target spec requires a blocking creature; resolution snapshots that permanent's current `blockingTargetIds` and grants trample to each surviving creature before the companion `DestroyTargetPermanentEffect()` resolves
- `TapCombatOpponentsOfTargetAtEndOfCombatEffect()` — one-shot spell effect for "At this turn's next end of combat, tap each creature that was blocked by one of those creatures this turn and it doesn't untap during its controller's next untap step" (Triton Tactics). Bind it to an `up to two target creatures` group alongside `BoostTargetCreatureEffect` and `UntapPermanentsEffect(TapUntapScope.ALL_TARGETS)`. Queues one `TapCombatOpponentsAtEndOfCombat` action per chosen creature; the handler reads the directional `GameData.combatOpponentIdsBlockedByThisTurn` map at end of combat and deduplicates attackers across the chosen creatures.
- `PutMinusOneCounterOnSourceAtEndOfCombatEffect()` — schedule the **source** permanent to get a -1/-1 counter at end of combat (delayed, so it stays full size during combat damage — unlike immediate `PutCountersOnSourceEffect(-1,-1,1)`). "Whenever this creature attacks or blocks, put a -1/-1 counter on it at end of combat." Put on ON_ATTACK and/or ON_BLOCK. Wicker Warcrawler
- `PutCounterOnSourceAtEndOfCombatEffect(CounterType, int)` / `(CounterType, int, CreateTokenEffect)` — generic sibling of the above: schedule the **source** to get `int` counters of any `CounterType` at end of combat, optionally also creating a token for its controller. "At end of combat, if this creature attacked or blocked this combat, put a -0/-1 counter on this creature and create a 0/1 white Deserter creature token." Put on ON_ATTACK + ON_BLOCK. Queues `PutCounterOnPermanentAtEndOfCombat` for the source; nothing happens if the source already left the battlefield. Kjeldoran Home Guard
- `RemoveCounterFromSourceAtEndOfCombatEffect(CounterType)` — schedule the **source** permanent to have one counter of the given type removed at end of combat (delayed, so it stays full size during combat damage), clamped at zero. "At end of combat, if this creature attacked or blocked this combat, remove a [type] counter from it." Put on ON_ATTACK and/or ON_BLOCK (scheduling only from those slots encodes the "attacked or blocked" condition). Removal-analog of `PutMinusOneCounterOnSourceAtEndOfCombatEffect`; drained in `CombatService.processEndOfCombatCounterRemovals`. Clockwork Beast (`CounterType.PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ZERO`)
- `PutCappedCountersOnSourceEffect(CounterType, DynamicAmount amount, int cap)` — put up to `amount` counters of the type on the **source**, never raising the total of that type above `cap`. "Put up to X [type] counters on this creature. This ability can't cause the total to be greater than [cap]." Clockwork Beast's `{X}, {T}` upkeep ability = `(CounterType.PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ZERO, new XValue(), 7)`. `PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ZERO` counters add 1 power each (`Permanent.getEffectivePower`/`getPowerModifiers`)
- `SacrificePermanentsEffect(count, PermanentPredicate, SacrificeRecipient)` — collapsed forced-sacrifice family. `SacrificeRecipient` = CONTROLLER / TARGET_PLAYER / TARGET_PLAYER_OR_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER / EACH_PLAYER / EACH_OPPONENT / DEFENDING_PLAYER. `DEFENDING_PLAYER` adds no target — it reads the `ON_ATTACK` trigger's `attackedTargetId` (the attacked player, or the attacked planeswalker's controller); use for "Whenever this creature attacks, defending player sacrifices a creature of their choice" (Thraximundar). `TARGET_PLAYER_OR_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER` adds no target — it piggybacks on a companion `DealDamageToTargetPlayerOrPlaneswalkerEffect`'s targetId (targeted player, or targeted planeswalker's controller, sacrifices; Nicol Bolas, Planeswalker -9). On `ON_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER` the `TARGET_PLAYER` recipient needs no `target(...)` — the effect reports `CombatDamageTriggerContextEffect.DAMAGED_PLAYER`, so `CombatDamageService` bakes the damaged player in (Akki Underminer). Bare `PermanentIsCreaturePredicate` → single-select "sacrifice a creature" (Cruel Edict, Grave Pact, Stitcher's Apprentice); any other filter (`PermanentTruePredicate` = any permanent) → multi-permanent choice (Storm Fleet Arsonist, Yawning Fissure, Destructive Force). int-count sugar ctor. Optional 4th arg `countPerSacrificingPlayer=true` re-evaluates the count once per sacrificing player, so a `PermanentCount(…, CountScope.CONTROLLER)` counts that player's OWN permanents — "each player sacrifices a Plains or a white permanent of their choice for each white permanent they control" (Omen of Fire); a per-player count of 0 skips that player. Call `withRecordedSacrificeCount()` on a direct single-player multi-permanent sacrifice when a following `EventValue` amount needs the actual count (Hellion Eruption)
- `ChooseKeptPermanentOfEachTypeThenSacrificeRestEffect()` / `(List<CardType> types, boolean sacrificeAllPermanents, boolean eachPlayerChooses[, SacrificeRecipient recipient])` — keeps one permanent per listed type for each affected player, then sacrifices the rest. The no-arg form is Tragic Arrogance (artifact/creature/enchantment/planeswalker; the controller chooses and lands are spared); Cataclysm uses artifact/creature/enchantment/land with both booleans `true`, so each player chooses and lands outside the kept set are sacrificed too. Liliana, Dreadhorde General supplies artifact/battle/creature/enchantment/land/planeswalker, `true`, `true`, and `EACH_OPPONENT`. Choices happen in APNAP order, single candidates auto-keep, one permanent may fill multiple type slots, and the applicable leftovers are sacrificed simultaneously
- `EachPlayerSacrificesDownToCountEffect(int count, PermanentPredicate filter)` — each player chooses matching permanents to keep down to `count` and sacrifices the rest in active-player order; the sacrifices happen simultaneously. Limited Resources uses `(5, PermanentIsLandPredicate)`
- `PlayersCantCastSpellTypesUntilEndOfYourNextTurnEffect(Set<CardType> restrictedTypes)` — every player can't cast the configured spell types until the end of the effect controller's next turn; the restriction expires during that turn's cleanup. Single Combat
- `SacrificePermanentOrLoseLifeEffect(PermanentPredicate filter, int lifeLoss)` — the player on the stack entry's `targetId` (the enchanted player, for a Curse upkeep trigger) sacrifices one matching permanent of their choice; if they control none, they lose `lifeLoss` life instead (edict-with-punisher). Non-targeting — the acted-on player is supplied on the stack entry, not chosen at cast. Cruel Reality (`PermanentAnyOfPredicate(creature, planeswalker)`, 5)
- `LoseLifeUnlessSacrificeNonlandOrDiscardEffect(int lifeLoss)` / `(int lifeLoss, LoseLifeRecipient recipient)` — a player loses `lifeLoss` life unless they sacrifice a nonland permanent of their choice or discard a card. The affected player picks; options pruned to what they can do (life always offered), so no-nonland/no-hand just loses life without a prompt. `recipient` (only `TARGET_PLAYER` / `TARGET_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER`): `TARGET_PLAYER` (compact-ctor default) acts on the stack entry's `targetId` player (Torment of Scarabs, Curse upkeep); `TARGET_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER` acts on the controller of the targeted permanent and excludes that permanent from the sacrifice options ("another nonland permanent") — pair it with a sibling `PutCounterOnTargetPermanentEffect` that owns the target, listed first (Torment of Venom). Single-player, single-pass slice of `TormentOfHailfireEffect`, reusing its `ChoiceContext.TormentPenaltyChoice` / `PermanentChoiceContext.TormentSacrifice` plumbing and the shared `GameData.torment` state. Non-targeting.
- `SacrificeOtherPermanentUnlessDiscardForEachLifeLostEffect()` — `ON_CONTROLLER_LOSES_LIFE`: for each 1 life lost, sacrifice a permanent other than the source unless you discard a card (lands OK; source excluded). Life lost → `eventValue`; loops via `GameData.torment`. Two-way `ChoiceContext.OathOfLimDulPenaltyChoice`; neither available ⇒ skip that life point. Oath of Lim-DAdz?l
