- `TargetPlayerChoosesCreatureDestroyEffect()` — SPELL, player-targeting spec: target opponent chooses a creature they control, then it is **destroyed** (regeneration/indestructible apply — this is the destroy analog of the "sacrifice a creature" edict). 0 creatures ⇒ nothing; 1 ⇒ auto; 2+ ⇒ target picks. Imperial Edict
- `OpponentChoosesCreatureToDestroyEffect()` — non-targeting: an opponent of the controller chooses **any** creature on the battlefield and it is destroyed (regeneration/indestructible apply). 0 ⇒ nothing; 1 ⇒ auto; 2+ ⇒ opponent picks. Pair after `DestroyTargetPermanentEffect` for "destroy target creature of your choice, then destroy target creature of an opponent's choice" (Diaochan, Artful Beauty)
- `OpponentChoosesCreatureTheyControlTokenCopyEffect()` — non-targeting: an opponent of the controller chooses a creature **they control**; the controller creates a token copy of it that gains haste and is exiled at the beginning of the next end step. 0 ⇒ nothing; 1 ⇒ auto; 2+ ⇒ opponent picks via `OpponentChoosesCreatureTheyControlToCopy`. Echo Chamber
- `EachOpponentChoosesCreatureYouGainControlEffect()` — non-targeting (`ControlStealingEffect`, PERMANENT): each opponent chooses a creature they control (APNAP; 0 ⇒ skip, 1 ⇒ auto, 2+ ⇒ prompt); then you gain permanent control of all chosen creatures simultaneously. Riches (Rags // Riches)
- `PlayerDestroysPermanentsEffect(count, PermanentPredicate, DestroyRecipient)` — a player chooses and **destroys** N of their own permanents matching the filter (regeneration/indestructible apply — the destroy analog of `SacrificePermanentsEffect`). `DestroyRecipient` = CONTROLLER / TARGET_PLAYER (TARGET_PLAYER makes the spec player-targeting). ≤N matching ⇒ all destroyed, no choice; >N ⇒ player picks which. int-count sugar ctor. "You destroy four lands you control" = `(4, PermanentIsLandPredicate, CONTROLLER)`; Burning of Xinye uses both recipients + `MassDamageEffect(4)`
- `SacrificeCreatureAndControllerGainsLifeEqualToToughnessEffect(boolean sacrificerIsController, boolean sacrificerGainsLife)` — sacrifice + life = toughness. `sacrificerIsController` `false` = target sacrifices (edict, Tribute to Hunger / Devour Flesh); `true` = controller sacrifices, non-targeting (Doomgape upkeep). `sacrificerGainsLife` `false` = controller gains the life (Tribute to Hunger); `true` = the sacrificing player gains it (Devour Flesh)
- `EachPlayerSacrificesGreatestManaValueCreatureUnlessPaysEffect()` — non-targeting SPELL: in APNAP order, each player sacrifices the creature they control with the greatest mana value **unless they pay that creature's mana cost**; a player with ties picks which tied creature is at risk. Punisher prompt via the may-ability system (decline or can't-pay ⇒ sacrifice). Tariff. Sequenced by `TariffSupport` + `gameData.tariffRemainingPlayers`; tie-break uses `PermanentChoiceContext.TariffTieBreak`
- `SacrificeCreatureToCreateTokensEqualToToughnessEffect(CreateTokenEffect template, PermanentPredicate filter)` — controller sacrifices a matching creature, then creates X copies of `template` where X = sacrificed creature's toughness (template `amount` ignored). Wrap in `MayEffect` for "you may sacrifice" (e.g. Feed the Pack)
- `SacrificeCreatureCreateSizedTokenEqualToPowerEffect(CreateTokenEffect template, PermanentPredicate filter)` — controller sacrifices a matching creature, then creates ONE `template` token whose power and toughness are each equal to the sacrificed creature's power (template `power`/`toughness`/`amount` ignored). The single-token, power-sized analog of the toughness effect above. Ooze Garden (green Ooze template; `PermanentNotPredicate(PermanentHasSubtypePredicate(OOZE))` for "non-Ooze")
- `TargetPlayerSacrificesCreatureThenCreateTokensIfSubtypeEffect(CardSubtype requiredSubtype, CreateTokenEffect tokenTemplate)` — targets a player (player-targeting spec); that player sacrifices a creature of their choice, and if it had `requiredSubtype` (last-known info) the same player creates the template tokens under their own control. Warren Weirding ("gains haste until end of turn" → template's `grantedKeywordsUntilEndOfTurn`)
- `TargetPlayerSacrificesCreatureThenDrawsPowerEffect()` — targets a player; that player sacrifices a creature of their choice, then the effect controller draws cards equal to the sacrificed creature's effective power captured before removal. Twisted Justice
- `TargetPlayerSacrificesRandomPermanentsUntilLandEffect()` — targeted player sacrifices a random permanent they control; repeats while the sacrificed permanent is nonland. Engine-chosen, fully synchronous. Tyrant of Discord
- `SacrificeTargetCreatureThenCreateTokensEqualToPowerEffect(CreateTokenEffect tokenTemplate)` — targets a creature (permanent-targeting spec); its controller sacrifices it, then that same player creates X copies of `template` where X = the creature's effective power captured before removal (template `amount` ignored). Mercy Killing (1/1 green-and-white Elf Warrior template). The power-based, targeted, controller-creates analog of `SacrificeCreatureToCreateTokensEqualToToughnessEffect`
- `SacrificeTargetCreatureThenCreateTokenEffect(CreateTokenEffect tokenTemplate)` — target creature's controller sacrifices it, then creates the fixed token template (Sarkhan the Mad)
- `SacrificeTargetArtifactCreatureThenMaySearchLibraryForNoncreatureArtifactEffect()` — targets an artifact creature; its controller sacrifices it, then may search that player's library for a noncreature artifact card and put it onto the battlefield (Arcum Dagsson). The handler snapshots the controller before sacrifice and queues the normal filtered `SearchLibraryEffect` under that player.
- `SacrificeOneOfTwoTargetCreaturesThenCounterOnOtherEffect()` — **two targets** (`targetIds`, both "a creature an opponent controls"): that opponent chooses one and sacrifices it, the other gets a -1/-1 counter. Wire with two `target(TargetFilters.creatureAnOpponentControls())` groups, the effect bound to the first. Retribution
- `ExileOneOfTwoTargetCreaturesThenPutCountersOnOtherEffect()` — **two targets** (both creatures controlled by the same player): the spell controller chooses one to exile on resolution, then the other gets two +1/+1 counters. Wire with two `target(TargetFilters.creature())` groups, `MultiTargetConstraint.CONTROLLED_BY_FIRST_TARGET`, and the effect bound to the first. Cannibalize
- `SacrificeOneOfTwoTargetCreaturesThenReturnOtherToHandEffect()` — **two targets** (`targetIds`, creatures controlled by the same player): that player chooses one and sacrifices it, the other returns to its owner's hand. Wire with two `target(TargetFilters.creature())` groups, `MultiTargetConstraint.CONTROLLED_BY_FIRST_TARGET`, and the effect bound to the first. Barrin's Spite
- `ExileTargetCreatureCardCreateTokensEqualToToughnessEffect(CreateTokenEffect tokenTemplate)` — SPELL: exile the single graveyard target (any graveyard; `benign(graveyardCard(ALL_GRAVEYARDS))`), then create X copies of `template` where X = the exiled card's printed toughness (template `amount` ignored). Morbid Bloom (1/1 green Saproling template). The graveyard-exile analog of `SacrificeCreatureToCreateTokensEqualToToughnessEffect`; the creature-card restriction is enforced by a `@ValidatesTarget` validator. Single target ⇒ whole spell fizzles (no tokens) if the card leaves the graveyard
- `ExileTargetCreatureCardCreateTokenEqualToPowerToughnessEffect(CreateTokenEffect tokenTemplate)` — SPELL: exile the single creature-card target from the controller's graveyard (`benign(graveyardCards(CardTypePredicate(CREATURE), CONTROLLERS_GRAVEYARD))`), then create one copy of `template` with power and toughness replaced by the exiled card's printed power and toughness. The creature-card and own-graveyard restrictions are enforced by a `@ValidatesTarget` validator. Single target ⇒ whole spell fizzles (no token) if the card leaves the graveyard. Ritual of the Returned (black Zombie template)
- `ExileTargetCreatureCardFromGraveyardGainLifeEqualToToughnessEffect()` — exile the single graveyard target (any graveyard; `benign(graveyardCard(ALL_GRAVEYARDS))`), then gain life equal to its printed toughness. Rotfeaster Maggot (`ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD`). The life-gain analog of `ExileTargetCreatureCardCreateTokensEqualToToughnessEffect`; the creature-card restriction is enforced by a `@ValidatesTarget` validator
- `ExileTargetCreatureCardFromGraveyardDealPowerDamageToSourceEffect()` — exile the single creature-card target from any graveyard, then that exiled card deals damage equal to its power to the source permanent. Selfless Exorcist; the card is represented as the damage source so its damage keywords remain effective
- `ExileTargetCardFromGraveyardAndGainLifeEffect(CardPredicate filter, int lifeGain)` — exile one matching card from any graveyard, then give the controller fixed life; if the target is gone or no longer matches, the combined effect gains no life. Grave Robbers (`DRK`)
- `ExileTargetCreatureCardFromGraveyardPutPowerCountersEffect([int graveyardTargetGroup, int creatureTargetGroup])` — exile the targeted graveyard creature card, then put that many +1/+1 counters on the targeted creature (X = the exiled card's printed power). Two independent target groups (defaults 0 and 1); Flesh // Blood's Flesh half
- `ForcedCostOrElseEffect(CostEffect, List<CardEffect>[, boolean optional[, boolean anyPlayerMayPay[, boolean payerIsEnchantedController]]])` — cost-like instruction; if it cannot be performed, resolve fallback effects. `optional=true` makes it a "you may [cost]. If you don't, [fallback]" choice (Yawgmoth Demon); default `false` is mandatory (Archdemon of Greed). `anyPlayerMayPay=true` (Icy Prison, Rhystic Shield) offers the pay prompt to each player in APNAP order — first accept stops the sequence; only a full decline resolves the fallback. `payerIsEnchantedController=true` / factory `enchantedControllerMayPay(...)` prompts the stack `targetId` player (Mind Whip enchanted controller; Pillar Tombs of Aku each-upkeep active player) instead of the source controller — works for `PayManaCost` and `SacrificePermanentCost`. Supported costs: `SacrificePermanentCost` (single), `SacrificeMultiplePermanentsCost` (N of a filter, e.g. Rathi Dragon), and `ReturnMultiplePermanentsToHandCost` (N of a filter, e.g. Ovinomancer). Supported fallbacks: `BoostAllOwnCreaturesEffect`, `TapPermanentsEffect(SELF|ENCHANTED)`, `LoseLifeEffect(CONTROLLER|TARGET_PLAYER)`, `DealDamageToPlayersEffect(CONTROLLER|ENCHANTED_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER, Fixed)`, `SacrificeSelfEffect`, `DestroyReferencedPermanentEffect(SOURCE)`, `DestroySourceAndDamageControllerIfDestroyedEffect`, `DealDamageToControllerThenTapSourceIfDamageDealtEffect`, `LoseLifeEffect(CONTROLLER, Fixed)`, `OpponentMayGainControlOfCreatureYouControlEffect(duration)` (Infernal Denizen), `OpponentGainsControlOfSourceCreatureEffect(duration)` + `RemoveAllCountersEffect(CounterType)` (Rogue Skycaptain)
- `ForcedCostOrElseEffect(CostEffect, List<CardEffect>[, boolean optional[, boolean anyPlayerMayPay[, boolean payerIsEnchantedController[, boolean payerIsDefendingPlayer]]]])` — cost-like instruction; if it cannot be performed, resolve fallback effects. `optional=true` makes it a "you may [cost]. If you don't, [fallback]" choice (Yawgmoth Demon); default `false` is mandatory (Archdemon of Greed). `anyPlayerMayPay=true` (Icy Prison, Rhystic Shield) offers the pay prompt to each player in APNAP order — first accept stops the sequence; only a full decline resolves the fallback. `payerIsEnchantedController=true` / factory `enchantedControllerMayPay(...)` prompts the stack `targetId` player (Mind Whip enchanted controller; Pillar Tombs of Aku each-upkeep active player) instead of the source controller — works for `PayManaCost` and `SacrificePermanentCost`. `payerIsDefendingPlayer=true` / factory `defendingPlayerMayPay(...)` prompts the defending player of the `ON_ATTACK` trigger (the entry's `attackedTargetId`: attacked player, or attacked planeswalker's controller) — Ogre Marauder, "unless defending player sacrifices a creature of their choice". Supported costs: `SacrificePermanentCost` (single), `SacrificeMultiplePermanentsCost` (N of a filter, e.g. Rathi Dragon), and `ReturnMultiplePermanentsToHandCost` (N of a filter, e.g. Ovinomancer). Supported fallbacks: `BoostAllOwnCreaturesEffect`, `TapPermanentsEffect(SELF|ENCHANTED)`, `LoseLifeEffect(CONTROLLER|TARGET_PLAYER)`, `DealDamageToPlayersEffect(CONTROLLER|ENCHANTED_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER, Fixed)`, `SacrificeSelfEffect`, `DestroyReferencedPermanentEffect(SOURCE)`, `DestroySourceAndDamageControllerIfDestroyedEffect`, `DealDamageToControllerThenTapSourceIfDamageDealtEffect`, `LoseLifeEffect(CONTROLLER, Fixed)`, `OpponentMayGainControlOfCreatureYouControlEffect(duration)` (Infernal Denizen), `OpponentGainsControlOfSourceCreatureEffect(duration)` + `RemoveAllCountersEffect(CounterType)` (Rogue Skycaptain), `MakeCreatureUnblockableEffect(true)` (Ogre Marauder)
- `ForcedCostOrElseEffect(CostEffect, List<CardEffect>[, boolean optional[, boolean anyPlayerMayPay[, boolean payerIsEnchantedController]]])` — cost-like instruction; if it cannot be performed, resolve fallback effects. `optional=true` makes it a "you may [cost]. If you don't, [fallback]" choice (Yawgmoth Demon); default `false` is mandatory (Archdemon of Greed). `anyPlayerMayPay=true` (Icy Prison) offers the pay prompt to each player in APNAP order — first accept stops the sequence; only a full decline resolves the fallback. `payerIsEnchantedController=true` / factory `enchantedControllerMayPay(...)` prompts the stack `targetId` player (Mind Whip enchanted controller; Pillar Tombs of Aku each-upkeep active player) instead of the source controller — works for `PayManaCost` and `SacrificePermanentCost`. Supported costs: `SacrificePermanentCost` (single), `SacrificeMultiplePermanentsCost` (N of a filter, e.g. Rathi Dragon), and `ReturnMultiplePermanentsToHandCost` (N of a filter, e.g. Ovinomancer). Supported fallbacks: `TapPermanentsEffect(SELF|ENCHANTED)`, `LoseLifeEffect(CONTROLLER|TARGET_PLAYER)`, `DealDamageToPlayersEffect(CONTROLLER|ENCHANTED_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER, Fixed)`, `SacrificeSelfEffect`, `ReturnToHandEffect.self()`, `EnergyCountersEffect`, `DestroyReferencedPermanentEffect(SOURCE)`, `DestroySourceAndDamageControllerIfDestroyedEffect`, `DealDamageToControllerThenTapSourceIfDamageDealtEffect`, `LoseLifeEffect(CONTROLLER, Fixed)`, `OpponentMayGainControlOfCreatureYouControlEffect(duration)` (Infernal Denizen), `OpponentGainsControlOfSourceCreatureEffect(duration)` + `RemoveAllCountersEffect(CounterType)` (Rogue Skycaptain)
- `ForcedCostOrElseEffect(CostEffect, List<CardEffect>[, boolean optional[, boolean anyPlayerMayPay[, boolean payerIsEnchantedController[, boolean payerIsDefendingPlayer]]]])` — cost-like instruction; if it cannot be performed, resolve fallback effects. `optional=true` makes it a "you may [cost]. If you don't, [fallback]" choice (Yawgmoth Demon); default `false` is mandatory (Archdemon of Greed). `anyPlayerMayPay=true` (Icy Prison) offers the pay prompt to each player in APNAP order — first accept stops the sequence; only a full decline resolves the fallback. `payerIsEnchantedController=true` / factory `enchantedControllerMayPay(...)` prompts the stack `targetId` player (Mind Whip enchanted controller; Pillar Tombs of Aku each-upkeep active player) instead of the source controller — works for `PayManaCost` and `SacrificePermanentCost`. `payerIsDefendingPlayer=true` / factory `defendingPlayerMayPay(...)` prompts the defending player of the `ON_ATTACK` trigger (the entry's `attackedTargetId`: attacked player, or attacked planeswalker's controller) — Ogre Marauder, "unless defending player sacrifices a creature of their choice". Supported costs: `SacrificePermanentCost` (single), `SacrificeMultiplePermanentsCost` (N of a filter, e.g. Rathi Dragon), and `ReturnMultiplePermanentsToHandCost` (N of a filter, e.g. Ovinomancer). Supported fallbacks: `TapPermanentsEffect(SELF|ENCHANTED)`, `LoseLifeEffect(CONTROLLER|TARGET_PLAYER)`, `DealDamageToPlayersEffect(CONTROLLER|ENCHANTED_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER, Fixed)`, `SacrificeSelfEffect`, `ReturnToHandEffect.self()`, `EnergyCountersEffect`, `DestroyReferencedPermanentEffect(SOURCE)`, `DestroySourceAndDamageControllerIfDestroyedEffect`, `DealDamageToControllerThenTapSourceIfDamageDealtEffect`, `LoseLifeEffect(CONTROLLER, Fixed)`, `OpponentMayGainControlOfCreatureYouControlEffect(duration)` (Infernal Denizen), `OpponentGainsControlOfSourceCreatureEffect(duration)` + `RemoveAllCountersEffect(CounterType)` (Rogue Skycaptain), `MakeCreatureUnblockableEffect(true)` (Ogre Marauder)
- `PayPerCounterOrElseEffect(CounterType, String costPerCounter, List<CardEffect> elseEffects)` — UPKEEP_TRIGGERED generic sibling of `DestroyUnlessPaysPerCounterEffect`: may pay `costPerCounter` × counters of that type on the source; if you don't, resolve `elseEffects` (any `ForcedCostOrElseEffect`-supported fallback). Zero counters = no prompt. Rogue Skycaptain — `SequenceEffect.of(PutCountersOnSelfEffect(WAGE), PayPerCounterOrElseEffect(WAGE, "{2}", [RemoveAllCountersEffect(WAGE), OpponentGainsControlOfSourceCreatureEffect(PERMANENT)]))`
- `PayPerCounterOrElseEffect(CounterType, String costPerCounter, List<CardEffect> elseEffects, List<CardEffect> paidEffects)` — same counter-scaled payment, but resolves `paidEffects` when the payment succeeds and `elseEffects` when it does not. Uses the resolution-time may-pay path when `paidEffects` is nonempty; Cyclone uses `WIND`, `{G}`, `SacrificeSelfEffect`, and `MassDamageEffect(CountersOnSource(WIND), true)`.
- `ForcedCostOrElseEffect` also supports `PayLifeCost` and `BouncePermanentOnUpkeepEffect` as a pay-or-return fallback (Umbilicus).
- `OpponentGainsControlOfSourceCreatureEffect(ControlDuration)` — non-targeting, mandatory: an opponent gains control of the **source** permanent (`ControlStealingEffect`). No prompt and no creature pick, unlike `OpponentMayGainControlOfCreatureYouControlEffect`. ForcedCostOrElse fallback (Rogue Skycaptain)
- `TargetPermanentControllerGainsControlOfSourceEffect(ControlDuration)` — non-targeting (`ControlStealingEffect`): the controller of the ability's targeted permanent — possibly you — gains control of the **source** permanent. Reads the target owned by a sibling effect, so list it **before** the effect that removes that target (Starke of Rath: `[TargetPermanentControllerGainsControlOfSourceEffect(PERMANENT), DestroyTargetPermanentEffect(false)]`)
- `TargetPermanentControllerGainsControlOfGrantingEquipmentEffect(ControlDuration, excludedSourceSubtype)` — non-targeting: the targeted permanent's controller gains control of the Equipment that granted the ability, unless the activating creature had the excluded subtype. The Equipment id and subtype result are bound at activation. Used by Shuriken.
- `OpponentMayGainControlOfCreatureYouControlEffect(ControlDuration)` — non-targeting: an opponent may gain control of a creature you control of their choice for the given duration (`ControlStealingEffect`). May-prompt then creature pick. ForcedCostOrElse fallback (Infernal Denizen)
- `PayManaCost(String manaCost[, DynamicAmount genericReduction][, boolean forCumulativeUpkeep][, int lifeAmount])` — payable side of `ForcedCostOrElseEffect`. `PayManaCost.withGenericIncrease(manaCost, amount)` adds a resolution-time `DynamicAmount` to the generic portion instead of subtracting it ("pay {1} for each other creature you control" — Fettergeist, base `"{0}"`)
- `RemoveCounterFromControlledPermanentCost()` / `RemoveCounterFromControlledPermanentCost(CounterType...)` / `RemoveCounterFromControlledPermanentCost(int count, PermanentPredicate filter, boolean excludeSource)` — activated-ability or `ForcedCostOrElseEffect` cost: remove one or more counters from permanents you control. The counted form can restrict eligible permanents and exclude the source; unlike `RemoveCounterFromControlledCreatureCost` it is not restricted to creatures. Chisei, Heart of Oceans; Power Conduit; Ion Storm; Tekuthal, Inquiry Dominus
- `PayManaCost(String manaCost[, DynamicAmount genericReduction][, boolean forCumulativeUpkeep][, int lifeAmount])` — payable side of `ForcedCostOrElseEffect`. `PayManaCost.withGenericIncrease(manaCost, amount)` adds a resolution-time `DynamicAmount` to the generic portion instead of subtracting it ("pay {1} for each other creature you control" — Fettergeist, base `"{0}"`). `PayManaCost.forSourceManaCost()` uses the source permanent's mana cost at resolution (Essence Leak)
- `RemoveCounterFromControlledPermanentCost()` / `RemoveCounterFromControlledPermanentCost(CounterType...)` — payable side of `ForcedCostOrElseEffect`: remove one counter from any permanent you control (unrestricted with the no-argument form, or restricted to listed types). Unlike `RemoveCounterFromControlledCreatureCost` it is not restricted to creatures; controlling no matching counters makes it unpayable so the penalty resolves with no prompt
- `CumulativeUpkeepEffect(String costPerAge)` — UPKEEP_TRIGGERED: put an age counter, then may pay `costPerAge` × age counters or sacrifice (`PayManaCost` with `forCumulativeUpkeep=true` so Adarkar Unicorn mana works)
- `CumulativeUpkeepEffect.flipCoin()` — UPKEEP_TRIGGERED: put an age counter, then may flip one coin per age counter or sacrifice; each result emits the controller's coin-flip win or loss triggers (Karplusan Minotaur)
- `CumulativeUpkeepEffect.discardCard()` — UPKEEP_TRIGGERED: put an age counter, then may discard one card per age counter or sacrifice (Vexing Sphinx)
- `CumulativeUpkeepEffect.life(int lifePerAge)` — life-only cumulative upkeep, no mana component (Glacial Chasm — `life(2)`). Passes an empty `costPerAge` through to `PayManaCost`, so the prompt/log read "Pay 2 life"
- `CumulativeUpkeepEffect(String costPerAge, int lifePerAge)` — same + pay `lifePerAge` Adz? age counters life (Infernal Darkness — `("{B}", 1)`)
- `CumulativeUpkeepEffect.sacrifice(PermanentPredicate)` — same, but cost is sacrifice one matching permanent per age counter (`SacrificeMultiplePermanentsCost`; Polar Kraken — land)
- `CumulativeUpkeepEffect.gainControlOf(PermanentPredicate)` — same, but cost is gain control of one matching permanent you don't control per age counter (`GainControlOfPermanentsCost`; Herald of Leshrac)
- `CumulativeUpkeepEffect.opponentToken(CreateTokenEffect token)` — cost is `OpponentCreatesTokensCost(ageCounters, token)`: an opponent creates one `token` per age counter (Varchild's War-Riders — 1/1 red Survivor). Always payable, so the only branch is the controller accepting or declining the prompt
- `CumulativeUpkeepEffect.opponentGainsLife(int lifePerAge)` — cost is `OpponentGainsLifeCost(lifePerAge × ageCounters)`: the source controller's opponent gains that much life (Wall of Shards). The cost is unpayable when the opponent cannot gain life; otherwise the controller accepts or declines the prompt
- `CumulativeUpkeepEffect.exileTopCard(List<CardEffect> unpaidEffects)` — cost is `ExileTopCardOfLibraryCost(ageCounters)`; too few cards in the library means the cost is unpayable and the unpaid branch resolves with no prompt. `unpaidEffects` are extra effects that resolve alongside the sacrifice when the cost isn't paid, modelling a "when a player doesn't pay this permanent's cumulative upkeep, …" companion trigger (Thought Lash — `ExileControllerLibraryEffect`)
- `CumulativeUpkeepEffect.withUnpaidEffects(String costPerAge, List<CardEffect> unpaidEffects)` — mana cumulative upkeep plus a "when a player doesn't pay this permanent's cumulative upkeep, …" companion trigger (Heart of Bogardan — `DealDamageToTargetAndTheirCreaturesEffect(Sum(Scaled(CountersOnSource(AGE), 2), Fixed(-2)))`). Age-relative amounts are snapshotted to a `Fixed` while the source is still on the battlefield, since the sacrifice resolves first (CR 608.2h)
- `CumulativeUpkeepEffect.drawCard()` — cost is `DrawCardsCost(ageCounters)`: draw one card per age counter (Psychic Vortex). Always payable — drawing from an empty library is a legal payment, the loss happens at the next SBA check — so the only branch is the controller accepting or declining the prompt
- `CumulativeUpkeepEffect.putCounterOnSelf(CounterType counterType)` — cost is `PutTypedCounterOnSourceCost(counterType, ageCounters)`: put one counter of that type on the permanent itself per age counter (Aboroth — `MINUS_ONE_MINUS_ONE`). Always payable (the payment only touches the source), so the only branch is the controller accepting or declining the prompt; placement goes through `PermanentCounterSupport.placeCounterOnPermanent`, so -1/-1 replacements and "a -1/-1 counter was put on a creature" triggers apply
- `CumulativeUpkeepEffect.putCounterOnOpponentCreature(CounterType counterType)` — cost is `PutCounterOnOpponentCreatureCost(counterType, ageCounters)`: put one counter of that type on a creature an opponent controls for each age-counter payment (Sheltering Ancient). The controller chooses separately among eligible opponent creatures for each payment; with none, the cost is unpayable and the permanent is sacrificed
- `ExileControllerLibraryEffect()` — the resolving ability's controller exiles all cards from their library (Thought Lash's unpaid-cumulative-upkeep trigger). Handled by `ExileControllerLibraryEffectHandler` and as a `ForcedCostOrElse` fallback in `DestructionSupport`; both go through `LibraryExileSupport`
- `ExileControllerHandWithSourceEffect()` — the resolving ability's controller exiles every card from their hand face up and tracks them with the source permanent for later source-linked effects (Wormfang Behemoth)
- `ExileAllButBottomCardOfTargetLibraryEffect()` — target player exiles every card in their library except the bottom card (Nicol Bolas, the Arisen −12). Targets a player through its benign `TargetSpec`.
- `DestroyUnlessPaysPerCounterEffect(CounterType, String costPerCounter[, boolean sacrifice])` — UPKEEP_TRIGGERED: may pay `costPerCounter` Adz? counters of that type on the source or destroy it (Musician music counters; no counter is placed). Grant via `GrantEffectToTargetEffect` (idempotent). `sacrifice=true` sacrifices instead of destroying (Phantasmal Sphere, wrapped in `SequenceEffect.of(PutCountersOnSourceEffect(1,1,1), …)` so the counter lands before the cost is sized)
- `AddCounterThenPayCountersOrTapAndDamageEffect(CounterType)` — single upkeep trigger: "put a [counter] on this, then you may pay {X} (X = that counter type on it); if you don't, tap this and it deals X damage to you." Places the counter, snapshots X, then reuses the `ForcedCostOrElse` may-pay-or-tap-self-and-damage-controller machinery (Primordial Ooze). One effect, not two, so counter+X stay in one ability
- `SacrificeAttackingCreaturesEffect(int base, int metalcraft)` — sacrifice attackers
- `SacrificeEachMatchingPermanentEffect(PermanentPredicate)` — "each [filter] permanent is sacrificed by its controller": sweeps every battlefield and sacrifices every match on behalf of its own controller, no choice and no target (the sacrifice analog of `DestroyAllPermanentsEffect`, not of `SacrificePermanentsEffect`). Matches are collected before the first sacrifice. Tide of War: `FlipCoinWinEffect(SacrificeEachMatchingPermanentEffect(PermanentIsBlockingPredicate()), SacrificeEachMatchingPermanentEffect(PermanentIsBlockedPredicate()))`
- `EachPlayerReturnsCardsFromGraveyardToBattlefieldEffect(int, CardPredicate)` or `(int, CardPredicate, CounterType[, boolean enterTapped])` — mass reanimate; optional `CounterType` adds one counter and `enterTapped` makes returned cards enter tapped (Pyrrhic Revival; Planar Birth: `Integer.MAX_VALUE`, basic-land filter, `null`, `true`)
- `ReturnCardsFromControllerGraveyardToBattlefieldEffect(CardPredicate, int)` — return up to N of controller's own graveyard cards to the battlefield (resolution-time choice; non-targeting). Reveillark. Optional trailing `boolean manaValueEqualsX` restricts matches to mana value == the spell's paid X — with `Integer.MAX_VALUE` this is "return each creature card with mana value X" (Immortal Servitude)
- `ReturnCreaturesOfChosenTypeFromGraveyardEffect()` — choose a creature type at resolution, then return every creature card of that type from your graveyard to the battlefield; Changeling cards match every creature type. Bloodline Bidding.
- `SacrificeSelfEffect()` — sacrifice self
- `SacrificeSelfAndControllerDrawsEffect(int cards)` — the **source permanent's** controller sacrifices it, then draws N. For an ability activated by an opponent (`.withActivatableByAnyPlayer().withActivatableOnlyByOpponents()`), where the entry's controller is the wrong player. Soul Ransom
- `SacrificeSelfThenEffect(CardEffect thenEffect)` — sac source; the payload resolves **only if the sacrifice happened** ("if you do"). `targetSpec()` delegates to the payload. Wrap in `MayEffect` for "you may sacrifice it. If you do, …": Impaler Shrike `DrawCardEffect(3)`, Mindstab Thrull / Dauthi Mindripper `DiscardEffect(3, TARGET_PLAYER)`; `FlipCoinWinEffect` for Sorcerer's Strongbox; bare for Booby Trap's engine-built trigger. Multi-step payloads must be a `SequenceEffect` of synchronous steps. Do NOT compose this as `SequenceEffect.of(SacrificeSelfEffect(), payload)` — a sequence has no "if you do" gate. On a death trigger it forwards `DyingCreatureCardAwareEffect.boundToDyingCard` to its payload, so `MayEffect(SacrificeSelfThenEffect(ReturnTriggeringCreatureToBattlefieldEffect()))` gets the dying card bound (Angelic Renewal).
- `SacrificeSelfThenDestroyTargetEffect()` — sac source; if sacrificed, destroy stack entry's targetId permanent (`RemovalEffect` DESTROY; `targetSpec()` harmful CREATURE). Wrap in `MayEffect` for "you may sacrifice this. If you do, destroy target creature" (Wasp of the Bitter End): target as trigger stacks, may at resolution
- `SacrificeSelfIfEvokedEffect()` — evoke sacrifice; ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD, fires only when cast for evoke cost
- `SacrificeUnlessDiscardCardTypeEffect(CardType)` / `(CardType, boolean random)` / `(CardType, boolean random, boolean drawCardIfNotDiscarded)` / `(CardType, CardEffect thenEffect)` — sacrifice unless discard (`random=true` = discard at random, Pillaging Horde; `null` type = any card; `drawCardIfNotDiscarded=true` = "…sacrifice this creature and draw a card", Lim-Dûl's Paladin). A non-null `thenEffect` is pushed as a reflexive trigger after a successful discard, and its target filters receive the discarded card's mana value as X (Argentum Masticore)
- `DamageControllerUnlessDiscardThenTapSourceEffect(int damage)` — UPKEEP drawback: source deals N damage to its controller unless they discard a card; taps the source iff the damage landed ("if it deals damage to you this way, tap it"). Mishra's War Machine (3). Empty hand auto-applies. Not a `ForcedCostOrElseEffect` (discard isn't a supported forced cost)
- `SacrificeUnlessReturnOwnPermanentTypeToHandEffect(CardType)` — sacrifice unless bounce own
- `SacrificeUnlessReturnPermanentTypeToHandEffect(CardType)` — sacrifice unless bounce a permanent of the required type to its owner's hand; the choice may include any player's permanent
- `SacrificeUnlessSacrificeOwnPermanentEffect(PermanentPredicate, String)` — sacrifice unless sacrifice a matching permanent you control
- `ExileUnlessDiscardCardTypeEffect(CardType)` — exile the source unless its controller discards a card of the required type (Body Snatcher)
- `SacrificeSelfUnlessSacrificeCreaturesWithTotalPowerEffect(int requiredPower)` — sacrifice this permanent unless you sacrifice any number of creatures with total power N or greater (Phyrexian Dreadnought, 12); source excluded, auto-sacrifices when the total can't be reached
- `ChampionCreatureEffect(CardSubtype...)` — champion a creature (no subtype = any creature; multiple = inclusive, e.g. Goblin or Shaman); exile on ETB, return when source leaves
- `SacrificeAtEndOfCombatEffect()` / `SacrificeAtEndOfCombatEffect(int damageToController)` — sacrifice the source at end of combat (Fog Elemental). With `damageToController > 0` the source also deals that much damage to its controller at end of combat ("sacrifice it and it deals N damage to you", Time Elemental = 5); the damage is a delayed trigger and fires even if the creature already left the battlefield. Put on ON_ATTACK / ON_BLOCK
- `SacrificeTargetThenRevealUntilTypeToBattlefieldEffect(Set<CardType>)` — Polymorph
- `SacrificeTargetPermanentsThenRevealTopCardEffect()` — sacrifice the selected permanents, then each affected controller reveals their top card and puts it onto the battlefield if it is a permanent card; nonpermanents stay on top. Vaevictis Asmadi, the Dire.
- `RevealTargetPlayerLibraryUntilCreatureStealRestToGraveyardEffect()` — target player reveals from top of library until a creature card; noncreatures revealed go to that player's graveyard, the creature is put onto the caster's battlefield under their control (Telemin Performance). Targets a player; harmful. Whole-library mill if no creature
- `RevealTargetPlayerLibraryUntilPredicateOrCountToBattlefieldRestToGraveyardEffect(DynamicAmount, CardPredicate)` — target player reveals until a matching card or the required count; the matching card enters the controller's battlefield and the rest go to the target player's graveyard (Thought Dissector). Targets a player; harmful
- `RevealUntilLandsMillTargetPlayerEffect(DynamicAmount|int landCount[, MillRecipient recipient])` — target player reveals from top of library until `landCount` land cards are revealed (or the library empties); all revealed cards go to that player's graveyard (Mind Funeral = 4). Targets a player; harmful. Pair with `target(PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(OPPONENT))` for "target opponent". `MillRecipient.EACH_OPPONENT` / `TARGET_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER` reveal without a target of their own. `landCount` is a `DynamicAmount`, so `new XValue()` gives the X-spell form (Mind Grind, `EACH_OPPONENT`). With `TARGET_PLAYER` it is also a `CombatDamageTriggerContextEffect` (`DAMAGED_PLAYER`), so on `ON_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER` the damaged player is baked in as `targetId` with no `target(...)` (Mirko Vosk, Mind Drinker)
- `TargetPlayerPutsCardsFromHandOnTopOfLibraryEffect(int count)` — target player chooses `count` cards from their hand and puts them on top of their library in any order, first chosen nearest the top (Stunted Growth = 3). Capped at hand size; empty hand resolves with no prompt. Targets a player; harmful. Reuses the `PutCardsFromHandOnLibraryCardChoice` top-only interaction flow
- `TargetPlayerDiscardsUnlessPutsCardOnTopOfLibraryEffect()` — "target player discards a card unless they put a card from their hand on top of their library" (Tainted Specter). The **target** player answers a may-prompt: accept puts one chosen hand card on top of their library (`PutCardsFromHandOnLibraryCardChoice` top-only), decline discards a card. Empty hand ⇒ nothing happens. Targets a player; harmful. Records the number of cards discarded this way (0 or 1) as the entry's event value, so a following `MassDamageEffect(new EventValue(), true)` gives "if that player discards a card this way, deal 1 damage to each creature and each player"
- `PutTargetPlayerHandOnLibraryThenSearchThatManyToHandEffect()` — target player puts their whole hand on top of their library, then the controller searches that library for that many cards which go into the target player's hand, then it is shuffled (Jester's Mask). Targets a player; harmful. Mandatory search (no fail-to-find), capped at library size; an empty hand just shuffles
- `RevealUntilNonlandCardsToHandRestToBottomEffect(int)` — reveal until N nonland to hand, rest (lands) to bottom in any order (Fathom Trawl)
- `RevealUntilNonlandToHandRestToBottomDealManaValueDamageEffect()` — reveal until a nonland, deal damage equal to its mana value to the any-target, put it into hand, then put revealed lands on the bottom in any order (Explosive Revelation)
- `RevealUntilNonlandBoostTargetCreatureByManaValueEffect()` — reveal until a nonland, give the target creature +X/-X until end of turn where X is that card's mana value, then put all revealed cards on the bottom in any order (Erratic Mutation)
- `RevealUntilNonlandCardsToHandEffect()` — reveal until a nonland card, then put every revealed card into hand; if the library empties first, put all revealed cards into hand (Treasure Hunt)
- `RevealUntilLandToBattlefieldRestToBottomEffect()` — reveal until a land, put that land onto the battlefield, rest to bottom in any order (Recross the Paths; used as a `ClashEffect` pre-clash body)
- `RevealUntilLandToBattlefieldRestToGraveyardEffect()` — reveal until a land, put that land onto the battlefield, and put the rest into the graveyard (Avenging Druid)
- `RevealUntilCardPredicateRestOnBottomRandomEffect(CardPredicate, LibrarySearchDestination)` — reveal until a card matching the predicate, put it in `BATTLEFIELD`/`HAND`, and put the rest on the bottom in a random order (Jalira, Master Polymorphist = BATTLEFIELD; Evolutionary Leap = HAND)
- `RevealUntilCountMatchingCardsToBattlefieldRestOnBottomRandomEffect(DynamicAmount, CardPredicate)` — reveal until N matching cards, then let the controller choose any number to put onto the battlefield; `allMatchingOntoBattlefieldTapped(...)` instead puts every matching card onto the battlefield tapped without a choice (Aurora Awakener; Skyserpent Seeker)
- `RevealUntilCountMatchingCardsToGraveyardRestOnBottomRandomEffect(DynamicAmount, CardPredicate)` — reveal until N matching cards, put all matching revealed cards into the graveyard, and put the rest on the bottom of the library in a random order (Old Stickfingers)
- `PutTargetOnBottomThenRevealUntilTypeToBattlefieldRestToBottomEffect(Set<CardType>)` — put target creature on its owner's library bottom, then reveal from that creature's controller's library until a matching type enters that player's battlefield; put the rest on the bottom in any order (Proteus Staff)
- `RevealTopXCardsLandsToBattlefieldTappedRestOnBottomRandomEffect(Condition untapCondition)` — reveal the top X cards (paid X from the stack entry), put every land card from among them onto the battlefield tapped, and the rest on the bottom of the library in a random order; when the condition is met at resolution those lands are untapped instead (Animist's Awakening, spell mastery via `GraveyardCardThreshold(2, instant/sorcery)`). Non-targeting, no player choice
- `RevealUntilBasicLandToHandRestToGraveyardEffect()` — reveal until a basic land, put that into hand, rest to graveyard (Hermit Druid). Nonbasics do not stop the reveal; empty/no-basic mills the whole revealed pile
- `RevealUntilCardPredicateToHandRestToGraveyardEffect(CardPredicate)` — reveal until a matching card, put it into hand, and put the rest into the graveyard; if no match is found, mill the entire revealed pile (Foster)
- `RevealUntilCreatureToBattlefieldRestToGraveyardEffect()` — reveal until a creature, put it onto the controller's battlefield, rest to graveyard (Gamekeeper). If no creature is found, the entire revealed library goes to the graveyard
- `ActivePlayerRevealsUntilCreatureToBattlefieldRestToGraveyardEffect()` — the active player reveals until a creature, puts it onto their battlefield, and puts the rest into their graveyard; retains a benign player target for a separate upkeep target restriction (Oath of Druids)
- `RevealUntilColorToHandRestExiledEffect(CardColor)` — reveal until a card of that color, put it into hand, exile all other revealed cards (Sacred Guide = WHITE). No card of that color ⇒ the whole revealed library is exiled

See EFFECTS_INDEX.md "Destruction" section for 10+ additional niche destruction/sacrifice effects.

### Sacrifice costs

- `ExilePermanentCost(PermanentPredicate, String[, excludeSource[, trackExiledManaValue]])` - activation cost that exiles one matching permanent you control; the optional tracking flag snapshots its mana value into `xValue` (Food Chain)
- `ExileArtifactsWithTotalManaValueCost()` - ability-only activation cost that prompts for one or more other artifact permanents you control, exiles the chosen subset, and sets `xValue` to their total mana value
- `ExileCreatureCost()` — SPELL-slot additional cast cost: exile a creature you control. The selected creature uses `PlayCardRequest.sacrificePermanentId`, and its card snapshot is retained on the spell's stack entry for conditional follow-up effects.

- `TargetPlayerBlightsEffect(int)` - targeted ability effect: the target player chooses a creature they control and puts that many -1/-1 counters on it; reuses `BlightEffectHandler` for the choice and counter placement (Champion of the Weird)
- `EachOpponentBlightsEffect(int)` - non-targeting effect: each opponent chooses a creature they control in APNAP order and puts that many -1/-1 counters on it; uses the shared blight counter-placement flow

- `BeholdAndExileCost(CardSubtype)` — SPELL-slot additional cast cost: choose a matching permanent you control or a matching card from your hand and exile it. Selection rides on `PlayCardRequest.beholdPermanentId` or `beholdHandCardIndex`; if the permanent spell resolves, the selected card returns to its owner's hand when that permanent leaves.
- `BeholdCost(CardSubtype, int)` — flashback-only additional cast cost: behold the exact number of distinct matching permanents you control and/or cards in your hand without moving them. Selections ride on `PlayCardRequest.beholdPermanentIds` and `beholdHandCardIndices`. `BeholdCost.optionalChosenCreatureType(int)` models an optional cost that first records a chosen creature type and then validates the selected creatures/cards against it.
- `BeholdEffect(CardSubtype, CardEffect)` — resolution-time choice of a matching permanent you control or card in your hand; the chosen object remains in place and the follow-up effect resolves only after a valid choice. Sarkhan, Dragon Ascendant.
- `RevealCardFromHandCost(CardPredicate, String[, boolean trackManaValue])` — SPELL-slot additional cast cost: reveal a matching card from your hand without moving it. The selected pre-removal hand index uses `PlayCardRequest.discardHandCardIndex`; with `trackManaValue=true`, the revealed card's mana value is snapshotted into the spell's X (Induce Despair)

- `ExileSelfCost()` — exile self as cost
- `SacrificeSelfCost()` / `(boolean trackPower)` — sacrifice self as cost; `trackPower=true` snapshots effective power into xValue at payment (Mausoleum Wanderer + `CounterUnlessPaysEffect(0, true, false)`)
- `RemoveAllCountersAsCostEffect(CounterType)` — remove all counters of a type as cost; count snapshotted into xValue (Jar of Eyeballs: `EYEBALL`)
- `RemoveAllCountersFromTargetCreatureEffect()` — resolution effect: remove every counter of every kind from target creature, without imposing any further counter restriction
- `RemoveAllCountersEffect(CounterType)` — resolution effect: remove all counters of a type from self; count snapshotted as the entry's event value so a later effect reads "that much" via `EventValue` (Ashling the Pilgrim + `MassDamageEffect(new EventValue(), true)`)
- `RemoveAllCountersOfTypeFromAllPermanentsEffect(CounterType)` — remove every counter of that type from every permanent on every battlefield (Corrosion LTB: rust)
- `RemoveAllCountersFromMatchingPermanentsEffect(CounterType, PermanentPredicate)` — remove every counter of that type from every permanent matching the predicate across all battlefields (Mine Layer's mine counters from lands)
- `RemoveAllCountersFromAllPermanentsEffect()` — remove every concrete counter from every permanent on every battlefield (Aether Snap)
- `RemoveAllCountersEffect(CounterType, CounterRemovalSubject.TARGET)` — TARGET subject of the above: remove every counter of that type from the target permanent (benign `TargetPredicates.creature()`; card's target filter narrows further), no-op if none. "Remove all -1/-1 counters from it" (Hapatra's Mark: `MINUS_ONE_MINUS_ONE`, paired with a `GrantKeywordEffect(HEXPROOF, TARGET)` on the same target group)
- `SacrificeCreatureCost()` or `(boolean trackMV)` or `(boolean trackMV, boolean trackPower)` or `(boolean, boolean, boolean trackToughness)` or `(boolean, boolean, boolean, boolean excludeSelf)` or `(ManaColor trackColorSymbols)` — sacrifice creature. The `ManaColor` ctor snapshots the number of that color's mana symbols in the sacrificed creature's mana cost into xValue (Fiery Bombardment: `RED` + `DealDamageToAnyTargetEffect(new XValue())`). The cost exposes the sacrificed card to the activated ability's stack entry for conditional follow-up effects; when both power and toughness are needed independently, use `SacrificedPermanentPower` and `SacrificedPermanentToughness` dynamic amounts.
- `SacrificeMultiplePermanentsCost(int count, PermanentPredicate filter)` — sacrifice N matching permanents. Works as an activation cost (Kuldotha Forgemaster) **and** as an additional cast cost in the SPELL slot (Phyrexian Tribute: `(2, PermanentIsCreaturePredicate)` + `DestroyTargetPermanentEffect`); spell payment via `PlayCardRequest.additionalCostSacrificePermanentIds`, harness `castSorceryWithSacrifices`
- `TapPermanentsAndPutCountersEffect(PermanentPredicate filter, CounterType counterType, String prompt)` — resolution-time choice: tap any number of untapped matching permanents you control, then put one counter of `counterType` on each permanent tapped this way (Urge to Feed: creature + Vampire filter, `PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ONE`)
- `TapAnyNumberOfPermanentsCost(PermanentPredicate filter)` — SPELL-slot additional cast cost: tap any number (including zero) of untapped matching permanents you control; the count becomes the spell's X (Burn at the Stake: `PermanentIsCreaturePredicate` + `DealDamageToAnyTargetEffect(new Scaled(new XValue(), 3))`). Payment via `PlayCardRequest.additionalCostSacrificePermanentIds`, harness `castSorceryTappingPermanents`
- `ReturnAnyNumberOfPermanentsToHandCost(PermanentPredicate filter)` — SPELL-slot additional cast cost: return any number (including zero) of matching permanents you control to hand; the count becomes the spell's X (Infernal Harvest: Swamp subtype + `DealDividedDamageEffect.xAmongTargetCreatures()`). Payment via `PlayCardRequest.additionalCostSacrificePermanentIds`, harness `castSorceryReturningPermanents`
- `ReturnAnyNumberOfPermanentsToHandEffect(PermanentPredicate filter)` — resolution-time choice of any number of matching permanents controlled by the spell's controller; returns them to their owners' hands and records the actual count as the resolving entry's event value for a following `EventValue` effect (Charge Across the Araba)
- `SacrificeCreatureOrPayManaCost("{M}")` — sacrifice a creature **or** pay `{M}` as an additional cast cost (Eaten Alive: `"{3}{B}"`). Sac via `sacrificePermanentId`; null id pays the mana on top of the spell cost
- `SacrificePermanentOrPayManaCost("{M}", filter, description)` — sacrifice a matching permanent **or** pay `{M}` as an additional cast cost. Sac via `sacrificePermanentId`; null id pays the mana on top of the spell cost. Eaten Alive uses a creature filter; Annihilating Glare uses an artifact-or-creature filter
- `SacrificeAllCreaturesYouControlCost()` — sacrifice all creatures you control (SPELL; Soulblast snapshots total power into X)
- `SacrificeAllPermanentsYouControlCost()` — sacrifice all permanents you control (SPELL; Kaervek's Spite). Legal with zero
- `DiscardHandCost()` — discard entire hand as cost (activation: Slate of Ancestry; SPELL: Kaervek's Spite). Empty hand is legal
- `DiscardCardOrPayManaCost("{M}")` — discard a card **or** pay `{M}` as an additional cast cost (Lightning Axe: `"{5}"`). Discard via `discardHandCardIndex`; null index pays the mana on top of the spell cost
- `DiscardCardOrPayLifeCost(N)` — discard a card **or** pay N life as an additional cast cost (Bitter Triumph). Discard via `discardHandCardIndex`; null index pays the life
- `SacrificePermanentCost(PermanentPredicate, String[, excludeSource[, trackSacrificedPower[, trackSacrificedManaValue[, trackSacrificedToughness]]]])` — sacrifice matching permanent; use creature+subtype predicates with `excludeSource=false` for source-eligible "sacrifice a [subtype]"; "Sacrifice an artifact" is `new SacrificePermanentCost(new PermanentIsArtifactPredicate(), "an artifact", false)` (Atog, Shrapnel Blast, …); `trackSacrificedPower=true` snapshots its effective power into xValue (Freyalise Supplicant); `trackSacrificedManaValue=true` snapshots its mana value instead (Soldevi Adnate); `trackSacrificedToughness=true` snapshots its toughness (Korozda Guildmage)
- `SacrificePermanentsSequenceCost(List<PermanentPredicate> filters, List<String> descriptions)` — sacrifice one distinct permanent per per-slot filter, in order (Angel's Herald: "a green creature, a white creature, and a blue creature"). Use this single cost rather than several `SacrificePermanentCost` entries — the activation resume path carries only one cost effect through interactive picks, so multiple distinct sacrifice costs on one ability silently skip the 2nd/3rd. Dead-end-safe (only offers picks that keep the remaining slots matchable)
- `DiscardCardTypeCost(CardPredicate, String)` — discard matching card
- `DiscardCardTypeCost(CardPredicate, String, int count)` — discard N matching cards (Haunted Dead = 2)
- `DiscardCardTypeCost(..., trackManaValue=true)` — snapshot discarded card's mana value into `xValue` (Mercurial Chemister)
- `DiscardLastDrawnCardCost()` — activation-only hand-card cost that permits only the last card the controller drew this turn; uses the shared `HandCardCost` discard-choice flow (Jandor's Ring)
- `DiscardCardTypeCost(predicate, label, manaValueEqualsX, count, sameName, trackManaValue, imprintOnSource=true)` — imprint the discarded card on the source so the same ability can ask what was discarded via `ConditionalEffect(new ImprintedCardMatches(<CardPredicate>, "a Zombie card", "discarded card"), …)` (Necromancer's Stockpile's "If the discarded card was a Zombie card")
- `ExileCardFromHandCost()` / `(CardPredicate, String label)` / `(CardPredicate, String label, int count)` — "Exile a card from your hand: ...". Same activation flow as `DiscardCardTypeCost` (shared `HandCardCost` interface: pre-validated before any cost is paid, suspends on a `DiscardCostChoice` hand pick), but the chosen card is exiled instead of discarded, so no discard triggers fire (Cadaverous Bloom)
- `PutCardFromHandOnTopOfLibraryCost()` — activation-only hand-card cost; puts the chosen card on top of its controller's library instead of discarding it (Hidden Retreat)
- `ExileCardFromHandCost()` / `(CardPredicate, String label)` / `(CardPredicate, String label, int count)` / `(..., boolean imprintOnSource)` — "Exile a card from your hand: ...". Same activation flow as `DiscardCardTypeCost` (shared `HandCardCost` interface: pre-validated before any cost is paid, suspends on a `DiscardCostChoice` hand pick), but the chosen card is exiled instead of discarded, so no discard triggers fire. `imprintOnSource=true` remembers the exiled card for resolution-time comparisons (Holistic Wisdom)
- `GemstoneCavernsStartOnBattlefieldEffect()` — pregame marker for Gemstone Caverns; the non-starting player may begin with the source on the battlefield, then chooses a card from hand to exile
- `ExileTopCardOfLibraryCost(int count)` — exile the top N cards of your library as activation cost; blocks if library too small (Royal Herbalist = 1)
- `ExileTopCardOfGraveyardCost()` — exile the top (most recently added) card of your graveyard as activation cost; no choice, blocks if the graveyard is empty (Alms)
- `ExileInstantOrSorcerySpellCost()` — activation-only cost that prompts for an instant or sorcery spell you control on the stack, then exiles it; copies are removed from the stack without entering exile (Nivmagus Elemental)
- `DiscardXCardsCost()` / `(CardPredicate, String label)` — requires exactly X hand cards as a SPELL additional cast cost, either chosen by the caster or selected at random via `DiscardXCardsCost.random()`; the chosen mode also supports activated-ability hand costs paired with `ActivatedAbility.withXValue()`. X=0 discards nothing. The predicate overload restricts which cards may pay it. `new DiscardXCardsCost(true)` snapshots the discarded cards' total mana value into the spell's resolution `xValue` (Nahiri's Wrath)
- `EscalateDiscardCost()` — escalate discard-per-extra-mode (SPELL)
- `EscalateManaCost(manaCost)` — escalate mana-per-extra-mode (SPELL)
- `EscalateSacrificeCost(count, filter)` — escalate sacrifice-per-extra-mode (SPELL)
- `DiscardRandomCardCost()` — SPELL or activation cost: discard one card at random from hand (no choice); requires a non-empty hand after the spell leaves hand (Sonic Burst, Coral Helm)
- `DiscardRandomCardCost()` — discard one card at random from hand (no choice); requires a non-empty hand for an ability, or another card besides the spell for a SPELL-slot additional cost (Coral Helm, Acceptable Losses)
- `DiscardRandomCardCost()` / `(int count)` — discard one or `count` cards at random from hand as an activation cost (no choice); requires at least `count` cards
- `RemoveCounterFromSourceCost(int, CounterType)` — remove counters from self (any concrete counter type, `CHARGE` included; `ANY` spends -1/-1 before +1/+1). Reports `count` from `sourceCountersRemoved()`
- `RemoveOneOrMoreCountersFromSourceCost(CounterType)` — remove a player-chosen positive number of counters from self; the number is the ability's `xValue`
- `RemoveOneOrMoreCountersFromControlledCreaturesCost(CounterType)` — remove a player-chosen positive number of counters from among creatures you control (splittable); the number is the ability's `xValue`. Needs `.withXValueFromControlledCreatureCounters()` on the ability (Ooze Flux)
- `RemoveCounterFromSourceEffect(CounterType, int amount)` — resolved (not a cost): remove up to `amount` counters of a type from the SOURCE permanent, clamped at zero (no-op if none). Self-targeting, so trigger collectors carry the source id. The dynamic form `(CounterType, DynamicAmount, true)` targets a player for the amount while still removing counters from the source (Descendant of Masumaro). Pair with a `SpellCastTriggerEffect(new CardColorPredicate(...), ...)` for "whenever you cast a [color] spell, remove a -1/-1 counter from this" (Shrewd Hatchling)
- `PutCounterOnSourceIfSacrificedCardHasSubtypeEffect(CardSubtype, CounterType, int)` — if the card sacrificed for the activated ability has the specified subtype, put counters on the source permanent. The sacrificed card is snapshotted during cost payment, so this remains available after the creature leaves the battlefield.
- `RemoveCounterOrSacrificeSelfEffect(CounterType[, List<CardEffect> thenEffects])` — `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`: remove one counter of the given type from the source permanent, or sacrifice the source when none remain. When `thenEffects` are supplied, they resolve against the permanent the source was attached to after the sacrifice (Cocoon)
- `RemoveCounterAndSacrificeSelfOnLastEffect(CounterType)` — `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`: remove one counter of the given type from the source permanent; if that was the last, put a separate sacrifice trigger on the stack. No-ops when the source has no such counters, matching vanishing (Calciderm)
- `RemoveCounterAndGainLifeEffect(CounterType, int lifeGain, CounterRemovalSubject.TARGET)` — remove one counter of a type from target creature; gain `lifeGain` life only if a counter was removed ("If you do") (Woeleecher: `MINUS_ONE_MINUS_ONE`, 2; Chainbreaker: `MINUS_ONE_MINUS_ONE`, 0 — removal with no life)
- `RemoveAndPutCounterOnAttackEffect(CounterType)` / `()` (=`MINUS_ONE_MINUS_ONE`) — **ON_ATTACK only, two targets**: remove one counter of the type from the first target (a creature you control; no-op if none) and put one of the same type on the second (up to one creature the defending player controls; fires the put triggers + respects can't-have). Independent halves (puts even with nothing to remove). Implements the marker `AttackCounterMoveEffect`, so `CombatAttackService` routes it through the bespoke two-step `AttackCounterMoveFirstTarget`/`AttackCounterMoveSecondTarget` pipeline instead of the single-target attack pipeline (which can't collect two targets). Reads `entry.getTargetIds()[0]`/`[1]`. Decimator Beetle
- `RemoveCounterAndGainLifeEffect(CounterType, int lifeGain, CounterRemovalSubject.SOURCE)` — SOURCE subject of the above: remove one counter from this permanent; gain `lifeGain` life only if a counter was removed ("If you do"). Non-targeting; wrap in `MayEffect` for the "you may" form (Living Artifact: `VITALITY`, 1, on `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`; Exemplar of Strength: `MINUS_ONE_MINUS_ONE`, 1, on `ON_ATTACK`)
- `PutOrRemoveCounterIfBlockedSinceLastUpkeepEffect(CounterType)` — UPKEEP_TRIGGERED: put one counter of that type on the source if it blocked or was blocked since the controller's last upkeep, otherwise remove one (clamped at zero). The window is `Permanent.blockedOrWasBlockedSinceLastUpkeep` (set at declare-blockers for both creatures, survives turn resets) and is consumed when the trigger resolves (Wiitigo: `PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ONE`)
- `PutOrRemoveCounterIfAttackedOrBlockedSinceLastUpkeepEffect(PermanentReference, CounterType)` — UPKEEP_TRIGGERED: put one counter on the referenced permanent if it attacked, blocked, or was blocked since the relevant last upkeep, otherwise remove one (clamped at zero). Uses `Permanent.attackedOrBlockedSinceLastUpkeep`; `ATTACHED` targets the enchanted creature (Shape of the Wiitigo), while the one-argument constructor uses `SOURCE`.
- `BountyOfTheLuxaEffect()` — one atomic `PRECOMBAT_MAIN_TRIGGERED` toggle: remove all `FLOOD` counters from the source; if none were removed, put a flood counter on it and draw a card, otherwise add `{C}{G}{U}`. Because a flood counter is only ever added one at a time the card alternates draw/ramp each turn. Card-specific (hardcodes flood/draw-1/`{C}{G}{U}`); kept as one record because trigger collectors push one entry per slot effect so the counter mutation and its two branches can't be split. NOT a `ManaProducingEffect` — a beginning-of-step trigger uses the stack (CR 605.1b), not a mana ability (Bounty of the Luxa)
- `RemoveCounterFromTargetPermanentEffect()` — remove one counter of any kind currently on target permanent (first present type when several); no-op if none. "Remove a counter from target permanent" (Medicine Runner). Overload `(CounterType, PermanentPredicate)` removes one counter of exactly that type (no-op if the target has none of it) and narrows legal targets to permanents matching the predicate — "remove a -1/-1 counter from another target creature you control" (Defiant Greatmaw: `MINUS_ONE_MINUS_ONE`, `AllOf(IsCreature, ControlledBySourceController, Not(IsSourceCard))`). Full form `(CounterType, PermanentPredicate, int amount)` removes **up to** `amount` — "remove up to four charge counters from target noncreature artifact" (Gremlin Mine: `(CHARGE, null, 4)`)
- `CrewCost(int)` — crew; `SaddleCost(int)` — Saddle; both tap any number of other untapped creatures you control with total effective power at least the threshold and use the shared power-based cost handler
- `TapCreatureCost(PermanentPredicate)` — tap creature; the full form `(PermanentPredicate, boolean excludeSelf, boolean trackTappedCreaturePower, boolean trackTappedCreatureForSourceAbility)` can also remember tapped permanents for a source's death trigger (Vodalian War Machine)
- `RemoveChosenCountersFromTargetPermanentEffect(int amount)` — target a permanent, then choose up to `amount` individual counters to remove as the effect resolves; the choices may mix counter kinds. Uses a sequential list choice with a `Done` option. Glissa Sunslayer
- `CrewCost(int)` — crew
- `TapCreatureCost(PermanentPredicate)` — tap creature
- `TapGrantingEquipmentCost()` — tap the Equipment that granted the activated ability; pair with a normal tap cost when the equipped creature and the Equipment both appear in the cost (Fishing Pole)
- `PayLifeCost(int)` — pay life; `PayLifeCost.halfLife()` pays half your life rounded up; `PayLifeCost.perSourceCounter(int, CounterType)` pays that much life **for each** counter of the type on the source permanent, sized at activation time so an activation with no such counters costs nothing (Tornado — `(3, VELOCITY)`). Works both as an activation cost and as a SPELL-slot additional cast cost ("As an additional cost to cast this spell, pay 3 life" — Fumarole); the cast is rejected when the life total is below the amount (CR 119.4)
- `PayEnergyCost(int)` — pay that many energy counters as an activated-ability cost or forced pay-or-fallback cost; activation is rejected when the controller has fewer energy counters, while forced costs resolve their fallback when payment is unavailable. Used for "Pay {E}{E}" abilities and Lathnu Hellion
- `RepeatableAdditionalManaCost(List<String> manaCosts)` — SPELL-slot "you may pay {1}{R} and/or {1}{G} any number of times" (Primitive Justice). One announced entry per repetition; paid as a mana suffix on the spell's total cost. Use `RepeatableAdditionalManaCost.singlePayment(...)` for independent optional costs that may each be paid at most once. When the payments buy targets, announced X must be `1 + repetitions`; pair with `targetX(filter, cap)`. Without an X-scaled target group (Taste of Paradise) X is unconstrained. Read the per-option payment count back with the `RepeatedAdditionalCostCount("{1}{G}")` amount or `RepeatedAdditionalCostPaid("{2}{U}")` condition
- `RepeatableAdditionalManaCost(List<String> manaCosts, boolean multikicker)` — SPELL-slot "you may pay {1}{R} and/or {1}{G} any number of times" (Primitive Justice). One announced entry per repetition; paid as a mana suffix on the spell's total cost. Use `RepeatableAdditionalManaCost.multikicker(...)` for multikicker cards so kicked-spell triggers count each payment. When the payments buy targets, announced X must be `1 + repetitions`; pair with `targetX(filter, cap)`. Without an X-scaled target group (Taste of Paradise) X is unconstrained. Read the per-option payment count back with the `RepeatedAdditionalCostCount("{1}{G}")` amount
- `ChooseXValueCost(int minValue, int maxValue)` — SPELL-slot additional cast cost that records a caster-chosen X in an inclusive range without consuming a resource; use `XValue` amounts for the spell's effects (Liquid Fire, `0`–`5`)
- `PayXLifeCost()` — additional cost: pay X life, where X is the announced X. SPELL-slot casts use the spell's X (Fire Covenant); activated abilities use the ability's `withXValue()` X (Krumar Initiate)
- `ExileCardFromGraveyardCost(CardType)` / `(CardSubtype)` / `(CardType, CardType alternateType)` + overloads — exile graveyard card (subtype ctor for "Exile an Elf card", Scarred Vinebreeder; two-type ctor for "instant or sorcery card", Disciple of the Ring)
- `ReturnCreatureToHandCost()` — additional spell cost: return a creature you control to hand (Familiar's Ruse)
- `ReturnPermanentToHandCost()` or `(PermanentPredicate filter)` — additional spell cost: return exactly one matching permanent you control to its owner's hand; the no-arg form matches any permanent. The permanent uses `PlayCardRequest.sacrificePermanentId` and is paid before mana (Disappearing Act; Deprive uses `PermanentIsLandPredicate`)
- `BlightCost()` — SPELL-slot additional cast cost: blight the selected creature you control for the announced X, putting X -1/-1 counters on it; selection uses `sacrificePermanentId` and payment is handled by `SpellCastingService`
- `PutCounterOnControlledCreatureCost(CounterType, int count)` — additional spell cost: put counter(s) on a creature you control (Scarscale Ritual: `MINUS_ONE_MINUS_ONE, 1`); creature supplied via `sacrificePermanentId`, paid in `SpellCastingService`
- `PutCountersOnControlledCreatureOrPayManaCost(CounterType, int count, String manaCost)` — additional spell cost: put counters on a creature you control **or** pay the listed mana (Wild Unraveling); the creature uses `sacrificePermanentId`, while a null id pays the mana on top of the spell's cost
- `BlightEffect(int count, CardEffect thenEffect[, boolean thenEffectTargets])` — resolution-time blight action: put `count` -1/-1 counters on a creature you control, then put `thenEffect` on the stack if the action was performed; set `thenEffectTargets` for a targeted reflexive follow-up that chooses its target after blighting (Warren Torchmaster); wrap in `MayEffect` for "you may blight N" (Blighted Blackthorn)

See EFFECTS_INDEX.md "Sacrifice costs" for additional cost effects.
- `DestroyDamagedCreatureEffect()` / `(boolean combatDamageOnly, boolean cannotBeRegenerated)` — `ON_ALLY_CREATURE_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_CREATURE` marker, self-scoped: fires only when the permanent holding it is the damage source. The flags optionally restrict it to combat damage and prevent regeneration. Expanded at trigger-collection time into a `DestroyTargetPermanentEffect` entry aimed at the damaged creature, so it never resolves directly. Granted to itself by Cruel Deceiver; Grotesque Hybrid uses `(true, true)`
- `ExileDamagedCreatureEffect()` / `(true)` — `ON_ALLY_CREATURE_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_CREATURE` marker, self-scoped by default or Equipment-scoped with `true`; exiles the damaged creature through a non-targeting `ExileTargetPermanentEffect` entry. Pit Spawn and Sword of Kaldra
- `TapAndSkipUntapDamagedCreatureEffect()` — `ON_ALLY_CREATURE_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_CREATURE` marker, self-scoped: whenever this creature deals damage to a creature, the damaged creature is tapped and skips its controller's next untap step. Expanded at trigger-collection time into `TapPermanentsEffect(TapUntapScope.TARGET)` + `SkipNextUntapEffect(TapUntapScope.TARGET)` on one non-targeting entry, so it never resolves directly. Matsu-Tribe Sniper
- `EquipmentDamagesOtherDefendingCreaturesEffect()` — `ON_ALLY_CREATURE_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_CREATURE` marker put on an **Equipment**: whenever equipped creature deals damage to a blocking creature, this Equipment deals that much damage to each other creature defending player controls. Expanded at trigger-collection time into a `DealDamageToEachMatchingPermanentEffect(…, TARGET_PLAYER)` entry that excludes the damaged blocker, so it never resolves directly. Kusari-Gama
- `EquipmentTapsAndLocksDamagedCreatureEffect()` — `ON_ALLY_CREATURE_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_CREATURE` marker put on an **Equipment**: whenever equipped creature deals damage to a creature, tap that creature and keep it from untapping while this Equipment remains on the battlefield. Expanded at trigger-collection time into `TapPermanentsEffect(TapUntapScope.TARGET)` + `DoesntUntapEffect.targetWhileSourceOnBattlefield()` sourced from the Equipment, so it never resolves directly. Neko-Te
- `DestroyDamagedCreatureAtEndOfCombatEffect(PermanentPredicate sourceFilter)` — `ON_ALLY_CREATURE_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_CREATURE` marker, **combat damage only**: whenever a creature you control matching `sourceFilter` deals combat damage to a creature, destroy that creature at end of combat. Unlike `DestroyDamagedCreatureEffect` the source may be any matching creature you control (the watcher included). Expanded at trigger-collection time into a `DestroyTargetPermanentAtEndOfCombatEffect` entry aimed at the damaged creature. Sosuke, Son of Seshiro (`PermanentHasSubtypePredicate(WARRIOR)`)
- `DestroyDamagedCreatureAtEndOfCombatEffect(PermanentPredicate sourceFilter)` — `ON_ALLY_CREATURE_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_CREATURE` marker, **combat damage only** by default: whenever a creature you control matching `sourceFilter` deals combat damage to a creature, destroy that creature at end of combat. `thisCreature()` is the self-scoped, combat-and-noncombat form for older "whenever this creature deals damage" wording. Unlike `DestroyDamagedCreatureEffect` the filtered form may watch any matching creature you control (the watcher included). Expanded at trigger-collection time into a `DestroyTargetPermanentAtEndOfCombatEffect` entry aimed at the damaged creature. Sosuke, Son of Seshiro (filtered combat form); Lowland Basilisk (`thisCreature()`)
- `SacrificeAnyNumberOfPermanentsCost(PermanentPredicate filter[, boolean trackSacrificedPower])` — `CostEffect`; SPELL-slot additional cast cost "you may sacrifice any number of permanents you control matching `filter`". Zero is a legal payment, so it never blocks castability; by default the count sacrificed becomes the spell's X, while `trackSacrificedPower=true` makes the pre-payment total effective power become X. Rides on `PlayCardRequest.additionalCostSacrificePermanentIds`. Devouring Greed uses count X; Corpse Cobble uses total-power X. Contrast `SacrificeMultiplePermanentsCost` (exact count) and `SacrificeXPermanentsCost(PermanentPredicate filter[, boolean requireAtLeastOne])` (activated abilities only; announced X, optionally requiring X ≥ 1)

## Counter spells

- `TargetSpellControllerLooksAtHandEffect()` — target spell controller's hand is shown privately to the counterspell's controller; place before the counter effect while the target spell is still on the stack (Lay Bare)

- `CounterSpellIfManaValueEqualsSourceCountersEffect(CounterType)` — ON_ANY_PLAYER_CASTS_SPELL: counter the triggering spell when its mana value equals the source permanent's counter count. The collector checks the equality and snapshots the triggering spell's mana value when the ability triggers (Chalice of the Void)
- `RevealTopCardAndCounterTriggeringSpellIfManaValueMatchesEffect()` — ON_OPPONENT_CASTS_SPELL inside a `MayEffect`: reveal the controller's top library card and counter the triggering spell if their mana values match; the revealed card stays on top (Counterbalance)

- `CounterSpellEffect()` — counter target spell (countered card → graveyard)
- `CounterUnlessExilesGraveyardEffect()` — counter target spell unless its controller exiles all cards from their graveyard; the spell controller may choose this even when their graveyard is empty (Grip of Amnesia)
- `MagesContestEffect()` — resolution-time life-bid auction between the caster and the targeted spell's controller; the high bidder loses the bid and the caster counters the spell only when they win (Mages' Contest)
- `CounterAbilityAndLockSourceEffect(EffectDuration)` — counter the targeted ability and lock its source permanent's activated abilities for the duration (Interdict)
- `CounterAbilityAndRemoveSourceAbilitiesEffect()` — counter the targeted activated or triggered ability; if its source is currently an artifact, creature, or planeswalker, it loses all abilities for as long as the effect's source permanent remains on the battlefield (Tishana's Tidebinder)
- `CounterSpellOrAbilityAndDestroySourceEffect()` — counter the targeted spell or ability; if it is a permanent's ability, destroy that ability's source permanent (Teferi's Response)
- `CounterSpellEffect(CounteredSpellDestination.EXILE)` — counter + exile instead of graveyard (Dissipate, Faerie Trickery)
- `CounterSpellEffect(CounteredSpellDestination.HAND)` — counter + return to owner's hand instead of graveyard (Remand)
- `CounterSpellAndExileThenGrantFreeCastPermissionEffect()` — counter target spell, exile it instead of
  the graveyard, and give this ability's controller a non-expiring free-cast permission while the
  card remains exiled (Kheru Spellsnatcher)
- `CounterSpellEffect(CounteredSpellDestination.LIBRARY_TOP)` — counter + put on top of owner's library instead of graveyard (Memory Lapse)
- `CounterEachTargetSpellEffect()` — "counter up to N target spells" (Double Negative). Bind to one `target(filter, 0, N)` spell-on-stack group; counters every spell chosen for the group. `null` filter = any spell. Non-modal multi-spell-target path (see `SpellCastingService.multipleSpellTargets`)
- `CounterSpellAndDiscardSplicedNamesEffect()` — counter target spell, then its controller reveals their hand and discards each card with the same name as a card spliced onto that spell (Minamo's Meddling). Spliced names are recorded at cast time in `GameData.spellCastSplicedNames`; with nothing spliced it is a plain counterspell and no hand is revealed
- `CounterSpellAndCreateTreasureTokensEffect()` — counter + treasures
- `CounterSpellAndExileAllWithSameNameEffect()` — counter + exile all same-name cards from controller's graveyard/hand/library, then shuffle (Counterbore)
- `CounterSpellIfNameFoundElsewhereEffect()` — ON_ANY_PLAYER_CASTS_SPELL: counter the triggering spell if a card with the same name is in a graveyard or a nontoken permanent with the same name is on the battlefield (Bazaar of Wonders)
- `CounterSpellIfControllerPoisonedEffect()` / `(int minimumPoisonCounters)` — counter if the target spell's controller has at least the configured poison-counter threshold
- `TargetSpellControllerCantCastSpellsThisTurnEffect()` — the targeted spell's controller can't cast spells this turn (Render Silent); list it before `CounterSpellEffect` in the SPELL slot
- `TargetSpellControllerLosesLifeEffect(int)` — target spell controller loses life (a `TriggeringSpellReferencingEffect`: also usable in `ON_BECOMES_TARGET_OF_SPELL`, where it hits the triggering spell's controller — Forsaken Wastes)
- `TriggeringObjectControllerGainsControlOfEnchantedPermanentEffect(ControlDuration)` — the controller of the spell or ability that caused an Aura's becomes-target trigger gains control of that Aura's enchanted permanent for the given duration; the triggering object is carried on the stack and the host is re-derived from the Aura (Fractured Loyalty)
- `TargetSpellControllerDiscardsEffect(int)` — target spell controller discards
- `TargetSpellControllerDrawsCardEffect()` — target spell controller draws a card; place before the counter (Dream Fracture; Vex wraps it in a may choice)
- `TargetSpellControllerCreatesTokensEffect(CreateTokenEffect)` — target spell controller creates the supplied tokens; place before the counter so the target remains on the stack (An Offer You Can't Refuse)
- `PutCardToBattlefieldForTargetSpellControllerEffect(CardPredicate, String)` — the controller of the targeted spell may put a matching card from their hand onto the battlefield; wrap in `MayEffect` with `MayChoicePlayer.TARGET_SPELL_CONTROLLER` and place before `CounterSpellEffect`, gated by `TargetSpellCanBeCountered` when the wording says "countered this way" (Fold into Aether)
- `TargetSpellControllerGainsLifeEqualToManaValueEffect()` — target spell controller gains life equal to that spell's mana value (including X); place before the counter (Illumination)
- `CounterUnlessPaysEffect(int)` or `(int amount, int lifeCost)` or `(int, boolean useX, boolean exileIfCountered)` or `(DynamicAmount)` or `(String manaCost)` — counter unless pays (`DynamicAmount` scales the cost, e.g. `PermanentCount(PermanentColorInPredicate(BLUE), CONTROLLER)` = "{1} for each blue permanent you control", Spell Syphon). `(amount, lifeCost)` is mana **and** life (Mundungu); `(String manaCost)` preserves exact colored costs such as `"{W}"` (Ayesha Tanaka)
- `CounterUnlessTakesDamageEffect(int)` — counter unless the target spell's controller accepts that damage from the countering spell; the target spell remains on the stack while the damage is dealt (Molten Influence)
- `CounterUnlessDiscardsEffect()` — counter unless controller discards a card (Ward—Discard a card)
- `CounterUnlessSacrificesEffect()` or `(PermanentPredicate filter[, String sacrificeDescription])` — counter unless controller sacrifices a matching permanent; the no-argument form is Ward—Sacrifice a permanent, while filtered variants can require a land (Mana Vortex)
- `AnyPlayerMaySacrificeCreatureToCounterSpellEffect()` — self-cast trigger for “any player may sacrifice a creature; if a player does, counter this spell”; offers players in APNAP order, counters immediately after the first sacrifice, and still offers remaining players their choices (Brain Gorgers)
- `CounterSpellsNamedLikeCardsExiledWithSourceEffect()` — non-targeting: counter all stack spells named like a card exiled with the source (Grimoire Thief; pair with `SacrificeSelfCost`)
- `ExileOtherSpellsAndCounterAbilitiesEffect()` — non-targeting: exile all other spells on the stack and counter all activated/triggered abilities (Summary Dismissal). Uncounterable spells are still exiled; Guile does not apply
- `CounterSpellAndGainControlIfArtifactOrCreatureEffect()` — counter target spell; if it was an artifact or creature spell, put that card onto the battlefield under your control instead of the graveyard (Desertion)
- `CounterlashEffect()` — counter target spell, then may cast from hand sharing a card type without paying mana cost
- `RegisterDelayedManaEqualToTargetSpellManaValueEffect(ManaColor)` / `(ManaColor, boolean optional, boolean anyColorCombination, boolean firstMainOnly)` — Scattering Stroke clash reward: wrap in `ClashEffect` before the counter; may add {C} equal to the countered spell's mana value at your next main phase. The 4-arg form is Plasm Capture (`COLORLESS, false, true, true`): mandatory, "X mana in any combination of colors", first (precombat) main phase only
- `RegisterDelayedManaAtNextMainPhaseEffect(ManaColor, int)` — schedule mandatory fixed mana at the beginning of your next main phase this turn (Conduit of Storms / Emrakul ON_ATTACK)
- `MayCastFromHandWithoutPayingManaCostEffect()` / `(boolean revealCardOnDecline)` — marker for may-cast-from-hand routing in PendingMayAbility; use `false` when declining must not publicly identify a hidden chosen card
- `MayCastForMiracleCostEffect()` / `MiracleMayCastEffect()` / `MiracleRevealEffect()` — Miracle cast path (Temporal Mastery)
- `MayCastForMadnessCostEffect()` / `MadnessMayCastEffect()` — Madness cast path (Asylum Visitor); discard exiles then offers cast-from-exile for madness cost
- `MayCastFromHandSharingNameWithSpellCastThisTurnEffect()` — Twinning Glass activated ability: offer to cast a nonland hand card for free if its name matches a spell any player cast this turn (reuses the Counterlash routing)
- `MayCastAnySpellFromHandWithoutPayingManaCostEffect(CardPredicate spellFilter)` — offer to cast one matching nonland hand card for free (reuses Counterlash routing). `()` / null filter = any nonland (Maelstrom Archangel, ON_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER); instant/sorcery filter = Wildfire Eternal (ON_ATTACKS_UNBLOCKED)
- `MasterOfPredicamentsEffect()` — combat-damage trigger with a hidden hand-card selection followed by the damaged player's mana-value guess; wrong nonland guesses reuse the free-cast may path
- `MayCastFromTargetPlayerHandWithoutPayingManaCostEffect(CardPredicate spellFilter)` — same, but eligible cards come from the *targeted* player's hand; the controller casts (Mindclaw Shaman; pair with `RevealTargetHandEffect`). `damagedPlayer()` uses the non-targeting combat-damaged player's hand (Mindleech Mass)
- `ReplaceControlledCounterWithExileAndPlayEffect()` — STATIC (Guile): your counters exile the spell instead and you may play it free
- `MayPlayExiledCardWithoutPayingManaCostEffect()` — marker for free-play-from-exile routing in PendingMayAbility (Guile's counter replacement, Spell Queller's leaves trigger)
- `CantBeCounteredEffect()` — can't be countered (static); `CantBeCounteredEffect(new SpellXAtLeast(5))` — conditional on stack-entry X (Banefire)
- `SpellsAndAbilitiesCantBeCounteredEffect()` — spells and abilities can't be countered while its source permanent is on the battlefield (static marker)
- `GrantControllerSpellsCantBeCounteredByColorsEffect(Set<CardColor>)` — controller's spells can't be countered by spells of the given colors until end of turn
