- `GrantControllerSpellsCantBeCounteredThisTurnEffect()` — controller's spells can't be countered until end of turn
- `GrantControllerCreatureSpellsCantBeCounteredThisTurnEffect()` — controller's creature spells can't be countered until end of turn
- `MakeTargetSpellUncounterableEffect()` — target spell can't be countered (activated/spell; targets a spell on the stack, Vexing Shusher)
- `CreatureSpellsCantBeCounteredEffect()` — creatures can't be countered (static)
- `SpellsCantBeCounteredEffect()` — spells can't be countered (static, global)
- `ControllerSpellsCantBeCounteredEffect([boolean noncreatureOnly])` — spells you control can't be countered (static); `true` restricts the protection to noncreature spells (Benevolent Geist)
- `ControllerCreatureSpellsCantBeCounteredEffect(int minimumPower)` — creature spells you control with power dz?A `minimumPower` can't be countered (static, Spellbreaker Behemoth)
- `CreatureEnteringDontCauseTriggersEffect()` — Torpor Orb (static)
- `CreatureDyingDontCauseTriggersEffect()` — creatures dying don't cause triggered abilities, including death and battlefield-leave triggers caused by that death (static; Hushbringer)
- `OpponentPermanentsEnteringDontCauseTriggersEffect()` — permanents entering don't cause triggered abilities of permanents controlled by this effect's controller's opponents to trigger (static; Elesh Norn, Mother of Machines)
- `ETBDoubleTriggerEffect(CardPredicate[, boolean requiresEnteringControllerMatch])` — add one ETB trigger for each matching permanent (static); the default controller restriction models Naban, while `false` models Yarok
- `AdditionalCreatureDeathTriggerEffect(PermanentPredicate)` — if a creature dying causes a triggered ability of a matching permanent you control to trigger, that ability triggers an additional time (static; Drivnod, Carnage Dominus)
- `CreaturesEnterAsCopyOfSourceEffect()` — Essence of the Wild (static); `(true, true)` = nontoken creatures enter as a copy of the enchanted creature (Infinite Reflection)
- `OtherNontokenCreaturesBecomeCopyOfEnchantedCreatureEffect()` — Infinite Reflection ETB (ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD, no target)
- `ExileOpponentCardsInsteadOfGraveyardEffect()` — Leyline of the Void (static)
- `ExileCreaturesDamagedBySourceInsteadOfDyingEffect()` — Frostwielder / Kumano's Blessing (static), or a targeted creature's temporary ability (Runesword); creatures this permanent (or Aura-enchanted creature) damaged this turn are exiled instead of dying
- `ExileOpponentCreaturesInsteadOfDyingEffect()` — Liesa, Forgotten Archangel (static); opponents' creatures are exiled instead of dying
- `ExileCreaturesInsteadOfDyingThisTurnEffect()` — creatures that would die this turn are exiled instead; `(true)` restricts the replacement to creatures controlled by opponents (Cry of the Carnarium, Malicious Eclipse)
- `ExileOpponentCreaturesInsteadOfDyingEffect()` — Liesa, Forgotten Archangel (static); opponents' creatures are exiled instead of dying. `withSourceTracking()` also tracks each exiled card with the replacement's source permanent for Gisa, Glorious Resurrector
- `ExileOpponentCreatureCardsInsteadOfGraveyardEffect()` — Anafenza, the Foremost (static); opponent-owned nontoken creatures and creature cards outside the battlefield are exiled instead of entering that opponent's graveyard
- `ExileInstantSorceryCardsInsteadOfGraveyardEffect()` — instant and sorcery cards are exiled instead of entering any graveyard; token objects are unaffected (static, Dryad Militant)
- `RevealAndPutOnBottomOfLibraryInsteadOfGraveyardEffect()` — Wheel of Sun and Moon (static, player aura; cards to enchanted player's graveyard go to bottom of their library instead; pair with `setEnchantPlayer(true)`)
- `ExileOwnCardsInsteadOfGraveyardEffect()` — controller's own cards are exiled instead of going to their graveyard (static, Forbidden Crypt)
- `ExileOwnCyclingCardsUnlessCycledEffect()` — cycling cards are exiled instead of going to your graveyard unless they were just cycled (static, Abandoned Sarcophagus; capability `OwnGraveyardExileReplacement`)
- `CastSpellsWithCyclingFromGraveyardEffect()` — cast spells with cycling from your graveyard (static, Abandoned Sarcophagus; capability `CastSpellsFromGraveyardPermission`)
- `CastSpellsFromGraveyardEffect(CardPredicate filter)` — cast matching spells from your graveyard for their normal mana costs (static, Yawgmoth's Agenda; capability `CastSpellsFromGraveyardPermission`)
- `CastSpellFromGraveyardOncePerYourTurnEffect(CardPredicate filter)` — "once during each of your turns, you may cast a [filter] spell from your graveyard" (static, Gisa and Geralf: `CardAllOfPredicate(CardTypePredicate(CREATURE), CardSubtypePredicate(ZOMBIE))`). Same `CastSpellsFromGraveyardPermission` capability with `oncePerControllerTurn() == true`: only usable on its controller's own turn, tracked per granting permanent in `GameData.oncePerTurnGraveyardCastPermissionsUsedThisTurn`
- `ReturnFromGraveyardInsteadOfDrawEffect()` — if you would draw, return a card from your graveyard to hand instead; lose if you can't (static, Forbidden Crypt)
- `FastingEffect()` — if you would begin your draw step, you may skip the entire step instead; accepting gains 2 life and suppresses draw-step triggers, while declining draws normally (MAY_SKIP_DRAW_STEP_DRAW)
- `PlayersCanDrawOnlyOneCardEachTurnEffect()` — static draw restriction: each player may actually draw only one card per turn; checked from `GameData.cardsDrawnThisTurn` before draw replacements, so a replacement that prevents an actual draw does not consume the limit (Spirit of the Labyrinth)
- `OpponentsCanDrawOnlyOneCardEachTurnEffect()` — static draw restriction: opponents of the source controller may actually draw only one card per turn; uses `DrawRestrictionEffect` source-controller scoping (Narset, Parter of Veils)
- `ObstinateFamiliarDrawReplacementEffect()` — if you would draw, you may skip that draw instead (static, controller-scoped; detected through `MaySkipDrawReplacementEffect`, with one `ReplaceSingleDrawEffect` choice per draw)
- `EnduringRenewalDrawReplacementEffect()` — if you would draw, reveal top; creature → GY, else draw (static, capability `RevealTopCreatureToGraveyardElseDrawReplacementEffect`)
- `DoubleDrawExceptFirstDrawStepDrawEffect()` — if you would draw, draw two instead, except your first draw each of your draw steps (static, Alhammarret's Archive)
- `PlayWithOwnHandRevealedEffect()` — play with your hand revealed (controller only; capability `PubliclyRevealedHandEffect`)
- `MakeDefendingPlayerPlayWithHandRevealedEffect()` — the trigger's defending player (`targetId`) plays with their hand revealed for as long as the source (`sourcePermanentId`) stays on the battlefield; recorded in `GameData.handsRevealedWhileSourceOnBattlefield`. `ON_ATTACKS_UNBLOCKED`; pair as `MayEffect(SequenceEffect.of(it, AssignNoCombatDamageEffect()))` (Stromgald Spy)
- `ReturnTriggeringCreatureToBattlefieldEffect()` — ON_ALLY_CREATURE_DIES return dying creature to the battlefield under the controller's control (Angelic Renewal, as a `SacrificeSelfThenEffect` payload)
- `TomorrowAzamisFamiliarDrawReplacementEffect()` — if you would draw, look at top 3 and put one into hand, rest on bottom in any order (static, capability `LookAtTopCardsChooseOneToHandDrawReplacementEffect`)
- `CounterThresholdDrawReplacementEffect` — capability for a controller-scoped optional draw replacement that is active while its source has at least a specified number of a specified counter; `ArchmageAscensionDrawReplacementEffect()` searches the library for a card to hand instead of drawing once the source has six quest counters
- `PlayWithOwnHandRevealedEffect()` — play with your hand revealed (controller only; capability `PubliclyRevealedHandEffect`)
- `MakeDefendingPlayerPlayWithHandRevealedEffect()` — the trigger's defending player (`targetId`) plays with their hand revealed for as long as the source (`sourcePermanentId`) stays on the battlefield; recorded in `GameData.handsRevealedWhileSourceOnBattlefield`. `ON_ATTACKS_UNBLOCKED`; pair as `MayEffect(SequenceEffect.of(it, AssignNoCombatDamageEffect()))` (Stromgald Spy)
- `ReturnTriggeringCardToOwnerHandEffect()` — ON_CREATURE_PUT_INTO_CONTROLLER_GRAVEYARD_FROM_BATTLEFIELD / ON_ANY_PERMANENT_PUT_INTO_GRAVEYARD_FROM_BATTLEFIELD: return the dead permanent's card to its owner's hand; the first slot uses last-known creature status and graveyard ownership (Enduring Renewal)
- `ReturnDyingCreatureToOwnerHandUnlessTargetPaysLifeEffect(int lifeCost)` — ON_CREATURE_PUT_INTO_CONTROLLER_GRAVEYARD_FROM_BATTLEFIELD: target opponent may pay `lifeCost` life; if they decline or cannot pay, return the owned dying creature to its owner's hand. The death collector binds the dying card id and the target is chosen as the trigger is put on the stack (Athreos, God of Passage)
- `PutTriggeringCardFromGraveyardOnTopOfLibraryEffect()` — ON_ALLY_CREATURE_DIES: put the dying creature's card from the controller's graveyard on top of that player's library (Mortuary); the death collector binds the card ID and the effect fizzles if the card leaves the graveyard before resolution
- `PutOnTopOfLibraryInsteadOfDyingEffect()` / `(boolean mayChoose)` — if this creature would die, put it on top of its owner's library instead (static replacement, Gravebane Zombie); `mayChoose=true` presents the choice to the affected permanent's controller and is used by Pulmonic Sliver (`TSP`)
- `PutOnBottomOfLibraryInsteadOfDyingEffect()` — if this creature would die, put it on the bottom of its owner's library instead (static replacement, Nissa's Chosen)
- `PutControlledCreatureOnTopOfLibraryEffect()` — "put a creature you control on top of its owner's library" (Nulltread Gargantuan ETB). Non-targeted, mandatory choice at resolution; the source itself is a legal pick (and forced when it's the only creature you control). One candidate resolves automatically; several prompt `PermanentChoiceContext.PutControlledCreatureOnTopOfLibrary`. No card target filter
- `CounterSpellIfManaValueEqualsXEffect()` — counters the target spell only if its mana value equals the ability's X, which a `DiscardCardTypeCost` with `trackManaValue` snapshots off the discarded card. Benign `SPELL_ON_STACK` spec. Hisoka, Minamo Sensei

## Bounce / return to hand

- `ReturnTriggeringArtifactToOwnerHandUnlessTargetTakesDamageEffect(int damage)` — ON_ANY_ARTIFACT_PUT_INTO_GRAVEYARD_FROM_BATTLEFIELD: the targeted opponent chooses whether the source deals `damage` to them; declining returns the triggering nontoken artifact to its owner's hand. The artifact id is bound from the graveyard event and the target is chosen as the trigger is put on the stack (Pia's Revolution)

- `ReturnToHandEffect.grantingEquipment()` — bounce the Equipment that granted the resolving ability; `grantingEquipmentSnapshot(id)` binds it at activation and does nothing if it left the battlefield before resolution (Razor Boomerang)
- `ReturnToHandEffect.triggering()` — bounce the permanent whose event produced the trigger, using the non-targeting `StackEntry.triggeringPermanentId` reference (Cloud Cover)

- `ReturnToHandEffect` — unified bounce, **static factories only**: `.target()` (bounce target), `.targetWithCastTimeXValue(amount)` (targeted bounce whose `targetX` count is evaluated from game state as the spell is cast — Aether Burst), `.targetCreaturesOfChosenType()` (bounce target creatures matching the creature type chosen while casting — Selective Snare), `.targetAndControllerLosesLife(1)` (Vapor Snag), `.self()` (bounce source permanent; declares source-self targeting so spell-cast trigger collectors retain the source id), `.selfSpell()` (the resolving instant/sorcery returns itself to its owner's hand off the stack instead of the graveyard — Redeem the Lost's won-clash reward), `.allPermanentsMatching(filter)` (mass bounce matching permanents; null = every permanent — pass `PermanentIsCreaturePredicate` for creatures), `.permanentsTargetPlayerControls(filter)` (River's Rebuke), `.permanentsTargetPlayerOwns(filter)` (Hurkyl's Recall, owner-based), `.aurasAttachedToTarget()` (return every Aura attached to the targeted permanent to its owner's hand, whoever controls the Aura — Scarab of the Unseen; benign `PERMANENT` spec, the Auras themselves aren't targeted), `.enchanted()` (bounce the permanent the source Aura is attached to — Sun Clasp; non-targeting; the host is snapshotted at activation, so `SacrificeSelfCost` still works — Phantom Wings), `.enchantedAndAuras()` (bounce the source Aura's enchanted permanent and every Aura attached to it, returning the Auras first so they do not become orphaned — Mark of Eviction)
- `ReturnTargetPermanentToHandOrExileIfUnearthedEffect` — returns a targeted permanent to its owner's hand, exiling it first when that permanent entered the battlefield through unearth (Meticulous Excavation)
- `ReturnTargetPermanentToHandAndReduceControllerSpeedEffect()` — returns the target permanent to its owner's hand, then reduces its controller's speed by one if that speed is greater than every other player's speed, with a floor of 1. The controller is snapshotted before the bounce. Spikeshell Harrier.
- `ReturnTargetPermanentAndAllWithSameNameToHandEffect(PermanentPredicate targetPredicate[, PermanentPredicate sameNamePredicate])` — targeted bounce of the chosen permanent plus every other same-name permanent matching the second predicate across all battlefields; only the chosen permanent is targeted (Echoing Truth)
- `ReturnAllPermanentsOfChosenColorToHandEffect()` / `(PermanentPredicate filter)` — the controller chooses a color at resolution, then returns every matching permanent of that effective color across all battlefields to its owner's hand (`null` filter = every permanent; `PermanentIsCreaturePredicate` restricts it to creatures). Used by Wash Out and Dromar, the Banisher
- `ReturnAllPermanentsOfColorToHandEffect(CardColor color[, PermanentPredicate filter][, boolean opponentsOnly])` — returns every permanent of the fixed effective color matching the optional filter across all battlefields to its owner's hand; `opponentsOnly` excludes the resolving source controller's battlefield. Llawan, Cephalid Empress
- `ReturnToHandEffect` — unified bounce, **static factories only**: `.target()` (bounce target), `.targetAndControllerLosesLife(1)` (Vapor Snag), `.self()` (bounce source permanent), `.selfSpell()` (the resolving instant/sorcery returns itself to its owner's hand off the stack instead of the graveyard — Redeem the Lost's won-clash reward), `.allPermanentsMatching(filter)` (mass bounce matching permanents; null = every permanent — pass `PermanentIsCreaturePredicate` for creatures), `.allPermanentsMatchingThen(filter, minimumControlledNontokenCount, thenEffect)` (mass bounce with a follow-up when enough controlled nontoken permanents were returned — Flood of Tears), `.permanentsTargetPlayerControls(filter)` (River's Rebuke), `.permanentsTargetPlayerOwns(filter)` (Hurkyl's Recall, owner-based), `.aurasAttachedToTarget()` (return every Aura attached to the targeted permanent to its owner's hand, whoever controls the Aura — Scarab of the Unseen; benign `PERMANENT` spec, the Auras themselves aren't targeted), `.enchanted()` (bounce the permanent the source Aura is attached to — Sun Clasp; non-targeting; the host is snapshotted at activation, so `SacrificeSelfCost` still works — Phantom Wings)
- `ReturnTargetPermanentToHandOrExileIfUnearthedEffect` — returns a targeted permanent to its owner's hand, exiling it first when that permanent entered the battlefield through unearth (Meticulous Excavation)
- `ReturnTargetAndAttachedMatchingToHandEffect(PermanentPredicate attachedFilter)` — bounce target creature **and** attached permanents matching `attachedFilter` (attachments first so they aren't orphaned). Word of Undoing: `AllOf(Aura, ColorIn(WHITE), OwnedBySourceController)`
- `ReturnTargetSpellToHandEffect()` — bounce target spell from the stack to its owner's hand (not countering; Hullbreaker Horror)
- `ReturnTargetSpellOrCreatureToHandEffect()` — bounce target spell from the stack or target creature permanent to its owner's hand; declares both target kinds and routes the chosen target by zone (Unsubstantiate)
- `ReturnTargetSpellOrPermanentToHandEffect()` — bounce target spell from the stack or any target permanent to its owner's hand; declares both target kinds and routes the chosen target by zone (Jeskai Revelation)
- `ReturnTargetPermanentToHandWithManaValueConditionalEffect(int, CardEffect)` — bounce + MV bonus
- `ReturnTargetCreatureToHandThenCreateTokensEqualToManaValueEffect(CreateTokenEffect)` — return the target creature, snapshot its mana value before it leaves, then create that many copies of the supplied token template under the spell controller's control (Aether Mutation)
- `ReturnTargetPermanentToHandOrLibraryTopByPredicateEffect(PermanentPredicate)` — bounce to hand, or to top of library instead when target matches predicate (Consign to Dream)
- `ReturnSelfToHandOnCoinFlipLossEffect()` — bounce self on coin flip loss
- `ReturnThisSpellToHandThenDiscardAtRandomEffect()` — "return this spell to its owner's hand, then discard a card at random" (Hanabi Blast). The spell is back in hand before the discard, so it is one of the random candidates (empty hand ⇒ it always discards itself); the handler picks over hand + spell card and then sets the spell's disposition to hand or graveyard
- `ReturnPermanentsOnCombatDamageToPlayerEffect()` / `(PermanentPredicate)` / `(PermanentPredicate, int fixedCount)` — Ninja-style
- `ReturnPermanentControlledByPlayerToHandEffect(PermanentPredicate, String[, boolean controllerChooses])` — mandatory resolution-time choice of a matching permanent controlled by the acting player; on `ON_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER`, the acting player is the damaged player. Set `controllerChooses=true` when the trigger controller chooses, as with Sigil of Sleep. Use `ReturnPermanentControlledByPlayerToHandEffect.controller(...)` when the resolving controller must be the acting player even though the stack entry has another target, as with Sparkcaster
- `ReturnPermanentControlledByPlayerToHandEffect(PermanentPredicate, String[, boolean controllerChooses])` — mandatory resolution-time choice of a matching permanent controlled by the acting player; on `ON_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER`, the acting player is the damaged player. Set `controllerChooses=true` when the trigger controller chooses, as with Sigil of Sleep
- `ReturnPermanentControlledByPlayerToHandThenEffect(PermanentPredicate, CardEffect, String)` — non-targeting resolution-time choice that returns a matching permanent to its owner's hand and resolves the follow-up only after a permanent was actually returned. Wrap in `MayEffect` for "you may return ... If you do, ..." (Indoctrination Attendant)
- `ReturnPermanentSharingTypeWithEnteringPermanentToHandEffect()` — non-targeting resolution-time choice for an enter trigger: returns another permanent controlled by the ability's controller that shares any effective permanent type with the entering permanent, excluding the entering permanent itself. Reads `StackEntry.triggeringPermanentId` and falls back to the entering card's printed permanent types if it has left the battlefield (Cloudstone Curio)
- `PutTargetOnBottomOfLibraryEffect()` — tuck bottom
- `PutTargetOnTopOfLibraryEffect()` — tuck top; `.self()` tucks the source instead; `new PutTargetOnTopOfLibraryEffect(PutOnTopOfLibraryScope.SELF_AND_TARGET)` tucks the source AND the target creature, then shuffles both owners' libraries (Void Stalker)
- `PutTargetCreatureOnTopOrOptionalBottomOfLibraryEffect(PermanentPredicate)` — tuck target creature on top, but if it matches the predicate the controller may put it on the bottom instead (Ether Well, `PermanentColorInPredicate(Set.of(RED))`)
- `PutTargetSpellOrCreatureOnTopOrBottomOfLibraryEffect()` — tuck target spell or creature on top, then its owner chooses whether it stays on top or moves to the bottom
- `PutTargetPermanentIntoLibraryNFromTopEffect(DynamicAmount)` — tuck the evaluated position N from the top; the `int` constructor remains for fixed positions
- `PutTargetPermanentIntoLibraryNFromTopThenEffect(DynamicAmount, CardEffect, ThenEffectRecipient)` — tuck target permanent at the evaluated position, then resolve an existing effect using the removed permanent's snapshotted controller/owner; use `int` for fixed positions
- `PutTargetPermanentIntoLibraryNFromTopOrBottomEffect(int)` — tuck target nonland permanent N from the top, then its owner chooses whether it stays there or moves to the bottom
- `PutTargetSpellOrPermanentIntoLibraryNFromTopEffect(int)` — tuck target spell or nonland permanent N from top (Commit = 1; not a counter)
- `PutTargetSpellOrPermanentOnTopOrBottomOfLibraryEffect()` — target red or green spell or permanent; its owner chooses top or bottom
- `PutSourceCardFromGraveyardIntoLibraryNFromTopEffect(position)` — ON_DEATH: put dying source into owner's library N from top (0=top: Undying Beast; 2=third: Enigma Sphinx)
- `PutSourcePermanentIntoLibraryNFromTopEffect(int position)` — put the source permanent into its owner's library at a fixed position from the top (Braided Quipu)
- `PutSourceCardFromExileIntoLibraryNFromTopEffect(position)` — exile trigger: put the source card from exile into its owner's library N from top (0-indexed; 2=third: God-Eternal Oketra)
- `ExileSourceCardFromGraveyardEffect()` — ON_DEATH: exile dying source from owner's graveyard ("When this dies, exile it", Cyclopean Mummy)
- `ReturnTopCreatureCardFromGraveyardToBattlefieldEffect(GraveyardOwner graveyard, boolean assignNoCombatDamageIfReturned)` — put the topmost creature card of `CONTROLLER`'s (your) or `TARGET_PLAYER`'s graveyard (the most recently put there; noncreature cards above it are skipped) onto the battlefield **under your** control. Non-targeting; no-op with no creature card. `assignNoCombatDamageIfReturned` makes the source assign no combat damage this turn only if a card came back (Bone Dancer's "if you do", `MayEffect(…(TARGET_PLAYER, true))` on `ON_ATTACKS_UNBLOCKED`). Mistmoon Griffin pairs `(CONTROLLER, false)` as `SequenceEffect.of(ExileSourceCardFromGraveyardEffect(), it)` on `ON_DEATH` so the dying source is exiled before the lookup
- `ExileIfHadCounterElseReturnWithCounterEffect(CounterType)` — ON_DEATH: exile if dying source had that counter, else return self to battlefield under your control with one of that counter (Bogardan Phoenix / death counters). Collector snapshots the counter at death and bakes `ExileSourceCardFromGraveyardEffect` or self `ReturnCardFromGraveyardEffect` with `enterWithCounter`
- `ReturnMatchingPermanentsUnlessOwnerPaysEffect(PermanentPredicate filter, String manaCost)` — `BoardWipeEffect`; punisher mass bounce: every permanent matching `filter` across all battlefields returns to its owner's hand unless that permanent's OWNER pays `manaCost` — "unless that player pays" refers back to the owner, so a permanent that changed hands is paid for by the player whose hand would receive it, not the current controller. One independent may-pay decision per permanent, offered in APNAP order by owner (CR 101.4). Cut the Tethers (`PermanentHasSubtypePredicate(SPIRIT)`, `"{3}"`)

## Graveyard return

- `GrantMayhemToGraveyardCardsEffect(CardPredicate filter)` — matching nonland cards in the controller's graveyard may be cast for their normal mana costs if they were discarded this turn; used for a static mayhem grant such as Green Goblin
- `GraveyardCast(null, "{cost}", List.of(), new CardDiscardedThisTurn())` — Mayhem-style casting option: cast this card from its controller's graveyard for the alternate cost only if that card was discarded or cycled this turn; normal timing rules apply and the card returns to the graveyard normally
- `ReturnCardsDestroyedThisWayFromGraveyardEffect()` — returns the resolving controller's cards whose ids were recorded by a preceding destruction event and that are still in that controller's graveyard; pair with `DestroyAllPermanentsEffect` for "return all cards in your graveyard destroyed this way"

- `ReturnTargetCardsFromGraveyardToBattlefieldEffect(CardPredicate, int, CounterType, int)` — returns up to the fixed cap of targeted graveyard cards and puts the specified number of counters on each returned permanent (Smile at Death).
- `DelveCost()` — flexible additional spell cost: exile any number of cards from your graveyard, up to the spell's generic mana requirement, reducing that generic cost by one per card. The cast request carries the selected graveyard indices; `SpellCastingService` pays the reduction through the normal mana path and then exiles the selected cards. Dig Through Time
- `EachPlayerReturnsCardsFromGraveyardToBattlefieldEffect(int, CardPredicate, boolean)` — mass reanimate for each player; when the boolean is true, only cards put into the graveyard from the battlefield this turn qualify (Second Sunrise)
- `SacrificeOtherCreaturesThenReturnCreatureCardsEffect()` — `BoardWipeEffect`; each player sacrifices all other creatures they control, then returns the creature cards that were already in their graveyard. The pre-resolution graveyard snapshot excludes creatures sacrificed by this effect (Bringer of the Last Gift)

- `ReturnCardFromGraveyardEffect.builder().destination(HAND|BATTLEFIELD|TOP_OF_OWNERS_LIBRARY)...build()` — unified graveyard return (see EFFECTS_INDEX.md for full builder API). `.targetGraveyard(true).targetPutIntoGraveyardFromBattlefieldThisTurn(true)` targets a creature card put into a graveyard from the battlefield this turn (Grim Return). `.grantHaste(true).sacrificeAtEndStep(true)` grants haste and schedules sacrifice at the next end step (Apprentice Necromancer). `.exileIfDying(true)` makes a returned permanent exile instead of dying without replacing returns to hand or library (Can't Stay Away). `.grantKeywords(Set.of(...))` and `.grantIndestructible(true)` grant indefinite abilities (Kheru Lich Lord, Fated Return). `.plusOneCountersIfCardType(CardType.CREATURE).plusOneCounterCount(1)` gates a counter rider on the returned permanent being a creature (Recommission)
- `ReturnCardFromGraveyardEffect.builder().destination(HAND|BATTLEFIELD|TOP_OF_OWNERS_LIBRARY)...build()` — unified graveyard return (see EFFECTS_INDEX.md for full builder API). `.targetGraveyard(true).targetPutIntoGraveyardFromBattlefieldThisTurn(true)` targets a creature card put into a graveyard from the battlefield this turn (Grim Return). `.targetNotPutIntoGraveyardThisCombat(true)` excludes targeted cards put into a graveyard during the current combat phase (Storrev, Devkarin Lich). `.grantHaste(true).sacrificeAtEndStep(true)` grants haste and schedules sacrifice at the next end step (Apprentice Necromancer). `.grantKeywords(Set.of(...))` and `.grantIndestructible(true)` grant indefinite abilities (Kheru Lich Lord, Fated Return). `.plusOneCountersIfCardType(CardType.CREATURE).plusOneCounterCount(1)` gates a counter rider on the returned permanent being a creature (Recommission)
- `ReturnCardFromGraveyardEffect.builder().destination(HAND|BATTLEFIELD|TOP_OF_OWNERS_LIBRARY)...build()` — unified graveyard return (see EFFECTS_INDEX.md for full builder API). `.targetGraveyard(true).targetPutIntoGraveyardFromBattlefieldThisTurn(true)` targets a creature card put into a graveyard from the battlefield this turn (Grim Return). `.grantHaste(true).sacrificeAtEndStep(true)` grants haste and schedules sacrifice at the next end step (Apprentice Necromancer). `.grantKeywords(Set.of(...))` and `.grantIndestructible(true)` grant indefinite abilities (Kheru Lich Lord, Fated Return). `.plusOneCountersIfCardType(CardType.CREATURE).plusOneCounterCount(1)` gates a counter rider on the returned permanent being a creature (Recommission)
- `ReturnCardFromGraveyardToHandOfOpponentsChoiceEffect(CardPredicate)` — targeted ETB return of a creature card from the controller's graveyard to hand where the controller chooses which opponent makes the graveyard target choice (Mausoleum Turnkey). The target is selected through the shared `SpellGraveyardTargetTrigger` flow, with `PermanentChoiceContext.MausoleumTurnkeyOpponentChoice` used only when multiple opponents exist; resolution delegates to `ReturnCardFromGraveyardEffect`.
- `ReturnCardFromGraveyardEffect.builder().destination(BATTLEFIELD).returnAtRandom(true).battlefieldIfCreatureElseExile(true).shuffleGraveyardBeforeRandomSelection(true).build()` — randomly reorder the controller's graveyard, select one card at random, put it onto the battlefield if it is a creature, and exile it otherwise (Search for Survivors)
- `ReturnCardFromGraveyardEffect.builder().destination(HAND|BATTLEFIELD|TOP_OF_OWNERS_LIBRARY)...build()` — unified graveyard return (see EFFECTS_INDEX.md for full builder API). `.targetGraveyard(true).targetPutIntoGraveyardFromBattlefieldThisTurn(true)` targets a creature card put into a graveyard from the battlefield this turn (Grim Return). `.grantHaste(true).sacrificeAtEndStep(true)` grants haste and schedules sacrifice at the next end step (Apprentice Necromancer). `.grantKeywords(Set.of(...))` and `.grantIndestructible(true)` grant indefinite abilities (Kheru Lich Lord, Fated Return). `.enterWithCounters(Set.of(...))` puts one counter of each listed type on the returned permanent as it enters (Perennation). `.plusOneCountersIfCardType(CardType.CREATURE).plusOneCounterCount(1)` gates a counter rider on the returned permanent being a creature (Recommission)
- `ReturnCardFromGraveyardEffect.builder().destination(HAND|BATTLEFIELD|TOP_OF_OWNERS_LIBRARY)...build()` — unified graveyard return (see EFFECTS_INDEX.md for full builder API). `.targetGraveyard(true).targetPutIntoGraveyardFromBattlefieldThisTurn(true)` = "target creature card in a graveyard that was put there from the battlefield this turn" (Grim Return). `.grantHaste(true).sacrificeAtEndStep(true)` returns a targeted creature with haste and schedules it for sacrifice at the next end step (Apprentice Necromancer). `.grantKeywords(Set.of(...))` grants the listed keywords indefinitely to the returned permanent (Kheru Lich Lord); `.exileAtYourNextEndStep(true)` waits for the returned permanent controller's next end step; `.grantIndestructible(true)` permanently grants indestructible to a targeted returned permanent (Fated Return)
- `TargetOpponentChoosesCardFromGraveyardEffect(CardPredicate filter)` — target opponent chooses a matching card in their graveyard and it enters the battlefield under the spell controller's control (Scrounge)
- `ReturnCardFromGraveyardEffect.builder().destination(HAND|BATTLEFIELD|TOP_OF_OWNERS_LIBRARY)...build()` — unified graveyard return (see EFFECTS_INDEX.md for full builder API). `.targetGraveyard(true).targetPutIntoGraveyardFromBattlefieldThisTurn(true)` = "target creature card in a graveyard that was put there from the battlefield this turn" (Grim Return). `.dynamicMaxManaValue(DynamicAmount)` snapshots a death-trigger mana-value limit when the trigger is collected (soulshift X)
- `SourceManaValueMinusOne()` — evaluates one less than the source permanent's mana value, including a last-known battlefield snapshot when a death trigger captures a strict lesser-than threshold (Scrap Trawler)
- `ReturnCardFromGraveyardEffect.builder().returnToHandAtEndStep(true)...build()` returns a card returned to the battlefield to its owner's hand at the beginning of the next end step (Cauldron Dance)

- `ReturnCardFromGraveyardEffect.builder().destination(HAND|BATTLEFIELD|TOP_OF_OWNERS_LIBRARY)...build()` — unified graveyard return (see EFFECTS_INDEX.md for full builder API). `.targetGraveyard(true).targetPutIntoGraveyardFromBattlefieldThisTurn(true)` = "target creature card in a graveyard that was put there from the battlefield this turn" (Grim Return)
- `ReturnCardFromGraveyardEffect.builder().destination(HAND|BATTLEFIELD|TOP_OF_OWNERS_LIBRARY)...build()` — unified graveyard return (see EFFECTS_INDEX.md for full builder API). `.targetGraveyard(true).targetPutIntoGraveyardFromBattlefieldThisTurn(true)` restricts the targeted card to one put into a graveyard from the battlefield this turn; combine it with `CardTypePredicate(CREATURE)` for Grim Return or `CardIsPermanentPredicate` for Salvager of Ruin. `.grantHaste(true).sacrificeAtEndStep(true)` returns a targeted creature with haste and schedules it for sacrifice at the next end step (Apprentice Necromancer)
- `ReturnCardFromGraveyardEffect.builder().destination(HAND|BATTLEFIELD|TOP_OF_OWNERS_LIBRARY)...build()` — unified graveyard return (see EFFECTS_INDEX.md for full builder API). `.targetGraveyard(true).targetPutIntoGraveyardFromBattlefieldThisTurn(true)` restricts the targeted card to one put into a graveyard from the battlefield this turn; `.dynamicMaxManaValue(DynamicAmount)` snapshots a death-trigger mana-value limit when the trigger is collected (soulshift X)
- `ForgottenLoreEffect(String repeatManaCost)` (default `{G}`) — SPELL, player-targeting spec (`harmful(PLAYER)`; pair with `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(OPPONENT)`): the targeted opponent chooses a card in your graveyard, then you may pay the configured cost to repeat with every already-chosen card excluded; the last chosen card goes to your hand. Used by Forgotten Lore (`{G}`) and Shrouded Lore (`{B}`). Alternates a mandatory `PendingInteraction.GraveyardChoice` (`MAY_ABILITY_TARGET`, cardPool = your graveyard minus chosen, completed via `graveyardTargetOperation.resolutionTimeForgottenLoreResume` — records only, moves nothing) with a `ChoiceContext.ForgottenLorePaymentChoice` list pick, both resuming over `GameData.forgottenLore` + `rerunCurrentEffectAfterInteraction`. The spell itself is excluded from the pool (CR 608.2n). No prompt when the controller can't afford the configured cost; loop ends when no unchosen card remains
- `OpponentChoosesOneOfTopTwoGraveyardCardsEffect()` — activated-ability effect, player-targeting spec (`harmful(PLAYER)`; pair with `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(OPPONENT)`): the targeted opponent chooses one of the top two cards of your graveyard (topmost first — cards are appended on entry); that card is exiled and the other one goes to your hand. Phyrexian Grimoire. Mandatory `PendingInteraction.GraveyardChoice` (`MAY_ABILITY_TARGET`, cardPool = the top two) completed via `graveyardTargetOperation.resolutionTimePhyrexianGrimoireResume` — records the pick only, the handler moves both cards on the rerun. One card in the graveyard ⇒ exiled with nothing to hand (no prompt); empty graveyard ⇒ nothing
- `ThranTomeRevealTopThreeOpponentChoosesEffect()` — activated-ability effect, player-targeting spec (`harmful(PLAYER)`; pair with `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(OPPONENT)`): reveal up to the top three cards of the controller's library; the targeted opponent chooses one, and the shared `LibrarySearch` choice moves only that card into the controller's graveyard while the other revealed cards remain on top. The handler queues `PendingThranTomeChoice` so the following `DrawCardEffect(2)` runs once after the choice. Empty library ⇒ no card is milled, then the following draw still resolves
- `RecordReturnedGraveyardCardValueEffect(ReturnedGraveyardCardValue value)` — graveyard-return glue (`NONLAND_MANA_VALUE` = Vengeful Rebirth, `POWER` = Morgue Burst): sets the entry's event value to that characteristic of the card on `entry.targetId` (a graveyard target) **only when it was actually returned to the controller's hand** (in-hand check gates on the return; land / illegal-removed target records 0). `TargetSpec.NONE`, added UNBOUND after the `ReturnCardFromGraveyardEffect` so the resolver keeps `targetId` on the graveyard card. Pair with a bound `DealDamageToAnyTargetEffect(new EventValue(), false, false, 0)` on an any-target group to deal "damage equal to that card's mana value to any target" (0 = no damage)
- `RecordReturnedGraveyardCardMatchEffect(CardPredicate)` — records 1 when the card returned by a preceding `ReturnCardFromGraveyardEffect` matches the predicate, otherwise 0. It confirms the card reached the controller's hand and stays UNBOUND so `entry.targetId` remains the graveyard card. Pair with `DrawCardEffect(new EventValue())` for conditional draw riders such as Cemetery Recruitment
- `AnimateReturnedPermanentIfSubtypeEffect(CardSubtype subtype, AnimatePermanentsEffect animation)` — unbound graveyard-return rider: finds the permanent created by the preceding targeted `ReturnCardFromGraveyardEffect` using the returned card ID, checks its current subtype, and delegates the supplied animation (Tune Up: Vehicle → permanent artifact creature). The nested animation is not a separate cast-time target.
- `AnimateReturnedPermanentsEffect(AnimatePermanentsEffect animation)` — unbound graveyard-return rider: finds the permanents created by the preceding targeted multi-card graveyard return using the returned card IDs and applies the supplied animation to each surviving permanent (Dance of the Manse).
- `ReturnSourceFromGraveyardAttachedToTargetEffect()` — GY activated: return source Aura attached to target creature (Gryff's Boon; wire `targetId`)
- `ReturnTriggeringLandFromGraveyardToBattlefieldEffect(UUID landCardId)` — Sacred Ground's trigger effect: return the identified land from the graveyard to the battlefield under its owner's control. Register the template with `null` on the `ON_ALLY_LAND_PUT_INTO_GRAVEYARD_BY_OPPONENT` slot; the collector stamps the real card id.
- `ReturnTargetCardOnDeathThisTurnEffect(boolean enterTapped)` — SPELL delayed trigger (Graceful Reprieve): if the targeted creature dies this turn, return that card to the battlefield under its owner's control. Pair with a creature `target(...)`. No-arg = untapped; pass `true` to return tapped (Supernatural Stamina, which also adds a `BoostTargetCreatureEffect(2, 0)` on the same target). `underControllerExcludingSource()` is the untapped activated-ability form for Adarkar Valkyrie: it returns under the registering ability's controller and excludes the source creature from targeting.
- `ResolveEffectOnTargetDeathThisTurnEffect(CardEffect effect)` — delayed trigger (Skeletonize, Initiate of Blood, Burn Away): if the targeted creature dies this turn, `effect` resolves under the registering player's control. The pushed trigger carries the dying creature's last-known controller in `targetId`, so wrapped effects can use that context (Burn Away's `ExileGraveyardCardsEffect(DYING_CREATURE_CONTROLLER)`). Pair with a creature `target(...)`/`targetFilter` and (typically) a `DealDamageToTargetCreatureEffect` in the same slot or ability. The pushed trigger carries the registering permanent as `sourcePermanentId`, so self-referential wrapped effects (`TransformToBackFaceEffect`, flipping) still find their source.
- `ExileDyingCreatureCardAndCreateTokenEffect(CreateTokenEffect token)` — ON_DAMAGED_CREATURE_DIES effect (Unscythe, Killer of Kings), wrap in `MayEffect`: exile the dying creature's card from its owner's graveyard and, if the exile happens, create `token`. Implements `DyingCreatureCardAwareEffect` so the death-trigger collector binds the dying card id (the may flow drops the stack entry's triggering-card id). No exile if the card already left the graveyard (a token, or removed in response) → no token. On an Equipment this fires for the currently-equipped creature's damage (`GraveyardService.triggerDamagedCreatureDiesAbilities` scans Equipment attached to the damaging creature).
- `ReturnTriggeringCardFromGraveyardToBattlefieldEffect()` — triggered-ability effect the death pipeline pushes for the above; returns the stack entry's card from its owner's graveyard to the battlefield, or under the delayed ability's controller for Adarkar Valkyrie. Not added to a card directly.
- `ReturnOneOfEachSubtypeFromGraveyardToHandEffect(List<CardSubtype>)` — one of each subtype
- `ReturnUpToOneCardOfEachManaValueFromGraveyardToHandEffect()` — return any number of cards with distinct mana values from the controller's graveyard to hand; choices are sequential and may be declined
- `ReturnOneCreatureOfEachManaValueFromGraveyardToBattlefieldEffect(List<Integer>)` — one creature card for each listed mana value from the controller's graveyard to the battlefield, processed in order; missing values are skipped, single matches return automatically, and multiple matches use mandatory sequential choices. Gruesome Menagerie.
- `ReturnCardsFromControllerGraveyardToHandEffect(CardPredicate, DynamicAmount)` — return up to the evaluated count of matching cards from the controller's graveyard to hand; if more matches exist than the count, the controller must choose exactly that many one at a time. Rofellos's Gift uses `EventValue()` after `RevealAnyNumberOfCardsFromHandEffect`.
- `PutTargetCardsFromGraveyardOnTopOfLibraryEffect(CardPredicate[, int maxTargets])` — graveyard to top of library; one-arg = any number, two-arg caps it (Reinforcements = 3). `fromOpponentGraveyard(filter, n)` targets a single opponent's graveyard instead and stacks the cards on that opponent's library (Misinformation = 3); `fromAnyPlayerGraveyard(filter, n)` allows any one player's graveyard, controller's included, and is usable from an activated ability via `Zone.GRAVEYARD` `targetIds` (Lodestone Bauble = 4 basic lands)
- `PutCardsFromControllerGraveyardOnTopOfLibraryEffect(int maxCount)` — at resolution, choose exactly up to five cards from the controller's graveyard (or all available cards when fewer) and order them on top of that library; reuses the multi-graveyard choice and library-reorder interactions (Stillness in Motion)
- `PutTargetCardFromGraveyardOnTopOrBottomOfLibraryEffect()` — target any card in any graveyard; the spell controller chooses top or bottom of that card's owner's library during resolution
- `PutTargetCardsFromGraveyardOnBottomOfLibraryEffect(CardPredicate[, int maxTargets])` — puts up to N target cards from your graveyard on the bottom of your library in the supplied order (Bow of Nylea)
- `ReturnUpToOneCardOfEachColorFromGraveyardToHandEffect()` — returns the selected cards from the controller's graveyard, with five optional color target groups assigned so multicolored cards can fill different colors (All Suns' Dawn)
- `ReturnTargetCardsFromGraveyardToHandEffect(CardPredicate, int[, boolean xScaled])` or `(CardPredicate, DynamicAmount)` — up to N cards to hand; `(CardPredicate, DynamicAmount)` makes the cap a cast-time count instead of a fixed number and declares a benign `player()` spec (Reap = `PermanentCount(PermanentColorInPredicate(BLACK), TARGET_PLAYER)`, "up to X … where X is the number of black permanents target opponent controls as you cast this spell"); `xScaled` = exactly X cards (Shattered Crypt). `upToOnePerCardType(filter, Set<CardType>)` caps the selection at one card matching each listed type (Pull from the Deep)
- `DeliverUntoEvilEffect()` — choose up to four target cards from your graveyard; if you control a Bolas planeswalker at resolution, return all surviving targets to hand, otherwise the opponent must choose two (or all remaining when fewer) to leave in the graveyard and the rest return to hand. Uses cast-time graveyard targeting, then a `PendingPileSeparation` with `CardPileDisposition.DELIVER_UNTO_EVIL`; pair with `ExileSpellEffect()` (Deliver Unto Evil)
- `ReturnTargetCardsFromGraveyardToHandEffect(CardPredicate, int[, boolean xScaled])` or `(CardPredicate, DynamicAmount)` — up to N cards to hand; `(CardPredicate, DynamicAmount)` makes the cap a cast-time count instead of a fixed number and declares a benign `player()` spec (Reap = `PermanentCount(PermanentColorInPredicate(BLACK), TARGET_PLAYER)`, "up to X … where X is the number of black permanents target opponent controls as you cast this spell"); `xScaled` = exactly X cards (Shattered Crypt). It also supports activated-ability graveyard target groups through `Zone.GRAVEYARD` target IDs
- `TargetCardGroupEffect` — associates positional graveyard-card target groups with the effects that resolve them. Use `ReturnTargetCardsFromGraveyardToHandEffect.withTargetGroups(...).fromSameGraveyard().toOwnersHands()` for cards such as Pulsemage Advocate, and `ReturnCardFromGraveyardEffect.builder().targetGroup(index)` for a separate reanimation target.
- `ReturnTargetInstantAndSorceryCardsFromGraveyardToHandEffect()` — returns the selected instant and sorcery cards from the controller's graveyard to hand; use with one optional instant-or-sorcery target group and `AT_MOST_ONE_INSTANT_AND_ONE_SORCERY`
- `ReturnTargetCardsFromGraveyardToBattlefieldEffect(CardPredicate)`, fixed-cap `(CardPredicate, int, boolean, boolean[, CardColor, CardSubtype])`, `(CardPredicate, DynamicAmount)`, or `withinTotalManaValue(filter, maxTotalManaValue)` — returns graveyard targets to the battlefield. The one-argument form uses the spell's paid X; the fixed-cap form can add persistent color/subtype characteristics (Ever After); the dynamic form evaluates an up-to target cap from cast or ETB context; the factory enforces an aggregate mana-value cap
- `SacrificeTargetArtifactAndReturnTargetArtifactFromGraveyardEffect()` — if both artifact targets remain legal, sacrifice the targeted battlefield artifact and return the targeted artifact card from that player's graveyard to the battlefield (Goblin Welder)
- `ReturnTargetCardsFromGraveyardToHandEffect(CardPredicate, int[, boolean xScaled])` or `(CardPredicate, DynamicAmount)` — up to N cards to hand; `(CardPredicate, DynamicAmount)` makes the cap a cast-time count instead of a fixed number and declares a benign `player()` spec (Reap = `PermanentCount(PermanentColorInPredicate(BLACK), TARGET_PLAYER)`, "up to X … where X is the number of black permanents target opponent controls as you cast this spell"); `xScaled` = exactly X cards (Shattered Crypt)
- `ReturnUpToOneOfEachFilterFromGraveyardToHandEffect(List<CardPredicate>)` — independently optional target groups: return up to one card matching each filter from the controller's graveyard to hand. The cast-time multi-graveyard choice validates a one-to-one assignment so one card cannot satisfy two groups (Rise from the Wreck)
- `ReturnUpToOneOfEachFilterFromGraveyardToDestinationsEffect(List<CardPredicate>, List<GraveyardChoiceDestination>, List<String>[, List<Integer>, boolean])` — independently selected single-card graveyard groups with a destination per group; optional minimum counts and distinct-card enforcement support effects with a required first target and an optional "other" target (Badlands Revival, Once and Future)
- `ReturnTargetCardsFromGraveyardToBattlefieldEffect(CardPredicate)` — exactly X target cards to the battlefield, with X taken from the spell's paid X value
- `ShuffleTargetCardsFromGraveyardIntoLibraryEffect(CardPredicate, int)` — target player shuffles up to N cards; `Integer.MAX_VALUE` represents any number. Spell use chooses the player at cast time; ETB use chooses the player first and then prompts for cards from that player's graveyard (Memory's Journey, Loaming Shaman)
- `ShuffleTargetCardsFromGraveyardIntoOwnersLibrariesEffect(CardPredicate, int)` — shuffle up to N target cards from any graveyards into their owners' libraries; cards may come from different graveyards, and each affected owner shuffles their own library (Turn the Earth)
- `ShuffleTargetCardsFromControllerGraveyardIntoLibraryEffect(CardPredicate, int)` — controller shuffles up to N target cards from their own graveyard into library (ETB: Ghostly Castigator; activated graveyard-targeting ability: Perpetual Timepiece; "up to" = you may)
- `ReturnDyingCreatureToBattlefieldEffect(boolean attachSource)` — reanimate a creature that just died under your control; `true` also attaches the source Equipment (Nim Deathmantle), `false` just reanimates (Oathkeeper, Takeno's Daisho)
- `ExileEquippedCreatureEffect()` — ON_DEATH on an Equipment: exile the creature it was attached to (Oathkeeper, Takeno's Daisho)
- `ReturnDyingOpponentCreatureUnderYourControlEffect([CardSubtype grantSubtype])` — ON_OPPONENT_CREATURE_DIES: steal the dying creature from its owner's graveyard onto your battlefield (Necroskitter; Lim-Dûl the Necromancer uses `ZOMBIE`); an optional subtype is granted only if the returned permanent is a creature. The collector adds the bare effect's "you may", or preserves a surrounding `MayPayManaEffect`, and stamps the dying card id
- `GainLifeEqualToDyingCreatureToughnessEffect()` — ON_OPPONENT_CREATURE_DIES: you gain life equal to the dying creature's last-known effective toughness (Grim Feast). The collector snapshots the toughness from the trigger context and bakes it into a concrete `GainLifeEffect`
- `EnchantedCreatureDiesLoseLifeEqualPowerGainLifeEqualToughnessEffect()` — ON_ENCHANTED_PERMANENT_PUT_INTO_GRAVEYARD: controller loses life = LKI power, you gain life = LKI toughness (Death Watch). One stack entry; bakes into `EnchantedCreatureControllerLosesLifeEffect` + `GainLifeEffect`
- `StealDyingOpponentPermanentUnlessPaysLifeEffect(int lifeCost)` — ON_OPPONENT_PERMANENT_PUT_INTO_GRAVEYARD_FROM_BATTLEFIELD punisher (`ControlStealingEffect`): any-type opponent permanent dies; that opponent pays `lifeCost` life or you put that card onto the battlefield under your control (Prince of Thralls, 3). Can't-pay = auto-steal; collector stamps dying card id + paying opponent
- `PutCardFromOpponentGraveyardOntoBattlefieldEffect(boolean tapped, CardPredicate filter, boolean requireManaValueEqualsX[, DynamicAmount maxManaValue])` — put target card matching `filter` from an opponent's graveyard onto battlefield under your control, correctly tracked as stolen (returns to owner on leaving). `(boolean tapped)` and `()` default to artifact-or-creature + MV==X + mill-X (Geth). Ashen Powder: `(false, new CardTypePredicate(CREATURE), false)` — creature only, any MV, no mill. A non-null `maxManaValue` adds a live maximum mana-value restriction, as used by Agadeem Occultist. On `ON_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER` the shared `SpellGraveyardTargetTrigger` flow narrows the choice to the damaged player's graveyard (`CombatDamageService` passes them as `graveyardOwnerId`) — Ink-Eyes, Servant of Oni's "from that player's graveyard".
- `LivingDeathEffect()` — SPELL slot, card-specific (Living Death). Each player exiles all creature cards from their graveyard, then every creature on every battlefield is sacrificed (`DestructionSupport.performSimultaneousSacrifice`), then each exiled card enters under the player whose graveyard it came from. Creatures sacrificed by step two are NOT reanimated; the returning cards enter as one CR 614.12 batch. `BoardWipeEffect`
- `ReturnSourceCardFromGraveyardToBattlefieldEffect(boolean tapped)` — ON_DEATH: return the dying source card from its owner's graveyard to the battlefield under its owner's control, optionally tapped. Battlefield analogue of `ReturnSourceCardFromGraveyardToOwnerHandEffect`; immediate (unlike `RegisterDelayedSelfReturnFromGraveyardEffect`) and counterless (unlike `UndyingReturnEffect`). Abnormal Endurance grants it with `GrantEffectToTargetUntilEndOfTurnEffect(ON_DEATH, ...)`
- `ReturnSourceAuraToCreatureOnDeathEffect()` — ON_ENCHANTED_PERMANENT_PUT_INTO_GRAVEYARD: return the source Aura from its controller's graveyard to the battlefield attached to a legal creature chosen by that controller (Screams from Within)
- `ReturnSourceAuraToRandomCreatureOnDeathEffect()` — ON_ENCHANTED_PERMANENT_PUT_INTO_GRAVEYARD: return the source Aura from its owner's graveyard to the battlefield attached to a legal creature chosen uniformly at random (Infectious Rage)
- `ReturnSourceAuraToCreatureOrNonAuraOnDeathEffect()` — ON_ENCHANTED_PERMANENT_PUT_INTO_GRAVEYARD: return the source Aura attached to a legal creature chosen by the dying creature's controller; if none exists, return it as a non-Aura enchantment and persist `DealDamageToPlayerAtUpkeepEffect` for that player (Takklemaggot)
- `DealDamageToPlayerAtUpkeepEffect(UUID)` — persistent `EACH_UPKEEP_TRIGGERED` damage effect for one fixed player's upkeep; deals that player 1 damage (Takklemaggot's non-Aura form)
- `ReturnSourceCardFromGraveyardToBattlefieldFaceDownEffect()` — ON_DEATH: return the dying source card from its owner's graveyard to the battlefield face down under the trigger controller's control. Used by Ashcloud Phoenix.
- `UndyingReturnEffect()` — Undying (CR 702.93) resolution: return the dying card from its owner's graveyard to the battlefield with a +1/+1 counter. Do NOT add to a card directly; it is pushed automatically by `PermanentRemovalService` when a creature with the `UNDYING` keyword dies with no +1/+1 counters. The keyword is loaded from Scryfall.
- `PersistReturnEffect()` — Persist (CR 702.79) resolution: return the dying card from its owner's graveyard to the battlefield with a -1/-1 counter. Do NOT add to a card directly; it is pushed automatically by `PermanentRemovalService` when a creature with the `PERSIST` keyword dies with no -1/-1 counters. The keyword is loaded from Scryfall.
- `PutCreatureFromOpponentGraveyardOntoBattlefieldWithExileEffect()` — opponent's creature with exile
- `GrantTargetCreatureCardGraveyardCastAndCopyActivatedAbilitiesEffect()` — target creature card in any graveyard may be cast this turn; when cast, source gains its activated abilities
- `GrantSourceActivatedAbilitiesUntilEndOfTurnEffect(List<ActivatedAbility>, String)` — delayed source grant used after casting the selected graveyard creature

## Draw / discard / hand manipulation

- `LookAtHandChooseCardToDiscardAndDrawEffect()` — ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD: look at a target player's hand; you may choose a card for that player to discard, then that player draws a card (Oildeep Gearhulk)

- `SerumPowderEffect()` — mulligan-time marker in `EffectSlot.MULLIGAN_ACTION`; the player may exile their hand and draw the same number of cards before taking the normal mulligan action. Handled by `SerumPowderHandler`.
- `DiscardEffect.cardsDrawnThisResolution(N, recipient)` — chosen discard restricted to cards drawn earlier by the same stack entry (Krovikan Sorcerer)
- `RevealAnyNumberOfCardsFromHandEffect(CardPredicate filter)` — controller chooses any number of matching cards from their hand; the selected cards are publicly revealed and the count is stored on the resolving stack entry for a following `EventValue` effect. Uses `PendingInteraction.RevealAnyNumberOfCardsFromHandChoice`; Brine Seer chains it into `CounterUnlessPaysEffect(new EventValue())`
- `RevealCardFromHandThenPutOntoBattlefieldIfSharesNameWithPermanentEffect()` — controller reveals one chosen card from hand; if it shares a name with any permanent on any battlefield, it enters the battlefield, otherwise it remains in hand. Uses a mandatory `PendingInteraction.RetracedImageCardChoice`.
- `EachOtherPlayerDrawsCardEffect(DynamicAmount)` or `(int)` — every player other than the resolving spell or ability's controller draws that many; the amount is re-evaluated per drawing player. Words of Wisdom uses `(1)` after `DrawCardEffect(2)`
- `EachOtherPlayerMayDrawUpToNCardsEffect(int)` — each player other than the resolving controller independently chooses 0..`max` cards in APNAP order and draws that many. Indentured Djinn uses `(3)` on `ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD`.
- `DredgeEffect(int millCount)` — marker on a card in `GRAVEYARD_DRAW_REPLACEMENT`; while its owner's library has at least `millCount` cards, that player may replace a draw by milling exactly that many cards and returning the dredge card to hand. `DredgeSupport` owns the draw replacement and graveyard-choice completion.
- `EffectSlot.GRAVEYARD_ON_SELF_RETURNED_TO_HAND` — self-trigger for a card in its owner's graveyard that fires after that card is put into its owner's hand from the graveyard (Golgari Brownscale).
- `DrawCardUnlessTargetSacrificesCreatureOrPaysLifeEffect(int lifeCost)` — targets an opponent; that opponent chooses to sacrifice a creature, pay `lifeCost` life, or let the effect's controller draw a card. Options are pruned when the sacrifice or payment is impossible; a single remaining draw option resolves automatically. Indulgent Tormentor (M15)
- `PutControllerCardFromHandOnTopOfLibraryEffect()` — controller must choose one card from a nonempty hand and put it on top of their library; empty hand resolves with no prompt and reuses the top-only `PutCardsFromHandOnLibraryCardChoice` interaction
- `EachPlayerPutsCardFromHandOnTopOfLibraryEffect()` — each player chooses one card from their hand and puts it on top of their library in APNAP order; players with empty hands are skipped. Used by Sadistic Augermage (RAV)

- `DrawCardEffect(DynamicAmount)` or `(int)` — controller draws that many; use `XValue` for "draw X", `PermanentCount`/`CardsInGraveyard`/`CountersOnSource` for "draw a card for each …"
- `DrawCardsThenEffect(DynamicAmount amount, CardEffect thenEffect)` or `(int, CardEffect)` — controller draws the requested number, then the follow-up is put on the stack as a reflexive triggered ability only when that many cards were actually drawn. The follow-up's target is chosen after the draw, so the draw itself is non-targeting. The follow-up amount is evaluated when that reflexive ability resolves. The Royal Scions
- `DrawCardIfSacrificedCardMatchesEffect(CardPredicate[, DynamicAmount])` — draws only when the permanent sacrificed as the spell's additional cost matches the card predicate; reads the payment-time card snapshot. Splitting the Powerstone uses `CardSupertypePredicate(LEGENDARY)`.
- `DrawCardEffect(new EventValue())` on `ON_CONTROLLER_LOSES_LIFE` — controller draws the amount of life lost by that trigger event (Vilis, Broker of Blood); the trigger collector snapshots the amount onto the stack entry.
- `DrawCardRevealDiscardUnlessLandEffect()` — controller draws a card and reveals it; if it isn't a land card, it is discarded (not opponent-caused; fires discard triggers). Card-filtering: only lands kept (Sindbad, `{T}:`). `CardDrawingEffect` (drawnCardAmount = 1)
- `DrawCardPerChosenTypeCountEffect()` — "Choose a creature type. Draw a card for each permanent you control of that type" (Distant Melody). Resolution-time creature-type choice (`beginSpellCreatureTypeChoice`, stored on `GameData.chosenSpellSubtype`), Changeling-aware count; draw sibling of `DealDamageEqualToChosenTypeCountEffect`
- `DestroyAllCreaturesOfChosenTypeEffect()` — "Destroy all creatures of the creature type of your choice" (Extinction). Resolution-time creature-type choice (`beginSpellCreatureTypeChoice`, stored on `GameData.chosenSpellSubtype`), then a Changeling-aware wipe of that type across every battlefield; `BoardWipeEffect`. For a fixed type use `DestroyAllPermanentsEffect(PermanentHasSubtypePredicate(...))`
- `TargetPlayerDiscardsAndDestroysCreaturesOfChosenTypeEffect()` — "Choose a creature type. Target player reveals their hand and discards all creature cards of that type. Then destroy all creatures of that type that player controls. They can't be regenerated" (Tsabo's Decree). One resolution-time type choice; reveals and automatically discards matching creature cards, then performs a targeted Changeling-aware non-regenerable destruction batch
- `VoidEffect()` — "Choose a number. Destroy all artifacts and creatures with mana value equal to that number. Then target player reveals their hand and discards all nonland cards with mana value equal to the number." Resolution-time number choice; the wipe is global and the discard is targeted.
- `GainLifePerChosenTypeCountEffect(int lifePerPermanent)` — "Choose a creature type. You gain `lifePerPermanent` life for each permanent you control of that type" (Luminescent Rain = `(2)`). Resolution-time creature-type choice (`beginSpellCreatureTypeChoice`, stored on `GameData.chosenSpellSubtype`), Changeling-aware count; gains `count*lifePerPermanent` life. Life sibling of `DrawCardPerChosenTypeCountEffect`
- `GainLifePerPermanentOfChosenColorEffect()` — "Choose a color, then gain 1 life for each permanent of that color" (Treva, the Renewer). Resolution-time color choice (`ChoiceContext.GainLifePerPermanentOfChosenColorChoice`), counts effective-color permanents on every battlefield, then gains that many life.
- `BoostTargetCreaturePerChosenTypeCountEffect(int powerPer, int toughnessPer)` — "Choose a creature type. Target creature gets `powerPer`/`toughnessPer` until end of turn for each permanent of the chosen type you control" (Pack's Disdain = `(-1, -1)`). Resolution-time creature-type choice (`beginSpellCreatureTypeChoice`, stored on `GameData.chosenSpellSubtype`), Changeling-aware count; applies `count*powerPer`/`count*toughnessPer` as a until-end-of-turn P/T modifier. Boost sibling of `DealDamageEqualToChosenTypeCountEffect`; pair with a `PermanentIsCreaturePredicate` target filter
- `BoostAllCreaturesOfChosenSubtypeEffect(int powerBoost, int toughnessBoost)` — "Choose a creature type. All creatures of that type get +P/+T until end of turn". Resolution-time creature-type choice (`beginSpellCreatureTypeChoice`, stored on `GameData.chosenSpellSubtype`), then applies the one-shot modifier to every matching creature on every battlefield (Changeling-aware)
- `BoostTargetCreatureByTargetPlayerCountEffect(DynamicAmount powerBoost, DynamicAmount toughnessBoost[, boolean harmful[, int targetPlayerGroup]])` — applies dynamic P/T modifiers to a creature target while evaluating both amounts with the separately declared player target as `CountScope.TARGET_PLAYER`. Use for "target creature gets ... for each [permanent] that player controls" when the player and creature are different target groups; the default player group is `0`
- `PayXLifeDrawXCardsEffect()` — SPELL: resolution-time X choice — controller picks X (capped at current life), pays X life, draws X cards (Necrologia). Pair with `setSpellCastTimingRestriction(YOUR_END_STEP)` for "cast only during your end step"
- `EachPlayerDrawsCardEffect(DynamicAmount)` or `(int)` — each player (turn order) draws that many; the amount is re-evaluated per drawing player, so player-relative amounts (`CardsInGraveyard(..., CONTROLLER)` = each player's own graveyard, Nature's Resurgence) count that player's objects. `XValue()` for "each player draws X" (Prosperity), `int` for a fixed count
- `DrawCardForTargetPlayerEffect(DynamicAmount, boolean requiresUntapped, boolean targets)` or `(int)` — target/entry player draws; `XValue` for "target player draws X"; draws for every target when the entry is multi-target
- `EachTargetPlayerDrawsCardsEqualToAttachedCountEffect(PermanentPredicate)` — each selected player draws cards equal to the number of matching permanents attached to the enchanted player; use `PermanentHasSubtypePredicate(CURSE)` for Curse-counting upkeep abilities
- `TargetPermanentControllerDrawsCardEffect()` — the controller of the permanent targeted by this stack entry draws a card (permanent analogue of `TargetSpellControllerDrawsCardEffect`). Piggybacks on `entry.getTargetId()`, so pair it with a sibling targeting effect that owns the permanent target (e.g. `PutCounterOnTargetPermanentEffect`); it contributes no target of its own. Gwafa Hazid, Profiteer ("Put a bribery counter on target creature you don't control. Its controller draws a card.")
- `DyingCreatureControllerMayDrawCardEffect()` — ON_ANY_CREATURE_DIES marker: whenever any creature dies, the DYING creature's controller (may be an opponent of the source) may draw a card (Fecundity). Unlike a plain `MayEffect(DrawCardEffect())` on that slot, which offers the draw to the source's controller
- `EnchantedCreatureControllerMaySearchLibraryForCreatureToBattlefieldEffect()` — ON_ENCHANTED_PERMANENT_PUT_INTO_GRAVEYARD marker: when an enchanted creature dies, its controller may search their library for a creature card and put it onto the battlefield (Pattern of Rebirth). The collector queues the may ability under the dying creature's controller, not the Aura's controller
- `DyingCreatureControllerMaySearchLibraryForSameNameEffect()` — ON_ANY_CREATURE_DIES marker: the dying creature's controller may search their library for one card with that creature's name and put it onto the battlefield, then shuffle; the death collector binds the name and wraps the existing `SearchLibraryEffect` (Verdant Succession)
- `DefendingPlayerMayDrawCardEffect()` — ON_ATTACK marker: "whenever this creature attacks, defending player may draw a card" (Sibilant Spirit). `CombatAttackService` routes the optional draw to the defending player (or the attacked planeswalker's controller), not the attacking creature's controller. Unlike a plain `MayEffect(DrawCardEffect())`, which offers the draw to the source's controller
- `TargetOpponentMayDrawCardEffect()` — "Target opponent may draw a card" (Soldevi Heretic). Declares `TargetSpec.NONE` — the opponent is derived (two-player engine), so the entry's target slot stays free for a sibling targeting effect. Its handler queues a `MayEffect(DrawCardEffect())` for that opponent, so the "may" is theirs; a plain `MayEffect(DrawCardEffect())` would offer it to the source's controller
- `TargetPlayerMayExileCardFromGraveyardEffect()` — the targeted player may exile a card from their graveyard; if they decline, the source's controller is offered a draw. The trigger chooses the player first, then routes the may choice to that player and reuses `TargetPlayerExilesCardFromGraveyardEffect` for the accepted exile
- `TargetOpponentCreatesTokenEffect(CreateTokenEffect token)` — "Target opponent creates [token]" (Phelddagrif); the token is created under the opponent's control. Declares `TargetSpec.NONE` (opponent derived, target slot stays free). Deliberately not a `TokenCreatingEffect` — it is a drawback, not AI value
- `DyingCreatureControllerDiscardsCardEffect()` — ON_ANY_CREATURE_DIES marker: whenever a qualifying creature dies, the DYING creature's controller (may be an opponent) discards a card (mandatory). Bereavement wraps it in `TriggeringCardConditionalEffect(CardColorPredicate(GREEN), …)` for "a green creature dies"
- `DyingCreatureControllerSacrificesPermanentsEffect(int count, PermanentPredicate filter)` — ON_ANY_CREATURE_DIES marker: whenever a qualifying creature dies, the DYING creature's controller (may be an opponent) sacrifices `count` matching permanent(s) of their choice (mandatory). Earthlink uses `(1, new PermanentIsLandPredicate())`
- Loot ("draw N, then discard M") — _no dedicated effect_: compose `DrawCardEffect(N)` + `DiscardEffect(M, DiscardRecipient.CONTROLLER)` (flat pair on a spell/ability effect list — one shared entry, so the draw resolves before the discard). On a single-effect wrapper or trigger slot, bundle as `SequenceEffect.of(new DrawCardEffect(N), new DiscardEffect(M, DiscardRecipient.CONTROLLER))`, e.g. `MayEffect(SequenceEffect.of(...), prompt)` for "you may draw a card. If you do, discard a card." Once you've drawn, the discard is unconditional — unlike rummage below, whose draw is contingent on an actual discard
- Thirst for Knowledge ("draw three, then discard two unless you discard an artifact") — compose `DrawCardEffect(3)` + `DiscardEffect(2, DiscardRecipient.CONTROLLER, CardType.ARTIFACT)`. The discard flow permits either one artifact card or two cards of any types; after an artifact is discarded, the second pick is optional
- `DiscardAndDrawCardEffect(int discard, int draw)` — rummage
- `TargetPlayerRandomDiscardThenDrawEffect()` — target player discards one card at random and draws a card only if a card was discarded; its player target is the damaged player when used on `ON_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER`
- `TargetPlayerDiscardsUpToThenDrawsThatManyEffect(DynamicAmount maxDiscard)` — target player chooses 0 through the evaluated maximum, discards that many, then draws that many (Mishra's Command)
- `DiscardAnyNumberEffect()` — controller chooses zero through their hand size to discard; records the chosen count as the stack entry event value so a following effect can use "discarded this way" (Sacred Rites). `DiscardAnyNumberEffect(true)` lets the controller choose only the number, then discards that many cards at random (Rites of Initiation)
- `DiscardCardAndBoostSelfEffect(int power, int toughness)` — discard a card, then source gets +power/+toughness until end of turn. Wrap in `MayEffect` for "you may discard a card. If you do, this creature gets +X/+Y until end of turn" (Furyblade Vampire). Uses `DiscardFollowUp.boost`; empty hand → no boost
- `ExileCardAndUntapSelfEffect()` — exile a card from the controller's hand, then untap the source permanent. Wrap in `MayEffect` for "you may exile a card from your hand. If you do, untap [source]" (Forsaken City); the interactive exile choice performs the untap only after a card is actually exiled
- `DiscardCardThenEffect(CardPredicate, CardEffect, String)` — discard one matching hand card; if you do, push thenEffect as a reflexive trigger. Pack Guardian = `MayEffect(DiscardCardThenEffect(CardTypePredicate(LAND), CreateTokenEffect(Wolf 2/2), "a land card"))`. Uses `DiscardFollowUp.thenEffect`
- `LastDiscardedCardManaValue()` (`model/amount/`) — mana value of the most recently discarded card; cleared by `DiscardEffectHandler` before each discard, so a later effect on the same spell scales off the card just discarded. Blast of Genius = `DrawCardEffect(3)` + `DiscardEffect(1, CONTROLLER)` + `DealDamageToAnyTargetEffect(new LastDiscardedCardManaValue())`
- `WebSlingingReturnedCreatureManaValue()` (`model/amount/`) — mana value of the creature returned to its owner's hand to pay a spell's web-slinging cost; available while the resulting permanent enters the battlefield (Scarlet Spider, Ben Reilly)
- `LastMilledCardColorSymbols(ManaColor)` (`model/amount/`) — count of that color's mana symbols in the most recently milled card's mana cost. Generic and colorless symbols do not count; hybrid and Phyrexian symbols count for each color they contain. Charmed Pendant uses one `AwardManaEffect` per color after `MillControllerCost(1)`
- `DrawDiscardBoostSelfByDiscardedManaValueEffect()` — draw 1, discard 1, then source gets +X/+0 until end of turn where X = the discarded card's mana value (Spellbound Dragon, `ON_ATTACK`). A pending hook (`pendingBoostSourceByDiscardedManaValue`) applies the boost after the interactive discard. Compare `DrawDiscardTransformIfCreatureDiscardedEffect` (same draw-discard-then-conditional shape, transform instead of boost)
- `DrawDiscardAndConniveEffect()` — draw 1, discard 1, then put a +1/+1 counter on the source if the discarded card is nonland (Doc Ock's Henchmen, `ON_ATTACK`). `DrawDiscardAndConniveEffect(true)` applies the same connive sequence to its targeted creature. A pending hook applies the counter after the interactive discard.
- `TargetPlayerDrawsThenDiscardsUntapSelfIfCardTypeEffect(CardType)` — target player draws 1, then discards 1; if the discarded card matches the type, untap the source permanent. Uses a pending discard hook for the interactive choice (Lumengrid Augur)
- `DiscardEffect(DynamicAmount, DiscardRecipient, boolean random[, CardType stopAfterDiscardingType, boolean onlyIfSacrificed])` — the whole discard family; `recipient` routes who discards, `random` picks chosen vs random discard, and `onlyIfSacrificed=true` makes an `ON_DEATH` effect trigger only when its source was sacrificed. `DiscardEffect.sacrificeOnly(N)` is the target-player form for "when you sacrifice this, target player discards N cards." `(int, recipient, random)` / `(DynamicAmount, recipient)` / `(int, recipient)` convenience ctors (last two non-random). `CountersOnSource(CHARGE)` for per-charge-counter (Shrine of Limitless Power), `XValue()` for Mind Shatter (`TARGET_PLAYER`, random)
- `RevealHandAndRandomDiscardCardTypeEffect(CardType)` — target player reveals hand, discards one card of type at random (Rag Man, CREATURE); player-targeting spec
- `TempestEfreetAnteExchangeEffect(int lifeCost)` — ante punisher (`benign(PLAYER)`, pair with a "target opponent" `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter`): target opponent may pay `lifeCost` life; if not, they reveal a random card that goes to the ability controller's hand and the sacrificed source goes to the opponent's graveyard. Payable opponents get a may-ability prompt (accept/decline in `mayfx/TempestEfreetAnteExchangeHandler`), unpayable ones exchange at once. Single-game zone movements only — no runtime ownership change (Tempest Efreet)
- `TimmerianFiendsAnteExchangeEffect()` — ante punisher (`harmful(PERMANENT, PermanentIsArtifactPredicate)`, pair with an artifact `PermanentPredicateTargetFilter`): the targeted artifact's owner may ante the top card of their library; if not, the artifact goes to the ability controller's graveyard and the sacrificed source to that owner's graveyard. Prompted via may-ability (accept/decline in `mayfx/TimmerianFiendsAnteExchangeHandler`); an empty library exchanges at once. Single-game zone movements only — no runtime ownership change (Timmerian Fiends)
- `BronzeTabletAnteExchangeEffect(int lifeCost)` — ante trade (`harmful(PERMANENT)`, pair with a "nontoken permanent an opponent owns" `PermanentPredicateTargetFilter`): exiles both the source and the targeted permanent, then the permanent's owner may pay `lifeCost` life; if they do, the source goes from exile to its owner's graveyard; if not, both stay exiled (ante swap). Payable owners get a may-ability prompt (accept/decline in `mayfx/BronzeTabletAnteExchangeHandler`), unpayable ones swap at once. Single-game zone movements only — no runtime ownership change (Bronze Tablet)
- `RebirthAnteEffect()` — non-targeting; "Each player may ante the top card of their library. If a player does, that player's life total becomes 20." `normalfx` seeds one accept/decline `PendingMayAbility` per player (APNAP, active first; empty-library players skipped); `mayfx/RebirthAnteHandler` antes the accepting player's top card (removed from the game → exile, like the Tempest Efreet ante convention) and sets their life to 20 via `applySetLifeTotal`. Rebirth
- `AnteTopCardEffect()` — mandatory spell effect: `normalfx/AnteTopCardEffectHandler` removes the controller's top library card and adds it to the ante representation (exile plus `GameData.antedCardIds`); an empty library does nothing. Contract from Below
- `AmuletOfQuozAnteEffect()` — ante punisher (`harmful(PLAYER)`, pair with a "target opponent" `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter`): target opponent may ante their top library card; if they don't, the controller flips a coin — win means the opponent loses the game, loss means the controller does. `normalfx` prompts the opponent (empty library ⇒ flip at once, no prompt), `mayfx/AmuletOfQuozAnteHandler` antes on accept (card → exile) or flips on decline. Losses go through the `resolveLoss` gate (Amulet of Quoz)
- `RevealTargetHandEffect()` — target player reveals their whole hand (logged); nothing further happens. Pair with a companion targeted effect on the same player, e.g. `LoseLifeEffect(1, TARGET_PLAYER)` (Thoughtcutter Agent). `TargetSpec.benign(PLAYER)`
- `CopyInstantOrSorceryFromTargetHandEffect()` — target player reveals their hand, the controller may choose an instant or sorcery card, and a separate copy is created in exile for an optional free cast; the original hand card is untouched. Uses `TargetHandSpellCopyChoice` and the shared free-cast queue (Reversal of Fortune)
- `MayCastCopyWithoutPayingManaCostEffect()` — marker for the optional free cast of a copy created from a hand card; accepting uses the shared exiled-copy queue and declining removes the copy from exile (Reversal of Fortune)
- `RevealOwnHandThenDamagedPlayerLosesGameIfSixDifferentManaValuesEffect()` — `ON_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER` trigger: reveals the ability controller's hand, then the damaged player loses the game if at least six distinct mana values are present. Implements `CombatDamageTriggerContextEffect(DAMAGED_PLAYER)` so the damaged player is supplied by either damage trigger path; wrap in `MayEffect` for Atemsis, All-Seeing. Losses use the normal outcome gate. Handler `RevealOwnHandThenDamagedPlayerLosesGameIfSixDifferentManaValuesEffectHandler`
- `ExileTargetPlayerHandEffect([boolean trackWithSource])` — exile target player's whole hand (no choice, no play permission; not a discard, so no discard triggers). `TargetSpec.harmful(PLAYER)`. The no-arg form does not track the cards; `trackWithSource=true` associates them with the source permanent for later return effects (Hypnox). Pair the no-arg form on the same SPELL slot with `ExileGraveyardCardsEffect(TARGET_PLAYER_ENTIRE)` for "exile all cards from target player's hand and graveyard" (Identity Crisis); both PLAYER specs collapse to one target
- `ExileTargetPlayerHandFaceDownAndReturnAtNextTurnEndStepEffect()` — exile target player's whole hand face down, remember those card IDs, and put the cards still in exile into that player's hand at the beginning of their next turn's end step. Does not discard any cards currently in hand when the delayed trigger resolves (Suppress)
- `ExileAllHandsEffect()` — non-targeting all-players counterpart: exile every card in every player's hand. Pair on the same SPELL slot with `ExileGraveyardCardsEffect(ALL_PLAYERS)` for "exile all cards from all hands and graveyards" (Worldfire)
- `DiscardHandEffect(DiscardRecipient)` — discard entire hand(s); no-arg = controller. `TARGET_PLAYER` implements `CombatDamageTriggerContextEffect` (DAMAGED_PLAYER), so it works as an ON_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER / ON_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER trigger with the damaged player baked in (Nicol Bolas)
- `DiscardEffect(DynamicAmount, DiscardRecipient, boolean random)` — the whole discard family; `recipient` dz??? {`CONTROLLER`, `TARGET_PLAYER`, `EACH_PLAYER`, `EACH_OPPONENT`}, `random` picks chosen vs random discard. `(int, recipient, random)` / `(DynamicAmount, recipient)` / `(int, recipient)` convenience ctors (last two non-random). `CountersOnSource(CHARGE)` for per-charge-counter (Shrine of Limitless Power), `XValue()` for Mind Shatter (`TARGET_PLAYER`, random)
- `EachOpponentDiscardsOrControllerGainsLifeEffect(int lifeGain)` — each opponent discards one card in APNAP order; the controller gains `lifeGain` life for each opponent who cannot discard. Empty hands are counted through the shared discard queue. Cruel Grimnarch
- `EachOpponentDiscardsOrControllerDrawsEffect()` — each opponent discards one card in APNAP order; the controller draws one card for each opponent who cannot discard. Empty hands are counted through the shared discard queue. Aclazotz, Deepest Betrayal
- `EachPlayerMayDiscardOrLoseLifeEffect(int lifeLoss)` — each player, in APNAP order, may discard a card; each player who declines loses `lifeLoss` life. Players with empty hands lose life automatically; players with cards receive the shared may-choice prompt.
- `RevealHandAndRandomDiscardCardTypeEffect(CardType)` — target player reveals hand, discards one card of type at random (Rag Man, CREATURE); `canTargetPlayer`
- `DiscardOwnHandThenEachOpponentSacrificesCreaturePerCardEffect()` — discard controller's hand, then each opponent sacrifices a creature of their choice for each card discarded this way. Sacrifice count = cards discarded (snapshotted before the discard); empty hand ⇒ nothing. Delegates to the discard-hand + forced-sacrifice (EACH_OPPONENT) handlers. Malfegor (ETB)
- `DiscardHandEffect(DiscardRecipient)` — discard entire hand(s); no-arg = controller
- `EachPlayerDiscardsFractionOfHandRoundedUpEffect(int divisor)` — each player discards `ceil(handSize/divisor)` cards of their choice, recomputed per player (APNAP). Use (not `DiscardEffect(EACH_PLAYER)`, which applies one shared amount) when the count is a per-player fraction. Pox (3)
