- `EachPlayerSacrificesFractionRoundedUpEffect(int divisor, PermanentPredicate filter)` — each player sacrifices `ceil(matching/divisor)` of their matching permanents of their choice, recomputed per player (APNAP simultaneous, CR 101.4). Pox: `(3, new PermanentIsCreaturePredicate())` + `(3, new PermanentIsLandPredicate())`
- `EachPlayerSacrificesDownToFewestEffect(PermanentPredicate filter)` — each player keeps a number of their matching permanents equal to the fewest any player controls and sacrifices the rest, of their own choice (APNAP simultaneous, CR 101.4; min=0 → everyone sacrifices all, no prompt). Balance: `(new PermanentIsLandPredicate())` + `(new PermanentIsCreaturePredicate())`
- `EachPlayerExilesPermanentsOrCardsFromHandEffect(DynamicAmount amount[, boolean opponentsOnly])` — each affected player exiles `amount` objects chosen from their own battlefield **and/or** hand. Use `opponents(amount)` for opponent-only effects. Descent into Madness uses the all-player form; Nicol Bolas, Dragon-God uses the opponent-only form.
- `EachPlayerDiscardsDownToFewestEffect()` — each player discards down to the smallest hand size, of their own choice (APNAP). Balance's discard step
- `EachPlayerMaySearchLibraryForBasicLandsToBattlefieldEffect(DynamicAmount count, boolean enterTapped)` — each player, APNAP, may search their library for up to `count` basic land cards onto the battlefield tapped or untapped, then shuffle; each search is a "may" and players with no basic land are skipped (`BasicLandSearchQueueSupport`, carried on `LibrarySearchFollowUp.BasicLandSearchQueue`). New Frontiers, Natural Balance, Veteran Explorer
- `NaturalBalanceEffect()` — land counts are read once, up front: players with 6+ lands choose which lands to sacrifice down to 5, players with 4 or fewer may search their library for up to `5 - lands` basic land cards onto the battlefield (untapped), then shuffle. Both queues are APNAP-ordered and disjoint; the engine runs the searches first and the sacrifices after (`BasicLandSearchQueueSupport`, carried on `LibrarySearchFollowUp.BasicLandSearchQueue`). Natural Balance
- `DiscardHandUnlessPaysLifeEffect(int lifeCost)` — target player discards their entire hand unless they pay `lifeCost` life; target chooses (can't-pay → auto-discard). Pair with `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter`. Tyrannize (7)
- `RevealHandDiscardMatchingCardsUnlessPaysLifeEffect(CardPredicate cardFilter, int lifeCost)` — target player reveals their hand; for each revealed card matching `cardFilter`, that player discards it unless they pay `lifeCost` life. One independent prompt per matching card (offered sequentially); can't-pay → auto-discard. Pair with `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter`. Sirocco (blue instant cards, 4)
- `DiscardOwnHandThenDrawThatManyEffect()` — discard entire hand, then draw that many
- `TargetPlayerDiscardsHandThenDrawsThatManyEffect()` — target player discards entire hand, then draws that many; player-targeting spec. Also a `CombatDamageTriggerContextEffect` (DAMAGED_PLAYER), so it works as an ON_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER trigger (Shocker). Collective Defiance
- `DefendingPlayerDiscardsHandThenDrawsThatManyEffect()` — the player being attacked (or the controller of the attacked planeswalker) discards their entire hand, then draws that many; reads the combat trigger's `attackedTargetId`. Robber Fly
- `TargetPlayerDiscardsThenDrawsThatManyEffect(N)` — target player discards N cards, then draws as many as they discarded (draw = `min(N, hand size)`); player-targeting spec. Forget
- `TargetPlayerDiscardsThenGainLifeForEachCardTypeEffect(int amount, CardType cardType, int lifePerCard)` — target player discards up to the fixed amount; after the chosen discard interaction, the controller gains `lifePerCard` for each discarded card matching `cardType` (Gerrard's Verdict)
- `TargetPlayerDrawsCardsEqualToHandThenDiscardsThatManyEffect()` — target player draws cards equal to their starting hand size, then chooses and discards that many cards; the count is snapshotted before drawing. Laquatus's Creativity
- `DiscardThenReturnFromGraveyardToHandEffect(DynamicAmount)` — controller discards `amount` cards, then returns a card from their graveyard to hand for each card discarded this way (returns `min(amount, hand size)`, chosen one at a time). Recall = `XValue()`; pair with a trailing `ExileSpellEffect()`
- `DiscardOwnHandThenDrawEqualToTargetPlayerHandSizeEffect()` — discard entire hand, then draw equal to target player's hand size (counted at draw time)
- `DiscardUnlessReturnLandToHandEffect()` — "discard a card unless you return a land you control to its owner's hand." May-ability escape: accept returns a chosen land (shared `BounceCreature` context, to owner's hand), decline discards a card; no lands ⇒ mandatory discard. Return-a-land sibling of `DiscardUnlessExileCardFromGraveyardEffect`. Pair with a leading `DrawCardEffect(N)` (Tragic Lesson = `DrawCardEffect(2)` + this)
- `DiscardOwnHandThenDrawEffect(DynamicAmount)` — discard entire hand, then draw equal to a DynamicAmount (evaluated at draw time; independent of discard count). Knollspine Dragon = `DamageDealtToTargetPlayerThisTurn`
- `ActivePlayerDiscardsHandThenDrawsEffect(int)` — the active player discards their entire hand, then draws the fixed number of cards; retains a player target for a separate upkeep target restriction. Oath of Scholars
- `ActivePlayerReturnsCardFromGraveyardToHandEffect(CardPredicate)` — the active player chooses a matching card from their graveyard at resolution and returns it to hand; retains a player target for a separate upkeep target restriction. Oath of Ghouls
- `ActivePlayerPutsCardFromHandOntoBattlefieldEffect(CardPredicate, String)` — the active player chooses a matching card from their hand and puts it onto the battlefield under their control; used with `MayChoicePlayer.ACTIVE_PLAYER` for Braids, Conjurer Adept (`PLC`)
- `EachPlayerDiscardsHandThenDrawsThatManyEffect()` — each player (APNAP) discards their entire hand, then draws that many
- `EachPlayerDiscardsHandThenDrawsGreatestDiscardedEffect()` — each player discards their hand, then all draw equal to the greatest number discarded (Windfall)
- `EachPlayerDiscardsAnyNumberThenDrawsThatManyEffect()` — each player (APNAP) discards any number of cards (their choice), then draws that many (Flux)
- `EachPlayerMayDiscardUpToThenTakeDamageEffect(int amount)` — each player (APNAP) may discard up to `amount` cards (their choice, capped at hand size), then is dealt `amount - discarded` damage; empty/zero-discard players take the full `amount` (Mind Bomb, `amount` = 3)
- `DestroyTokensCreatedWithSourceEffect(boolean cannotBeRegenerated)` — destroy every surviving token created with the source permanent (tracked in `GameData.sourceCreatedTokens`); END_STEP_TRIGGERED / ON_SELF_LEAVES_BATTLEFIELD (Tombstone Stairwell)
- `EachPlayerCreatesTokenEffect(CreateTokenEffect token)` / `(token, boolean recordAsCreatedWithSource)` — each player (turn order) creates the wrapped `token` under their own control; the token's dynamic amount is re-evaluated per creating player, so `CountScope.CONTROLLER` reads each player's own board (Waiting in the Weeds)
- `EachPlayerPaysAnyLifeForTokensEffect(CreateTokenEffect token)` — starting with controller, each player may pay any amount of life, round-robin until a full round of no payments; each creates one `token` per life paid (Plague of Vermin)
- `EachPlayerPaysAnyManaForTokensEffect(CreateTokenEffect token)` — each player may pay any amount of mana (single pass, APNAP order, mana-payment `XValueChoice` capped by potential mana); each then creates one `token` per mana paid. Players with no mana are skipped. Liege of the Hollows (`ON_DEATH`)
- `ExileTopCardsMayPlayUntilNextTurnEffect(DynamicAmount count)` or `(int count)` — exile top N from library, may play until end of your next turn (owner-relative expiry via `ExileSupport.grantPlayUntilOwnersNextTurn`). Use `EventValue()` for "equal to the excess damage dealt this way" (Archaic's Agony)
- `ExileTopCardsMayPlayUntilNextEndStepEffect(int count)` — exile top N from the controller's library and grant play permission until that player's next end step (including the current turn's end step when it has not begun)
- `ExileTopCardsForEachDyingCreatureCounterMayPlayUntilNextEndStepEffect()` — ally-creature-death trigger marker; the trigger collector snapshots the dying creature's total counters and resolves the preceding effect with that count (Shadow Urchin)
- `ExileTopCardsChooseOneMayPlayUntilNextTurnEffect(DynamicAmount count)` or `(int count)` — exile top N from library, then choose one exiled card to play until end of your next turn. Use `EventValue()` for a count supplied by a delayed death trigger (End-Blaze Epiphany); only the chosen card receives play permission.
- `ExileTopCardMayPlayUntilNextUpkeepEffect()` — exile the top card of **your** library; may play it (any type) until the beginning of your next upkeep, then it just loses play permission and stays in exile (Elkin Bottle). Grants `exilePlayPermissions` + queues a `RevokeExilePlayPermissionAtNextUpkeep` delayed action (drained in `StepTriggerService.handleUpkeepTriggers` when the controller is active). Unlike `ExileTopCardsMayPlayUntilNextTurnEffect` the window ends one turn earlier (upkeep, not turn-end); unlike Grinning Totem the card is never moved to a graveyard
- `ExileTopCardsFaceDownMayPlayUntilNextUpkeepEffect(int count)` — exile top N of **your** library **face down**; may look at / play them until the beginning of your next upkeep; unplayed cards go to owner's graveyard then (Three Wishes). Grants `exilePlayPermissions` + queues `ExileToOwnerGraveyardAtNextUpkeep` per card (same cleanup as Grinning Totem / `expiresAtNextUpkeep`)
- `PutTopCardOfGraveyardOnBottomOfLibraryEffect()` — put the top (most recently added) card of **your** graveyard on the bottom of your library; no-op if the graveyard is empty (Soldevi Digger, `{2}`)
- `PutTopCreatureFromTargetGraveyardOnLibraryTopEffect()` — if the top (most recently added) card of **target player's** graveyard is a creature, put it on top of that player's library; no-op if empty or non-creature (Guiding Spirit, `{T}`; benign PLAYER target)
- `ExileTopCardOfOpponentLibraryControllerMayPlayThisTurnEffect()` — target opponent exiles the top card of their library; the source's **controller** may play that card (lands and spells, normal costs/timing) until **end of turn** (Knacksaw Clique). Card owned by the opponent; grants `exilePlayPermissions` to the controller + `exilePlayPermissionsExpireEndOfTurn`. Two-player: single opponent derived
- `ExileTopCardOfDamagedPlayerLibraryGainLifeAndMayCastThisTurnEffect()` — the damaged player exiles the top card of their library; the source's controller gains life equal to its mana value and may cast it until end of turn, spending mana of any type. Lands are exiled without a cast permission (Daxos of Meletis)
- `ExileTopUntilNonlandOfTargetOpponentMayCastThisTurnEffect()` — target opponent digs until a nonland; controller may **cast** that card without paying until end of turn (Nicol Bolas, God-Pharaoh +2). Lands stay exiled with no permission; free cast via `exilePlayWithoutPayingManaCost` + EOT expiry
- `EachOpponentExilesTopUntilTotalManaValueMayCastThisTurnEffect(int totalManaValueThreshold)` — each opponent exiles from the top of their library until those cards have total mana value at least the threshold; the controller may cast the exiled nonland cards without paying their mana costs until end of turn (Dream Harvest). Lands remain exiled without permission
- `ExileTopUntilNonlandMayCastOrPutIntoHandEffect()` / `(DynamicAmount maxManaValue)` — exiles from your library until a nonland; if its mana value is at most the evaluated limit, you may cast it for free or put it into your hand, otherwise it goes directly to hand. The no-arg form uses life gained this turn. Intermediate lands remain exiled
- `EachOpponentExilesFromHandEffect(N)` — each opponent exiles N cards from hand (their choice), non-targeting, APNAP (Nicol Bolas, God-Pharaoh +1). Fewer than N ⇒ whole hand. Reuses `EXILE_FROM_HAND_CHOICE` with a remaining-choosers queue
- `ExileTopCardMayPlayThisTurnEffect(boolean withoutPayingManaCost)` / `(int count, boolean withoutPayingManaCost)` — exile the top card (or top `count` cards, Act on Impulse = 3) of **your** library; may play them (any type, lands included) until **end of turn**. `false` = normal costs/timing; `true` = cast it without paying its mana cost. Grants `exilePlayPermissions` + `exilePlayPermissionsExpireEndOfTurn` (+ `exilePlayWithoutPayingManaCost` for the free variant, consumed in `SpellCastingService.playCardFromExile` and cleared at cleanup). Oracle's Vault (`{2},{T}` = `false` + `PutCountersOnSelfEffect(BRICK)`; `{T}` = `true`, gated on 3 brick counters). Kept separate from `ExileTopCardsMayCastMatchingThisTurnEffect` for its unfiltered play wording and existing single-purpose callers
- `ExileTopCardMayPlayThisTurnWhenInstantOrSorceryDealsDamageToPlayerEffect()` — trigger-only marker for Syr Carah's "whenever an instant or sorcery spell you control deals damage to a player" ability. The `ON_ALLY_INSTANT_OR_SORCERY_DEALS_DAMAGE` collector queues one trigger for each player with positive damage in the spell's damage batch; its normal-effect handler delegates the actual exile and end-of-turn play permission to `ExileTopCardMayPlayThisTurnEffect(false)`
- `ExileRandomCardFromHandMayPlayThisTurnEffect()` — target/active player exiles a **random** hand card; may play it this turn (EOT permission); unplayed → owner's graveyard at the next end step via `ExileToOwnerGraveyardAtNextEndStep` (Elkin Lair, `EACH_UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`)
- `ExileRandomInstantOrSorceryFromTargetHandMayCastFreeEffect()` — target player reveals one random hand card; if it is an instant or sorcery, exile it and let the effect controller cast it for free until the chronological next end step, returning it to its owner's hand if uncast (Planeswalker's Mischief)
- `ExileRandomCardFromGraveyardMayPlayThisTurnEffect()` — controller exiles a **random** card from their graveyard; may play it this turn (EOT permission). Empty graveyard = no-op (Tersa Lightshatter)
- `ExileTopCardsMayCastMatchingThisTurnEffect(int count, CardPredicate filter[, boolean withoutPayingManaCost])` — exile top N; grant end-of-turn cast permission only for cards matching `filter` (Chandra, Dressed to Kill: count 1/5 + `CardColorPredicate(RED)`; Vance's Blasting Cannons: `(1, new CardNotPredicate(new CardTypePredicate(LAND)))` = "if it's a nonland card, you may cast that card this turn"). The optional flag also grants free casting (Narset, Enlightened Master). A `null` filter matches everything
- `DealDamageEqualToManaSpentToCastToAnyTargetEffect(CardPredicate)` — whenever you cast a matching spell, deal damage equal to mana spent to cast it to any target (`ON_CONTROLLER_CASTS_SPELL` or emblem staticEffects; Chandra Dressed to Kill emblem)
- `CreateEmblemEffect(List<CardEffect> staticEffects, String reminderText[, EmblemRecipient recipient])` — **the** effect for every "you get an emblem with …" ultimate. Declare the emblem's contents on the card: the handler stores `staticEffects` verbatim on the `Emblem` and logs `<player> gets an emblem with "<reminderText>".` (pass the text unquoted). `recipient` defaults to `CONTROLLER`; `TARGET_PLAYER` gives the emblem to the targeted player and makes the effect declare a benign `PLAYER` target — pair with `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(OPPONENT)` (Garruk, Apex Predator −8); `EACH_PLAYER_DEALT_DAMAGE_THIS_WAY` gives one emblem per player this same stack entry actually damaged (Chandra, Roaring Flame −7) — list the damage effect first in the ability. Emblems are never removed
- `EmblemStepTriggerEffect(EmblemTriggerStep step, List<CardEffect> effects, String reminderText)` — marker payload for an emblem reading "At the beginning of your upkeep/draw step/end step, …" or "At the beginning of each opponent's upkeep, …". Never resolved directly: `StepTriggerService` scans all emblems at that step, applies the controller/opponent relation, and pushes `effects` as a triggered ability of the emblem; effects that declare a permanent target use trigger-time target selection (Chandra, Roaring Flame's emblem: UPKEEP + `DealDamageToPlayersEffect(3, CONTROLLER)`; Sarkhan, the Dragonspeaker's emblem: DRAW_STEP + `DrawCardEffect(2)`; Ajani, Adversary of Tyrants': END_STEP + `CreateTokenEffect(3, Cat 1/1 white lifelink)`; Sorin, Solemn Visitor: OPPONENT_UPKEEP + active-player sacrifice)
- `DrawCardOnAllyLandEntersEffect` (capability interface; use `.Marker()`) — emblem marker for "Whenever a land you control enters, you may draw a card." `TriggerCollectionService` queues a resolution-time `MayEffect(DrawCardEffect(1))` for the emblem controller when their land enters
- `DealDamageToAnyTargetOnAllyLandEntersEffect(CardSubtype landSubtype, int damage)` — emblem marker for "Whenever a [subtype] you control enters, this emblem deals N damage to any target." `TriggerCollectionService` queues the existing any-target damage choice for each trigger
- `ExileTargetSpellUntilSourceLeavesEffect()` — ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD "exile target spell" that records the exiling permanent on the exiled card (Spell Queller). Not countering, so "can't be countered" does not stop it; put the mana-value limit on the card's `StackEntryPredicateTargetFilter`. Pair with `MayCastCardsExiledWithSourceEffect()`
- `MayCastCardsExiledWithSourceEffect()` — ON_SELF_LEAVES_BATTLEFIELD "the exiled card's owner may cast that card without paying its mana cost" (Spell Queller). The collector bakes the leaving permanent's id in; each card exiled with that source is offered to its **owner** via `MayPlayExiledCardWithoutPayingManaCostEffect`. Declining leaves it exiled
- `MayCastCardExiledWithSourceEffect([DynamicAmount manaValue])` — activated ability: the **controller** may cast **one** spell exiled with the source without paying its mana cost (Shell of the Last Kappa). The optional amount requires exact mana-value equality (Kaho, Minamo Historian). Offers are exclusive — accepting one withdraws the rest. Pair with `ExileTargetSpellUntilSourceLeavesEffect`; works after `SacrificeSelfCost`
- `ExileTriggeringSpellWithDreamCounterEffect()` — spell-cast trigger marker: when the triggering hand-cast instant or sorcery resolves successfully, exile it with one dream counter; countered, fizzled, or explicitly self-exiled spells do not receive one (Goliath Daydreamer)
- `ExileTriggeringSpellAndReturnToHandAtNextEndStepEffect()` — spell-cast trigger marker: when the triggering instant or sorcery resolves successfully, exile it instead of putting it into its owner's graveyard and return it at the next end step; countered, fizzled, explicitly self-exiled, or non-owned spells are not returned (Feather, the Redeemed)
- `MayCastExiledCardsWithDreamCounterEffect()` — `ON_ATTACK`: offer the controller one of their exiled spells with a dream counter to cast without paying its mana cost; offers are exclusive and resolve through `MayPlayExiledCardWithoutPayingManaCostEffect` (Goliath Daydreamer)
- `PossibilityStormCastTriggerEffect()` — ON_ANY_PLAYER_CASTS_SPELL: cast-from-hand spells are exiled, the caster digs until a shared card type, may cast it free, then all cards exiled with the enchantment go on the bottom in a random order (Possibility Storm)
- `EyeOfTheStormCastTriggerEffect()` + `EyeOfTheStormExileAndCopyEffect(UUID originalSpellCardId, UUID sourcePermanentId, UUID castingPlayerId)` — ON_ANY_PLAYER_CASTS_SPELL for instant or sorcery cards; exile the spell tracked by the source, copy every instant or sorcery card exiled with that source, then let the caster choose any subset and order of the generated copies for free casting. Copies do not retrigger the ability (Eye of the Storm)
- `MayCastExiledCardThenBottomRestEffect(UUID sourcePermanentId)` — internal may-ability marker: free-cast the offered exiled card, then bottom the rest of what that permanent exiled in a random order
- `ExileTargetSpellWithDelayCountersEffect()` — "target spell's controller exiles it with X delay counters on it" (Ertai's Meddling). Not countering, so "can't be countered" does not stop it. Tracked in `GameData.delayedSpellExiles`
- `RemoveDelayCounterFromExiledSpellEffect(UUID cardId)` — the paired upkeep trigger; on the last delay counter the card leaves exile and is put back onto the stack (not cast) with its original X and targets
- `ExileTargetPermanentMayPlayUntilNextTurnEffect()` — exile the target permanent, its owner may play it until end of their next turn (e.g. Suspend Aggression; pair with a permanent target filter). Tokens exiled this way cease to exist
- `ExileTargetPermanentMayPlayWithOpponentTaxEffect()` — exile target nonland permanent; its owner may play it for as long as it remains exiled, and spells cast by an opponent of the effect controller this way cost {2} more (Soul Partition)
- `ExileUpToOneCardFromGraveyardEffect()` — "you may exile target card from a graveyard" as a non-targeting, resolution-time, cross-graveyard "up to one" select (choose zero = the optional "you may"). Rides any resolution path — `ON_DEATH` and a folded cycling ability `[this, DrawCardEffect(1)]` (Grixis Sojourners). Completed in `GraveyardChoiceHandlerService.handleMultipleCardsChosen` via `graveyardTargetOperation.resolutionTimeExileResume`: exiles the chosen card, then resumes remaining effects (e.g. the cycling draw). Graveyard analogue of `ExileUpToNAttackingCreaturesEffect`
- `ExileCardsFromGraveyardEffect(int maxTargets, int lifeGain[, boolean lifeGainPerExiledCard, CardPredicate filter, boolean assignNoCombatDamage])` — exile up to N target graveyard cards (chosen as the spell/ability goes on the stack; zero = the "you may") and gain life. `lifeGainPerExiledCard` makes `lifeGain` per card actually exiled, `filter` restricts which cards are choosable, `assignNoCombatDamage` adds the entry's `sourcePermanentId` to `creaturesPreventedFromDealingCombatDamage` when at least one card was exiled. Its conditional-rider overload `(maxTargets, conditionalFilter, opponentLifeLoss, controllerLifeGain, singleGraveyard[, conditionalLifePerMatchingCard])` applies life changes when matching cards were exiled; the final flag scales them once per matching card. Rooftop Percher `(2, 3)`, Scarab Feast `(3, 0)`, Rysorian Badger `(2, 1, true, CardTypePredicate(CREATURE), true)` on `ON_ATTACKS_UNBLOCKED`, Skullsnatcher `(2, 0)` on `ON_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER` (damaged player's graveyard only). Implements `GraveyardCardChoosingEffect`
- `ExileCardsFromGraveyardEffect(int maxTargets, int lifeGain[, boolean lifeGainPerExiledCard, CardPredicate filter, boolean assignNoCombatDamage][, boolean singleGraveyard, boolean trackWithSource])` — exile up to N target graveyard cards (chosen as the spell/ability goes on the stack; zero = the "you may") and gain life. `lifeGainPerExiledCard` makes `lifeGain` per card actually exiled, `filter` restricts which cards are choosable, `assignNoCombatDamage` adds the entry's `sourcePermanentId` to `creaturesPreventedFromDealingCombatDamage` when at least one card was exiled, `singleGraveyard` restricts the selection to one graveyard, and `trackWithSource` records each exiled card with the source permanent for a later return effect. Rooftop Percher `(2, 3)`, Scarab Feast `(3, 0)`, Rysorian Badger `(2, 1, true, CardTypePredicate(CREATURE), true)` on `ON_ATTACKS_UNBLOCKED`, Skullsnatcher `(2, 0)` on `ON_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER`, and Gravegouger `(2, true, true)` on `ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD`. Implements `GraveyardCardChoosingEffect`
- `ReturnAllCardsExiledWithSourceToOwnerGraveyardEffect()` — returns every card in `GameData.exiledCards` tracked by the source permanent to its owner's graveyard. Pair with a self-leaves-the-battlefield trigger when the oracle says to return cards exiled with the source (Gravegouger)
- `ExileTargetCardFromGraveyardMayPlayUntilNextTurnEffect(CardPredicate filter, boolean ownGraveyardOnly)` — exile a targeted graveyard card matching the filter, controller may play it until end of their next turn (e.g. Practiced Scrollsmith; ETB graveyard-target flow via `MultiGraveyardChoice`)
- `ExileTargetInstantOrSorceryFromOpponentGraveyardMayCastEffect()` — exile a targeted instant/sorcery from an opponent's graveyard; controller may cast it **this turn**, spending mana of any type, and it is exiled instead of going to a graveyard (Nita, Forum Conciliator). Uses `exilePlayPermissions` + `exilePlayPermissionsExpireEndOfTurn` + `exilePlayAnyManaType` + `exileInsteadOfGraveyard`. Targets graveyard (per-instance spec: any graveyard unless `ownGraveyardOnly`)
- `ExileNonlandCardFromTargetHandOrGraveyardEffect(boolean grantPlayPermission)` — target opponent reveals their hand; the controller chooses one nonland card from that hand or that player's graveyard and exiles it. When `grantPlayPermission` is true, the controller may cast it for as long as it remains exiled, spending mana of any type. Uses a mixed hand/graveyard card-ID choice; Psychic Intrusion uses the default `true`, while Never Happened uses `false`
- `ExileGraveyardInstantsOrSorceriesAndCastCopiesEffect(boolean mayCastCopies)` — exile **every** targeted instant/sorcery card from its graveyard, copy each, and either cast each copy without paying its mana cost or offer each copy as a may-cast choice. Leave the effect **unbound** (plain `addEffect`) so it reads the whole target list, and declare one `target(new GraveyardCardPredicateTargetFilter(instantOrSorcery, scope))` group per graveyard. Copies are queued through `ExileFreeCastQueueSupport.queueCopiesForFreeCast` or the may-ability pipeline, so a copy that needs a target pauses for the choice and the rest are still cast; each copy ceases to exist on resolution. The no-argument constructor retains Spelltwine's automatic-cast behavior. Pair with `ExileSpellEffect()` for the "Exile Spelltwine" rider
- `ExileRandomCardsFromGraveyardAndChooseCopyEffect(int count, CardPredicate filter)` — exile up to `count` cards at random from the controller's graveyard, then choose one matching exiled card, copy it, and offer the copy as a free cast (Capricious Hellraiser)
- `ExileTargetCardFromGraveyardAndMayCastCopyEffect(CardPredicate filter, GraveyardSearchScope scope[, int lifeLossOnCast, boolean withoutPayingManaCost])` — exile one targeted matching graveyard card, create a copy in exile, and offer that copy for casting during the resolving ability. The original remains exiled; declining or resolving the copy removes the copy from exile. The two-argument form casts for free; the life-loss form pays the copy's normal cost
- `SpendAnyManaTypeToCastEffect(Set<CardType> spellTypes)` — `EffectSlot.STATIC` marker: "You can spend mana of any type to cast [types] spells." Colored requirements of a matching spell (hand or library top) may be paid with mana of any type (Vizier of the Menagerie, CREATURE). Read via the `AnyManaTypeCastEffect` capability interface in `CastingPermissionService.canSpendAnyManaTypeToCast`
- `GrantTargetGraveyardCardCastEffect(CardPredicate filter, GraveyardSearchScope scope, boolean exileInsteadOfGraveyard)` — "you may cast target [filter] card from [scope] graveyard" for its **normal cost** (Toshiro Umezawa: instant, `CONTROLLERS_GRAVEYARD`, `true`). On resolution it records a one-shot `GameData.GraveyardCardCastPermission` against that card, so the player casts it through the normal graveyard-cast path (`SpellCastingService`) rather than during resolution; the permission is cleared at cleanup. `exileInsteadOfGraveyard` stamps `StackEntry.exileInsteadOfGraveyard` on the resulting spell. Same permission map as Havengul Lich's `GrantTargetCreatureCardGraveyardCastAndCopyActivatedAbilitiesEffect`
- `PlayTargetCardFromGraveyardWithoutPayingManaCostEffect(CardPredicate filter)` — "you may play target [filter] card from your **own** graveyard without paying its mana cost" (Horde of Notions). On resolution offers a may-play: land → battlefield, else cast for free. Targets the controller's own graveyard (kept `@ValidatesTarget` validator enforces own-only); routed by `MayCastHandlerService.handlePlayFromGraveyardChoice`
- `PlayImprintedCardWithoutPayingManaCostEffect()` — Hideaway "you may play the exiled card without paying its mana cost" activated ability (Howltooth Hollow). Offers a may-play of the source permanent's imprinted (face-down exiled) card: land → battlefield (counts as the land play for the turn), else cast from exile for free. Routed by `MayCastHandlerService.handlePlayImprintedCardChoice`. Gate with `ConditionalEffect(<play condition>, …)` (e.g. `NoPlayerHasCardsInHand`); pair with `ImprintFromTopCardsEffect(N)` on ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD
- `ChooseCardsFromTargetHandEffect(int|DynamicAmount count, List<CardType> excludedTypes[, List<CardType> includedTypes], HandChoiceDestination destination[, boolean returnOnSourceLeave])` — reveal target's hand, caster chooses N card(s) → `DISCARD` / `EXILE` / `TOP_OF_LIBRARY` (Duress, Kitesail Freebooter, Agonizing Memories). `count` accepts an `XValue()` for "choose X cards" (Mind Warp). Overload `(int count, List<CardType> excludedTypes, CardPredicate filter, HandChoiceDestination destination)` further narrows choosable cards by a `CardPredicate` — e.g. "nonlegendary" via `CardNotPredicate(CardSupertypePredicate(LEGENDARY))` (Lay Bare the Heart, on top of `excludedTypes=[LAND]`). Overload `(int count, List<CardType> excludedTypes, HandChoiceDestination destination, int declineFallbackDiscardCount)` makes the pick a "you may" one and falls back to the target discarding N cards of their own choice when declined or unchoosable (Nightsnare, 2)
- `ChooseCardsFromTargetHandEffect(int|DynamicAmount count, List<CardType> excludedTypes[, List<CardType> includedTypes], HandChoiceDestination destination[, boolean returnOnSourceLeave])` — reveal target's hand, caster chooses N card(s) → `DISCARD` / `EXILE` / `TOP_OF_LIBRARY` (Duress, Kitesail Freebooter, Agonizing Memories). `count` accepts an `XValue()` for "choose X cards" (Mind Warp). Overload `(int count, List<CardType> excludedTypes, CardPredicate filter, HandChoiceDestination destination)` further narrows choosable cards by a `CardPredicate` — e.g. "nonlegendary" via `CardNotPredicate(CardSupertypePredicate(LEGENDARY))` (Lay Bare the Heart, on top of `excludedTypes=[LAND]`). Full ctor `(DynamicAmount count, excludedTypes, includedTypes, destination, returnOnSourceLeave, filter, boolean upTo, boolean exileAllCopiesOfChosenNames)` adds `upTo` (each pick declinable — "up to X cards") and `exileAllCopiesOfChosenNames` (EXILE only: also exile every card sharing a chosen name from the target's hand, graveyard, and library, then shuffle) — Reap Intellect. With `upTo`, the `filter` is re-applied to every follow-up pick
- `ChooseTwoFilteredCardsFromTargetHandToDiscardEffect(CardPredicate first, CardPredicate second)` — reveal hand; choose one matching each predicate (skip empty bands); discard those (Distended Mindbender). Second pick via `RevealedHandChoice.followUpFilter`
- `ChooseCardFromTargetHandToBattlefieldEffect(CardPredicate predicate, String label, boolean grantHaste, boolean sacrificeAtEndStep)` — reveal a target player's hand, then optionally put one matching card onto the battlefield under the effect controller's control; the flags grant haste and schedule sacrifice at the next end step (Treacherous Urge)
- `RevealCardsChooseOneToDiscardEffect(PermanentPredicate countFilter)` — target reveals X cards **of their choice** (X = number of the caster's permanents matching `countFilter`), then the caster picks one for the target to discard (Thieving Sprite, `PermanentHasAnySubtypePredicate(FAERIE)`). Unlike `ChooseCardsFromTargetHandEffect` the rest of the hand stays hidden; uses shared `RevealCardsDiscardChoice` (same path as Blackmail), phase 1 skipped when the hand is already ≤ X
- The same `RevealCardsChooseOneToDiscardEffect` also accepts a `DynamicAmount` directly for mana-symbol and other non-permanent counts (Disciple of Phenax uses `ColorManaSymbolsAmongControlledPermanents(BLACK)`).
- `PayAnyAmountOfLifeRevealAndExileEffect()` — "Pay any amount of life. Target opponent reveals that many cards from their hand. You choose one of them and exile it." (Vizkopa Confessor). Prompts the controller for 0..their life via `XValueChoice`, pays it, then runs the shared `RevealCardsDiscardChoice` flow with an `EXILE` destination; paying 0 reveals and exiles nothing. Targets an opponent — pair with `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(OPPONENT)`
- `PayXLifeEffect()` — controller chooses and pays any amount of life during resolution, recording the amount on the stack entry's event value for a following effect. The choice is capped at the controller's current life total. Plunge into Darkness uses it before `LookAtTopCardsEffect.chooseOneToHandRestToExile(EventValue)`.
- `TargetRevealsCardsControllerChoosesDiscardEffect(int revealCount[, int discardCount])` — target player reveals `revealCount` cards **of their choice** from hand (whole hand if fewer); the controller sees only those and picks `discardCount` of them (default 1; fewer if the hand held fewer) for the target to discard (Blackmail = reveal 3/discard 1; Noggin Whack = reveal 3/discard 2). Two-stage `RevealCardsDiscardChoice` interaction, discard picks looped one at a time; player-targeting spec. Contrast `ChooseCardsFromTargetHandEffect` (whole hand revealed, controller chooses)
- `RevealTargetHandDrawPerMatchingCardEffect(List<CardSubtype> subtypes, List<CardColor> colors)` — target opponent reveals hand; draw one card per card matching any subtype/color (counted once). Pair with `target(PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(OPPONENT))` (Baleful Stare)
- `ChoosePositiveNumberEffect()` — controller chooses a positive integer during resolution and stores it on the source permanent; uses the open-ended `XValueChoice` interaction
- `ChooseAnyNumberEffect()` — controller chooses a nonnegative integer during resolution and stores it on the source permanent; uses the open-ended `XValueChoice` interaction
- `TargetPlayerChoosesNumberEffect()` — target player chooses a nonnegative integer during resolution and stores it on the resolving stack entry's event value for following effects; targets a player
- `RevealTargetHandDrawIfExactlyChosenNumberOfChosenColorEffect()` — target opponent reveals hand; draw one card if exactly the source's chosen number of cards have the source's chosen color. Pair with `target(PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(OPPONENT))` (Scrying Glass)
- `RevealHandChooseCreatureGainLifeDiscardEffect(List<CardColor> colors)` — target opponent reveals hand; caster chooses one creature card whose colors include any of `colors` (empty = any color), gains life equal to its toughness, then the target discards it. Pair with `target(PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(OPPONENT))`; player-targeting spec (Talara's Bane = `List.of(GREEN, WHITE)`)
- `DiscardAllCardsOfChosenColorEffect()` — caster chooses a color, target player discards all cards of that color from hand. Pair with `target(PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(ANY))` (Persecute). `damagedPlayer()` is the non-targeting combat-damaged-player form (Crosis, the Purger)
- `DiscardAllCardsOfSacrificedCreatureColorsEffect()` — target player reveals their hand and discards every card sharing a color with the creature sacrificed as the spell's additional cost; multicolored sacrificed creatures match any of their colors, while colorless ones only reveal the hand (Mind Extraction)
- `RevealHandAndDiscardMatchingCardsEffect(CardPredicate)` — target player reveals their hand and discards every card matching the predicate. Pair with `target(PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(ANY))` (Trapfinder's Trick)
- `PutFaceUpExiledCardOwnedByTargetIntoGraveyardEffect([CardPredicate filter])` — controller may put one matching face-up card owned by the target from exile into that player's graveyard; a null filter matches any card. Used by Binding Negotiation and Memory Theft (`CardHasAdventurePredicate`)
- `LookAtHandEffect()` — look at target player's hand; `CombatDamageTriggerContextEffect` (DAMAGED_PLAYER) so a combat-damage trigger needs no `target(...)` (Walker of Secret Ways)
- `LookAtDefendingPlayerHandEffect()` — non-targeting combat trigger: the controller looks at the defending player's hand, using the attacked player or attacked planeswalker's controller from `attackedTargetId` (Port Inspector)
- `LookAtFaceDownCreatureEffect()` — the controller privately looks at the card represented by target face-down creature; benign creature target narrowed by `PermanentIsFaceDownPredicate` (Smoke Teller)
- `LookAtHandChooseNonlandToBottomAndDrawEffect()` — look at target player's hand; caster **may** choose a nonland card (optional decline); if chosen, target reveals it, bottoms it, then draws a card. Pair with `target(PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(ANY))` (Vendilion Clique, `ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD`)
- `ShuffleHandIntoLibraryAndDrawEffect()` — wheel for each player; `(false)` limits it to the resolving entry's controller (Whirlpool Drake)
- `RevealHandAndTopCardChooseOneToBottomEffect()` — target player reveals their hand and the top card of their library; the controller chooses one revealed card to put on the bottom of that player's library. Pair with `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(ANY)` (Psychotic Episode)
- `ShuffleHandIntoLibraryAndDrawEffect()` — wheel
- `ShuffleAnyNumberCardsFromHandIntoLibraryThenDrawThatManyEffect()` — controller chooses any number of cards from their hand, shuffles them into their library, then draws that many (Credit Voucher); uses an `XValueChoice` followed by the existing mandatory hand-card shuffle interaction
- `PutHandOnBottomOfLibraryAndDrawEffect()` — target player puts hand on bottom of library, draws that many (Teferi's Puzzle Box, `EACH_DRAW_TRIGGERED`)
- `SylvanLibraryDrawEffect()` — controller draws two additional cards, then must resolve up to two cards drawn this turn (still in hand): put each on top of library or pay 4 life (Sylvan Library; wrap in `MayEffect` in `DRAW_TRIGGERED`). Reads `GameData.cardsDrawnThisTurnIds`; prompts `PendingInteraction.SylvanLibraryChoice`
- `DrawThenPutCardsFromHandOnTopOrBottomOfLibraryEffect(int drawCount, int putCount[, HandToLibraryPlacement placement])` — draw `drawCount`, then choose `putCount` hand cards (multi-select) and put them back on your library. `PLAYER_CHOICE` (default) puts them **all** on top or **all** on the bottom (single pick applied to every chosen card, Dream Cache `(3, 2)`); `TOP` (Brainstorm) and `BOTTOM` (Amass the Components `(3, 1, BOTTOM)`) skip the destination prompt. Two chained interactions (`PutCardsFromHandOnLibraryCardChoice` → `PutCardsFromHandOnLibraryDestinationChoice`), reusing the existing choose-multiple-cards + choose-from-list frontend flows
- `RegisterNextDrawLookAtTopReplacementEffect()` — Aladdin's Lamp `{X}, {T}`. One-shot delayed replacement of the controller's **next draw this turn**: reads X from the entry's `xValue`, stores it in `GameData.pendingNextDrawLookAtTop` (per player). `DrawService.resolveDrawCard` consumes it: look at top X, keep the chosen card on top, shuffle the rest to the bottom, then draw it (real draw). Reuses `LibrarySearch` with the `LibrarySearchDestination.DRAW_CHOSEN_REST_TO_BOTTOM_RANDOM` destination (completion + final draw in `LibraryChoiceHandlerService`). Non-targeted; cleared at cleanup ("this turn"). X==1 / small library ⇒ a plain draw
- `EachPlayerShufflesZonesIntoLibraryEffect([boolean includeOwnedPermanents])` — Timetwister-style; `true` also shuffles every permanent each player owns into their library (Sway of the Stars)
- `ShuffleControllerHandAndGraveyardIntoLibraryEffect()` — controller shuffles their hand and graveyard into their library; pair with `DrawCardEffect` for a controller-only reset
- `EachPlayerShufflesGraveyardIntoLibraryEffect()` — each player shuffles their graveyard into their library (empty GY still shuffles library; Survive)
- `EachPlayerShufflesHandAndPermanentsIntoLibraryAndDrawsEffect()` — each player shuffles their hand and all permanents they *own* (collected across every battlefield, so stolen permanents go home) into their library, then draws that many cards (The Great Aurora). Tokens are moved to the library, dropped (CR 111.7), but still count toward the draw; the graveyard is untouched. Draws run through `DrawService`, so an over-draw arms the empty-library loss
- `EachPlayerMayPutCardFromHandToBattlefieldEffect` — each player, in APNAP order, may put one matching card from hand onto the battlefield; the normal mode holds choices and enters them simultaneously (Show and Tell), while `hypergenesis()` starts with the controller, enters each choice immediately, and repeats controller-first rounds until a full round puts nothing.
- `EachPlayerMayPutLandsFromHandToBattlefieldEffect()` — each player, in APNAP order, may put any number of land cards from their hand onto the battlefield untapped (The Great Aurora). Interactive per player via `PendingInteraction.PutLandsFromHandChoice` (multi-card pick, 0..all); the chosen lands enter as one simultaneous batch. Distinct from `PutCardToBattlefieldEffect.tappedAnyNumber` (controller only, one at a time, tapped)
- `PutAllLandsFromHandAndDiscardEffect()` — reveal the controller's hand, put every land card from it onto the battlefield untapped as one simultaneous batch, then discard the cards remaining in hand (Manabond; wrap in `MayEffect` for the optional instruction)
- `EachPlayerKeepsCardsShufflesRestIntoLibraryEffect(int keepCount)` — each player (APNAP order) chooses up to `keepCount` cards in their hand to keep, shuffles the rest into their library; interactive per-player choice (Worldpurge, `keepCount=7`)
- `EachPlayerChoosesOneCardOfEachColorThenDiscardsRestEffect()` — each player reveals their hand, chooses one card for each of white, blue, black, red, and green that is present, then discards all other nonland cards; a multicolored card may satisfy multiple color choices, and all choices finish before APNAP discard batches (Noxious Vapors)
- `EachPlayerLosesUnspentManaEffect()` — each player's mana pool is emptied ("loses all unspent mana"; Worldpurge)
- `AlternatingHandExileEffect()` — target opponent reveals their hand, then that player and the spell's controller alternately exile a card from it (target picks first) until it is empty; the target's picks return to their hand, the controller's picks go to their graveyard. Harmful `PLAYER` spec; alternation carried by `PendingInteraction.AlternatingHandExileChoice`. Struggle for Sanity

## Library manipulation

- `RevealTopCardMayPutMatchingOntoBattlefieldEffect(CardPredicate)` — reveal the relevant player's top card; if it matches the predicate, that player **may** put it onto the battlefield (else, or on decline, it stays on top). Gate to the Aether uses an artifact/creature/enchantment/land `CardAnyOfPredicate` on `EACH_UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`; the effect uses the active player from the trigger entry
- `RevealTopCardMayPutMatchingOntoBattlefieldWithCounterAndTypeEffect(CardPredicate, CounterType, CardType)` — reveal the relevant player's top card; if it matches, that player may put it onto the battlefield with the specified counter and an additional persistent card type. Arbiter of the Ideal uses artifact/creature/land, a manifestation counter, and enchantment.
- `SearchLibraryForNonlandCardAndRevealEffect(int lifeLoss)` — search for a nonland card, reveal the selected card, return it to the library, and shuffle; the selected name is carried through the asynchronous library choice to the following `LoseLifeToOpponentsWhoCastNamedSpellThisTurnEffect` (Grim Reminder uses `6`)
- `LoseLifeToOpponentsWhoCastNamedSpellThisTurnEffect(String cardName, int amount)` — each opponent who cast a spell named `cardName` this turn loses `amount` life once; uses the shared life-loss primitive (Grim Reminder)
- `SearchLibraryForCardsWithTargetCreatureNameEffect(int count)` — target creature; search your library for up to `count` cards with that creature's name, reveal them, put them into your hand, then shuffle. Resolves the name from the target creature and reuses the standard restricted library-search interaction. Pack Hunt
- `SearchLibraryForCardWithSameNameAsAnotherCreatureYouControlEffect()` — choose another creature you control during resolution, then search your library for one card with that name, reveal it, put it into your hand, and shuffle. Non-targeted; Pattern Matcher
- `SearchLibraryForCardWithSameNameAsCreatureInHandEffect()` — choose and reveal a creature card from your hand during resolution, then search your library for one card with that name, reveal it, put it into your hand, and shuffle. The revealed creature remains in hand; Assembly Hall
- `SearchLibraryEffect(DynamicAmount count, CardPredicate filter, LibrarySearchDestination destination, ManaValueBound manaValueBound, int castFromGraveyardCount, boolean requireDifferentNames)` — unified library search (collapsed the `SearchLibraryFor*` family). Convenience: `()` unrestricted-to-hand (Diabolic Tutor), `(filter)` filtered-to-hand, `(filter, destination)`, `(count, filter, destination)`, `(filter, int count, int cfg)` flashback tutor (Increasing Ambition `(null,1,2)`), `(filter, destination, bound)`, `(count, filter, destination, bound, requireDifferentNames)` for Uncage the Menagerie. destination dz??? `HAND`/`BATTLEFIELD`/`BATTLEFIELD_TAPPED`/`TOP_OF_LIBRARY`; by-name via `CardNamedPredicate` (Squadron Hawk); MV bound via filter + `ManaValueBound` — `(exact, offset)` = X-relative (Citanul Flute, Birthing Pod `(true,1)`, Green Sun's Zenith `CardColorPredicate(GREEN)`), or `(DynamicAmount, exact, offset)` for a board-derived bound (Beseech the Queen — `PermanentCount` of lands controlled, null filter). `requireDifferentNames=true` excludes already-chosen names on multi-picks; count 0 still searches and shuffles. The battlefield overload may carry an `EnterWithCountersEffect` so each found permanent receives an as-entered counter before ETB processing (Neoform).
- `SearchLibraryForCardToBattlefieldOrHandByManaValueEffect(CardPredicate filter, int maxManaValue)` — search for one matching card, reveal it, put it onto the battlefield if its mana value is at most the threshold, otherwise put it into the controller's hand (Guidelight Pathmaker)
- `SearchLibraryEffect(..., LibrarySearchPlayer searchPlayer)` — optional library owner override; `ACTIVE_PLAYER` searches the player whose upkeep caused an each-upkeep trigger, while the default `CONTROLLER` searches the stack entry controller's library (Oath of Lieges).
- `SearchLibraryForCardsToExileAndCreateTokensEffect(CardPredicate filter, CreateTokenEffect tokenTemplate)` — search for any number of matching cards, exile each selected card, then create one `tokenTemplate` token per selected card and shuffle; uses `LibrarySearchDestination.EXILE_AND_CREATE_TOKENS` so the selection remains resumable after a source permanent is sacrificed (Myr Incubator)
- `SearchLibraryForCardsToTopEffect(CardPredicate filter[, int maxCount])` — search library for up to `maxCount` cards matching a predicate, reveal them, then shuffle and put the chosen cards on top in any order. The one-argument form allows any number. Reuses `PendingInteraction.SearchLibraryToTopChoice` and `PendingInteraction.LibraryReorder`; unlike `SearchLibraryEffect`+`TOP_OF_LIBRARY`, it supports an arbitrary subset rather than one card. Used by Scouting Trek with `CardPredicateUtils.basicLand()` and Congregation at Dawn with a three-card creature limit
- `SearchOutsideGameForCreatureToHandEffect()` — optionally reveal a creature card from the controller's sideboard and put it into hand (Vivien, Arkbow Ranger).
- `SearchOutsideGameOrExileForCardToHandEffect(CardPredicate filter)` — optionally choose one matching card from the controller's sideboard or a face-up card they own in exile and put it into hand; uses a mixed resolution-time card choice (Coax from the Blind Eternities).
- `LibraryOfLatNamEffect()` — SPELL, "an opponent chooses one" of two modes for you: accept schedules a `DrawCardsAtNextUpkeep` delayed draw-3, decline pushes `SearchLibraryEffect()` (tutor to hand). The opponent decides via the may-ability accept/decline prompt (`LibraryOfLatNamEffectHandler` → `MayPenaltyChoiceHandlerService.handleLibraryOfLatNamChoice`). Library of Lat-Nam
- `MisfortuneEffect()` — SPELL, "an opponent chooses one" of two modes for you: accept → +1/+1 counter on each creature you control + gain 4 life; decline → -1/-1 counter on each creature that player controls + 4 damage to them. Opponent decides via the may-ability accept/decline prompt (`MisfortuneEffectHandler` → `MayPenaltyChoiceHandlerService.handleMisfortuneChoice`, which pushes the chosen mode's existing effects onto a stack entry controlled by you). Misfortune
- `CovenantOfMindsEffect()` — SPELL, targets an opponent: reveal top 3 cards of your library; the targeted opponent chooses via the may-ability accept/decline prompt. Accept → the revealed cards go to your hand; decline → they go to your graveyard and you draw five. (`CovenantOfMindsEffectHandler` → `MayPenaltyChoiceHandlerService.handleCovenantOfMindsChoice`). Covenant of Minds
- `CombustibleGearhulkEffect()` — ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD, targets an opponent: that opponent chooses whether the controller draws three cards or mills three cards; in the mill mode, the source deals damage to that opponent equal to the total mana value of cards that actually reached the controller's graveyard. (`CombustibleGearhulkEffectHandler` → `CombustibleGearhulkChoiceHandler` → `MayPenaltyChoiceHandlerService.handleCombustibleGearhulkChoice`). Combustible Gearhulk
- `IntuitionEffect()` — SPELL, targets an opponent (pair with `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(OPPONENT)`): you search your library for three cards and reveal them, the targeted opponent chooses one for your hand, the rest go to your graveyard, then you shuffle. Two prompts: `PendingInteraction.IntuitionSearchChoice` (your exactly-three pick) then a `LibraryRevealChoice` for the opponent. Intuition
- `MurmursFromBeyondEffect()` — SPELL, reveal the top three cards of your library; an opponent chooses one to put into your graveyard and the rest go into your hand. The controller chooses which opponent when there is more than one; the revealed-card prompt uses `LibraryRevealChoice` with `PendingMurmursFromBeyondChoice` routing. Murmurs from Beyond
- `SearchLibraryForBasicLandsToBattlefieldTappedAndHandEffect(subtype, extraCardCondition)` — Cultivate (`()`), Nissa's Pilgrimage (Forest-only + spell-mastery extra card)
- `SearchLibraryForCardToHandAndCardToGraveyardEffect()` / `.upToCreaturesRevealed()` — Final Parting (two unrestricted, mandatory) / Jarad's Orders (up to two creatures, revealed; one to hand, other to GY)
- `SearchLibraryForOneCardOfEachColorToHandEffect()` — search for one white, one blue, one black, one red, and one green card (five different cards, WUBRG order); each colour is a separate revealed HAND pick (may fail to find), colours absent from the library are skipped, library shuffled once after the last colour; Conflux
- `SearchLibraryAndGraveyardForNamedCardsToHandEffect(List<String> cardNames)` — search library and graveyard for up to one of each listed name to hand, then shuffle (GY auto-take, library remainder via `namedToHandPicks`; may find any/all/none). Nissa's Encouragement
- `SearchLibraryForInstantsWithManaValuesToHandEffect()` — search for an instant with mana value 3, then 2, then 1 (Firemind's Foresight); each value is a separate revealed HAND pick (may fail to find), absent values skipped, library shuffled once after the last value via `LibrarySearchFollowUp.instantManaValueToHandPicks`
- `TargetPlayerSearchesLibraryForBasicLandToBattlefieldTappedEffect()` — target player searches their library for a basic land card, puts it onto the battlefield tapped, then shuffles; targets a player (player-targeting spec), mandatory search that may fail to find; Fertilid
- `SearchTargetPlayersLibraryForCardToTopEffect()` — each target player searches their own library for a card, then shuffles and puts that card on top; target players are processed sequentially through the shared library-search interaction flow; Scheming Symmetry
- `SacrificeAnyNumberOfLandsAndSearchThatManyLandsToBattlefieldTappedEffect()` — controller sacrifices any number of their lands (multi-permanent choice, 0 to all), then searches their library for up to that many land cards to the battlefield tapped, then shuffles; search count = lands sacrificed, may fail to find; Scapeshift
- `SacrificeAnyNumberOfPermanentsThenDrawPerSacrificedEffect(PermanentPredicate filter)` — controller sacrifices any number of their permanents matching `filter` (multi-permanent choice, 0 to all), then draws a card for each one sacrificed; Reprocess (`PermanentAnyOfPredicate` of artifact/creature/land)
- `SacrificeAnyNumberOfPermanentsThenAddManaPerSacrificedEffect(PermanentPredicate filter, ManaColor color)` — controller sacrifices any number of their permanents matching `filter` (multi-permanent choice, 0 to all), then adds one mana of `color` for each one sacrificed; Mana Seism (`PermanentIsLandPredicate`, `COLORLESS`). Spell effect, not a mana ability
- `SacrificeAnyNumberOfPermanentsEffect(PermanentPredicate filter)` — controller sacrifices any number of their permanents matching `filter` (multi-permanent choice, 0 to all), recording the actual count as the stack entry's event value for a following `EventValue` effect; Landslide uses it for Mountains before `DealDamageToTargetPlayerOrPlaneswalkerEffect(EventValue)`
- `ChooseFivePermanentsSearchSameNameToBattlefieldTappedEffect()` — controller chooses up to five different permanents they control (multi-permanent choice, capped at 5), then for each may search their library for a same-named card put onto the battlefield tapped, then shuffles; one optional single-name search per chosen permanent; Clarion Ultimatum
- `SearchLibraryForSameNameCreatureForEachControlledCreatureEffect()` — for each creature you control (no choice), you may search your library for a creature card with the same name and put it onto the battlefield untapped, then shuffle; one optional single-name search per creature; Doubling Chant
- `DichotomancyEffect()` — for each tapped nonland permanent target opponent controls, the controller may search that opponent's library for a same-named card and put it onto the battlefield under their control; the shared same-name queue carries the searched library owner and battlefield controller, then the opponent's library is shuffled; Dichotomancy (`PLC`)
- `SearchLibraryForTargetCreatureNameToBattlefieldEffect([boolean permanentCardOnly])` — target a nontoken creature, then search your library for a card with the same name and put it onto the battlefield; `permanentCardOnly=true` restricts the search to permanent cards; the target is benign and must still be a nontoken creature on resolution; Mask of the Mimic / Bifurcate
- `DrawPerSourceCounterThenDamageEffect(CounterType)` — EACH_DRAW_TRIGGERED: the draw-step player draws one extra card per counter of that type on the source, then the source deals that much damage to them; Malignant Growth (`GROWTH`). Implements the `OpponentDrawStepOnlyEffect` marker so the trigger is skipped entirely on the controller's own draw step
- `TargetPlayerLosesLifeAndSearchesLibraryToHandEffect(int lifeLoss)` — EACH_DRAW_TRIGGERED: the draw-step player (`entry.getTargetId()`) loses `lifeLoss` life, then does a mandatory unrestricted tutor of their own library to hand, then shuffles; Maralen of the Mornsong (with static `PlayersCannotDrawCardsEffect()`)
- `SearchLibraryForCurseToBattlefieldAttachedToEnchantedPlayerEffect()` — Curse (name not shared with one already on enchanted player) onto battlefield attached to enchanted player; Curse of Misfortunes
- `SearchLibraryForEquipmentToBattlefieldAndAttachEffect()` — search for an Equipment card, put it onto the battlefield, then choose a creature you control to attach it to, then shuffle; Stonehewer Giant. Controller picks the creature via a follow-up `PermanentChoiceContext.AttachEquipmentToCreature` (no interaction if no creatures)
- `SearchLibraryForAuraToBattlefieldAttachedToTargetCreatureEffect()` — search for an Aura card that could enchant the creature recorded (non-targeting) as the entry's `targetId` (the lone attacker), put it onto the battlefield attached to that creature, then shuffle; Sovereigns of Lost Alara. Wrap in `MayEffect`. Host is fixed (not chosen); uses `LibrarySearchDestination.BATTLEFIELD_ATTACHED_TO_PERMANENT`
- `SearchTargetLibraryEffect(int|DynamicAmount count, CardPredicate filter, LibrarySearchDestination destination, boolean canFailToFind)` — search target player's library for up to `count` cards matching `filter`, move them to `destination`, then that player shuffles. Targets a player; the target-library mirror of `SearchLibraryEffect`. `filter == null` searches the whole library (and exiles face down); `canFailToFind` false = bare quantity (CR 701.23d), true = stated quality or "up to" (CR 701.23b). Destinations: `EXILE` (Jester's Cap `3`/`null`/false, Earwig Squad, Nightmare Incursion `PermanentCount(SWAMP, CONTROLLER)`/`null`/true), `GRAVEYARD` (Life's Finale `3`/`CardTypePredicate(CREATURE)`/true), `BATTLEFIELD_UNDER_SEARCHER` (Bribery — enters under the SEARCHER's control), `EXILE_PLAYABLE` (Praetor's Grasp) and `EXILE_PLAYABLE_UNTIL_NEXT_UPKEEP` (Grinning Totem). A prevented search still shuffles the target's library
- `ChooseCardNameAndExileFromZonesEffect(List<CardType> excludedTypes[, CardType requiredType])` — choose a card name, then exile matching cards selected from the target player's hand, graveyard, and library before shuffling. Legacy constructors allow any number; `(excludedTypes, requiredType, maxCount, drawForHandExiled)` supports a finite cap and optionally makes the target draw once per selected hand card (Lost Legacy, The Stone Brain). Targets player, benign
- `ChooseNameRevealHandDamagePerCopyAndExileEffect(List<CardType> excludedTypes, int damagePerCard)` — choose a card name; target reveals hand; deal `damagePerCard` per copy revealed from hand; exile ALL copies from hand/graveyard/library (mandatory, no choice) and shuffle (Thought Hemorrhage, lands excluded, 3). Targets player, harmful
- `ChooseNameAndNumberRevealLibraryDamageEffect(List<CardType> excludedTypes, int damage)` — choose a card name and a number > 0; target player reveals their library; if it holds exactly that many cards with that name, deal `damage` to them; they shuffle either way (Mindblaze, lands excluded, 8). Targets player, harmful
- `LiarsPendulumEffect()` — controller chooses a card name; target player guesses whether a card with that name is in the controller's hand; if wrong, the controller may accept a pending `RevealTargetHandEffect()` + `DrawCardEffect()` sequence. Targets a player benignly; the card's activated-ability filter narrows it to an opponent
- `RevealHandChooseCardFromItAndExileAllCopiesEffect(CardPredicate choosableFilter[, boolean chooseAnyNumber])` — target reveals hand; controller picks a card **in that hand** matching `choosableFilter`; default mode exiles ALL copies of that name from hand/graveyard/library and shuffles, while `chooseAnyNumber=true` exiles the chosen card and offers any number of the remaining copies; no damage. Targets player, harmful; `CombatDamageTriggerContextEffect` (DAMAGED_PLAYER) so a combat-damage trigger needs no `target(...)`, while a spell pairs it with `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter`. The prompt label comes from `CardPredicateUtils.describeFilter`. Nothing happens when the hand holds no legal card. Shimian Specter (`CardNotPredicate(CardTypePredicate(LAND))`), Lobotomy (`CardNotPredicate(CardPredicateUtils.basicLand())`), Pick the Brain (`CardNotPredicate(CardTypePredicate(LAND)), true`)
- `SearchTargetPlayerLibraryAndCastEffect(Set<CardType> castableTypes)` — search target opponent's library for a card of one of the types, caster may cast it without paying its mana cost, then that player shuffles (Knowledge Exploitation, INSTANT/SORCERY). Targets player; uses `CAST_WITHOUT_PAYING`
- `RevealTopCardOfLibraryEffect(LibraryOwner owner)` / `(LibraryOwner owner, int lifeGainIfLand)` — reveal the top card of the owner's library, card stays on top. `TARGET_PLAYER` = target player's library (Aven Windreader, Prophecy), `CONTROLLER` = your own (the Deceivers). `lifeGainIfLand > 0` gains the controller that much life if it's a land (Prophecy)
- `RevealTopCardGainLifeEqualToManaValueEffect()` — reveal top card of controller's library; gain life = its mana value; card stays on top (Sifter Wurm, after `ScryEffect(3)` ETB)
- `EachPlayerRevealsTopCardLosesLifeEqualToManaValueThenToHandEffect()` — each player (APNAP order) reveals their top card, loses life = its mana value, then puts it into their hand; empty library skipped (Duskmantle Seer, `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`)
- `TargetPlayersRevealTopCardsLoseLifeEqualToOtherManaValueThenToHandEffect()` — two target players reveal their top cards; each loses life equal to the other player's revealed card's mana value, then puts their own revealed card into hand. Empty libraries and illegal targets reveal no card; remaining legal targets still resolve (Parker Luck, `CONTROLLER_END_STEP_TRIGGERED`)
- `EachPlayerRevealsTopCardIfAllCreaturesToBattlefieldEffect()` — each player reveals their top card; if every card revealed this way is a creature, all revealed cards enter under their owners' control as one simultaneous batch; otherwise the cards remain on top (Game Preserve, `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`)
- `EachPlayerRevealsTopCardsLandsToBattlefieldTappedRestExiledEffect(int count)` — each player (APNAP order) reveals up to `count` cards, puts revealed lands onto their battlefield tapped, and exiles the rest; empty libraries are skipped and no shuffle or choice is involved (Clear the Land, `5`)
- `EachPlayerRevealsTopCardMayGraveyardOrDrawEffect()` — each player reveals their top card; the controller may put all revealed cards into their owners' graveyards, otherwise each player draws the revealed card (Dakra Mystic, `{U}, {T}` activated ability)
- `RevealTopCardCreatureToBattlefieldElseGraveyardEffect(boolean grantHaste, boolean sacrificeAtEndStep)` — reveal top card; creature → battlefield, otherwise → graveyard (mandatory). No-arg `()` = both false (Call of the Wild `{2}{G}{G}`). `(true, true)` = entering creature gains haste and is sacrificed at the next end step (Impromptu Raid `{2}{R/G}`)
- `RevealTopCardLandToBattlefieldElseToHandEffect()` — reveal top card; land → battlefield, otherwise → hand (mandatory, no choice). Skyward Eye Prophets (`{T}` activated ability)
- `RevealTopCardCreatureTokenLandToBattlefieldElseGainLifeEffect(CreateTokenEffect creatureToken, int lifeGain)` — reveal top card; creature → create `creatureToken`, land → onto the battlefield under the controller's control, noncreature nonland → gain `lifeGain` life. Only the land branch moves the card; empty library does nothing. Implements `TokenCreatingEffect` + `LifeGainEffect`. Druidic Satchel (`{2}, {T}`, 1/1 green Saproling / 2 life)
- `RevealTopCardPutLandsIntoGraveyardRepeatEffect()` — reveal the controller's library one card at a time, binning each land into the graveyard until a non-land (stays on top) or empty (Countryside Crusher, `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`)
- `RevealTopCardRemoveTargetFromCombatIfMatchEffect(CardPredicate)` — reveal top; if match, remove the engine-set attacking creature (targetId) from combat; then bottom the card (Lost in the Woods, ON_CREATURE_ATTACKS_YOU)
- `RevealTopCardsMatchingToHandRestToBottomEffect(int count[, CardPredicate matcher])` — reveal top `count`; creature cards of the source permanent's chosen creature type (Changeling-aware) → hand, rest → bottom in any order (async `LibraryReorder`). Reads `Permanent.getChosenSubtype()`; pair with `ChooseSubtypeOnEnterEffect` (Brass Herald, count=4). Pass a `matcher` for a fixed match instead (Sylvan Messenger, count=4, Elf-or-Changeling)
- `LookAtTopCardsRevealTwoTypesToHandThenRestEffect` — look at/reveal top N, may put up to one card from each bounded type/subtype category into the chosen destination (sequential single picks enforce the per-category cap), rest disposed. Factories: `.creatureAndLandToHandRestOnBottom(4)` (Gift of the Gargantuan) / `.creatureAndEnchantmentToHandRestToGraveyard(5)` (Benefaction of Rhonas) / `.angelDemonDragonToHandRestOnBottom(6)` (Kaalia, Zenith Seeker) / `.creatureAndLandToBattlefieldRestOnBottomRandom(DynamicAmount)` (Green Sun's Twilight). Bounded follow-ups run via `LibrarySearchFollowUp`; battlefield picks use simultaneous entry
- `RevealTopTenCardsForColorPairsEffect()` — reveal the top ten; choose one exactly-two-color card for each represented color pair, put chosen cards into hand, and shuffle the rest onto the library bottom (Niv-Mizzet Reborn)
- `LookAtTopCardsRevealTwoTypesToHandThenRestEffect` — look at/reveal top N, may put up to one card from each bounded type/subtype category into hand (sequential single picks enforce the per-category cap), rest disposed. Factories: `.creatureAndLandToHandRestOnBottom(4)` (Gift of the Gargantuan, private look, rest on bottom) / `.creatureAndEnchantmentToHandRestToGraveyard(5)` (Benefaction of Rhonas, public reveal, rest to graveyard) / `.angelDemonDragonToHandRestOnBottom(6)` (Kaalia, Zenith Seeker, private look, unchosen cards randomized onto the bottom). Bounded follow-ups run via `LibrarySearchFollowUp`
- `RevealTopCardsForCardTypesCastThisTurnEffect(int count)` — reveal the top `count` cards, then offer at most one card for each distinct card type among the controller's noncreature spells cast this turn; put the unchosen cards on the bottom in a random order. Used by Hurkyl, Master Wizard. Dynamic follow-ups reuse `LibrarySearchFollowUp.SecondBoundedPick`
- `RevealTopCardsForEachCardTypeEffect(int count)` — reveal the top `count` cards, then offer at most one card for each represented card type; put the unchosen cards on the bottom in a random order. Used by Atraxa, Grand Unifier. Dynamic follow-ups reuse `LibrarySearchFollowUp.SecondBoundedPick`
- `RevealTopCardCreatureGainToughnessLosePowerToHandEffect()` — reveal top; if creature, gain life = toughness, lose life = power, then → hand; non-creature stays on top (Sapling of Colfenor, `ON_ATTACK`)
- `PhyrexianPortalEffect()` - activated ability, targets an opponent: if your library has 10+ cards, that opponent splits your top ten into two face-down piles; you search the pile you pick for a card (to hand, may fail to find), shuffle the rest of it into your library, and the other pile is exiled. Reuses the card-pile flow (`PendingPileSeparation` with `CardPileDisposition.SEARCH_ONE_TO_HAND`) plus a `PendingPortalPileSearch`-marked `LibraryRevealChoice` (Phyrexian Portal)
- `RevealTopCardsAndSeparateEffect(int|DynamicAmount count[, CardPileDisposition disposition][, boolean controllerSeparates])` — Fact-or-Fiction pile separation with a fixed or dynamic count. By default an opponent separates and the controller chooses; `controllerSeparates=true` reverses those roles (Steam Augury, Epiphany at the Drownyard). `CardPileDisposition.HAND_WITH_FACE_DOWN_PILE` supports a hidden pile. Non-targeting unless the targeted-separator overload is used
- `RevealTopCardsAndSeparateEffect(int count, CardPileDisposition disposition, boolean controllerSeparates, boolean targetedSeparator, boolean faceDownPile)` — Fortune's Favor variant: target opponent privately looks at the top four cards, separates them into a face-down Pile 1 and face-up Pile 2, and the controller chooses which pile goes to hand; the other goes to the graveyard. The target is a benign PLAYER; the shared pile flow hides Pile 1's card identities after separation
- `DeathOrGloryEffect()` — separate all creature cards in the controller's graveyard into two piles; the opponent chooses which pile to exile, and the other pile returns under the controller's control. Reuses `PendingPileSeparation` with `CardPileDisposition.OPPONENT_CHOOSES_EXILE`; the controller makes the split, the opponent makes the exile choice, and returned creatures enter as one batch. Handled by `DeathOrGloryEffectHandler` + `GraveyardReturnSupport`
- `SeparateCreaturesIntoPilesAndChooseAttackersEffect()` — at the beginning of combat on an opponent's turn, the source controller separates the active player's creatures into two piles; the active player chooses a pile, and only creatures in that pile can attack for the rest of the turn. Reuses `PendingPileSeparation` with `CardPileDisposition.ATTACKERS`; creatures entering or becoming creatures after the choice cannot attack. Handled by `SeparateCreaturesIntoPilesAndChooseAttackersEffectHandler` + `FightOrFlightSupport` (Fight or Flight)
- `SeparateCreaturesIntoPilesAndChooseBlockersEffect()` — at the beginning of combat on the controller's turn, the source controller separates the defending player's creatures into two piles; the defending player chooses a pile, and only creatures in that pile can block for the rest of the turn. Reuses `PendingPileSeparation` with `CardPileDisposition.BLOCKERS`; creatures entering or becoming creatures after the choice cannot block. Handled by `SeparateCreaturesIntoPilesAndChooseBlockersEffectHandler` + `StandOrFallSupport` (Stand or Fall)
- `RevealTopCardsBottomThenDamageIfCopyRevealedEffect(int count, int damage)` — reveal top `count`, bottom them in any order (async `LibraryReorder`); if a card sharing the source's name was revealed, deal `damage` to the any-target (Stomping Slabs 7/7). Any-target chosen on cast; no damage if no copy revealed
- `ScryEffect(int)` / `ScryEffect(DynamicAmount)` / `(int, LibraryOwner)` — split the top N (or dynamic X) cards between the selected library's top and bottom in chosen orders; `TARGET_PLAYER` uses the target player's library and declares a benign player target
- `RevealUntilLandBottomThenDamageEffect()` — reveal until a land or library empty, deal damage equal to the number of nonland cards revealed (double if the land is a Mountain), then put the revealed cards on the bottom in any order (Goblin Charbelcher)
- `ScryEffect(int)` / `ScryEffect(DynamicAmount)` — scry N (or dynamic X)
- `SurveilEffect(int)` — surveil N
- `SurveilThenEffect(int, CardEffect)` / `.direct(int, CardEffect)` — surveil N, then queue a reflexive continuation or resolve it directly on the same entry
- `SurveilEffect(int)` / `SurveilEffect(DynamicAmount)` — surveil N (or a dynamically evaluated count, such as `CountersOnTargetPermanent`)
- `LookAtTopCardPutLandOrCreatureWithinLoyaltyEffect()` — look at the controller's top card (private); if it's a land, or a creature whose mana value ≤ the loyalty counters on the source permanent, the controller **may** put it onto the battlefield (else it stays on top). Nissa, Steward of Elements 0. Loyalty threshold read from `entry.getSourcePermanentId()` at resolution; the "may" runs via a paired mayfx handler
- `LookAtTopCardMayPutMatchingOntoBattlefieldEffect(CardPredicate[, boolean enterTapped])` — look at the controller's top card (private); if it matches the predicate, the controller **may** put it onto the battlefield (else it stays on top). The optional `enterTapped` flag handles Explorer's Scope; Into the Wilds uses the untapped one-argument form. The "may" runs via a paired mayfx handler
- `LookAtTopCardMayPutCreatureOntoBattlefieldElseToHandEffect()` — look at top (private); if creature, **may** put onto battlefield; if declined or non-creature, put into hand. Believe (Reason // Believe aftermath). Paired mayfx handler
- `LookAtTopCardMayPutMatchingOntoBattlefieldElseToHandEffect(predicate, tapped)` — look at top (private); if it matches the predicate, **may** put it onto the battlefield, optionally tapped; if declined or nonmatching, put it into hand. Risen Reef. Paired mayfx handler
- `LookAtTopCardMayRevealMatchingToHandEffect(CardPredicate, OtherwiseDestination)` — private look at top; if matching, may reveal to hand. `OtherwiseDestination.GRAVEYARD` also offers a graveyard may for a card that stays out of hand (Archghoul of Thraben), `TOP` leaves it on top (Domri Rade +1), and `BOTTOM` moves it to the bottom when it is not put into hand (Vivien's Grizzly). The legacy boolean constructor maps `true` to `GRAVEYARD` and `false` to `TOP`. Two-stage may (`Stage.MAY_HAND` / `MAY_GRAVEYARD`)
- `ShuffleLibraryEffect(boolean targetPlayer)` — shuffle library (false=controller's, true=target player's)
- `LookAtTopCardsExileRandomRestOnTopEffect(int count, int exileCount)` — look at the top `count` cards of your library, exile `exileCount` of them **at random** (engine picks, not the player), then put the rest back on top in any order (async `LibraryReorder` with `toBottom=false` when 2+ remain). Orcish Librarian `(8, 4)`. A short library just exiles everything it can
- `LookAtTopCardsOfTargetLibraryEffect(DynamicAmount count, TargetLibraryAction action)` (convenience `int` overload) — look at top N of target player's library, then apply the action: LOOK_ONLY (pure peek, Dewdrop/Orcish Spy), MAY_EXILE_ONE (Psychic Surgery; no target = own library, Puresight Merrow), MAY_EXILE_ANY_NUMBER (may exile any number, one pick at a time, rest on top in any order — Ancestral Knowledge, count=10, no target), MAY_SHUFFLE (Visions count=5, benign PLAYER target, reuses `ShuffleLibraryEffect(true)` may-handler), PUT_ONE_INTO_GRAVEYARD (Cruel Fate, Wu Spy), REVEAL_AND_PUT_ONE_INTO_GRAVEYARD (public reveal plus the same mandatory choice, Balshan Beguiler), EXILE_ONE (mandatory exile of one, rest on top in any order — Sealed Fate, count `new XValue()`), EXILE_ONE_FACE_DOWN_REST_TO_BOTTOM_RANDOM (mandatory face-down exile with persistent cast permission and random bottoming of the remainder — Gonti, Lord of Luxury), EXILE_TWO_FACE_DOWN_REST_TO_BOTTOM_RANDOM (mandatory face-down exile of two with persistent cast permissions and random bottoming of the remainder — Black Cat, Cunning Thief), MAY_PUT_TOP_ON_BOTTOM (Coral Fighters, Precognition; count=1, harmful PLAYER target — the card is shown in a may-ability prompt and, if accepted, moved to the bottom via `PutTopCardOfTargetLibraryOnBottomEffect`)
- `LookAtTopCardsOfTargetLibraryEffect(DynamicAmount count, TargetLibraryAction action)` (convenience `int` overload) — look at top N of target player's library, then apply the action: LOOK_ONLY (pure peek, Dewdrop/Orcish Spy), MAY_EXILE_ONE (Psychic Surgery; no target = own library, Puresight Merrow), MAY_EXILE_ANY_NUMBER (may exile any number, one pick at a time, rest on top in any order — Ancestral Knowledge, count=10, no target), MAY_SHUFFLE (Visions count=5, benign PLAYER target, reuses `ShuffleLibraryEffect(true)` may-handler), PUT_ONE_INTO_GRAVEYARD (Cruel Fate, Wu Spy), REVEAL_AND_PUT_ONE_INTO_GRAVEYARD (public reveal plus the same mandatory choice, Balshan Beguiler), EXILE_ONE (mandatory exile of one, rest on top in any order — Sealed Fate, count `new XValue()`), EXILE_ONE_FACE_DOWN_REST_TO_BOTTOM_RANDOM (mandatory face-down exile with persistent cast permission and random bottoming of the remainder — Gonti, Lord of Luxury), MAY_PUT_TOP_ON_BOTTOM (Coral Fighters, Precognition; count=1, harmful PLAYER target — the card is shown in a may-ability prompt and, if accepted, moved to the bottom via `PutTopCardOfTargetLibraryOnBottomEffect`)
- `ExileOneFromTopCardsFaceDownWithSourceEffect(int count)` — look at the controller's top cards, exile one face down tracked with the source permanent, and put the rest on the bottom in any order. Pair with `AllowCastFromCardsExiledWithSourceEffect(false, CardTypePredicate(CREATURE), false, false, 0)` for Vivien, Champion of the Wilds; unlike Gonti's target-library action, this does not grant a separate persistent cast permission or any-mana payment.
- `PutTopCardOfTargetLibraryOnBottomEffect()` — move the top card of the stack entry's `targetId` player's library to the bottom of that library. Declares a benign PLAYER `TargetSpec` so the may-ability accept path carries the pre-chosen player onto the stack entry; only used as the body of `LookAtTopCardsOfTargetLibraryEffect(1, MAY_PUT_TOP_ON_BOTTOM)`
- `ExileSpellEffect()` — exile this spell instead of putting it into the graveyard after resolution (marker). Pair with Miracle / Recall
- `MiracleCast("{cost}")` — casting option (not an effect): declare on card; DrawService offers reveal on first draw; trigger may cast for cost (CR 702.94). See Temporal Mastery
- `ShuffleIntoLibraryEffect()` — shuffle spell into library
- `ShuffleSelfAndGraveyardIntoLibraryEffect()` — shuffle self + graveyard into library
- `ShuffleSelfFromGraveyardIntoLibraryEffect()` — triggered ability: shuffle the source card from its owner's graveyard into their library (pair with `ON_SELF_PUT_INTO_GRAVEYARD_FROM_ANYWHERE`, e.g. Purity)
- `ExchangeGraveyardAndLibraryEffect()` — exchange the controller's graveyard and library, then shuffle the new library (Morality Shift)
- `ShuffleGraveyardIntoLibraryEffect(boolean targetPlayer[, CardPredicate filter])` — shuffle graveyard into library (targetPlayer=true targets, false=controller's). Optional `filter` moves only the matching cards and leaves the rest in the graveyard — `new CardTypePredicate(CardType.CREATURE)` for "shuffle all creature cards from your graveyard into your library" (Barishi); omit it (or pass null) for the whole graveyard
- `ShuffleTargetCardsFromGraveyardIntoLibraryEffect(CardPredicate, int)` — shuffle up to N cards from a targeted player's graveyard; `Integer.MAX_VALUE` represents any number. The effect also implements `GraveyardCardChoosingEffect` so targeted ETB abilities can choose the player and card targets in sequence (Memory's Journey, Loaming Shaman)
- `ShuffleTargetCardsFromGraveyardIntoOwnersLibrariesEffect(CardPredicate, int)` — spell-only multi-target effect for up to N cards from any graveyards; each selected card is shuffled into its owner's library and each affected owner shuffles (Turn the Earth)
- `ShuffleTargetCardsFromControllerGraveyardIntoLibraryEffect(CardPredicate, int)` — shuffle up to N from controller's own graveyard (ETB multi-target or activated graveyard targets; Ghostly Castigator, Perpetual Timepiece)
- `ShuffleCardFromControllerGraveyardIntoLibraryEffect(CardPredicate)` — "you may shuffle up to one card from your graveyard into your library"; resolution-time optional single-card choice from controller's own graveyard (non-targeted, pairs with `CounterSpellEffect`; Put Away)
- `ShuffleTargetPermanentIntoLibraryEffect()` — target permanent's owner shuffles it into their library (Deglamer; constrain to artifact/enchantment etc. via the card's target filter)
- `ShuffleSelfIntoOwnerLibraryEffect()` — non-targeted: the source permanent's owner shuffles it into their library (Blitz Hellion's `END_STEP_TRIGGERED`). Acts on the stack entry's source permanent via `removePermanentToLibraryShuffled`
- `CastTopOfLibraryWithoutPayingManaCostEffect(Set<CardType>)` — cast top free
- `MayPlayCardsFromTopOfLibraryForLifeUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — until end of turn, the controller may play cards from the top of their library; spells use an alternative cost of life equal to mana value, while lands use normal land-play timing and allowances
- `ExileTopCardOfEachLibraryAndMayCastSpellsEffect()` — exile the top card of each player's library, then let the controller cast any number of the exiled spells without paying their mana costs; uncast cards remain exiled (Etali, Primal Storm)
