- `CantBecomeUntappedEffect()` — STATIC: the permanent can't become untapped. Checked by the untap-step gate, untap effects, tap-or-untap effects, and untap-cost validation; grant to an Aura's host with `GrantEffectEffect(..., GrantScope.ENCHANTED_CREATURE)`
- `RemoveAllCountersAndLockPermanentEffect()` — Suncleanser mode 1: strip every counter kind from target creature and lock it out of counters while the source stays on the battlefield (`GameData.countersLockedPermanentsWhileSourceOnBattlefield` → `GameQueryService.cantHaveCounters`)
- `RemoveAllCountersAndLockPlayerEffect()` — Suncleanser mode 2: clear the target player's poison and lock them out of counters while the source stays (`GameData.countersLockedPlayersWhileSourceOnBattlefield` → `GameQueryService.canPlayerGetPoisonCounters`); pair with a `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(OPPONENT)` for "target opponent"
- `TargetPlayerCantGainLifeRestOfGameEffect()` — the stack entry's target player can't gain life for the rest of the game (persistent, per-player). Non-targeting on `ON_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER`; Stigma Lasher
- `AllDamageDealtWithWitherEffect()` — STATIC global: all damage is dealt as though its source had wither (creature damage becomes -1/-1 counters; player damage normal). Everlasting Torment
- `NoncombatDamageToOpponentCreaturesAsMinusCountersEffect()` — STATIC: the controller's sources deal noncombat damage to opponents' creatures as -1/-1 counters instead (combat damage unaffected). Like wither, still counts as damage dealt. Checked in `GameQueryService.noncombatDamageToOpponentCreatureAsCounters` (noncombat path only). Soul-Scar Mage
- `DoubleLifeGainEffect()` — STATIC: controller's life gain is doubled (Boon Reflection). Applied in `LifeSupport.applyGainLife`; multiple copies stack multiplicatively (2^count)
- `AdditionalLifeGainEffect(int amount)` — STATIC: the controller gains `amount` additional life whenever they would gain life (Angel of Vitality). Multiple copies add together and modify each positive life-gain event once
- `OpponentLifeGainBecomesLifeLossEffect()` — STATIC: if an opponent of the controller would gain life, that player loses that much life instead (Tainted Remedy). Applied in `LifeSupport.applyGainLife`/`applySetLifeTotal` via `GameQueryService.lifeGainBecomesLifeLoss`
- `RedistributePlayerLifeTotalsEffect()` — the resolving controller redistributes the players' current life totals among them (each total is reassigned to a player of their choice). Reverse the Sands

## Poison counters

`MeliraPoisonReplacementEffect()` — STATIC replacement marker: the first poison-counter event affecting its controller becomes one poison counter, and later poison-counter events that turn are ignored. Melira, the Living Cure

`ReturnTargetCardOnDeathThisTurnEffect()` also supports another target creature or artifact for Melira, the Living Cure; the delayed return fires when that permanent is put into a graveyard this turn.

- `GiveTargetPlayerPoisonCountersEffect(DynamicAmount)` — give the targeted player an evaluated number of poison counters; combine `TargetPlayerPoisonCounters` with `Max`, `Sum`, and `Scaled` for threshold differences (Vraska, Betrayal's Sting)

- `EachOpponentWithPoisonCountersLosesLifeEffect(int amount, int minimumPoisonCounters)` — each opponent currently meeting the poison-counter threshold loses the fixed amount of life at resolution. Non-targeting; use for “each opponent who has three or more poison counters loses 3 life” (Feed the Infection). Uses `LifeSupport.applyLifeLoss`, so life-loss triggers still fire.
- `GivePoisonCountersEffect(int, PoisonRecipient)` — give poison; recipient routes CONTROLLER (self) / TARGET_PLAYER / EACH_PLAYER / EACH_OPPONENT / TARGET_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER / ENCHANTED_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER. `TARGET_PERMANENT_CONTROLLER` reads the controller of a non-targeting permanent baked onto a trigger (Noxious Assault). On an `ON_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER` trigger, `TARGET_PLAYER` binds to the damaged player (Pit Scorpion: "whenever this deals damage to a player, that player gets a poison counter")
- `GivePoisonCountersEffect(int, TARGET_PLAYER, CardPredicate spellFilter)` — `ON_CONTROLLER_CASTS_SPELL` trigger descriptor (Hand of the Praetors)
- `RemoveAllPoisonCountersAndDamageTargetPlayerEffect()` — target player loses all poison counters, source deals that much damage to them (Leeches). One effect because the damage equals the counters removed

## Creature pump / boost

- `BoostTargetCreatureEffect.forTargetGroup(power, toughness, group)` — positional target-group form for activated abilities with separate creature targets; the normal constructors remain unbound or use the card's `target(...).addEffect(...)` binding.

- `BoostTargetCreatureEffect(DynamicAmount power, DynamicAmount toughness)` or `(int, int)` — target +X/+Y. Any "for each …", "+X/+X" (X paid), or "where X is …" target-pump = this effect + a `model/amount/DynamicAmount` — never a new per-variant class. The amount evaluates against the SOURCE, so counting refers to the effect's controller, not the pumped target. E.g. `(new XValue(), new XValue())` (Untamed Might), `(new PermanentCount(new PermanentIsCreaturePredicate(), CountScope.CONTROLLER), same)` (Elder of Laurels), `(new Sum(new Fixed(1), new CardsInGraveyard(filter, CountScope.CONTROLLER)), new Fixed(0))` (Ancestral Anger). Add a trailing `GrantDuration.UNTIL_YOUR_NEXT_TURN` — `(int, int, GrantDuration)` — for "gets -2/-0 until your next turn" (Jace, Telepath Unbound +1); the pump then rides a floating continuous effect and survives end-of-turn cleanup
- `BoostTargetAndSharingCreaturesUntilEndOfTurnEffect(DynamicAmount power, DynamicAmount toughness)` or `(int, int)` — target creature and every other creature sharing at least one effective color with it get +X/+Y until end of turn. The affected creature set is snapshotted on resolution across all battlefields; a colorless target affects only itself. Used by Wojek Siren
- `UntapTargetAndSharingCreaturesEffect()` — untaps the target creature and every other creature sharing at least one effective color with it. The affected creature set is determined on resolution across all battlefields; a colorless target affects only itself. Used by Rally the Righteous
- `DealDamageToTargetAndSharingCreaturesEffect(DynamicAmount damage)` or `(int)` — deals damage to the target creature and every other creature sharing at least one effective color with it. The affected creature set is snapshotted on resolution across all battlefields; a colorless target affects only itself. Used by Cleansing Beam
- `BoostTargetPermanentEffect(int power, int toughness, PermanentPredicate filter)` — target permanent gets +X/+Y until end of turn, with an optional permanent filter. Use when the target can be a noncreature permanent, such as a Vehicle.
- `BuffTargetCreatureIndefinitelyEffect(int power, int toughness, Set<Keyword> keywords)` or `(int, int)` — target creature gets +power/+toughness and gains `keywords` **indefinitely** (no duration, CR 611.2b). Use for "this effect lasts indefinitely" pumps (Riding the Dilu Horse); NOT for until-EOT pumps (use `BoostTargetCreatureEffect` + `GrantKeywordEffect`). Recorded as a `PERMANENT` floating continuous effect on the target — +P/+T in sublayer 7c, keywords in layer 6 (read off the float by `GameQueryService.assembleStaticBonus`); copies stack additively
- `BoostTargetCreatureWhileSourceTappedEffect(int power, int toughness)` — target creature gets +power/+toughness **for as long as the source permanent remains tapped** (CR 611.2b; Tawnos's Weaponry). Records a `WHILE_SOURCE_TAPPED` floating `BuffTargetCreatureIndefinitelyEffect` keyed to the source, read in sublayer 7c; expired when the source untaps (`CreatureControlService.onSourceUntapped` → `GameData.expireTappedSourceFloatingEffects`) or leaves. Pair with `MayNotUntapDuringUntapStepEffect` static + a `{T}` cost so the controller can keep the source tapped
- `BoostAllCreaturesWhileSourceTappedEffect(int power, int toughness)` — all creatures get +power/+toughness for as long as the source permanent remains tapped (Thran Weaponry). The handler snapshots creatures at resolution and records one source-linked `WHILE_SOURCE_TAPPED` `BuffTargetCreatureIndefinitelyEffect` per creature, so later creatures are not affected; the existing untap/leave cleanup ends all boosts
- `CardNamedPredicate(String cardName)` — card filter for exact name match (use with graveyard-count boosts above)
- `UnspentMana(ManaColor color)` — the amount of that color in the controller's mana pool,
  including colored mana in restricted buckets
- `BoostSelfEffect(DynamicAmount, DynamicAmount)` or `(int, int)` — self +X/+Y; one-shot in trigger/ability slots, continuous in STATIC. Any "for each …" self-boost = this effect + a `model/amount/DynamicAmount` (`PermanentCount`, `CardsInGraveyard`, `AttachmentsOnSource`, `CreaturesBlockingSource`, `OpponentPoisonCounters`, `ImprintedCreaturePower/Toughness`, `ImprintedCardManaValue`, `LandsMatchingImprintedName`, `ChosenPermanentPower`, `XValue`, `Scaled`, `Fixed`) — never a new per-variant effect class. `ChosenPermanentPower` = effective power (at resolution) of the permanent chosen during activation, e.g. the creature tapped by `TapCreatureCost(…, trackTappedCreaturePower=true)` — Impelled Giant's "+X/+0 where X is the power of the creature tapped this way"
- `BoostSelfOrEnchantedCreatureUntilEndOfTurnEffect(DynamicAmount, DynamicAmount)` or `(int, int)` — boosts the source permanent until end of turn, or its attached creature while the source is attached. Used for a bestow permanent whose ability says "this creature gets …; if this permanent is an Aura, enchanted creature gets … instead" (Erebos's Emissary)
- `AttachedBoostEffect(DynamicAmount, DynamicAmount, GrantScope[, boolean amountsFromAttachedCreature])` — STATIC +X/+Y on the enchanted/equipped creature (`ENCHANTED_CREATURE`/`EQUIPPED_CREATURE`). Attached-scope sibling of `BoostSelfEffect`; any "for each …" aura/equipment boost = this effect + a `DynamicAmount`. `CountScope.CONTROLLER` = the aura/equipment's controller (CR 109.5); `CountScope.ATTACHED_CONTROLLER` = the enchanted/equipped creature's controller ("its controller", not CR 109.5 "you"; nothing when unattached; Righteous Authority). Negative per-count = wrap in `Scaled(…, -1)`. Blanchwood Armor, Blackblade Reforged, Bonehoard, Runechanter's Pike, Quag Sickness, Strata Scythe, Righteous Authority — never a new `BoostCreaturePer*` class. Optional 4th arg `amountsFromAttachedCreature=true` evaluates the amounts from the **attached creature's** perspective instead, so `IfSourceAttacking` reads its combat state (Tahngarth's Rage: `IfSourceAttacking(Fixed(3), Fixed(-2))` / `IfSourceAttacking(Fixed(0), Fixed(-1))`)
- `ShrinkEnchantedCreatureEffect(DynamicAmount amount)` — STATIC `-X/-Y` on the enchanted creature where `Y = min(X, toughness - 1)`, so it can never kill the creature by itself. Unlike `AttachedBoostEffect`, the amount is evaluated from the **enchanted creature's** perspective: `IfSourceAttacking` reads its combat state, `DEFENDING_PLAYER` the player it attacks, `CONTROLLER` "its controller". Snowblind
- `BoostSelfWhenCombatOpponentMatchesEffect(PermanentPredicate opponentFilter, int power, int toughness)` — "Whenever this creature blocks or becomes blocked by one or more [filter] creatures, it gets +X/+Y until end of turn." Place in ON_BLOCK and/or ON_BECOMES_BLOCKED (plain, non-PER_BLOCKER). Non-targeting: the trigger always goes on the stack; the handler applies the boost only if a combat opponent (a creature this blocks, or a creature blocking it) matches the filter, and fires once regardless of how many match. Dwarven Soldier (`PermanentHasSubtypePredicate(ORC)`, +0/+2). The opponent-filtered, self-boost analog of Deathgazer's `DestroyCombatOpponentAtEndOfCombatEffect` Optional 4th arg `Set<Keyword> grantedKeywords` also grants those keywords until end of turn — Crimson Roc (`PermanentNotPredicate(PermanentHasKeywordPredicate(FLYING))`, +1/+0, `Set.of(FIRST_STRIKE)`, ON_BLOCK only, for "whenever this creature blocks a creature without flying").
- `DoubleSelfPowerToughnessEffect()` — double self P/T
- `DoubleOtherOwnCreaturesPowerEffect()` — double the power of each other creature you control until end of turn; each creature gets a power modifier equal to its own current effective power at resolution
- `DoubleAllOwnCreaturesPowerToughnessEffect()` — double P/T of each creature you control (per-creature current effective P/T; Unnatural Growth). Pair with `EACH_BEGINNING_OF_COMBAT_TRIGGERED` for "at the beginning of each combat"
- `BoostAllOwnCreaturesEffect(DynamicAmount, DynamicAmount[, PermanentPredicate, GrantDuration])` — all own +X/+Y; `(int, int[, PermanentPredicate])` convenience wraps in `Fixed`. Defaults to end of turn; `GrantDuration.UNTIL_YOUR_NEXT_TURN` stores a floating layer effect for the controller's next turn. Any "where X is …" / power- or graveyard-derived mass own-pump = this effect + a `DynamicAmount` (evaluated once at resolution) — e.g. `new GreatestPowerAmongControlled()` (Overwhelming Stampede), `new CardsInGraveyard(new CardTypePredicate(CREATURE), CONTROLLER)` (Garruk, the Veil-Cursed). Never a new per-variant class
- `BoostAllCreaturesEffect(DynamicAmount, DynamicAmount)` or `(…, PermanentPredicate)` or `(…, PermanentPredicate, EachPermanentScope)` — creatures +X/+Y; `(int, int[, PermanentPredicate])` / `(int, int, EachPermanentScope)` convenience wraps in `Fixed`. Scope `ALL_PLAYERS` (default, both sides) or `TARGET_PLAYER` ("creatures target player controls", player-targeting spec, Shields of Velis Vel). "X paid" mass pump = `new Scaled(new XValue(), mult)` / `new XValue()` (Ichor Explosion, Flowstone Slide)
- `BoostAllCreaturesOfChosenSubtypeEffect(int powerBoost, int toughnessBoost)` — resolution-time chosen-creature-type mass modifier for all matching creatures on all battlefields (Outbreak); use for fixed P/T changes keyed by a type chosen while the spell resolves
- `StaticBoostEffect(int, int, Set<Keyword>, GrantScope, PermanentPredicate)` — static +X/+Y + keywords
- `BoostOwnCreaturesByManaSymbolEffect(ManaColor, int powerPerSymbol, int toughnessPerSymbol)` — chroma anthem: each creature you control gets +P/+T per mana symbol of that color in its own cost (hybrid/Phyrexian symbols of that color count). Light from Within
- `BoostOtherMulticoloredCreaturesByColorCountEffect(int powerPerColor, int toughnessPerColor)` — anthem: each *other* multicolored creature you control gets +P/+T per color it has. Monocolored/colorless creatures and the source itself are unaffected (layer-5-aware color count). Knight of New Alara `(1, 1)`
- `SetBasePowerToughnessEffect(int, int)` — set target creature's base P/T until end of turn; `(int, int, GrantScope)` for continuous static (e.g. `ENCHANTED_CREATURE`, Deep Freeze), with an optional `PermanentPredicate` filter for narrowed global scopes (Harmonious Archon); `SetBasePowerToughnessEffect.toughnessOnly(int)` — "has base toughness X" only, base power untouched (Chariot of the Sun); `SetBasePowerToughnessEffect.powerOnly(int)` — "has base power X" only, base toughness untouched (Vhati il-Dal)
- `SetBasePowerToughnessEffect(int, int)` — set target creature's base P/T until end of turn; `(int, int, GrantScope)` for continuous static (e.g. `ENCHANTED_CREATURE`, Deep Freeze), with an optional `PermanentPredicate` filter for narrowed static scopes; `SetBasePowerToughnessEffect.toughnessOnly(int)` — "has base toughness X" only, base power untouched (Chariot of the Sun); `SetBasePowerToughnessEffect.powerOnly(int)` — "has base power X" only, base toughness untouched (Vhati il-Dal)
- `SetSelfBasePowerToughnessIndefinitelyEffect(int power, int toughness)` — non-targeting self ability: permanently sets the source's base P/T, using the layer-7b floating setter and permanent legacy overrides (Surge Engine)
- `SetSelfPowerToughnessFromTargetCreatureEffect(int powerFromTargetToughnessOffset, int toughnessFromTargetPowerOffset, PermanentPredicate targetPredicate)` — sets the SOURCE's P/T until end of turn from the target creature's current toughness/power plus the given offsets; values locked in at resolution (no CDA). `targetSpec()` is a benign CREATURE narrowed by `targetPredicate`. Sworn Defender = `(-1, 1, PermanentAllOfPredicate(IsCreature, InCombatWithSource))` — power becomes target's toughness − 1, toughness becomes target's power + 1
- `SetTargetBasePowerToughnessUntilNextUpkeepThenAddCounterEffect(int power, int toughness)` — Cycle of Life: target creature has base P/T until the controller's NEXT UPKEEP (floating 7b with `EffectDuration.UNTIL_CONTROLLERS_NEXT_UPKEEP`, so it survives end-of-turn cleanup), then a `+1/+1` counter arrives at that upkeep via a `PutCounterOnPermanentAtNextUpkeep` delayed action. `(power, toughness, CounterType, amount)` for other counters
- `SetAllOwnCreaturesBasePowerToughnessEffect(DynamicAmount, DynamicAmount[, PermanentPredicate])` or `(int, int[, PermanentPredicate])` — set base P/T of matching creatures you control to X/X until end of turn (layer 7b, modifiers apply on top). The optional filter is evaluated with source context, so `PermanentNotPredicate(PermanentIsSourcePermanentPredicate)` models "other creatures you control". X-cost ability = `new XValue()` (Mirror Entity)
- `SetAllUnblockedCreaturesBasePowerToughnessEffect(int, int)` — set base P/T of every unblocked creature (any player's) until end of turn (layer 7b). "Unblocked" = attacking + no blocker, locked in at resolution (Inkfathom Witch)
- `GrantBaseStatsToCounterBearersEffect(CounterType, int power, int toughness, Set<Keyword>)` — resolves to a game-wide continuous rule: "every creature with a [type] counter has base P/T power/toughness and has [keywords] for as long as it has such a counter" (layer 7b set + layer 6 keywords). Installs source-independent PERMANENT floating effects scoped by `PermanentHasCountersPredicate`, so the rule survives the source leaving and lapses when the counter is removed (scope re-evaluated each pass); idempotent. Put in `ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD` and pair with an upkeep `PutCounterOnTargetPermanentEffect(theCounter)`. Aven Mimeomancer (feather counter → base 3/1 + flying)
- `BecomeCreatureTypeWithBasePowerToughnessEffect(int power, int toughness, CardSubtype addedSubtype[, CardSubtype requiredSubtype])` — one-shot non-targeting SELF effect: permanently adds `addedSubtype` (into `grantedSubtypes`) and sets base P/T **indefinitely** (permanent base override, layer 7b via fresh timestamp — not until end of turn). Optional `requiredSubtype` = intervening "if" checked at resolution (source must already have that subtype, granted counts). Figure of Destiny's level-up chain. Pair permanent keyword grants (flying/first strike) as STATIC `ConditionalEffect(new SourceHasSubtype(subtype), new GrantKeywordEffect(kw, SELF))`
- `BecomeCreatureEffect(int power, int toughness, List<CardSubtype> subtypes[, Set<Keyword> keywords[, Set<CardColor> protectionFromColors]])` — one-shot non-targeting SELF effect: permanently changes the source into only a creature with the supplied base P/T and creature subtypes. Optional keywords are added to the runtime card copy; optional protection colors are installed as permanent floating protection. Uses a runtime card copy so the source's noncreature card types are removed. Lurking Jackals, Opal Archangel, Opal Guardian
- `BecomeEnchantmentEffect()` — one-shot non-targeting SELF effect: restores the source permanent's original card form, including its enchantment type and abilities. Opal Acrolith
- `GrantProtectionFromTriggeringSpellColorsEffect()` — one-shot non-targeting SELF effect: adds permanent protection from every color of the spell that caused the trigger to the source's runtime card. Opal Titan
- `SwitchPowerToughnessEffect()` — switch P/T
- `SwitchAllCreaturesPowerToughnessEffect()` — switch the power and toughness of each creature on the battlefield until end of turn; the affected creatures are fixed when the effect resolves
- `BoostByBushidoEffect()` — continuous Bushido lord: each OTHER Samurai its controller controls gets +1/+1 for each point of Bushido that creature has. Takeno, Samurai General

`DynamicStaticBoostEffect(DynamicAmount, DynamicAmount, GrantScope[, PermanentPredicate])` — dynamic continuous +X/+Y for a creature scope; amounts are evaluated from the static source and its controller. Use for board-count anthems such as Hold the Gates; pair a separate `GrantKeywordEffect` when the static text also grants a keyword. `CreaturesEnteredBattlefieldThisTurn(CountScope.CONTROLLER)` counts creature cards that entered under the source controller this turn, including tokens.

- `BoostLegendaryCreaturesByOtherLegendaryCreaturesEffect(int powerPerCreature, int toughnessPerCreature)` â€” each legendary creature the source controller controls gets +P/+T for each other legendary creature that player controls; a legendary source that is also a creature receives the bonus. Heroes' Podium `(1, 1)`

## P/T setting / counters

- `TotalManaValueOfCardsExiledWithSource()` — summed mana value of the cards exiled with the source permanent (Jadeheart Attendant)
- `ColorsAmongCardsExiledWithSource()` — number of distinct colors among cards exiled with the source permanent (Sunbird Effigy)

- `BecomeCreatureTypeWithBasePowerToughnessEffect.replacingSubtype(...)` — permanent subtype replacement with an optional base-P/T change; use the overload without P/T to retain the current base stats.

- `SetCreatureTypesToImprintedCreatureEffect(List<CardSubtype> retainedSubtypes)` — static layer-4 effect that replaces the source's creature subtypes with the imprinted creature card's creature subtypes while retaining the supplied types. Used with `SetPowerToughnessToAmountEffect(ImprintedCreaturePower, ImprintedCreatureToughness)` for Duplicant.

- `SetPowerToughnessToAmountEffect(DynamicAmount power, DynamicAmount toughness)` — CDA that sets P/T on a 0/0 base (pass the same amount for both). Replaced the `PowerToughnessEqualTo*` family + `BoostSelfBySlimeCountersOnLinkedPermanentEffect`. Amounts: `PermanentCount(IsLand/IsCreature/IsArtifact/HasSubtype…, CONTROLLER)` (lands/creatures/artifacts/Swamps you control), `CardsInGraveyard(filter, CONTROLLER|ANY_PLAYER)`, `CardsInExile(filter, CONTROLLER)` (exile by owner — Seize the Storm flashback cards), `CardsInHand(CONTROLLER)` (hand size), `ControllerLifeTotal()` (life total), `CountersOnLinkedPermanent(type, id)` (linked-permanent counters), `ColorManaSymbolsInGraveyard(color, CONTROLLER)` (chroma of graveyard cards — Umbra Stalker = black), `ChosenNumberOnSource()` (the number last chosen for this permanent — Shapeshifter: power = `ChosenNumberOnSource()`, toughness = `Sum(Fixed(7), Scaled(ChosenNumberOnSource, -1))`). `TotalPowerOfCardsExiledWithSource()`/`TotalToughnessOfCardsExiledWithSource()` (summed P/T of the cards exiled tracked with this permanent — Sutured Ghoul). Compose offsets/negation with `Sum`/`Scaled`/`Fixed`. Pair "choose a number" with `ChooseNumberOnEnterEffect`/`ChooseNumberEffect`
- `PutCountersOnSourceEffect(int power, int toughness, int amount)` — counters on self; it can also be granted through `GrantTriggeredAbilityEffect` for a creature's `ON_ALLY_CREATURE_DEALS_DAMAGE_TO_CREATURE` trigger
- `TurnOtherNontokenCreaturesFaceDownOnEnterEffect()` — as-enters replacement that turns all other nontoken creatures face down as 2/2 creatures; it skips creature tokens and double-faced cards. Ixidron
- `PutCountersOnSourceIfFirstResolutionThisTurnEffect(String abilityKey, CounterType, int)` — put counters on the source only on the first resolution of that keyed triggered ability in a turn; the resolution is counted when the effect executes, so countered triggers do not consume it
- `DiscardCardsAndPutCountersOnSourceEffect(CardPredicate cardFilter, int countersPerCard, int drawCardsPerCard, String cardDescription)` — controller chooses any number of matching hand cards to discard; the source gets `countersPerCard` +1/+1 counters and the controller draws `drawCardsPerCard` cards per card discarded. The three-argument constructor keeps draws at zero. Uses an X-value count choice followed by filtered sequential discard choices, then applies the follow-up synchronously when the discard completes. Mind Maggots (`CardTypePredicate(CREATURE)`, 2); Nantuko Cultivator (`CardTypePredicate(LAND)`, 1 counter, 1 draw)
- `RenownEffect(int amount)` — renown N (CR 702.112a) on `ON_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER`; counters + `Permanent.renowned`, gated by the `SourceIsRenowned` condition; the flip fires `ON_SELF_BECOMES_RENOWNED` (Relic Seeker) and `ON_ALLY_CREATURE_BECOMES_RENOWNED` (Valeron Wardens) triggers
- `SourceIsSaddled` — source permanent is saddled until end of turn; use as the attack-time condition for "whenever this creature attacks while saddled" (Brightfield Glider)
- `MonstrosityEffect(int amount)` — resolves a monstrosity ability once, putting +1/+1 counters on the source and marking it `Permanent.monstrous`; use `SourceIsMonstrous` for the source's permanent bonuses and activation restriction
- `PutCountersOnSourceEqualToEnteringPowerEffect(int power, int toughness, boolean optional)` — ON_ANY_OTHER_CREATURE_ENTERS_BATTLEFIELD: put counters on self = entering creature's power; `optional` = "you may" (Hamletback Goliath)
- `PutCountersOnSourceEqualToDyingPowerEffect(int power, int toughness, boolean optional)` — ON_ANY_CREATURE_DIES: put counters on self = the dying creature's last-known effective power; `optional` = "you may" (Kresh the Bloodbraided). Death sibling of the entering-power effect
- `EachPlayerLosesLifeAndPutCountersOnSourceEqualToLifeLostEffect(int lifeLossPerPlayer)` — UPKEEP_TRIGGERED: each player loses N life, then the source gets a +1/+1 counter for each 1 life actually lost this way (Blood Tyrant)
- `PutCountersOnEnchantedCreatureEffect(CounterType, int amount)` — activated-ability effect on an Aura: put `amount` counters on the creature it is attached to (Krasis Incubation `{1}{G}{U}, Return this Aura to its owner's hand: Put two +1/+1 counters on enchanted creature`). Implements `AttachedPermanentSelfTargetingEffect` + a self-targeting `TargetSpec`, so `ActivatedAbilityExecutionService` captures `attachedTo` onto the stack entry **before** costs are paid — a bounce/sacrifice cost that detaches the Aura still leaves the counters landing on the creature it had enchanted (CR 608.2h)
- `PutCountersOnSelfEffect(CounterType)` — one counter of a type on self (charge, +1/+1, study, invitation, etc.)
- `PutCounterOnSelfThenDrawPerCounterEffect(CounterType)` — put one counter of that type on the source, **then** draw a card for each counter of that type on it (Mind Unbound, `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`, `LORE`). One effect, not counter+draw as two slot effects, because separate upkeep effects become separate LIFO stack entries and the draw would resolve first
- `PutCountersOnSelfEffect(CounterType, int count)` — N counters of a type on self (e.g. Withengar Unbound: 13 +1/+1); on `ON_EQUIPPED_CREATURE_DEALS_COMBAT_DAMAGE`, the source is the Equipment when its equipped creature deals combat damage (Umezawa's Jitte)
- `PutCountersOnSelfEffect(CounterType, DynamicAmount)` — dynamic count on self, e.g. `(CounterType.TOWER, new XValue())` for "{X}: Put X tower counters" (Helix Pinnacle)
- `DoubleCountersOnSourceEffect(CounterType)` — double the specified counter type on the source permanent, using the shared counter-placement pipeline (The Millennium Calendar)
- `PutCountersOnGrantingEquipmentEffect(CounterType)` — counters on the Equipment that granted the ability rather than on the activating permanent (Hankyu's aim counters); bound to the granting Equipment at activation time
- `RemoveAllCountersAsCostEffect(CounterType[, boolean fromGrantingEquipment])` — activation cost: remove all counters of that type and snapshot the count into `xValue` (read back with `XValue`). `fromGrantingEquipment = true` pays from the granting Equipment (Hankyu); default pays from the activating permanent (Jar of Eyeballs)
- `PutCounterOnTargetPermanentEffect(CounterType, int)` — counters on target permanent (`PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ONE`/`MINUS_ONE_MINUS_ONE`/…); `(…, new XValue())` for "X counters"; `(…, count, boolean regenerateIfSurvives)` (Gore Vassal); `withTargetRestriction(…, targetPredicate)` to restrict legal targets; `(…, count, PermanentPredicate)` for a non-targeting own-permanent choice
- `PutCounterOnChosenOwnPermanentEffect(CounterType, DynamicAmount, PermanentPredicate)` — non-targeting resolution-time choice of one matching permanent you control; puts the dynamically evaluated number of counters on it and records the chosen permanent on the stack entry for a following non-targeting effect. The `int` overload wraps `Fixed`
- `DistributeCountersAmongTargetsEffect(CounterType, DynamicAmount total, DivisionMode, boolean removeAtNextCleanup, boolean removeAtNextEndStep, boolean etbAssignments, PermanentPredicate targetRestriction)` — the counter analogue of `DealDividedDamageEffect`. `evenlyAmongTargets(CounterType, int total)` = "distribute two counters among one or two target creatures": even split of `total` across the chosen target group (1 target → 2, 2 targets → 1 each), the forced CR 601.2d distribution for two counters among ≤2 targets; bind to `target(creatureFilter, 1, 2).addEffect(...)`. Splendid Agony (`MINUS_ONE_MINUS_ONE`, harmful spec), Common Bond (`PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ONE`, benign), Contagion (`MINUS_TWO_MINUS_ONE`, harmful). `chosenAmongTargetCreatures(CounterType, DynamicAmount total)` = a genuine "distribute X counters among any number of target creatures" — no `target()` call; targets and per-target counts come from the cast-time damageAssignments map (harness `castInstant(player, idx, Map)`), total evaluated at cast time (Spoils of War, `CardsInGraveyard(artifact-or-creature, OPPONENTS)`). `chosenUntilNextCleanup(CounterType, int total)` schedules every placed counter for removal at the next cleanup step. `chosenUntilNextEndStep(CounterType, int total)` creates one delayed removal trigger per counter placed, as used by Shambling Swarm.
- `PutPlusOnePlusOneCounterOnEachCreatureTargetPlayerControlsEffect()` — +1/+1 on each creature the target player controls (bind to the player target group via `target(...).addEffect(...)`)
- `PutCounterOnEachControlledPermanentEffect(CounterType, int, PermanentPredicate)` — counters on each own permanent matching predicate (use `PermanentIsCreaturePredicate` for "each creature you control")
- `PutCountersOnControlledCreaturesByPowerAboveBasePowerEffect(CounterType)` — for each creature controlled by the resolving controller, puts counters equal to its current effective power minus its current layered base power when that difference is positive; used by Sovereign Okinec Ahau
- `RemoveCounterFromEachControlledPermanentEffect(CounterType, int, PermanentPredicate)` — remove up to N counters from each own permanent matching predicate, clamped at zero (Heartmender's "remove a -1/-1 counter from each creature you control")
- `RemoveCounterFromEachMatchingPermanentEffect(CounterType, int, PermanentPredicate, EachPermanentScope)` — remove up to N counters from each matching permanent across `ALL_PLAYERS` or the `TARGET_PLAYER` battlefield; the target-player form reads the stack entry's target (Temporal Distortion's "remove all hourglass counters from permanents that player controls")
- `PutCounterOnEachMatchingPermanentEffect(CounterType, int|DynamicAmount, PermanentPredicate, EachPermanentScope)` — counters on each matching permanent across `ALL_PLAYERS`/`TARGET_PLAYER` (each attacking / other / all creatures; each creature target player controls)
- `PutCounterOnReferencedPermanentEffect(CounterType[, int|DynamicAmount][, PermanentPredicate condition])` — counter(s) on the **attached** permanent, i.e. the source Aura *or* Equipment's `attachedTo` host (Aura vs Equipment is not a semantic axis). `DynamicAmount` supports values such as `XValue()`; `PermanentReference.RETURNED` instead finds the permanent created by a preceding targeted graveyard return. Works in UPKEEP_TRIGGERED (Primal Cocoon, the M13 Ring cycle), ON_ENCHANTED_PERMANENT_TAPPED (Spirit Shackle), ON_ANY_CREATURE_DIES (Sadistic Glee — the counter lands on the host, not the creature that died), and targeted graveyard-return riders (Abuelo's Awakening). The optional `condition` gates the placement at resolution ("if it's blue"), it is not a targeting restriction
- `PutCounterOnReferencedPermanentEffect(PermanentReference.TRIGGERING, CounterType)` — same effect, but on the permanent whose event produced the trigger ("put a wind counter on **it**"). Reads `StackEntry.triggeringPermanentId`, set by the becomes-tapped trigger collectors; never targets/fizzles. Freyalise's Winds
- `AddCounterToEnchantedCreatureThenDestroyAtThresholdEffect(CounterType, int threshold)` — one Aura upkeep trigger (Consuming Ferocity): put a counter of that type on enchanted creature; if it then has `threshold`+ such counters, **the creature** (CR 608.2h) deals damage equal to its power to its controller, then it is destroyed and can't be regenerated. Single effect because it is a single triggered ability
- `RemoveCounterFromSourceThenDestroyEnchantedAtZeroEffect(CounterType, int damageToController)` — Aura trigger (Orcish Mine): remove one counter of that type from the Aura; if it was the last, destroy the enchanted permanent and the Aura deals that much damage to its controller. Use on `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED` + `ON_ENCHANTED_PERMANENT_TAPPED`
- `TurnFaceUpReplacementEffect` / `PutCountersOnTurnFaceUpEffect(int)` — replacement behavior for "as this creature is turned face up, put N +1/+1 counters on it". Register it in `ON_TURNED_FACE_UP`; the turn-face-up action applies the counters immediately, so it is not queued as a triggered ability (Hooded Hydra)
- `TurnFaceUpCopyEffect(PermanentPredicate[, Map<EffectSlot, List<CardEffect>>])` — replacement behavior for "as this creature is turned face up, you may choose another matching permanent and become a copy of it". The turn-face-up flow completes the choice before processing ordinary `ON_TURNED_FACE_UP` effects, then adds the retained copy exception and any additional slot effects
- `TurnSourceFaceDownEffect()` — source-only effect that restores the permanent's original card and turns it face down as a 2/2 creature; use inside the copied permanent's upkeep `MayEffect` for Vesuvan Shapeshifter's recurring face-down option
- `EnterWithCountersEffect(CounterType, DynamicAmount)` — "enters the battlefield with … counters" (as-enters replacement effect): fixed = `Fixed(n)`, X paid = `XValue()`, "for each …" = a counting amount (`CreatureDeathsThisTurn`, `LifeGainedThisTurn(CONTROLLER)`, `DistinctManaCostsAmongCardsInGraveyard()`, `Sum(PermanentCount(...), CardsInGraveyard(...))`, …). "If kicked" / "Raid —" variants wrap it in `ConditionalEffect(new Kicked()/new Raid(), …)`
- `DevourEffect(int multiplier)` — Devour N as-enters replacement (`ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD`): controller may sacrifice any number of their *other* creatures; enters with `multiplier ×` that many +1/+1 counters and records the devoured creatures. Read the count with the `CreaturesDevoured` amount, or a per-subtype count with `DevouredCreaturesOfSubtype(CardSubtype)`. Tar Fiend = `new DevourEffect(2)` + `DiscardEffect(new CreaturesDevoured(), DiscardRecipient.TARGET_PLAYER)`; Voracious Dragon = `new DevourEffect(1)` + `DealDamageToAnyTargetEffect(new Scaled(new DevouredCreaturesOfSubtype(CardSubtype.GOBLIN), 2))` ("twice the number of Goblins it devoured"; a devoured Changeling counts as any type)
- `SacrificePermanentsAsEntersForCountersEffect(PermanentPredicate filter, int countersPerPermanent)` — as-enters replacement (`ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD`, CR 614.1c): "As this permanent enters, sacrifice any number of permanents matching `filter`. It enters with that many +1/+1 counters on it." Non-keyword generalization of `DevourEffect` (any permanent type, nothing recorded on the permanent). The entering permanent itself is never offered; nothing to sacrifice ⇒ no prompt. Shimatsu the Bloodcloaked = `(new PermanentTruePredicate(), 1)`
- `SacrificeAllPermanentsAsEntersEffect(PermanentPredicate filter)` — as-enters replacement (`ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD`): sacrifice every permanent you control matching `filter` before the entering permanent is placed on the battlefield. The entering permanent is not included. Overlaid Terrain uses `PermanentIsLandPredicate`.
- `ExileAnyNumberOfCreatureCardsFromGraveyardOnEnterEffect()` — as-enters replacement (CR 614.1c, `ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD`): controller exiles any number of creature cards from their own graveyard, tracked with the entering permanent. Pair with `SetPowerToughnessToAmountEffect(TotalPowerOfCardsExiledWithSource, TotalToughnessOfCardsExiledWithSource)` in STATIC for the Sutured Ghoul CDA. Empty graveyard ⇒ no prompt
- `ExileUpToXCreatureCardsFromGraveyardOnEnterWithCountersEffect(int countersPerCard)` — as-enters replacement: controller exiles up to the entering creature's paid X creature cards from their graveyard, tracked with the entering permanent, which receives `countersPerCard` +1/+1 counters for each card exiled
- `ExileXCreatureCardsFromGraveyardOnEnterWithCountersEffect(CounterType...)` — as-enters replacement: controller must exile exactly the entering creature's paid X creature cards from their graveyard or the card goes to its owner's graveyard; for each card exiled, the controller chooses one supplied counter type and the creature receives one counter of that type. Frankenstein's Monster uses `PLUS_TWO_PLUS_ZERO`, `PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ONE`, and `PLUS_ZERO_PLUS_TWO`
- `PayAnyAmountOfLifeOnEnterEffect([DynamicAmount maxAmount])` — as-enters replacement (`ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD`): controller picks 0..their current life total, optionally capped by `maxAmount`, and loses that much life before ETB triggers; the amount is stored via `Permanent.setChosenNumber`. Pair with `SetPowerToughnessToAmountEffect(ChosenNumberOnSource, ChosenNumberOnSource)` in STATIC for the Minion of the Wastes and Nameless Race CDAs (paying 0 ⇒ 0/0, dies to SBA)
- `SacrificeAnyNumberOfCreaturesSetPowerToughnessOnEnterEffect()` — as-enters replacement (`ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD`, CR 614.1c): controller sacrifices any number of their *other* creatures (zero legal) before ETB triggers; the sacrificed creatures' total effective power/toughness is stamped on the entering permanent as a durable layer-7b base-P/T override (`permanentBasePowerOverride`/`permanentBaseToughnessOverride`), so counters and boosts apply on top. No other creatures or an empty pick ⇒ 0/0, dies to SBA. Dracoplasm
- `ExploitEffect()` — Exploit (CR 702.110): wrap in `MayEffect` on `ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD`; may sacrifice any controlled creature (incl. self). Success fires `ON_EXPLOIT` if source was on battlefield at resolution start. Pair `ON_EXPLOIT` + `CounterSpellEffect` + `StackEntryHasTargetPredicate` for Overcharged Amalgam
- `RemoveCountersToCreateTetraviteTokensEffect(CreateTokenEffect tokenTemplate)` — Tetravus upkeep trigger 1: "you may remove any number of +1/+1 counters from this creature. If you do, create that many tokens from `tokenTemplate`." Prompts a 0..N number choice (0 = decline), removes that many +1/+1 counters and creates that many tokens, each recorded in `GameData.sourceCreatedTokens` as "created with" the source. Paired with the exile effect below
- `ExileTetraviteTokensToPutCountersOnSelfEffect()` — Tetravus upkeep trigger 2: "you may exile any number of tokens created with this creature. If you do, put that many +1/+1 counters on this creature." Multi-permanent choice over the source's tracked, still-alive tokens (any controller); exiles the chosen ones and puts that many +1/+1 counters on the source
- `GraveyardEnterWithAdditionalCountersEffect(CardSubtype, int)` — graveyard static: while in your graveyard, creatures of that subtype you control enter with N extra +1/+1 counters (Dearly Departed / HUMAN)
- `ControlledCreaturesEnterWithAdditionalCountersEffect(CardSubtype, DynamicAmount)` — battlefield static: while on the battlefield, each other creature of that subtype you control enters with the evaluated number of extra +1/+1 counters (fixed int constructor for Sage of Fables / WIZARD; `PermanentCount` for Giada, Font of Hope / ANGEL)
- `ControlledCreaturesEnterWithAdditionalCountersEffect(CardSubtype, int)` — battlefield static: while on the battlefield, each other creature of that subtype you control enters with N extra +1/+1 counters (Sage of Fables / WIZARD)
- `ControlledPermanentsEnterWithAdditionalCountersByManaValueEffect(PermanentPredicate, int, int, int)` — battlefield static: matching permanents you control enter with one counter amount at or below a mana-value threshold and another amount above it; use `ControlledPermanentEntryReplacementEffect` for the entry-replacement capability (Thunderous Velocipede)
- `ControlledCreaturesEnterWithAdditionalCountersThisTurnEffect(int[, EffectDuration])` — one-shot: each creature you control enters with N extra +1/+1 counters; defaults to this turn and supports `UNTIL_YOUR_NEXT_TURN` (Zameck Guildmage; Arlinn, the Pack's Hope)
- `ControlledPermanentsEnterWithAdditionalCountersEffect(PermanentPredicate, int)` — battlefield static: matching permanents you control enter with N additional +1/+1 counters; use a composite predicate for one logical condition spanning multiple subtypes (Arlinn, Voice of the Pack / Wolf or Werewolf)
- `ControlledCreaturesEnterWithSourcePowerCountersEffect(CardSubtype)` — battlefield static: each other creature you control enters with additional +1/+1 counters equal to the source's power, and with that subtype in addition to its other types (Master Biomancer / MUTANT)
- Increment keyword — keyword-driven (`Keyword.INCREMENT`, auto-loaded from Scryfall): +1/+1 counter on self when mana spent on a cast spell exceeds self's current power or toughness. Add nothing to the card; behavior lives in `TriggerCollectionService.collectIncrementTriggers` (resolution effect: `IncrementTriggerEffect`). E.g. Ambitious Augmenter
- `SpellManaSpentGreaterThanSourcePower` — spell-cast trigger condition: the mana spent on the triggering spell is greater than the source permanent's current power at trigger and resolution. Liberator, Urza's Battlethopter.
- `ProliferateEffect()` — proliferate once; `ProliferateEffect(DynamicAmount)` evaluates the number of proliferate events from the resolving stack entry (used when a preceding library choice records how many cards were selected)
- `AdjustEachCounterKindOnTargetEffect()` — targets any permanent (benign `PERMANENT`); "for each kind of counter on target permanent, put another of that kind on it or remove one" (Quarry Hauler). Snapshots the counter kinds present and prompts ADD/REMOVE once per kind via the generic list-choice; no-op if none. Distinct from `ProliferateEffect` (adds only, all counter-bearing permanents) and `DoubleCountersOnTargetPermanentEffect` (doubles, no choice)
- `DoublePlusOneCountersOnControlledCreaturesEffect()` — non-targeting; doubles the number of +1/+1 counters on each creature the controller controls (Kalonian Hydra, `ON_ATTACK`). Skips creatures with no +1/+1 counters and those that can't have counters; fires the counters-put-on-self triggers afterwards
- `DoubleCountersOnEachControlledPermanentEffect(PermanentPredicate)` — non-targeting; doubles every kind of counter on each permanent the controller controls that matches the predicate, using the shared replacement and counter-trigger pipeline. Use an `AnyOfPredicate` for “each Spider and legendary creature” effects
- `DoublePlusOneCountersOnTargetCreatureEffect()` — benign creature-targeting effect; doubles the number of +1/+1 counters on each chosen creature, using the shared counter-placement replacement and trigger pipeline (Solidarity of Heroes)
- `KickerEffect(String cost)` — mana kicker declaration; `KickerEffect(PayLifeCost)` declares a life-payment kicker; `KickerEffect.tap(PermanentPredicate, String)` declares a non-mana tap kicker; `KickerEffect.returning(PermanentPredicate, String)` declares a return-to-hand kicker; `KickerEffect.discarding(String, CardPredicate, String)` declares a typed discard kicker
- `BuybackEffect(String cost)` / `BuybackEffect(int discardCount[, boolean randomDiscard])` / `BuybackEffect(PayLifeCost, int discardCount, boolean randomDiscard)` — STATIC declaration of a spell's optional buyback cost (CR 702.27). Paid when the caster announces buyback (wire `PlayCardRequest.buyback`; chosen discard costs use `discardHandCardIndices`, while random discard costs are selected automatically); the stack entry is stamped `StackEntry.buyback`, read at resolution by the `BuybackPaid` condition. Pair with `ConditionalEffect(new BuybackPaid(), ReturnToHandEffect.selfSpell())` in the SPELL slot so the spell returns to its owner's hand as it resolves; countered or fizzled spells (which never resolve) still go to the graveyard. Harness `castInstantWithBuyback` / `castSorceryWithBuyback`
- `SpliceEffect(CardSubtype onto, String cost)` — STATIC: splice onto [subtype] for [cost] (CR 702.47). Cast via `harness.castWithSplice` / `GameService.playCardWithSplice`; host spell must have the subtype (e.g. Arcane). Through the Breach
- `SpliceEffect(CardSubtype onto, String cost, PermanentPredicate tapCost)` — same, for a non-mana splice cost "Tap an untapped white creature you control" (Hundred-Talon Strike: `(ARCANE, "", creature + white)`). The permanent to tap is passed as the extra `spliceCostPermanentIds` argument of `harness.castWithSplice` / `GameService.playCardWithSplice`, one id per spliced card that has a tap cost, in splice order; tapping as a cost ignores summoning sickness
- `SpliceEffect.returning(CardSubtype onto, PermanentPredicate returnCost)` — same, for a bounce splice cost "Return a blue creature you control to its owner's hand" (Veil of Secrecy). The permanent to return rides on the same `spliceCostPermanentIds` argument as a tap cost, one id per spliced card with a permanent cost, in splice order
- `SpliceEffect.exilingGraveyard(CardSubtype onto, int count)` — same, for the graveyard splice cost "Exile four cards from your graveyard" (Horobi's Whisper). No extra cast argument: splice has no client-side choice UI, so the engine exiles the oldest `count` cards of the caster's graveyard; too few cards means the cast is rejected
- `SpliceEffect(CardSubtype onto, String cost)` — STATIC: splice onto [subtype] for a mana cost; use `SpliceEffect(CardSubtype onto, List<CastingCost>)` for composed costs such as sacrificing permanents, or the three-argument constructor to add splice-only effects alongside the card's normal SPELL effects (CR 702.47). Cast via `harness.castWithSplice` / `GameService.playCardWithSplice`; host spell must have the subtype (e.g. Arcane). Through the Breach, Torrent of Stone, Roar of Jukai
- `RemoveXCountersFromSourceCost(CounterType)` — `CostEffect`; removes X counters of that type from the source permanent, X chosen at activation and bounded by the counters actually present (prompted like a variable loyalty cost, not an `{X}` mana cost). The chosen X rides on the activation's `xValue`, readable back with an `XValue` amount; the engine prompts with an `XValueChoice` when the client does not announce X. Night Dealings (`CounterType.THEFT`), Cruel Sadist (`CounterType.PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ONE`), Talon of Pain (`CounterType.CHARGE`)

## Keywords / abilities

- `GrantTargetCreatureHexproofFromChosenColorAndCantBeBlockedByItUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — on resolution, the controller chooses a color; the target creature gains hexproof from that color and can be blocked only by creatures that aren't that color until end of turn. Pass `true` for the self-scoped form (Sungold Sentinel); the no-arg form remains targeted (Skrelv, Defector Mite)

- `GainKeywordsOfCreatureCardsExiledWithSourceEffect()` — STATIC self-only effect: the source gains the watched keyword set found on every creature card exiled with it, and carries over fixed protection effects represented by `ProtectionGrantingEffect`. Used by Death-Mask Duplicant.
- `GrantEffectToTargetEffect.toSourcePermanent(EffectSlot, CardEffect)` — indefinite, non-targeting grant of a triggered ability to the source permanent.

- `GrantHexproofFromOwnColorsEffect()` — STATIC: each other creature you control has hexproof from each of its current colors; the colors are recomputed from the affected creature's layered characteristics (Tam, Mindful First-Year)
- `GrantKeywordEffect(Keyword, GrantScope)` or `(Keyword, GrantScope, PermanentPredicate)` or `(Set<Keyword>, GrantScope)` — grant keywords. Add a trailing `GrantDuration` (`(Keyword, GrantScope, GrantDuration)` / `(Set<Keyword>, GrantScope, GrantDuration)`) for one-shot duration: `END_OF_TURN` (default), `UNTIL_YOUR_NEXT_TURN`, `UNTIL_YOUR_NEXT_UPKEEP`, or `WHILE_SOURCE_ON_BATTLEFIELD` ("for as long as you control [source]" — Aegis Angel; floating layer-6 effect keyed to the source, survives cleanup, ends when the source leaves the battlefield or changes controller), or `INDEFINITE` (no stated duration, so it lasts until the end of the game per CR 611.2a — Nature's Blessing; `PERMANENT` floating layer-6 effect mirrored into `Permanent.persistentGrantedKeywords`, neither cleared by turn cleanup). In `STATIC` slot the grant is continuous and the duration is ignored. Static land scopes `OWN_LANDS` / `ALL_LANDS` grant to lands you control / every land on the battlefield matching `filter` (Shimmer: `(PHASING, ALL_LANDS, new PermanentHasSourceChosenSubtypePredicate())`). `GrantKeywordEffect.toTargetIf(Keyword, PermanentPredicate grantCondition)` — grant to target ONLY if it matches `grantCondition` (checked at resolution; target stays legal, only the grant is conditional — Vampire's Zeal, Blessing of Belzenlok). `grantCondition` is distinct from `filter`/the spec predicate.
- `GrantKeywordToOwnCreaturesFromGraveyardEffect(Keyword, Condition)` — while the source card is in its controller's graveyard and the condition is met, grants the keyword to that controller's creatures. Use a board condition such as `new ControlsPermanent(new PermanentHasSubtypePredicate(CardSubtype.PLAINS))` for Valor.
- `GrantKeywordEffect(Keyword, GrantScope.TARGET_AND_SHARING_CREATURES)` — target creature and every other creature sharing one of its effective colors gain the keyword until end of turn; the affected set is snapshotted on resolution across all battlefields. A colorless target affects only itself (Surge of Zeal).
- `GrantProtectionSharedByOwnCreaturesUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — at resolution, snapshots every modeled protection ability held by creatures the controller controls and grants those protection variants to each creature they control until end of turn. Parameterized protection is carried through `GrantEffectEffect`; color and transient non-subtype/opponent-creature protection use the existing protection state. Concerted Effort.
- `CantHaveOrGainKeywordEffect(Keyword)` — marker granted with `GrantEffectEffect` for "can't have or gain [keyword]". It removes the keyword from the affected permanent, blocks later layer-6 grants while active, and one-shot keyword grants are skipped while the restriction applies (Archetype of Courage).
- `GrantEffectToOwnCreaturesUntilEndOfTurnEffect(EffectSlot, CardEffect[, PermanentPredicate])` — snapshot grant of a temporary triggered ability to creatures you control until EOT (later ETBs/control changes excluded); the optional predicate narrows the snapshot. Driven/Despair (`ON_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER` + draw / discard). Own-creatures analog of `GrantEffectToTargetUntilEndOfTurnEffect`
- `GrantEffectEffect(CardEffect, GrantScope)` / `(…, PermanentPredicate)` — continuous grant of a CardEffect to matching permanents. Granting `CumulativeUpkeepEffect` is collected at upkeep via `GrantedUpkeepEffectSupport` (Breath of Dreams). `GrantScope.SELF_AND_PAIRED` grants to the source + its soulbond partner — use with `ConditionalEffect(SourceIsPaired, …)` for non-keyword paired grants such as protection (Diregraf Escort)
- `GrantEffectToTargetEffect(EffectSlot, CardEffect)` / `(EffectSlot, CardEffect, EffectDuration)` — grant a triggered ability to the target permanent. Default duration `PERMANENT` (Balduvian Shaman → `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED` + `CumulativeUpkeepEffect("{1}")`; Musician → `DestroyUnlessPaysPerCounterEffect(MUSIC, "{1}")`); `UNTIL_END_OF_TURN` uses temporary storage. Skips if the target already has an equal effect in that slot (Musician "if it doesn't have"). Collected by step/combat trigger services alongside native card effects. `GrantEffectToTargetEffect.toEnchantedPermanent(slot, effect, duration)` grants to the Aura's enchanted permanent instead (no target, no skip-if-present) — Genju of the Fields
- `GrantEffectToTargetUntilEndOfTurnEffect(EffectSlot, CardEffect)` — until EOT grant of a temporary triggered ability to the target creature(s). Collected for `ON_DEATH`, `ON_SELF_LEAVES_BATTLEFIELD` (including `SelfExiledFromBattlefieldEffect`), `ON_DEALT_DAMAGE`, `ON_ATTACK`, `ON_COMBAT_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER`, `UPKEEP_TRIGGERED` and `ON_BECOMES_BLOCKED` (Barreling Attack: trample + "when that creature becomes blocked this turn, it gets +1/+1 for each creature blocking it")
- `RemoveKeywordEffect(Keyword, GrantScope)` or `(Keyword, GrantScope, PermanentPredicate filter)` — one-shot keyword removal until end of turn (floating layer-6 removal). Scopes handled by the normal handler: `SELF`, `TARGET`, `OPPONENT_CREATURES` (mass removal from opponents' creatures — Invert the Skies), and `ALL_CREATURES` (every creature — Hour of Devastation strips indestructible). In `STATIC` slot it's a continuous removal via the static handler's creature-scope matcher. Pass an `EffectDuration` — `(Keyword, GrantScope, EffectDuration)` or `(Keyword, GrantScope, filter, EffectDuration)` — for a non-end-of-turn loss; `EffectDuration.PERMANENT` = indefinite loss that lasts as long as the permanent stays on the battlefield (Elder Land Wurm's "When this creature blocks, it loses defender", ON_BLOCK). A later grant of the same keyword still re-adds it (timestamp ordering).
- `GrantChosenKeywordEffect(List<Keyword> options, GrantScope scope)` — prompt to choose one keyword from options, grant it until end of turn. `GrantScope.TARGET` grants to the target permanent (bind to its target group in multi-target spells — Golem Artisan, Practiced Offense); `GrantScope.SELF` is the untargeted form granting to the source permanent (Urza's Avenger, Illusionary Presence). No other scope is accepted
- `GrantChosenLandwalkToSelfEffect()` — prompt for any implemented land type, then the source gains that type of landwalk until end of turn. Supports nonbasic land types through a defender-land predicate. Illusionary Presence
- `GrantChosenSnowLandwalkToTargetEffect()` — prompt for a land type, then target creature gains **snow** landwalk of it until end of turn (can't be blocked while the defender controls a land that is both snow and that type, CR 702.14c). Never use a landwalk `Keyword` for this — those check the subtype only and would give plain landwalk. Stores a predicate in `Permanent.unblockableIfDefenderControlsUntilEndOfTurn`. Barbarian Guides (paired with `ReturnTargetPermanentToHandAtEndStepEffect`)
- `GrantKeywordEffect(..., GrantScope.TRIGGERING_PERMANENT)` — one-shot keyword grant to the permanent referenced by a triggered entry's `triggeringPermanentId`, such as the Vehicle a creature crews.
- `GrantLandwalkOfSacrificedLandToTargetEffect()` — target creature gains plain landwalk of each land type of the land sacrificed to pay the ability's `SacrificePermanentCost`, until end of turn. Reads the sacrificed land's card off `Permanent.chosenCard` (recorded at payment) and delegates the grant to `GrantKeywordEffectHandler`. benign CREATURE spec. Excavator
- `GrantFlashToCardTypeEffect(CardPredicate)` / `(CardPredicate, boolean appliesToAllPlayers)` — flash to card types (static); the 2-arg form grants it to every player, not just the controller (Aluren)
- `GrantFlashToSpellsThisTurnEffect()` — one-shot: controller may cast spells this turn as though they had flash (Alchemist's Refuge)
- `GrantFlashToCardTypeThisTurnEffect(CardType[, EffectDuration])` — one-shot: controller may cast spells of the given type as though they had flash; defaults to this turn and supports `UNTIL_YOUR_NEXT_TURN` (Winding Canyons; Arlinn, the Pack's Hope)
- `GrantFlashToCardTypeUntilNextTurnEffect(CardPredicate)` / `(CardType)` — one-shot: controller may cast matching spells as though they had flash until their next turn (Teferi, Time Raveler)
- `OpponentsCanCastSpellsOnlyAtSorcerySpeedEffect()` — static timing restriction: opponents of the source's controller can cast spells only during sorcery timing (Teferi, Time Raveler)
- `MayCastTopInstantOrSorceryFromGraveyardUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — until EOT, cast the top GY card if it is an instant/sorcery (normal cost; exile instead of GY). Bösium Strip; tracked in `GameData.mayCastTopInstantOrSorceryFromGraveyardUntilEndOfTurn`
- `GrantFlashToNextSpellOfTypeThisTurnEffect(CardType)` — one-shot: the next spell of that type the controller casts this turn can be cast as though it had flash (Quicken)
- `EmpowerNextCreatureSpellThisTurnEffect(boolean uncounterable, int additionalPlusOneCounters)` — one-shot: the next creature spell the controller casts this turn can't be countered / its creature enters with extra +1/+1 counters (Savage Summoning)
- `MakeNextSpellUncounterableThisTurnEffect()` — one-shot: the next spell the controller casts this turn can't be countered; tracked in `GameData.pendingNextSpellUncounterableThisTurnCount` and consumed by `GameData.recordSpellCast`
- `MakeNextInstantSorceryUncounterableThisTurnEffect()` — one-shot: the next instant or sorcery spell the controller casts this turn can't be countered; tracked in `GameData.pendingNextInstantSorceryUncounterableThisTurnCount` and consumed by `GameData.recordSpellCast`
- `SetSelfKeywordIndefinitelyEffect(Keyword keyword, boolean gained)` — "this creature gains/loses [keyword]" indefinitely (CR 611.2b), NOT until end of turn. Records a PERMANENT floating continuous effect on the source; latest activation per keyword replaces the previous one. Mist Dragon
- `SetSelfCantBeBlockedIndefinitelyEffect()` — non-targeting self ability: the source gains an unblockable restriction indefinitely by floating a self-scoped `GrantEffectEffect(CantBeBlockedEffect, SELF)` (Surge Engine)
- `FlashCastWithCleanupSacrificeEffect()` — STATIC on the card: the Mirage flash clause ("cast as though it had flash"; sacrificed at the next cleanup step if cast when a sorcery couldn't have been cast). No handler — casting timing, entry stamping and the cleanup sweep are engine-side (Ward of Lights, Necromancy)
- `BecomeAuraReanimateFromGraveyardEffect()` — ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD: intervening-if still on battlefield, become an Aura, put target creature card from any graveyard onto the battlefield under your control, and attach (Necromancy). `graveyardCard(ALL_GRAVEYARDS)` targetSpec; ETB graveyard-target flow chooses as the trigger goes on the stack
- `LicidBecomeAuraEffect(endCost)` — activated: source loses the ability, becomes an Aura with enchant creature and attaches to target creature; benign `CREATURE` targetSpec (Quickening Licid)
- `LicidEndEffect()` — the `endCost` payment installed on the Aura face by `LicidBecomeAuraEffect`; detaches and reverts to the printed creature. Never used on a card class
- `ProtectionFromChosenColorEffect(GrantScope.ENCHANTED_CREATURE)` — STATIC on an Aura: enchanted creature has protection from the Aura's chosen colour; pair with `ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD ChooseColorOnEnterEffect()`. The Aura is never detached by the protection it grants (Ward of Lights). No-arg = self-scoped, `ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD` (Voice of All)
- `GrantProtectionChoiceUntilEndOfTurnEffect(GrantScope.OWN_CREATURES, PermanentPredicate filter)` — untargeted mass grant: choose one colour, every creature you control matching `filter` gains protection from it until end of turn (Brave the Elements, `new PermanentColorInPredicate(Set.of(CardColor.WHITE))`). `null` filter = each creature you control; membership fixed on resolution; no prompt if nothing qualifies. The same record's `TARGET` / `SELF` scopes are the single- and self-target forms; `filter` is rejected on those
- `ReboundCastFromExileEffect()` — internal delayed-trigger effect: if the rebound card is still in exile, queues the existing `MayPlayExiledCardWithoutPayingManaCostEffect` offer; the `ExileFreeCastSupport` path handles the optional free recast
- `GrantProtectionFromColorsUntilYourNextTurnEffect(Set<CardColor>)` — target creature you control gains protection from the listed colors until your next turn. Uses a floating layer-6 effect, so the grant survives cleanup and expires with the ability controller's next turn. Sygg, Wanderbrine Shield.
- `GrantProtectionFromTargetPermanentColorsUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — target permanent's colors are snapshotted on resolution; each creature the ability controller controls gains protection from those colors until end of turn. Samite Elder.
- `GrantProtectionFromOpponentCreaturesUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — target creature you control gains protection from creatures controlled by its opponents until end of turn. The source/controller comparison is evaluated live, so the protection follows a later control change; cleared by `resetModifiers()`. Crypsis.
- `GrantMadnessEqualToManaCostEffect(CardPredicate)` — owned off-BF matching cards gain madness; cost = mana cost (static; Falkenrath Gorger)
- `GrantSpellCastingAbilityToSpellsEffect(Keyword, CardPredicate)` — spells you cast matching the predicate have the granted ability (static). `CONSPIRE`, `CONVOKE`, and `IMPROVISE` are consumed by cast-cost gates; `REBOUND` is consumed when a resolving hand spell is disposed. Other abilities are rejected as unsupported
- `GrantSpellCastingAbilityToSpellsEffect(Keyword, CardPredicate)` — spells you cast matching the predicate have the granted ability (static). `CONSPIRE` and `CONVOKE` are consumed by cast-cost gates; `REBOUND` is consumed when a resolving hand spell is disposed; `DELVE` adds a `DelveCost` to the cast-cost path. Other abilities are rejected as unsupported
- `CanAttackAsThoughHasteUnlessEnteredThisTurnEffect()` — STATIC self-scoped: this creature can attack as though it had haste unless it entered the battlefield this turn (Chaos Lord); attack-only bypass read in `AttackLegalityService.canAttack`. Gate the parity half of the card with `ConditionalEffect(new TotalPermanentCountEven(), …)`
- `ActivateCreatureAbilitiesAsThoughHasteEffect()` — controller may activate abilities of creatures they control as though they had haste (static; lifts summoning-sickness on ability activation only, does NOT grant haste — Thousand-Year Elixir)
- `SpendWhiteManaAsRedEffect()` — controller may spend white mana as though it were red mana (static; Sunglasses of Urza). Rides the per-player `ManaPool.isWhiteSpendableAsRed` flag honored by `ManaCost.canPay`/`pay`; set via `GameQueryService.canSpendWhiteManaAsRed`
- `SpendWhiteManaAsAnyColorEffect()` — controller may spend white mana as mana of any color, and other mana only as colorless (static; Celestial Dawn). Rides the per-player `ManaPool.isWhiteSpendableAsAnyColor` flag; `ManaCost` rewrites the pool (non-white colored → colorless, white → the pips the cost needs) before the ordinary payment path
- `ReplaceColoredManaWithWhiteUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — this turn, colored mana produced by the controller's spells and abilities is white (False Dawn); tracked by `GameData.playersWithColoredManaReplacementThisTurn`
- `SpendWhiteManaAsAnyColorUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — this turn, the controller may spend white mana as though it were mana of any color without changing how other mana is spent (False Dawn); tracked by `GameData.playersWithWhiteManaAsAnyColorThisTurn`
- `GrantActivatedAbilityEffect(ActivatedAbility, GrantScope)` or `(ActivatedAbility, GrantScope, PermanentPredicate)` — grant ability. `GrantScope.SELF_AND_PAIRED` = source + its soulbond partner (Deadeye Navigator). Static land scopes `OWN_LANDS` / `ALL_LANDS` grant to lands you control / every land (Blanket of Night = `{T}: Add {B}` on `ALL_LANDS`); `ALL_LANDS_INCLUDING_SELF` also covers the source land itself (subtype grants only — Urborg, Tomb of Yawgmoth). A `WHILE_SOURCE_REMAINS` `OWN_CREATURES` grant is represented as one source-linked floating scope, so creatures that enter later and creatures controlled after the source changes controller are evaluated live.
- `GrantKeywordEffect(Keyword, GrantScope.SELF_AND_PAIRED)` — grant keyword to source + soulbond partner while paired (Lightning Mauler haste); wrap in `ConditionalEffect(new SourceIsPaired(), …)` on STATIC
- `GrantTriggeredAbilityEffect(EffectSlot, CardEffect, GrantScope)` — continuously grant a *triggered* ability (STATIC slot); Tandem Lookout grants `(ON_DAMAGE_TO_PLAYER, DrawCardEffect(1), SELF_AND_PAIRED)` inside `ConditionalEffect(new SourceIsPaired(), …)`. Unlike `GrantEffectToTargetUntilEndOfTurnEffect` it is layer-recomputed, so it turns off when the condition stops holding. `ON_ATTACK` grants are collected off each attacker in `CombatAttackService` (Thorncaster Sliver: `(ON_ATTACK, DealDamageToAnyTargetEffect(1), ALL_OWN_CREATURES, PermanentHasSubtypePredicate(SLIVER))`)
- `SoulbondChoosePartnerEffect()` — Soulbond self-ETB: choose another unpaired creature you control to pair with (wrap in `MayEffect` on `ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD`)
- `SoulbondPairWithEnteringEffect()` — Soulbond "another creature enters": may pair this unpaired creature with the entering unpaired creature (`ON_ALLY_CREATURE_ENTERS_BATTLEFIELD`; collector wraps as may)
- `ConditionalEffect(new SourceIsPaired(), wrapped)` — "as long as ~ is paired with another creature"
- `BoostPairedCreatureOfTargetEffect(power, toughness)` — one-shot soulbond rider: "if it's paired with a creature, that creature also gets +X/+Y until end of turn". Boosts the target's `pairedWithId` partner only; pair it with `BoostTargetCreatureEffect` in the same target group (Joint Assault)
- `GrantAdditionalBlockEffect(int)` or `(int, PermanentPredicate controlledFilter)` — block N additional (filter → each controlled permanent matching predicate, e.g. Cenn's Tactician)
- `GrantAdditionalBlockToTargetUntilEndOfTurnEffect(int)` — target creature can block N additional creatures **this turn** (one-shot, until-EOT; SPELL slot). Temporary sibling of `GrantAdditionalBlockEffect`. Act of Heroism. `(int, GrantScope.SELF)` grants it to the source instead (untargeted activated ability — Mounted Archers)
- `RegenerateEffect()` or `(boolean targetsPermanent)` or `(boolean targetsPermanent, boolean opponentMayDrawOnRegenerate)` or `withMinusOneCounterOnRegenerate()` or `enchantedCreature()` or `withGainControlOnRegenerate()` — regenerate. Rider flags tag the shield so secondary effects fire only when it is actually spent: Soldevi Sentry's `opponentMayDrawOnRegenerate` (`Permanent.opponentDrawRegenerationShield`), Matopi Golem's `putMinusOneCounterOnRegenerate` (`Permanent.minusOneCounterRegenerationShield`) and Debt of Loyalty's `gainControlOnRegenerate` (`Permanent.gainControlRegenerationShields` — remembers the spell's controller, who gains permanent control when the shield is spent). Do NOT pair a plain `RegenerateEffect` with a sibling rider effect — that would pay out on activation even if the creature never regenerates. Plain shields are consumed first, so a tagged shield is only spent once it is all that is left. A plain `RegenerateEffect()` on an Aura/Equipment ability is auto-redirected to the attached creature; use `enchantedCreature()` when the source is not always an Aura (Nurturing Licid), so the ability no-ops instead of regenerating itself
- `RegeneratesIfWouldBeDestroyedEffect()` — STATIC self-replacement: "if this creature would be destroyed, regenerate it" — always-on intrinsic regeneration (regenerates every time, no shield consumed); honored by `GraveyardService.tryRegenerate`. Mossbridge Troll
- `TotemArmorEffect()` — STATIC Aura replacement: if its attached creature would be destroyed, remove all damage from that creature and destroy this Aura instead; it is not regeneration, so it does not tap or remove the creature from combat and is not stopped by regeneration-prevention effects. Eland Umbra
- `PreventTargetCreatureRegenerationThisTurnEffect()` — "target creature can't be regenerated this turn": sets `cantRegenerateThisTurn` on the target so `GraveyardService.tryRegenerate` refuses it for the rest of the turn (beats shields AND intrinsic regen); cleared at turn cleanup. `targetSpec()` narrows to a CREATURE (harmful). Also a `CombatOpponentReferencingEffect` for ON_BLOCK / ON_BECOMES_BLOCKED PER_BLOCKER (Lim-DAdz?l's Cohort). Hurr Jackal
- `PhaseOutSelfAndCombatOpponentEffect()` — "whenever this creature blocks or becomes blocked by a creature, this creature and that creature phase out": a `CombatOpponentReferencingEffect` for ON_BLOCK / ON_BECOMES_BLOCKED PER_BLOCKER. Handler phases out the stack entry's source permanent and its non-targeting target via `PhasingService.phaseOut(...)` — both leave the battlefield for `GameData.phasedOutPermanents` (CR 702.26b), are removed from combat (CR 506.4), and phase in during their own controller's next untap step (CR 702.26a). Dream Fighter
- `PhaseOutEffect(PhaseOutSubject.SOURCE)` — "this creature phases out" with no combat opponent (activated ability). Single-permanent sibling of the above; source plus attachments move to `GameData.phasedOutPermanents` and phase in on its controller’s next untap step. Mist Dragon, Crystal Golem. Also usable as a `ForcedCostOrElseEffect` fallback ("phases out unless you pay {cost}" — Vaporous Djinn)
- `PhaseOutEffect(PhaseOutSubject.ATTACHED)` — "{cost}: Enchanted creature phases out" on an Aura (Vanishing). Non-targeting; finds host via the source's `attachedTo`; the Aura phases out indirectly with the host (CR 702.26g)
- `PhaseOutAttackingCreatureAtEndOfCombatEffect()` — "Whenever a creature you control attacks, it phases out at end of combat" (Teferi's Veil). Put on `ON_ALLY_CREATURE_ATTACKS`; non-targeting — the triggering attacker is the entry's `targetId`. Resolution queues a `PhaseOutAtEndOfCombat` delayed action drained in `CombatService.processEndOfCombatPhaseOuts()`, so the creature still deals its combat damage before phasing out via `PhasingService.phaseOut(...)`
- `PreventPhaseOutEffect(PhaseOutSubject)` — "until your next upkeep, [permanent] can't phase out". `TARGET` = chosen permanent (Spatial Binding, the only subject with a `benign(PERMANENT)` spec); `SOURCE` = the ability's own permanent (Ertai's Familiar, non-targeting). Marks `Permanent.cantPhaseOutUntilUpkeepOf`; `PhasingService` skips marked permanents, `StepTriggerService.handleUpkeepTriggers` clears the mark at the marking player's next upkeep. `ATTACHED` is not supported.
- `PhaseOutEffect(PhaseOutSubject.TARGET)` — "target permanent phases out". The only subject with a `targetSpec()` (generic `harmful(PERMANENT)`); pair with a `PermanentPredicateTargetFilter` to restrict the type (Reality Ripple).
- `PhaseOutPermanentsEffect(PermanentPredicate filter, boolean controllerOnly)` — "all lands you control phase out": mass, non-targeting phase-out of every matching permanent (own battlefield only when `controllerOnly`). Taniwha (`UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`, `PermanentIsLandPredicate`).
- `PhaseOutChosenTypeNontokenPermanentsEffect()` — each upkeep the active player chooses artifact/creature/land/non-Aura enchantment; all nontoken permanents of that type (any controller) phase out. Teferi's Realm (`EACH_UPKEEP_TRIGGERED`). Choice via `ChoiceContext.TeferisRealmTypeChoice`.
- `TimeAndTideEffect()` — "simultaneously, all phased-out creatures phase in and all creatures with phasing phase out". Non-targeting; `PhasingService.applyTimeAndTide` collects both halves then moves. Attachments follow (CR 702.26g). Time and Tide
- `EquipoiseEffect()` — upkeep: for each land/artifact/creature target player controls in excess of yours, choose that many of theirs to phase out (lands → artifacts → creatures). `harmful(PLAYER)`; pair with `PlayerPredicateTargetFilter(ANY)`. Equipoise
- `PreventRegenerationOfCreaturesDamagedBySourceThisTurnEffect()` — until end of turn, the source (or targeted creature) gains "Creatures dealt damage by this creature this turn can't be regenerated this turn": sets `damagedCreaturesCantRegenerateThisTurn` on that permanent, and `GraveyardService.tryRegenerate` refuses any creature that source damaged this turn (`GameData.creatureCardsDamagedThisTurnBySourcePermanent`, so pre-activation damage counts). Cleared at turn cleanup. Bone Shaman / Runesword
- `ProtectionFromColorsEffect(Set<CardColor>)` — protection from colors (static)
- `GrantProtectionFromColorUntilEndOfTurnEffect(CardColor, GrantScope.OWN_CREATURES)` — fixed-color protection until end of turn for each creature controlled by the ability controller; use one conditional instance per printed condition (Dominaria's Judgment)
- `ProtectionFromColoredSpellsEffect()` — protection from colored spells only (static)
- `ProtectionFromSubtypesEffect(Set<CardSubtype>[, boolean creatureSourcesOnly])` — protection from subtypes (static); `creatureSourcesOnly=true` = "protection from [subtype] creatures" (source must also be a creature)
- `GrantProtectionFromChosenTypeToOwnCreaturesEffect(CardSubtype recipientSubtype)` — STATIC: creatures you control with `recipientSubtype` have protection from creatures of the source's chosen type; pair with `ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD ChooseSubtypeOnEnterEffect()` (Riders of Gavony)
- `ProtectionFromManaValueEffect(int minManaValue)` — protection from sources with mana value ≥ N (static, Mistmeadow Skulk)
- `ProtectionFromEverythingEffect()` — protection from everything: every source, any colour/type/subtype/mana value (static, Progenitus)
- `GrantProtectionFromEverythingUntilNextTurnEffect()` — controller gains protection from everything until their next turn; also uses the player-wide next-turn damage-prevention window (The Stasis Coffin)
- `GrantSubtypeToTargetCreatureEffect(CardSubtype[, EffectDuration])` — target creature "becomes a [subtype] in addition to its other types"; permanent grants use `grantedSubtypes`, while source-linked durations use a layered floating grant
- `GrantSubtypeToTargetCreatureEffect(CardSubtype)` — target creature "becomes a [subtype] in addition to its other types" (permanent, added to `grantedSubtypes`)
- `GrantSubtypeToChosenPermanentEffect(CardSubtype)` — permanently grants a subtype to the permanent chosen earlier in the same resolution (`chosenPermanentId`), for compound effects such as amass
- `GrantSubtypeUntilEndOfTurnEffect(CardSubtype, GrantScope)` — additively grants a creature subtype until end of turn; `TARGET_PLAYERS_CREATURES` snapshots each creature controlled by the targeted player and records temporary layered grants. Curious Colossus.
- `TargetCreatureBecomesSubtypeUntilEndOfTurnEffect(CardSubtype)` — target creature **becomes** the given creature type until end of turn, **replacing** all its other creature types (Boldwyr Intimidator: "target creature becomes a Coward"). Sets `Permanent.transientCreatureTypeOverride`, read by the layered pass; contrast the additive/permanent `GrantSubtypeToTargetCreatureEffect`. The `(CardSubtype, GrantScope)` overload widens it to a group: `TARGET_PLAYERS_CREATURES` (Polymorphist's Jest) or `OWN_CREATURES` (every creature you control, no target — Dragonshift overloaded)
- `TargetCreatureBecomesChosenSubtypeUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — at resolution, choose a creature type other than Wall, then target creature becomes that type until end of turn, replacing all its other creature types (Unnatural Selection)
- `SourceBecomesSubtypeUntilEndOfTurnEffect(CardSubtype)` — self analog (no target): the **source** permanent becomes the given creature type until end of turn, replacing all its other creature types (Paragon of the Amesha: "this creature becomes an Angel"). Pair with `BoostSelfEffect` + `GrantKeywordEffect(..., GrantScope.SELF)` for "becomes a [type], gets +X/+X, and gains [keywords]"
- `TargetCreatureBecomesSubtypeUntilEndOfTurnEffect(CardSubtype)` — target creature **becomes** the given creature type until end of turn, **replacing** all its other creature types (Boldwyr Intimidator: "target creature becomes a Coward"). Sets `Permanent.transientCreatureTypeOverride`, read by the layered pass; contrast the additive/permanent `GrantSubtypeToTargetCreatureEffect`
- `SourceBecomesSubtypeUntilEndOfTurnEffect(CardSubtype)` or `(List<CardSubtype>)` — self analog (no target): the **source** permanent becomes the given creature type(s) until end of turn, replacing all its other creature types (Paragon of the Amesha: "this creature becomes an Angel"). Pair with `BoostSelfEffect` + `GrantKeywordEffect(..., GrantScope.SELF)` for "becomes a [type], gets +X/+X, and gains [keywords]"
- `GrantBasicLandTypeToTargetEffect(EffectDuration[, CardSubtype fixedSubtype][, boolean replacing][, List<CardSubtype> allowedTypes])` — target land becomes a chosen basic land type. Default adds "in addition to its other types" (Navigator's Compass / Aquitect's Will); `replacing=true` makes the land **become** the type, losing its others per rule 305.7 (Tideshaper Mystic, UNTIL_END_OF_TURN; Orcish Farmer = fixed SWAMP, UNTIL_CONTROLLERS_NEXT_UNTAP_STEP — survives end of turn, cleared at the land controller's next untap step). A non-empty `allowedTypes` list narrows the resolution-time choice (Tundra Kavu = PLAINS or ISLAND).
- `NonbasicLandsBecomeTypeEffect(CardSubtype)` — STATIC, global: every nonbasic land (any controller) becomes the basic land type, losing its other land types/abilities and producing that type's mana per rule 305.7. Basic lands unaffected. Blood Moon (MOUNTAIN)
- `LoseAllLandTypesEffect(GrantScope, PermanentPredicate)` — STATIC layer-4 effect: matching lands lose all land subtypes while retaining their other card types; the layer-6 `LosesAllAbilitiesEffect` and a `GrantActivatedAbilityEffect` can be composed with it for Alpine Moon.
- `ControlledLandsBecomeTypeEffect(CardSubtype)` — STATIC: every land **you** control (basic or nonbasic) becomes that basic land type, losing its other land types/abilities per rule 305.7. Other players' lands unaffected. Celestial Dawn (PLAINS)
- `LandsOfSubtypeBecomeTypeEffect(CardSubtype from, CardSubtype to)` — STATIC, global: every land (any controller, basic or nonbasic) carrying the `from` land subtype becomes the `to` basic land type, losing its other land types/abilities and producing that type's mana per rule 305.7. Lands lacking `from` are unaffected. Layer-4 (`LayerSystemService`), managed like Blood Moon. Conversion ("All Mountains are Plains" = `(MOUNTAIN, PLAINS)`)
- `BoostCreaturesOfChosenSubtypeEffect(p, t[, CounterType scalingCounter[, boolean allControllers[, boolean excludeSelf]]])` — STATIC: creatures of the source's `chosenSubtype` get +p/+t. `allControllers=true` boosts every controller's (Brass Herald); `excludeSelf=true` / `BoostCreaturesOfChosenSubtypeEffect.otherOwnCreatures(p, t)` = "**other** creatures you control of the chosen type" (Adaptive Automaton). `scalingCounter` multiplies by that counter's count on the source (Door of Destinies)
