- `AllPermanentsGainChosenColorEffect()` — STATIC layer-5 additive color grant: all permanents (any controller, including lands and the source) gain the source's chosen color *in addition to* their other colors (not replacing). Pair with `ChooseColorOnEnterEffect` (Painter's Servant). Battlefield permanents only — does not recolor spells on the stack or cards in other zones
- `BecomeAllColorsUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — self-scoped layer-5 color set: the source permanent becomes all five colors until end of turn (no target/choice). Floats a `BecomeChosenColorsUntilEndOfTurnEffect` with every color on the source (Scrapbasket `{1}:`)
- `SetChosenColorUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — target permanent becomes the color of your choice until end of turn; `SetChosenColorUntilEndOfTurnEffect(false, false)` is the self-scoped form with no target. Used by Distorting Lens, Blind Seer, and Rainbow Crow
- `BecomeColorlessUntilEndOfTurnEffect(boolean targeted)` — layer-5 color set: a permanent becomes colorless (empty color set, CR 105.3) until end of turn. `false` = self-scoped, no target (Raging Spirit `{2}:`); `true` = `benign(PERMANENT)` target (Ersatz Gnomes `{T}:`). Until-end-of-turn sibling of the static `BecomeColorlessEffect`, whose `GrantScope.ALL_PERMANENTS` form covers every battlefield permanent, including the source (Thran Lens)
- `BecomeColorlessIndefinitelyEffect(boolean targeted)` — the permanent becomes **colorless indefinitely** (CR 611.2b); floats `BecomeColorlessUntilEndOfTurnEffect` with `EffectDuration.PERMANENT`. `false` = self-scoped, `true` = targets a permanent. Indefinite sibling of `BecomeColorlessUntilEndOfTurnEffect`; use it (not `SetTargetColorEffect(null)`) inside an activated ability, since `SetTargetColorEffect` also declares a spell-on-stack target and would make the ability ask for one. Xathrid Gorgon
- `BecomeChosenColorsIndefinitelyEffect([boolean targeted])` — controller chooses one or more colors; the permanent becomes those colors **indefinitely** (floats `BecomeChosenColorsUntilEndOfTurnEffect` with `EffectDuration.PERMANENT`). No-arg = self-scoped, wrap in `MayEffect` for "you may" (Shyft upkeep); `true` = targets a permanent (Prismatic Lace)
- `SetTargetColorEffect(CardColor, boolean spellOnly)` — SPELL or ability; a **null** color = becomes colorless, `spellOnly` narrows the spec to `SPELL_ON_STACK` (Ersatz Gnomes); "target spell or permanent becomes [color]" **indefinitely** (Purelace → white). Layer-5 setter with `EffectDuration.PERMANENT` (no wear-off). Targets spell OR permanent like Glamerdye (spec `benign(PERMANENT)` + `targetsSpellOnStack`, no `target()` call); a color set on a permanent spell carries to the permanent via `GameData.spellColorOverrides` (CR 613.7). `chosenColorForSpell()` reuses the same indefinite spell path with a resolution-time color choice
- `SetSelfColorIndefinitelyEffect(CardColor)` — non-targeting self ability: the source becomes the specified color indefinitely by floating the existing `SetTargetColorEffect` layer-5 setter (Surge Engine)
- `ChooseSubtypeOnEnterEffect()` — choose creature type ETB
- `AllColorWordsBecomeChosenColorEffect()` — global text-changing static: every color word on every spell and permanent becomes the source's chosen color word. Pair with `ChooseColorOnEnterEffect` (read back via `Permanent.getChosenColor()`). Records no `TextReplacement` — the substitution is derived from the battlefield on each text-change query, so it starts and stops with this permanent; consumed directly by `TextChangeTransformer`, no static handler. Swirl the Mists

- `RedirectAllDamageToTargetCreatureToControllerEffect()` — spell targeting a creature: all damage that would be dealt to that creature this turn is dealt to the spell's controller instead (Sivvi's Valor). Installs an unlimited any-source `CreatureDamageRedirectShield` whose destination is a player; the existing combat and noncombat creature-damage paths process it through `pendingSourceRedirectDamage`
- `RedirectAllDamageFromTargetAttackingCreatureToSelfEffect()` — activated ability with a benign attacking-creature target: all combat and noncombat damage that would be dealt to the controller this turn by that attacking creature is dealt to the ability's source creature instead (Shimian Night Stalker). Uses a source-specific turn redirect shield and leaves damage to the controller's other permanents untouched
- `RedirectAllCreatureDamageToControllerEffect()` — spell: damage that would be dealt to any creature this turn is dealt to the spell's controller instead (Blood of the Martyr). Registers the controller in `GameData.playersRedirectingAllCreatureDamage`; both combat and noncombat creature-damage paths process the replacement through `pendingSourceRedirectDamage`
- `RedirectAllDamageToEnchantedCreatureControllerEffect()` — STATIC Aura marker: all damage that would be dealt to the enchanted creature is dealt to its current controller instead (Treacherous Link). The combat and noncombat creature-damage paths query the attached Aura dynamically and route the replacement through `pendingSourceRedirectDamage`
- `BecomeChosenColorEffect()` — STATIC self-scoped color setter: the source permanent becomes its stored chosen color, replacing its other colors. Pair with `ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD ChooseColorOnEnterEffect` (Alloy Golem)

## Provider map

`PayXManaRevealTopCardsToHandRestToBottomRandomEffect(CardPredicate filter)` is a resolution-time optional pay-X library reveal: reveal the top X cards, put every matching card into hand, and put the rest on the bottom in a random order. Choosing X=0 declines; Depala, Pilot Exemplar uses a Dwarf-or-Vehicle filter.

`RemoveAllCountersThenDestroyReferencedPermanentAndDamageControllerEffect(PermanentReference, CounterType, int)` is a non-targeting threshold effect: it removes the named counters from the referenced permanent, destroys it, then has that permanent deal fixed damage to its controller. Pair it with `StateTriggerEffect`'s `PermanentPredicate` reference overload when a state trigger needs to preserve a permanent reference.

All normal (stack-resolution) effects: one `NormalEffectHandlerBean` `@Component` per effect in `service/effect/normalfx/`, auto-registered by `GameEngineConfig`. Shared logic in `*Support` classes in the same package.

"You may …" accept/decline abilities follow the mirror pattern: one `MayEffectHandlerBean` `@Component` per effect in `service/effect/mayfx/` (declares `handledEffect()`, implements `handle(GameData, Player, boolean accepted, PendingMayAbility)`), auto-registered into `MayEffectHandlerRegistry` by `GameEngineConfig`. `MayAbilityHandlerService.handleMayAbilityChosen` runs its non-effect preambles, then iterates the ability's `effects()` in list order and dispatches to the first registered handler. The effect-keyed routing chain is now fully migrated; the residue in `MayAbilityHandlerService` is the generic accept/stack-construction path, the graveyard target-selection helpers, the CR 603.5 resolution-time may path, and Galvanoth's guarded `CastTopOfLibraryWithoutPayingManaCostEffect` branch — all kept because they are entangled with target-selection/construction state, not because they are unmigrated routing. Beans that just orchestrate a sub-service (`MayCastHandlerService`, `MayMiscHandlerService`, `MayCopyHandlerService`, `MayPenaltyChoiceHandlerService`, `TariffSupport`) inject it; inline beans inject `GameLogService`/`GameQueryService`/`InputCompletionService`/etc. directly. An interface-typed may-effect gets one bean per concrete implementor (registry keys on `effect.getClass()`), e.g. `CounterUnlessEffect` → `CounterUnlessPaysEffect` + `CounterUnlessDiscardsEffect`.

| Category | Handler package | Shared helpers |
|----------|-----------------|----------------|
| Damage | `normalfx/*EffectHandler` | `DamageSupport` |
| Destruction | `normalfx/*EffectHandler` | `DestructionSupport` |
| Bounce | `normalfx/*EffectHandler` | `BounceSupport` |
| Counter | `normalfx/*EffectHandler` | `CounterSupport` |
| Library/search/mill/shuffle | `normalfx/*EffectHandler` | `LibraryRevealSupport`, `LibrarySearchSupport`, `LibraryShuffleSupport` |
| Graveyard/exile | `normalfx/*EffectHandler` | `GraveyardReturnSupport` |
| Draw/discard/choices | `normalfx/*EffectHandler` | `PlayerInteractionSupport` |
| Life | `normalfx/*EffectHandler` | `LifeSupport` |
| Boost/tap/keyword/animation | `normalfx/*EffectHandler` | `TapUntapSupport`, `AnimationSupport` |
| Permanent control/tokens/counters | `normalfx/*EffectHandler` | `PermanentControlSupport`, `PermanentCounterSupport` |
| Static effects | `staticfx/*Handler` (see **STATIC_EFFECT_HANDLERS.md**) | `StaticEffectSupport` |
| Cast-cost modifiers (reductions/taxes) | `cast/costmod/*Handler` (see **COST_MODIFICATION_HANDLERS.md**) | `CostModificationSupport` |
| Prevention | `normalfx/*EffectHandler` | `PreventionSupport` |
| Turn | `normalfx/*EffectHandler` | `TurnSupport` |
| Copy/retarget | `normalfx/*EffectHandler` | `CopySupport`, `TargetRedirectionSupport` |
| Exile / return from exile | `normalfx/*EffectHandler` | `ExileSupport` |
| Combat restriction / equip / win | `normalfx/*EffectHandler` | `CardSpecificSupport` (card-specific) |
- `ChooseTwoCreaturesByPowerDifferenceEffect()` — choose exactly two creatures you control at resolution; draw cards equal to the absolute difference between their powers, then give both +X/+X and trample until end of turn. With one creature, X is 0 and it still gains trample.

Tablet of the Guilds uses `ChooseColorOnEnterEffect(2)` to store two distinct colors on the entering permanent, then `GainLifeForEachChosenColorSpellCastEffect()` in `ON_CONTROLLER_CASTS_SPELL` to materialize one `GainLifeEffect` per selected color present in the spell.

`PreventAllButOneDamageToControllerAndPlaneswalkersEffect()` is a static recipient-scoped prevention marker for Ajani Steadfast's emblem; `DamagePreventionService.applyAllButOneDamagePrevention` applies it to player and planeswalker damage in combat and noncombat paths.

`PreventDamageFromChosenSourceEffect.nextDamageToTargetCreature()` is Samite Blessing's activated-ability form: the source is chosen on resolution, and the recipient-specific one-shot shield is consumed only by that source's damage to the targeted creature.

- `RemoveAllCountersFromTargetPermanentEffect()` — remove every concrete counter from target permanent; no-op if it has none and does not stop it from receiving counters later. "Remove all counters from target permanent" (Vampire Hexmage)

- `SharedColorDiscardAlternativeCostEffect()` — battlefield static permission to cast a colored spell by discarding a different card from hand that shares a color with it instead of paying its mana cost. Symmetric across players; payment moves the selected card to the graveyard and fires discard triggers (Dream Halls).
- `CombatDamageAmountAwareEffect` — exposes the dynamic amount that should receive the combat damage event value when a trigger is collected. Used by `AwardPersistentManaEffect` and `EnergyCountersEffect`, including when the trigger comes from an attached Aura.
- `AwardPersistentManaEffect(ManaColor, DynamicAmount[, Recipient])` — triggered mana production that adds the dynamic amount as persistent mana, surviving step and phase transitions until end of turn. The default recipient is the controller; `Recipient.TARGET_PLAYER` routes it to the stack entry's recorded player (Shizuko, Caller of Autumn). `EventValue` reads the combat damage that caused the trigger (Mark of Sakiko).
- `AttachAllEquipmentToSourceEffect()` — non-targeting ETB effect that attaches every legally attachable Equipment permanent on the battlefield to the source permanent, including Equipment controlled by opponents. Used by Vulshok Battlemaster.
- `AttachMatchingEquipmentToCreatedPermanentEffect(PermanentPredicate)` — non-targeting follow-up effect for a token-creation sequence; attaches matching Equipment to the first permanent created earlier by the same resolution, using the stack entry controller as the source-controller context. Used by Helm of Kaldra.
`PreventNextDamageByTargetCreatureEffect()` is a benign creature-targeted SPELL effect for Awe Strike: the target is the damage source, so resolution installs a source-wide one-shot shield and the spell controller gains the prevented amount. The shared source-shield path covers combat and noncombat damage to any recipient.
- `ExileTopCardMayDealDamageOrPlayEffect()` — exile the controller's top card; offer to deal damage to the target creature equal to that card's mana value, otherwise grant permission to play the exiled card until end of turn
- `RedirectTargetCreatureNextDamageFromChosenSourceToSelfEffect()` — activated ability targeting a creature: the next single damage event a chosen source (picked on resolution) would deal to that creature this turn is dealt to the ability's source permanent instead, then consumed (Shaman en-Kor). Same `creatureDamageRedirectShields` machinery as Oracle's Attendants, but redirects to the ability source and only for the next event (`CreatureDamageRedirectShield.NEXT_EVENT`)
- `ReturnEnchantedCreatureAndSelfToHandAtEndOfCombatEffect()` — Aura attack trigger that returns the source Aura and its enchanted creature to their owners' hands at end of combat (Contempt); queues the Aura first so it is not orphaned before the creature returns
- `EachCreatureControllerSacrificesPermanentUnlessPaysEffect(String manaCost)` — SPELL: "For each creature, its controller sacrifices a permanent of their choice unless they pay `manaCost`." Players choose creatures to pay for, then choose arbitrary permanents for the remaining creatures in active-player order; payments and sacrifices are applied together (Fade Away, `"{1}"`)
- `DestroyEquippedCreatureAndCombatOpponentEffect()` — immediate combat trigger for Dead-Iron Sledge: destroys the equipped creature and its combat opponent as one batch. The trigger entry captures the equipped creature in `triggeringPermanentId` and carries the opponent as its non-targeting `targetId`; use ON_BLOCK + ON_BECOMES_BLOCKED (`TriggerMode.PER_BLOCKER`).
- `ReturnTargetCreatureUnlessControllerPaysEffect(String manaCost)` — return target creature to its owner's hand unless its controller pays the mana cost; prompt the target controller when able to pay, and bounce on inability or decline (Crystal Shard).
- `RevealHandAndDiscardDuplicateNonlandCardsEffect()` — target player reveals their hand, then every nonland card whose name appears more than once in that hand is discarded automatically (Hint of Insanity)
- `TaintedPactEffect()` — exile the top card of your library and offer each unique card to your hand one at a time; a decline continues the process, acceptance stops it, and a duplicate name stops it without a choice. `TaintedPactCardChoiceEffect` carries the names exiled by this resolution through the pending may-ability flow.
- `UnattachEnchantedEquipmentEffect()` — non-targeting ETB effect; unattaches the Equipment enchanted by the source Aura and applies its sacrifice-on-unattach behavior (Carry Away)
- `RedirectCombatDamageToTargetAttackingCreatureEffect()` — SPELL (harmful attacking-creature target): "all combat damage that would be dealt to you this turn is dealt to target attacking creature instead" (Turn the Tables). Installs a player-only, combat-only `TurnDamageRedirectToCreatureShield`; the combat path records redirected damage against the target attacking creature so combat-damage triggers and lifelink still see it. Cleared at turn cleanup
- `RedirectCombatDamageFromTargetUnblockedCreatureToItsControllerEffect()` — SPELL (benign unblocked-attacking-creature target): "all combat damage that would be dealt to you this turn by target unblocked creature is dealt to its controller instead" (Mirror Strike). Installs a source-specific turn shield and applies it only to combat damage headed to the spell controller; redirected damage uses the normal source-redirect path and the shield clears at turn cleanup
- `BoostTargetCreatureAndAllWithSameNameEffect(DynamicAmount, DynamicAmount)` / `(int, int)` — target creature and each other creature with the same name get +X/+Y until end of turn; only the named target is targeted, and the effect fizzles if it leaves. Echoing Decay uses `(-2, -2)`.
- `PreventDamageToCreaturesByCreaturesOfSharedColorEffect()` — STATIC: prevent damage dealt to a creature by another creature when the two creatures currently share a color. Uses layer-aware effective colors and applies to both combat and noncombat creature damage (Well-Laid Plans).
- `GrantBloodthirstToCastSpellEffect(int amount)` — "it gains bloodthirst N" (Bloodlord of Vaasgoth). Place inside a `SpellCastTriggerEffect` in `ON_CONTROLLER_CASTS_SPELL`; resolves by stamping `StackEntry.grantedBloodthirst` on the triggering spell (grants accumulate, CR 702.54c), which `StackResolutionService` carries to `Permanent.grantedBloodthirst` and `BattlefieldEntryService.applyGrantedBloodthirst` turns into +1/+1 counters as an as-enters replacement gated on `OpponentDealtDamageThisTurn(1)` (CR 702.54a). Printed bloodthirst N is just `ON_ENTER_BATTLEFIELD ConditionalEffect(new OpponentDealtDamageThisTurn(1), new EnterWithCountersEffect(PLUS_ONE_PLUS_ONE, new Fixed(N)))` — no new effect needed
- `SetChosenColorForTargetCreaturesUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — any number of target creatures become one color chosen at resolution; prompts once and applies the existing temporary color-setting layer to every surviving target. Used by Sway of Illusion
- `ChooseNameRevealTopCardsToHandRestToExileEffect(int count)` — controller names a non-basic-land card, reveals the top `count` cards of their library; every revealed card with that name goes to hand, the rest are exiled (Desperate Research = 7)
`CopyNextLoyaltyAbilityThisTurnEffect()` is a one-shot delayed trigger for "when you next activate a loyalty ability this turn, copy that ability". It uses `GameData.pendingNextLoyaltyAbilityCopyThisTurnCount` and is cleared at turn cleanup.
`CopyNextSpellCastThisTurnEffect()` is a one-shot delayed trigger for "copy the next spell you cast this turn". It uses `GameData.pendingNextSpellCopyThisTurnCount`; permanent spell copies become tokens and may choose new targets. The `(CardPredicate, Set<CardSupertype>)` form handles a filtered next spell and characteristic exceptions such as removing legendary from a creature copy. Used by Flamehold Grappler and The Clone Saga.
`CopyNextExhaustAbilityThisTurnEffect()` is a one-shot delayed trigger for "when you next activate an exhaust ability that isn't a mana ability this turn, copy it". It uses `GameData.pendingNextExhaustAbilityCopyThisTurnCount`; the pending copy is consumed only by a non-mana exhaust activation and is cleared at turn cleanup.
`PreventNextDamageByTargetCreatureEffect()` is a benign creature-targeted SPELL effect for Awe Strike: the target is the damage source, so resolution installs a source-wide one-shot shield and the spell controller gains the prevented amount. The shared source-shield path covers combat and noncombat damage to any recipient.
- `RedirectTargetCreatureNextDamageFromChosenSourceToSelfEffect()` — activated ability targeting a creature: the next single damage event a chosen source (picked on resolution) would deal to that creature this turn is dealt to the ability's source permanent instead, then consumed (Shaman en-Kor). Same `creatureDamageRedirectShields` machinery as Oracle's Attendants, but redirects to the ability source and only for the next event (`CreatureDamageRedirectShield.NEXT_EVENT`)
- `ReturnEnchantedCreatureAndSelfToHandAtEndOfCombatEffect()` — Aura attack trigger that returns the source Aura and its enchanted creature to their owners' hands at end of combat (Contempt); queues the Aura first so it is not orphaned before the creature returns
- `ExileMatchingCardsFromTargetPlayerHandEffect(CardPredicate filter)` — exile every matching card from a target player's hand (no choice or play permission; not a discard). `TargetSpec.harmful(PLAYER)`. Pair with `RevealTargetHandEffect()` and `ExileGraveyardCardsEffect(TARGET_PLAYER_ALL_MATCHING, filter)` for Thought Distortion's filtered hand-and-graveyard exile
- `SetSelfBaseToughnessFromTargetPowerEffect(int toughnessOffset, PermanentPredicate targetPredicate)` — sets the source creature's base toughness to the target creature's current power plus the offset, indefinitely (Sentinel)
- `DealDamageToCreatureWithLeastToughnessEffect(DynamicAmount)` / `(int)` — non-targeting damage to the creature with the least effective toughness among all creatures on the battlefield. If multiple creatures are tied, the effect controller chooses one at resolution via `PermanentChoiceContext.LeastToughnessDamageChoice`. Purging Scythe
- `DestroyCreatureWithLeastPowerEffect(boolean cannotBeRegenerated)` — non-targeting destruction of one creature with the least effective power among all creatures on the battlefield. If multiple creatures are tied, the effect controller chooses one at resolution; the configured regeneration prohibition applies to both automatic and prompted destruction. Porphyry Nodes
- `ExileTopCardsChooseOneMayPlayThisTurnEffect(DynamicAmount count)` or `(int count)` — exile top N from library, then choose one exiled card to play until end of the current turn; only the chosen card receives play permission (Chandra, Flameshaper)
- `CombatAttackTargetScope.CONTROLLER` makes `MaximumCombatCreaturesEffect` count only creatures declared against the effect permanent's controller; attacks against that controller's planeswalkers are not counted (Crawlspace).
- `FlipCoinDestroyTargetOrRepeatEffect()` — Crooked Scales: flip a coin; on a win destroy target group 1 creature, on a loss offer to pay `{3}` and repeat with the same two targets, otherwise destroy target group 0 creature. Uses the existing `MayPayManaEffect` and `DestroyTargetPermanentEffect` resolution paths.
- `RegisterDelayedDestroyAllPermanentsEffect()` — registers a delayed trigger that puts `DestroyAllPermanentsEffect(new PermanentTruePredicate())` onto the stack at the beginning of the next end step. Used by Bearer of the Heavens; the wipe evaluates the battlefield when it resolves.
- `MadcapExperimentEffect()` — reveal until an artifact card, put it onto the battlefield, randomize the other revealed cards onto the bottom, then deal damage to the controller equal to the number of cards revealed (Madcap Experiment)
- `EachPlayerMayScryEffect(int)` — each player is offered a may-scry choice in active-player-first order; accepted choices reuse `ScryEffect` and its normal scry interaction. The per-player flow is carried through the pending may-ability queue (Eager Construct)
- `ReplaceControllerLossWithExileAndStartingLifeEffect()` — static loss replacement: if the controller would lose the game, exile this permanent and set that player's life total to their starting life total. Reached through `GameOutcomeService.resolveLoss` and implemented by `ExquisiteArchangelLossReplacer`; exiling the source makes it one-shot (Exquisite Archangel)
 - `EachPlayerMayScryEffect(int)` / `EachPlayerMayScryEffect.forOpponents(int)` — each player, or only opponents of the effect controller, is offered a may-scry choice in active-player-first order; accepted choices reuse `ScryEffect` and its normal scry interaction. The per-player flow is carried through the pending may-ability queue (Eager Construct, Kaya, Intangible Slayer)
`ExileTargetPlayerLibraryEffect()` — the target player exiles every card from their library; delegates to `LibraryExileSupport.exileEntireLibrary` (Jace, the Mind Sculptor).
`ShuffleTargetPlayerHandIntoLibraryEffect()` — the target player puts their hand into their library, then shuffles it through `LibraryShuffleHelper` (Jace, the Mind Sculptor).
- `CanBlockAnyNumberOfCreaturesUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — target creature can block any number of creatures this turn (one-shot, until-EOT; SPELL slot). Give No Ground
- `PreventDamageEffect.fromNonHumanSources()` — SPELL: "Prevent all damage that would be dealt this turn by non-Human sources" (Repel the Abominable). Stores a turn-scoped flag; a source is non-Human when its effective creature types do not include Human, or when a nonpermanent source card has no Human creature type. Covers combat and noncombat damage from permanents, spells, and abilities. Cleared at turn cleanup
`CopySpellEffect.asToken()` marks a spell copy as a token without granting haste or offering a retarget choice; use it for Drafna, Founder of Lat-Nam.
- `CopyControllerCastSpellOnSpellCastEffect.asArtifactToken(CardPredicate)` — optional controller spell-cast copy that becomes a token and gains artifact as an additional type (Tawnos, the Toymaker)
- `AlternativeCostForSpellsEffect.onceDuringControllerTurn(CardPredicate)` — zero alternative cost usable once during each turn of the source controller (One with the Multiverse)
- `WebSlingingEffect(String manaCost, CardPredicate filter)` — static permission to use the mana cost by returning a tapped creature controlled by the caster to its owner's hand for a matching spell; read directly by `CastingCostService`/`SpellCastingService`.
- `PlayLandsFromTopOfLibraryEffect()` — static permission to play the controller's top library card when it is a land; normal land-play timing and land-per-turn limits still apply (One with the Multiverse)
- `AlternativeCostForSpellsEffect.allowedZones` — optional source-zone restriction for an alternative cost; One with the Multiverse's once-during-controller-turn free cast is limited to `HAND` and `LIBRARY`
- `LookAtTopCardsEffect.mayRevealOneToHandRestOnBottomRandom(count, predicate, effectIfNoCardChosen)` — optional one-card matching reveal to hand, random-bottom the rest, and resolve the supplied follow-up when no card is chosen or no match is found (Contagious Vorrac's land reveal plus proliferate)
- `LookAtTopCardsEffect.mayChooseAnyNumberToHandRestToGraveyardPayLife(count, lifePerSelectedCard)` — optional any-number hand choice that pays separately for each selected card; unselected cards go to the graveyard (Moonlight Bargain)
- `LookAtTopCardsEffect.mayPutUpToMatchingOntoBattlefieldTappedRestOnBottomRandom(count, predicate, maxCount, recordChosenCount)` — optional up-to-`maxCount` matching cards onto the battlefield tapped, random-bottom the rest, and optionally record the number selected on the resolving stack entry (Expand the Sphere)
- `LookAtTopCardsEffect.putOneIntoGraveyardRestOnTop(count)` — mandatory pick of one card from the top `count` for the graveyard, with the unchosen cards reordered on top (Celestus Sanctifier)
`ExileBottomCardsToSourceEffect(DynamicAmount)` — exile that many cards from the controller's library bottom and track them with the source permanent (The Temporal Anchor's scry trigger; use `EventValue` for the number bottomed)
- `DiscardHandThenDrawAndReturnArtifactOrCreatureCardsEffect()` — discard the controller's hand, draw that many, then optionally choose one discarded artifact or creature at each of mana values 1, 2, and 3 to return simultaneously to the battlefield. Queen Kayla bin-Kroog
`MillControllerThenIfMilledEffect` also accepts a `DynamicAmount` count; use `XValue()` for X mill spells and `EventValue()` in a synchronous follow-up for the number of matching milled cards. Liliana's Indignation uses `LoseLifeEffect(Scaled(EventValue(), 2), TARGET_PLAYER)`.
`PayXManaReturnTargetCreatureWithManaValueXEffect(CardPredicate filter)` — resolution-time optional pay-X return: after paying X, choose a matching creature card with mana value X from your graveyard and return it during the same resolution. Used by Relentless Dead.
- `ReduceOwnColoredCastCostEffect(ManaColor, DynamicAmount)` — spell-self reduction of matching colored mana symbols by the evaluated amount, spilling into generic mana after the colored component is exhausted. Khalni Hydra counts green creatures you control.
- `PreventFixedDamagePerSourceToControllerAndCreaturesEffect(int amount)` — STATIC: "If a source would deal damage to you or a creature you control, prevent N of that damage" (Hedron-Field Purists). Implements `ControllerAndCreaturesDamagePreventionEffect`; evaluated through conditional static effects at damage time and applied from both damage-prevention choke points, covering combat and noncombat damage
- `ReduceDamageToControlledCreaturesEffect(int amount)` — STATIC replacement: "If a source would deal damage to a creature you control, it deals that much damage minus N to that creature instead" (Lashknife Barrier). Implements `ControlledCreaturesDamageReductionEffect`; affects creatures only, applies to combat, noncombat, spell, and ability damage, and still applies when damage cannot be prevented
`OpponentCausedDiscardTriggerEffect(CardEffect)` — trigger-only `ON_CONTROLLER_DISCARDS` wrapper; fires only when an opponent's spell or ability caused the controller's discard, then queues the wrapped effect as one triggered ability (Spiritual Focus).
`DamageSourceControllerGainsControlOfDamagedPermanentEffect()` — ON_DEALT_DAMAGE marker: whenever a source deals damage to this creature, that source's controller gains permanent control of it (Crag Saurian). Implements `DamageSourceControllerAwareEffect`.
`TriggeringSpellControllerSacrificesPermanentsEffect(DynamicAmount count, PermanentPredicate filter)` / `(int count, PermanentPredicate filter)` — a `TriggeringSpellReferencingEffect` for becomes-target triggers: the controller of the triggering spell or ability sacrifices matching permanents, reusing `SacrificePermanentsEffectHandler` and its choice flow. Used by Lava Runner for one land.
- `CrewAndSaddlePowerModifierEffect` — static pilot-style modifier used only while the source creature pays a crew or saddle cost; `powerBonus()` adds to the contribution and `usesToughnessInsteadOfPower()` can replace power with effective toughness. Impls `PowerBoostForCrewAndSaddleEffect` and `UseToughnessForCrewAndSaddleEffect`.
- `DealDamageToEachPlayerWithoutMaxSpeedEffect(int damage)` — deals damage to each player whose speed is below max speed, using the normal damage pipeline. Used by Outpace Oblivion.
- `CumulativeUpkeepEffect.putCardsFromSingleGraveyard(int cardsPerAge)` — cost is `PutCardsFromSingleGraveyardOnBottomOfLibraryCost(cardsPerAge, ageCounters)`: choose exactly `cardsPerAge` cards from one graveyard separately for each age-counter payment, then put all chosen cards on the bottoms of their owners' libraries (Jötun Grunt)
- `PutCardFromGraveyardOnBottomOfLibraryCost()` — cost for putting one card from the controller's graveyard on the bottom of its owner's library; used with `ForcedCostOrElseEffect`, and an empty graveyard makes the cost unpayable (Anurid Scavenger)
- `PutCardsFromGraveyardOnBottomOfLibraryCost(int count)` — cost for putting exactly `count` cards from the controller's graveyard on the bottom of that player's library; used with `ForcedCostOrElseEffect`, and too few cards makes the cost unpayable (Gurzigost)
- `CantBeAttackedWhileAttachedToCreatureEffect()` — STATIC attack-target restriction: the source permanent cannot be selected as a combat attack target while attached to a creature; `AttackLegalityService` reads it from the potential target's own STATIC effects
`RegisterDelayedUnblockedAttackerGainLifeEffect()` — Delif's Cone: a target-aware delayed trigger for the chosen creature's unblocked attack; it queues a may containing `GainLifeEffect(SourcePower())` and `AssignNoCombatDamageEffect()`, and expires at turn cleanup.
- `RegisterDelayedChooseOpponentGainsControlOfSourceEffect()` registers a delayed next-end-step trigger controlled by the activator; when it resolves, that player chooses an opponent to gain control of the source permanent (Rainbow Vale).
- `RevealRandomCardFromTargetPlayerHandDealDamageEqualToManaValueEffect()` - target player reveals a card at random from their hand, then takes damage equal to that card's mana value; empty hand or a land card means no damage (Planeswalker's Fury)
- RIX-specific reusable effects include `AscendEffect`, `CraftyCutpurseEffect`, `DoubleSelfCombatDamageToPlayersEffect`, `DestroyRandomAttackingCreatureEffect`, `EachOpponentLosesLifeEqualToCardsInTheirGraveyardEffect`, `EachPlayerChoosesCreaturesWithTotalPowerAtMostThenSacrificeRestEffect`, `ExileCardFromHandWithSourceEffect`, `ExileCreatedPermanentsAtEndStepUnlessConditionEffect`, `ExileTargetPermanentMayCastWithoutPayingManaCostEffect`, `ExileTargetPlayerHandFaceDownWithSourceThenDrawEffect`, `ExileTopCardOfEachPlayersLibraryAndMayCastSpellsEffect`, `ExileTopCardsFromEnchantedCreatureOwnerAndAllowCastEffect`, `GrantTriggeredAbilityToCastSpellEffect`, `LosesAllNonManaAbilitiesEffect`, `OpponentsCantCastSpellTypesNextTurnEffect`, `PayXManaGainXLifeAndDrawXCardsEffect`, `PlaneswalkerLoyaltyAbilitiesCantBeActivatedEffect`, `PutSelfOnBottomOfOwnersLibraryCost`, `RevealTopCardPutIntoHandAndDrainOpponentsEffect`, `SacrificeTargetPermanentAtEndStepIfManaValueAtMostEffect`, `SearchOutsideGameToHandEffect`, and `TargetCreatureDealsPowerDamageToAbilityControllerEffect`. See `EFFECTS_INDEX.md` for construction and behavior.
- `GuardianAngelPermissionEffect()` - SPELL (benign ANY_TARGET): records the controller and target so the controller may repeatedly pay `{1}` to add one damage-prevention shield to that same permanent or player until end of turn. The special action is handled by `GameService.payGuardianAngel` and cleared by `TurnCleanupService`.
`GainControlOfTargetCreatureOfChosenPlayerEffect(ControlDuration duration, int targetGroup)` gives the ability controller control of the creature in the selected target group, chosen by the player in target group 0; resolution rechecks the opponent-control relationship (Preacher).
- `CastFromLibraryWhileSearchingEffect()` â€” static marker: while its card's owner is searching their library, they may cast that card from the library by paying its normal mana cost; the permission applies before a card is found during that search (Panglacial Wurm, `CSP`)
`PutCountersOnDragonOfEachColorEffect()` — puts one +1/+1 counter on a controller-chosen Dragon of each color, recording the distinct Dragons that received counters for its conditional win rider (Call the Spirit Dragons)
`WinGameIfEventValueAtLeastEffect(int threshold)` — wins the game for the resolving effect's controller when the current stack entry's recorded event value reaches the threshold; delegates to the normal win-game checks
`DyingCreatureCountersAwareEffect` — ally-creature-death capability used when an effect needs the dying permanent's complete concrete counter snapshot before stacking; implemented by `MoveDyingSourceCountersToTargetCreatureEffect`
- `RegisterDelayedAttackTokenCreationEffect(int amount, CreateTokenEffect tokenEffect, boolean sacrificeAtEndStep)` — registers "whenever you attack this turn, create [amount] [tokenEffect] tokens that are tapped and attacking". The attack-trigger path asks for the attack target of each token and can schedule the created tokens for sacrifice at the next end step. Used by Dalkovan Encampment.
`PayXManaDealXDamageToAnyTargetEffect(String manaCost[, TargetPredicate targetRestriction])` uses any target by default and accepts a narrower target predicate such as `TargetPredicates.player()` for Electro, Assaulting Battery.
`LandPlayFromExileTriggerEffect(List<CardEffect>)` — `ON_CONTROLLER_PLAYS_LAND` trigger descriptor for "whenever you play a land from exile"; the land-play event carries its source-zone marker so ordinary land plays do not trigger it. Gwen Stacy // Ghost-Spider.
`PlayFromOutsideHandTriggerEffect(List<CardEffect>)` — `ON_CONTROLLER_CASTS_SPELL` / `ON_CONTROLLER_PLAYS_LAND` trigger descriptor for "whenever you cast a spell or play a land from anywhere other than your hand"; checks the spell cast zone or land-play source zone before queuing the resolved effects. Shadow of the Goblin.
- `UntapControlledTapOpponentTargetsEffect()` - for each target, untap it if the effect controller controls it and tap it otherwise; supports abilities that target mixed permanents and branch on their controller (Teferi, Who Slows the Sunset +1)
`OpponentChoosesPermanentToSacrificeEffect(PermanentPredicate filter)` — non-targeting resolution choice: the controller chooses an opponent, then that opponent chooses one matching permanent controlled by the controller to sacrifice. One matching permanent auto-sacrifices; multiple matching permanents prompt the chosen opponent. Used by Demonic Hordes.
- `BecomeTargetPermanentCopyOfTriggeringSpellUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — the chosen permanent becomes a copy of the permanent spell that caused a `SpellCastTriggerEffect` until end of turn; use the optional target group and carry the triggering spell id through the multi-target trigger context. The copy reverts at cleanup and respects `isCantBeCopied()` (The Myriad Pools)
- `SelfExiledWhileActivatingCraftAbilityEffect(CardEffect wrapped)` — trigger descriptor for a wrapped effect that fires from `ON_SELF_LEAVES_BATTLEFIELD` only when the source is exiled as a craft material while activating a craft ability. Used by Market Gnome
- `PermanentAttacksPlayerWithMostLifePredicate()` / `PermanentAttacksWhileSourceControllerHasMostLifePredicate()` — attack-trigger filters for direct player attacks tied for the highest life total among all players, used by Preacher of the Schism; attacks against planeswalkers and battles do not match
- `OpponentChoosesPermanentToExileUntilSourceLeavesEffect(PermanentPredicate filter)` — non-targeting resolution choice: the controller chooses an opponent, then that opponent chooses one matching permanent controlled by the controller to exile and track with the source until its leaves-the-battlefield return effect resolves. Used by Wormfang Crab.
- RIX-specific reusable effects include `AscendEffect`, `CraftyCutpurseEffect`, `DoubleSelfCombatDamageToPlayersEffect`, `DestroyRandomAttackingCreatureEffect`, `EachOpponentLosesLifeEqualToCardsInTheirGraveyardEffect`, `EachPlayerChoosesCreaturesWithTotalPowerAtMostThenSacrificeRestEffect`, `ExileCardFromHandWithSourceEffect`, `ExileCreatedPermanentsAtEndStepUnlessConditionEffect`, `ExileTargetPermanentMayCastWithoutPayingManaCostEffect`, `ExileTargetPlayerHandFaceDownWithSourceThenDrawEffect`, `ExileTopCardOfEachPlayersLibraryAndMayCastSpellsEffect`, `ExileTopCardsFromEnchantedCreatureOwnerAndAllowCastEffect`, `GrantTriggeredAbilityToCastSpellEffect`, `LosesAllNonManaAbilitiesEffect`, `OpponentsCantCastSpellTypesNextTurnEffect`, `PlayersCantCastSpellTypesUntilEndOfYourNextTurnEffect`, `PayXManaGainXLifeAndDrawXCardsEffect`, `PlaneswalkerLoyaltyAbilitiesCantBeActivatedEffect`, `PutSelfOnBottomOfOwnersLibraryCost`, `RevealTopCardPutIntoHandAndDrainOpponentsEffect`, `SacrificeTargetPermanentAtEndStepIfManaValueAtMostEffect`, `SearchOutsideGameToHandEffect`, and `TargetCreatureDealsPowerDamageToAbilityControllerEffect`. See `EFFECTS_INDEX.md` for construction and behavior.
- `RemoveUpToCountersFromTargetEffect(int maxAmount, PermanentPredicate permanentPredicate)` — target an opponent or an artifact, creature, or planeswalker and remove up to the cap across any counter kinds present. The controller chooses an amount for each kind in turn; player targets support poison and energy counters. Price of Betrayal uses `(5, artifactOrCreatureOrPlaneswalker)`
- `PutTriggeringCardFromGraveyardOnBottomOfLibraryEffect()` models the mandatory ally-creature-death trigger that moves the dying card to its owner's library bottom; the death collector binds the card ID and resolution fizzles if it left the graveyard.
- `TargetPermanentBecomesSubtypeEffect(CardSubtype)` — targeted permanent, indefinite type-replacing subtype effect for wording such as “becomes a [subtype]”; pair with a targeted card-type setter when the permanent also becomes a creature.
- `GrantProtectionFromChosenColorUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — target creature gains protection from the source permanent's chosen color until end of turn, using the source snapshot when a sacrifice cost removes it (Floating Shield)
`RemoveProtectionFromColorUntilEndOfTurnEffect(CardColor)` — target creature loses all protection from the specified color until end of turn, including printed and static protection; later protection grants can re-add it. Cephalid Snitch.
- `ReturnNControlledPermanentsToHandEffect(int count, PermanentPredicate filter, String noun)` — mandatory resolution-time choice of `count` matching permanents controlled by the resolving player; if fewer than `count` are available, returns all of them. Uses `MultiPermanentChoiceContext.ReturnNControlledPermanentsToHand`
- `ExilePermanentYouControlAndTrackWithSourceEffect(PermanentPredicate filter)` — non-targeting resolution-time choice: the controller chooses exactly one matching permanent they control, exiles it, and tracks it with the source until the source leaves. Wormfang Newt.
`SearchLibraryForAuraToBattlefieldAttachedToSourceOrHandEffect()` — searches for an Aura that can enchant the source permanent; it enters attached to the source if it remains on the battlefield, otherwise the card is revealed and put into hand (Auratouched Mage).
- `GrantProtectionChoiceToTargetAndSharingCreaturesUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — targeted Radiance grant: choose one colour, then the target creature and every other creature on the battlefield sharing at least one effective colour with it gain protection from that colour until end of turn. The sharing set is fixed on resolution; a colourless target affects only itself (Bathe in Light)
- `CantBlockTargetAndSharingCreaturesUntilEndOfTurnEffect()` — targeted Radiance one-shot: marks the target creature and every other creature sharing at least one effective color with it so they can't block this turn. The affected set is snapshotted on resolution across all battlefields; a colorless target affects only itself. Used by Incite Hysteria
`CounterUnlessDiscardsHandEffect()` — counter target spell unless its controller discards their hand; the controller may accept even with an empty hand (Perplex)
